<p> Ottawa Citizen - RSS Feedhttps : **28;17382;TOOLONG , 24 Mar 2020 21:30:49 +0000Responding to new COVID-19 reality , Ottawa restaurants launch local delivery servicehttp : **33;17412;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;17447;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef Harriet Clunie " alt= " Chef Harriet Clunie " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;17483;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1588507 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;17513;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mandated last week to offer only take-out and delivery service during the COVID-19 crisis , a handful of chef-run Ottawa restaurants have launched a local delivery service that competes with Uber Eats and its peers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Love Local Delivery now brings food from a selection of restaurants and is looking to add others to its roster . So far , food is available for delivery from the Lowertown restaurant Das Lokal , the Vanier taco eatery Ola Cocina , brunch place Bibi&amp;rsquo ; s and Italian restaurant North and Navy , both in Centretown , the innovative Canadian cuisine-themed Grey Jay Hospitality in Little Italy , Grunt in Mechanicsville and Heartbreakers Pizza on Parkdale Avenue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The basic flat fee for deliveries within a five-kilometre radius of each restaurant is $5 , with deliveries beyond that range available at extra cost . The restaurants have partnered with Responsible Choice , an Ottawa service that had driven home the cars of people who had gone to bars or restaurants and had alcohol . You can visit the &lt;a href= " https : **33;17549;TOOLONG ... Local Delivery website&lt;/a&gt; but have to phone the restaurants to order . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurants are offering a range of items other than dishes from their menus , including ready-to eat dinners for up to six people , frozen prepared meals and meal kits . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;17584;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;17626;TOOLONG ... respond to Glebe bookstore 's blunt appeal for **25;17661;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;17688;TOOLONG ... It&amp;rsquo ; s really just marketing . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants are among the innumerable small businesses reeling from the impact of COVID-19 . Most eateries closed entirely after Ontario&amp;rsquo ; s chief medical officer of health recommended last week that dining rooms be shut for an unspecified period of time to help prevent the spread of the virus . After , many restaurant workers were laid off for the foreseeable future and left to file for Employment Insurance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last week , a Gofundme campaign was launched in the hopes of amassing a $350,000 relief fund for Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s minimum wage workers including restaurant and bar workers , small business owners and self-employed people , with the intention of giving requesters $1,000 grants to tide them over . It has collected $3,265 so far . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Even if EI is a possibility for some , 55 per cent of minimum wage is not going to be enough to live on , &amp;rdquo ; says the campaign&amp;rsquo ; s web page . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumTue , 24 Mar 2020 21:30:49 **45;17785;TOOLONG ... NewsNewsOttawa chef to star on reboot of Cook Like a Chefhttp : **36;17832;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;17870;TOOLONG ... title= " Katie Ardington , executive chef for Bekta , Play and Gezellig , is one of four chefs that will star in ' Cook Like A Chef ' , a show that focuses on secrets of the cooking world . " alt= " Katie Ardington , executive chef for Bekta , Play and Gezellig , is one of four chefs that will star in ' Cook Like A Chef ' , a show that focuses on secrets of the cooking world . She 's photographed here at Bekta. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;17906;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1577698 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;17936;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the early 2000s , when Katie Ardington was training to be a chef , she supplemented her studies at Algonquin College&amp;rsquo ; s culinary program with a steady diet of one particular cooking show on TV. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ardington&amp;rsquo ; s taste in viewing ran to Cook Like a Chef , a made-in-Toronto hit on Food Network Canada that featured professional chefs , including Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Ren&amp;eacute ; Rodriguez , demonstrating their abilities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I loved the fact it was actual and relatable chefs teaching and sharing their knowledge . It was helpful , and overall a nice reference point for techniques , &amp;rdquo ; says Ardington , now the executive chef at Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Beckta restaurant group . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now , in a rebooted Cook Like a Chef that began airing in early March , Ardington herself will be on the show&amp;rsquo ; s roster of new-generation stars ( her episode , specifically , will air March 27 ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Making her debut as a television chef , Ardington spent a week last fall filming her five episodes at the east-end Ottawa studio of Gusto Worldwide Media , the company behind the new series . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;17972;TOOLONG ... title= " Katie Ardington , executive chef for Bekta , Play and Gezellig , is one of four chefs that will star in ' Cook Like A Chef ' , a show that focuses on secrets of the cooking world . She 's photographed here at Bekta. " alt= " Katie Ardington , executive chef for Bekta , Play and Gezellig , is one of four chefs that will star in ' Cook Like A Chef ' , a show that focuses on secrets of the cooking world . She 's photographed here at Bekta. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;18008;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1577698 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; An intense week of work called for Ardington to film five episodes , each pegged to a different ingredient , following two rehearsal days . Ardington shared her expertise on chocolate , white fish , mushrooms , beef tenderloin and eggs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m very passionate about eggs , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; Although it was a simple ingredient , it&amp;rsquo ; s one of my favourite ingredients . It has such multiple uses in the kitchen . It was fun to show my little tricks . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even as Ardington was teaching , she was learning &amp;mdash ; the ins and outs of making a TV show , that is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The show was definitely a learning experience , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; It was exciting to see what goes into the production . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before a sequence during the chocolate episode was shot last fall , Chris Knight , Gusto&amp;rsquo ; s president and CEO and the creator of the original Cook Like a Chef series , pitched in , leading Ardington and the show&amp;rsquo ; s crew as they walked and talked through what the sequence would involve , as if it were an intricate piece of choreography , set around a large oval table . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It happens really , really fast . You only get one shot , &amp;rdquo ; Knight said of his live-to-tape , 30-minute show . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While there was no hard-and-fast script to follow , there were topics to cover &amp;mdash ; how to make a chocolate bowl , how to make chocolate dirt &amp;mdash ; and interactions between Ardington and her assisting chefs that were must-dos . Knight gave Ardington her line : &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s all chocolate , all the time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 480 " src= " https : **34;18038;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;18074;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Cook Like a Chef is his baby , &amp;rdquo ; Ardington says of Knight . &amp;ldquo ; It was one of his shows that he&amp;rsquo ; s most proud of and loves , he really wants to be involved in it . &amp;rdquo ; She adds that it was easy to take Knight&amp;rsquo ; s direction and sync up with his vision for the show and how she could talk about food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In an interview , Knight says that creating the original Cook Like a Chef , which still airs on selected streaming services , was a highlight of his 22-year career in food television . The early 2000s show , which was shot on a Toronto sound stage , was ahead of its time and so complicated to make that it was never copied , Knight says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It took me this long ( to reboot it ) because it was so iconic and it stood out . I was afraid to mess with it , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; This is theatre in the round . You have to be able to move and speak with joy and passion . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Knight says he interviewed roughly 150 chefs as potential stars for the reboot , which he calls a modern update &amp;ldquo ; true to the concept of Cook Like a Chef , &amp;rdquo ; but with better cameras , new music and more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Knight knew Ardington not only because both are Ottawa-based . The chef&amp;rsquo ; s sister , Steph Brown , has worked at Gusto for five years as a production manager and showrunner . Still , Ardington auditioned as did the other chefs , and she was picked , along with Montreal-based chefs Derek Dammann and Natalia Machado and Toronto&amp;rsquo ; s Craig Wong. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve always thought she had this quiet confidence , &amp;rdquo ; Knight says of Ardington. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; She obviously has the culinary chops , &amp;rdquo ; he adds . In addition to working almost 15 years in the Beckta group , Ardington , a Brockville native , did a stint several years ago as the chef at 24 Sussex Drive , cooking for Prime Minister Justin Trudeau and his family . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In addition to appearing on the CTV Life channel , the new Cook Life a Chef will be available internationally on Gusto TV in the U.S. , the U.K. , Latin America and Singapore . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But even if Ardington&amp;rsquo ; s profile as a TV chef rises , she doesn&amp;rsquo ; t see herself setting aside her restaurant work . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t do it ( Cook Like a Chef ) to open more opportunities , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &amp;ldquo ; I did it because that show alone was very exciting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ll see what happens , &amp;rdquo ; she continues . &amp;ldquo ; But my passion stays in restaurant kitchens and it will be hard to ever leave that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;18110;TOOLONG ... to get one of the first six Ontario assessment centres for COVID-19&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;18145;TOOLONG ... cook charged with seven voyeurism counts&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;18180;TOOLONG ... COVID-19 briefing : Essential points&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 12 Mar 2020 15:49:57 **45;18215;TOOLONG ... dining diary : Ikanos rolls out a deluxe seafood menu during Montreal En Lumierehttp : **33;18262;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;18297;TOOLONG ... title= " Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " alt= " Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18333;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568307 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last fall , I had the great fortune to enjoy the fabled tasting menu at the Los Angeles restaurant Providence , a mecca for seafood lovers that has two Michelin stars to its name . Since then , I&amp;rsquo ; ve wondered when I would have another seafood experience that I could mention in the same breath . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Happily , I can now mention the exceptional tasting menu I had last week at Ikanos in Montreal , during the Montreal En Lumiere festival of culture and gastronomy. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Granted , my impeccable meal at Providence , which I wrote about &lt;a href= " https : **33;18363;TOOLONG ... , was completely over the top . Sashimi of Japanese Amberjack belly with shaved white truffle . Morsels of sea urchin and box crab under a canopy of scallop mousseline and caviar . Roasted carrot &lt;span class= " " &gt;coconut curry with spiny lobster . And that 's just a few of the courses in a calvacade of delicacies. &lt;br&gt;&lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " " &gt;But w&lt;/span&gt; hile dinner at Ikanos was not as exalted as at Providence , it was also roughly a quarter of the price , factoring in the exchange rate . And I don&amp;rsquo ; t think anyone would have felt shortchanged after trying , say , grilled Dungeness crab and Greek sea bream , or monkfish Rossini . The Ikanos dinner , at $94 , was an absolute steal for seafood-loving gastronomes , and a stellar example of the admirable standards of fine-dining in Canada , even if Michelin hasn&amp;rsquo ; t yet deigned to come in and assess our country&amp;rsquo ; s best and most interesting restaurants . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here&amp;rsquo ; s a rundown of the dishes featured in Ikanos&amp;rsquo ; six-course tasting menu , to be served until March 1 when the Montreal En Lumiere festival , which invited me as a guest , concludes : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18398;TOOLONG ... title= " Oyster tart at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " alt= " Oyster tart at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18434;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568306 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; This little beaut began the meal with a sophisticated pop of rich pastry suffused with bivalve-y brine , and sealed the deal with dollops of creme fraiche and Northern Divine caviar farmed sustainably by the Sechelt , B.C. , company of the same name . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;div&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18464;TOOLONG ... title= " Sea urchin course at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " alt= " Sea urchin course at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18500;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568308 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m always hopeful but cautious when I&amp;rsquo ; m served sea urchin . Great specimens are like ambrosia , but lesser examples can taste of iodine . Ikanos&amp;rsquo ; sea urchin was very good , and&amp;nbsp ; the combination with roasted butternut squash and hazelnut was novel and satisfying . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18530;TOOLONG ... title= " Dungeness crab at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Dungeness crab at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18566;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568316 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Grilled Dungeness crab arrived in a tabletop smoker and smelled as great as it tasted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18596;TOOLONG ... title= " Grilled sea bream at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " alt= " Grilled sea bream at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18632;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568305 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Ikanos&amp;rsquo ; kitchen is something called a Josper oven , which is basically a charcoal grill and oven in one . It worked its indoor barbecue magic on Dungeness crab , and on the grilled and simply served imported Greek sea bream . Not shown in the photo is the bowl of farm-to-table vegetables that accompanied the fish , which good enough to almost upstage it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18662;TOOLONG ... title= " Monkfish Rossini at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " alt= " Monkfish Rossini at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18698;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568317 " /&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;div&gt;&lt;span class= " st " &gt;&lt;br&gt; In French cuisine , tournedos Rossini at its essence involves some pan-fried filet mignon topped with seared foie gras.The monkfish Rossini served at Ikanos was a clever seafood version that topped a meaty slab of monkfish with a chunk of monkfish liver , which is after all , the foie of the sea . Whelks , much beloved in Montreal , mushrooms and a reduction of red wine and fish fumet finished the hefty dish . **35;18728;TOOLONG &lt;div&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18765;TOOLONG ... title= " Dessert at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " alt= " Dessert at Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18801;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568315 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " doctext " &gt;&lt;span class= " dochighlight " &gt;I have a seafood-averse friend who could n't believe that the dessert at Ikanos combined white chocolate , passion fruit and yuzu with a foam of oyster and salicorn ( also known as sea asparagus or sea beans ) . I guess that foam is like the oyster-y equivalent of the bacon garnish that dotted desserts years ago and thankfully is on the outs . For me the jury is still out on oyster foam with sweets -- I do n't know if the dessert would have suffered without it -- but I do appreciate the froth of audacity and the fact that the meal started with oysters and ended with oysters , if not with oysters on the half-shell. **32;18831;TOOLONG &g; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ikanos is a lovely , elegant space . &lt;span class= " doctext " &gt;&lt;span class= " dochighlight " &gt;Opened in the summer of 2014 , it seats 80 in what was formerly an high-ceilinged artist 's loft . **26;18865;TOOLONG Service , which included a visit to the table by chef/co-owner Constant Mentzas was impeccable and personable &amp;mdash ; as polished and winning as my dinner . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;18893;TOOLONG ... title= " Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " alt= " Ikanos during 2020 's Montreal en Lumiere festival " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;18929;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1568307 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I don&amp;rsquo ; t know when I&amp;rsquo ; ll dine again at Providence , which , after all , would require a flight across the continent before I could dig into its caviar and truffles . Until then , I think visits to the very laudable and much more affordable Ikanos would do very well . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Peter Hum ate at Ikanos as a guest of Montreal En Lumiere. &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;18959;TOOLONG " **32;18986;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 28 Feb 2020 17:00:24 **45;19020;TOOLONG ... of Ottawa 's modern franchise : What 's behind Ottawa 's changing food scenehttp : **35;19067;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19104;TOOLONG ... title= " Pi Co is a new pizza franchise that has spread from Toronto to Ottawa . Chris McCrudden opened his first location in late January and plans to open a second one downtown this spring . A margherita pizza . " alt= " Pi Co is a new pizza franchise that has spread from Toronto to Ottawa . Chris McCrudden opened his first location in late January and plans to open a second one downtown this spring . A margherita pizza . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;19140;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1562659 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;19170;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the beginning of this year , quietly but clearly , franchise fever climbed up a notch in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Westboro , workers at the brand new Pi Co. stretched dough balls to make Neapolitan pizzas , then slid pies with their customer-chosen toppings into a sleek white ceramic pizza oven for a 90-second spin in 715 F heat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I decided to venture into something new . I wanted to make people happy on the spot , &amp;rdquo ; says Pi Co. &amp;rsquo ; s owner-operator Chris McCrudden , explaining his switch from working in retail to opening a restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 37-year-old already plans to open a second location downtown , at Metcalfe and Nepean streets , this spring , and has larger dreams of expanding into the suburbs . &amp;ldquo ; The plan is to scale across the city , &amp;rdquo ; McCrudden says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He adds : &amp;ldquo ; Everyone likes pizza and nobody&amp;rsquo ; s doing this . You can&amp;rsquo ; t find pizza like this anywhere . &amp;rdquo ; That is , except in Toronto , where Pi Co. has opened 16 locations since 2016. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19206;TOOLONG ... title= " Pi Co is a new pizza franchise that has spread from Toronto to Ottawa . Chris McCrudden opened his first location in late January and plans to open a second one downtown this spring . " alt= " Pi Co is a new pizza franchise that has spread from Toronto to Ottawa . Chris McCrudden opened his first location in late January and plans to open a second one downtown this spring . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;19242;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1563197 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , in the ByWard Market , staff at the just-opened Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s Fresh Pasta To Go were doling out spanking fresh , made-in-house rigatoni , spaghetti , fusilli and linguine swaddled in pools of sauce in their take-out cartons , following the processes and recipes chef Gabriele Dal Moro instituted at his tiny store in Venice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t find something like that in North America , &amp;rdquo ; says Santhosh Reddy , the Toronto-based master franchisor for Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Reddy asserts that while you can get fresh pasta at some restaurants and quick-service pasta at many others , Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s is the only business that unites fresh pasta and quick service . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a unique model . There&amp;rsquo ; s nobody who&amp;rsquo ; s doing it right now , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Reddy visited Venice in late 2016 to celebrate his wedding anniversary and liked chef Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s business and food there so much that he resolved to bring the concept to Canada . By the spring of 2018 , Reddy had opened the first of his four Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s in Toronto . He says he envisions opening two more Ottawa locations in the next two years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa has no shortage of pizza and pasta eateries , either independently operated or run as a corporate chain location or franchise . But Pi Co. and Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s , which are the inaugural Ottawa locations of relatively new Toronto-based companies , are part of a larger wave of recently arrived franchises of companies that are headquartered elsewhere in Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19272;TOOLONG ... title= " Mohammad Shaik makes a fresh batch of spaghetti to be served in dishes of Dal Moro 's fresh pasta to go , apparently a unique concept that serves fresh pasta in a quick service model . " alt= " Mohammad Shaik makes a fresh batch of spaghetti to be served in dishes of Dal Moro 's fresh pasta to go , apparently a unique concept that serves fresh pasta in a quick service model . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;19308;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1563223 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the last four or five years , restaurant industry watchers say , franchise restaurants , both next-generation and old-guard , have expanded their presence in Canada , especially in urban centres , in sync with changing demographics and dining-out preferences . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Millennials with specific preferences when they go out to eat &amp;mdash ; think predilections for convenience , speedy service , vegan and vegetarian options , organic ingredients , sustainable eating &amp;mdash ; are creating demand for new kinds of franchises that feel very 2020 . ( Among the earliest franchise locations to open in Canada were A&amp;amp ; W in 1956 , McDonald&amp;rsquo ; s in 1967 and the Canadian company Pizza Pizza in the late 1960s. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Demand , too , is fuelled by the cultural diversification of urban centres . The multicultural breadth of restaurants may be most striking in Vancouver or Toronto , but Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s dining-out scene is arguably responding to a change in the population makeup . Why else , for example , would the Taiwanese bubble tea franchises Presotea , Chatime and CoCo come to town in recent years , or , for that matter , the Montreal-based globally minded sandwich franchise Centrale Bergham ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here&amp;rsquo ; s another sign of the shift in restaurant franchises : It would have been laughable , years ago , for this newspaper&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant critic to review an East Side Mario&amp;rsquo ; s , or for that matter , a Milestones . But in the last few years , I&amp;rsquo ; ve reviewed franchises including Sansotei , the Centretown ramen mainstay , and Yunshang Rice Noodle , a few blocks south on Bank Street , where Chinese &amp;ldquo ; Crossing the Bridge&amp;rdquo ; noodles are the delicious specialty . I&amp;rsquo ; d be missing out if I didn&amp;rsquo ; t pay attention to these franchises , as their respective soups are arguably the best of their kind in town . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; There is a huge paradigm shift in the food service industry , &amp;rdquo ; says Altaf Sovani , academic chair of Algonquin College&amp;rsquo ; s school of hospitality and tourism . &amp;ldquo ; You have brand new , creative innovative chains coming , quick-service franchises or fast casual , they are going all over Canada now . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19338;TOOLONG ... title= " Coconut curry broth at Yunshang Rice Noodle. " alt= " Coconut curry broth at Yunshang Rice Noodle. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19374;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1563201 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Asad Amin , vice-president of Ipsos Food and Beverage group , says that as recently as four years ago , independent restaurants were outpacing chain restaurants nationally . But since then , Amin says he&amp;rsquo ; s seen stronger growth among chains outpacing independents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Independents are growing still nationally , but chains and franchises are overtaking them , &amp;rdquo ; Amin says . In particular , premium casual and upscale fast-casual restaurants are on the rise nationally , he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s Reddy , who was a business analyst before he jumped into the restaurant business , says Ottawa is ripe for not just his business but other new franchises as well . &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s more demand for new types of food . We wanted to be part of the changing scene . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That scene will include several Toronto-based franchises not far from Reddy&amp;rsquo ; s pasta business . Gyubee , an all-you-can-eat Japanese barbecue restaurant , opened early February at York and Dalhousie streets . Further south on Dalhousie , a franchise of the Taiwanese business Hot-Star , which specializes in crispy and massive chicken cutlets , is to open , continuing its expansion beyond Asia in North America . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Further afield , by this summer , the hip Toronto-based chain The Burger&amp;rsquo ; s Priest , which has more than 20 locations in the Greater Toronto Area , expects to have two locations open in Ottawa , one on Baseline Road near Clyde Avenue and then another on Bank Street near Heron Road . Elgin Street awaits a franchise of the international sandwich maker German D&amp;ouml ; ner Kebab , a few doors down from locations of the Japanese cheesecake chain Uncle Tetsu and The Fry , a franchise that sells Korean fried chicken . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The newcomers will join recent arrivals to Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s franchise landscape such as the Chocolats Favoris shops in Kanata , Barrhaven , Orl&amp;eacute ; ans and Gatineau , the over-the-top burger place La Belle et La Boeuf in Gatineau , rotisserie chicken place Benny &amp;amp ; Co. in Orl&amp;eacute ; ans and the Centrale Bergham sandwich shops . All of these businesses expanded from Quebec into the Ottawa-Gatineau area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alex Rechichi , CEO of the Crave It Restaurant Group in Oakville , calls The Burger&amp;rsquo ; s Priest , a chain in his group&amp;rsquo ; s portfolio , &amp;ldquo ; the anti-corporate corporate brand , &amp;rdquo ; because its practices , such as using &amp;ldquo ; ultra , ultra fresh&amp;rdquo ; meat ground from whole muscle rather than lesser cuts , are rooted in the award-winning and tiny original Burger&amp;rsquo ; s Priest that opened in Toronto in 2010. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We do things like an independent would , &amp;rdquo ; Rechichi says , adding that it was &amp;ldquo ; a natural progression&amp;rdquo ; for The Burger&amp;rsquo ; s Priest to come to Ottawa given the past successes that some of Rechichi&amp;rsquo ; s previous ventures , including Mucho Burrito and Extreme Pita , enjoyed here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19404;TOOLONG ... title= " A burger from The Burger 's Priest . ( Credit : The Burger 's Priest ) " alt= " A burger from The Burger 's Priest . ( Credit : The Burger 's Priest ) " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19440;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1563216 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , for all of their attributes , franchises still have their skeptics and detractors. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Not surprisingly , Stephen Beckta , one of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s leadings restaurateurs whose businesses include Beckta Dining &amp;amp ; Wine , Play Food &amp;amp ; Wine and Gezellig , prefers to support independent restaurants . &amp;ldquo ; On the whole , there is usually much more soul to them than chains that are designed and run from afar , &amp;rdquo ; Beckta says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Beckta says he doesn&amp;rsquo ; t avoid chain restaurants outright , and he even lauds The Works , which got its start in Ottawa before expanding widely across Ontario , because his son is a big fan of its burgers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But he continues : &amp;ldquo ; There are some things they ( chains and franchises ) do very well , and we independents can always learn from ( them ) , &amp;rdquo ; Beckta continues . &amp;ldquo ; But my default is always to support , and enjoy , the small chef- and owner-operated spots in Ottawa . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Similarly , Chris Knight , CEO of the Ottawa-based food-show production company Gusto Worldwide Media , places much higher value on independent restaurants over franchises and chains . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For starters , Knight points out that last month at the Canadian Culinary Championships in Ottawa , where a dozen Canadian chefs representing their respective regions vied for the champion&amp;rsquo ; s prize , none of the chef-testants came from franchise or chain operations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The innovation and creativity still remains in the smaller restaurant , typically ones where the chef still works the line and smells like onions and garlic , &amp;rdquo ; says Knight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And while franchise operators are proud to consistently serve the same food from location to location , building up the trust of customers who want a familiar , reliable experience , Knight decries the homogeneity of dining scenes that can arise . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Small companies are the ones that innovate , &amp;rdquo ; Knight contends . &amp;ldquo ; Large companies are the ones that have plastic-coated menus where the food is the same everywhere . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Knight&amp;rsquo ; s view , Lansdowne Park&amp;rsquo ; s franchise-heavy array of restaurants &amp;mdash ; from Jack Astor&amp;rsquo ; s Bar and Grill to Milestones to the pizza place Industria Pizzeria to Joey Lansdowne &amp;mdash ; is a collection of formulaic , too-similar businesses that eat into the revenues of independent restaurants not just in the neighbouring Glebe but the city at large . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s sad indeed with smaller restaurants fighting to survive , &amp;rdquo ; Knight says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19470;TOOLONG ... title= " Streetscapes 's of franchised restaurants in Lansdowne , Sunday , February 9 , 2020. " alt= " Streetscapes 's of franchised restaurants in Lansdowne , Sunday , February 9 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;19506;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1563220 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; That feeling of d&amp;eacute ; j&amp;agrave ; vu ( or deja mang&amp;eacute ; ) can rear its head when you realize that when it comes to many chains and franchises , especially in shopping mall food courts , big-box mall parking lots and highway-side rest stops , they are properties of a handful of massive restaurant companies that aggregate a multitude of brands in their portfolios . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For example , Toronto-based Recipe Unlimited Co. ( formerly Cara Operations Ltd. ) tallied 208 corporate chain locations and more than 1,100 franchise locations in its Sept. 29 , 2019 management&amp;rsquo ; s discussion and analyst statement , including locations of Swiss Chalet , Harvey&amp;rsquo ; s , Montana&amp;rsquo ; s , East Side Mario&amp;rsquo ; s , Milestones and The Keg. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Montreal-based Foodtastic has such brands as La Belle et La Boeuf , the pizzeria Monza , the sushi eatery Blossom and Big Rig Kitchen and Brewery . Last summer , Foodtastic bought a majority stake in that Ottawa-based business , launched in 2012 by former Ottawa Senators defenceman Chris &amp;ldquo ; Big Rig&amp;rdquo ; Phillips . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among Canadian franchise aggregators , the Montreal-based MTY Food Group may have the largest number of brands under its banner &amp;mdash ; almost 80 of them , ranging from Thai Express and Extreme Pita to The Works to Mucho Burrito to the steak-centric restaurants Houston and Madisons. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Ottawa , the concentration of franchises is most clearly seen in its suburbs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of Barrhaven&amp;rsquo ; s 90 restaurants , about half of them are franchises , a third of them are owner-operated , and the rest are chains , says Andrea Steenbakkers , executive director of the Barrhaven BIA. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She explains that Barrhaven , located beyond the Greenbelt and bounded by Highway 416 and the Rideau River , is its &amp;ldquo ; own little entity , &amp;rdquo ; a community of 100,000 that is less &amp;ldquo ; **30;19536;TOOLONG ; than downtown Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Not a lot of people are coming to Barrhaven for restaurants , &amp;rdquo ; she says . Restaurant-goers are largely residents of the suburb , and indeed , so too are most of the franchise owners , says Steenbakkers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These ( franchise ) restaurants are really firmly rooted in our community , &amp;rdquo ; she says , adding that the profits of these businesses ultimately circulate within the suburb&amp;rsquo ; s economy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; She sees the appeal of franchises for their operators . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a simple way for people to go into business , &amp;rdquo ; she says , with a business model , training and other aspects already well-developed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , Steenbakkers says she would like to see more owner-operated restaurants in Barrhaven , and she names the veteran pasta place La Porto A Casa , the Chinese eatery Golden Restaurant and newcomer Italian restaurant Juluca&amp;rsquo ; s as notable independent successes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Kanata North BIA , which covers March Road and the western stretch of Carling Avenue , counted 17 franchises among its list of 37 restaurants , including a Big Rig , two Subways and three Tim Hortons locations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Kanata Central BIA , which includes the Kanata Centrum and Signature Centre shopping centres , says that 16 of its 30 member restaurants are franchises. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , at Kanata Centrum , there are signs of franchise renewal . While familiar high-volume restaurants Milestones , Jack Astor&amp;rsquo ; s , Baton Rouge and Boston Pizza take up the largest lots , tucked away is the wee franchise Bangkok Buri , which exemplifies some smaller-scale but contemporary thinking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19568;TOOLONG ... title= " Mikhael Absi , manager of Bangkok Buri with a plate of penang curry , in front of one of his colourful evocative walls . " alt= " Mikhael Absi , manager of Bangkok Buri with a plate of penang curry , in front of one of his colourful evocative walls . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;19604;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1563195 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are just two Bangkok Buris &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; one at Union Station in Toronto and the Kanata location , which opened in September 2018 . ( The franchise&amp;rsquo ; s website says a Barrhaven location is in the offing. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Kanata , its operator is Mikhael Absi , a veteran of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s franchise scene who in the last 27 years has been involved in a Quiznos , an East Side Mario&amp;rsquo ; s and a Baton Rouge , to give a partial list . Absi also opened five Mucho Burritos in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Absi is Lebanese , not Thai , and he first cooked Thai food in 2018 . But he says Bangkok Buri&amp;rsquo ; s food has its own virtues , including ingredients cut freshly every day &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; nothing is pre-made , &amp;rdquo ; he says &amp;mdash ; and many vegan and gluten-free options . &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s a biggie , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bangkok Buri&amp;rsquo ; s fast-casual fare also has some foodie cred because the dishes that Absi executes were developed in collaboration with Toronto restaurateur Monte Wan , who owns the well-regarded Thai restaurants Khao San Road and Nana . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Absi&amp;rsquo ; s a staunch advocate for franchises because if they&amp;rsquo ; re good , they engender trust among their customers . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a matter of knowing who to go back to , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ipsos vice-president Amin says the increasing culinary diversity of franchises and chains such as Bangkok Buri speaks to the cross-acculturation of the dining public , which would include both expats from countries whose food is being served and &amp;ldquo ; young local Canadians , well travelled and with diverse palates&amp;rdquo ; seeking dishes they have eaten abroad or are curious to try . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Amin adds that when he looks at the uptake of dishes from other cultures , for example from Asian countries , consumption by Asian expatriates is actually declining , while demand is growing among other Canadians . The bottom line is that the changing tastes of the larger public is fuelling the success of these &amp;ldquo ; multicultural&amp;rdquo ; restaurants , Amin says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; About 100 metres away from Bangkok Buri is the first of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s Chicken &amp;amp ; Taters franchises. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19634;TOOLONG ... title= " The staff of Mary Brown 's Chicken and Taters pose for a photo with chicken . From left : Muskan Sandhu , Sukheep Kaur , Jane Arenburg , and Sam Paquette. " alt= " The staff of Mary Brown 's Chicken and Taters pose for a photo with chicken . From left : Muskan Sandhu , Sukheep Kaur , Jane Arenburg , and Sam Paquette. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;19670;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1563196 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rooted in the original Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s fried chicken eatery that opened in St. John&amp;rsquo ; s , Newfoundland five decades ago , the Kanata Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s opened in early 2019 . Some fried chicken lovers would say its arrival is long overdue &amp;mdash ; &amp;nbsp ; it is one of more than 150 Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s locations across Canada . Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s locations have since opened on Carling Avenue in the Cineplex Ottawa parking lot and in Barrhaven , at the Chapman Mills Marketplace retail district . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Like many of the recently arrived franchises , Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s stresses freshness and on-site preparation . Sahal Sidana , its manager , adds that the chicken he serves is also halal &amp;mdash ; butchered in keeping with Islamic strictures so that Muslim customers can partake. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sidana , a 24-year-old who came to Canada from India , studied culinary management at Algonquin College before he joined the Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s team . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He said he had thought of opening a restaurant of his own , but then thought better of it , when he pondered the investment required and the recipes that would have to be developed , among other things . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You&amp;rsquo ; re not sure that people even like it , &amp;rdquo ; he added . Whereas when Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s came to Kanata , there was instant buzz , Sidana says , because its crispy , golden-battered reputation preceded it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Opening a franchise is much better than opening your own restaurant , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; You don&amp;rsquo ; t need to do a lot of hard work ( that ) you can easily get through the company . There are a lot of people to help you . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A manager now , Sidana says his goal is to own a Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s or another brand of franchise one day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Franchises and chains bound for Ottawa&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Burger&amp;rsquo ; s Priest &lt;br&gt; What : Toronto-based burger chain &lt;br&gt; Where : Baseline Road near Clyde Avenue , Bank Street near Heron Road &lt;br&gt; Online : theburgerspriest.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hot-Star Chicken &lt;br&gt; What : Taiwanese massive , flattened , crispy chicken cutlets &lt;br&gt; Where : 412 Dalhousie St. &lt;br&gt; Online : hot-star.ca &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; German D&amp;ouml ; ner Kebab &lt;br&gt; What : international kebab sandwiches franchise &lt;br&gt; Where : 272 Elgin St. &lt;br&gt; Online : germandonerkebab.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Recently arrived franchises and chains&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gyubee &lt;br&gt; What : all-you-can-eat Japanese grill &lt;br&gt; Where : 95 York St. &lt;br&gt; Online : gyubeejapanesegrill.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pi Co. &lt;br&gt; What : customized Neapolitan pizzas &lt;br&gt; Where : 236 Richmond Rd. , 170 Metcalfe St. ( to come ) &lt;br&gt; Online : pi-co.ca &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dal Moro&amp;rsquo ; s Fresh Pasta To Go &lt;br&gt; What : fast-casual pasta , originated in Venice , jumped to Toronto and now Ottawa &lt;br&gt; Where : 8 ByWard Market Sq . &lt;br&gt; Online : **34;19700;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chocolats Favoris &lt;br&gt; What : Quebec-based chocolate shops &lt;br&gt; Where : 449 Hazeldean Rd . Unit E-1 ( Kanata ) , 1055 Greenbank Rd . Unit 7 ( Barrhaven ) , 180 Vanguard Rd . ( Orl&amp;eacute ; ans ) , 455 Boul. de la Gappe ( Gatineau ) , 181 Rue Principale ( Gatineau , Alymer sector ) &lt;br&gt; Online : chocolatsfavoris.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mary Brown&amp;rsquo ; s Chicken &amp;amp ; Taters &lt;br&gt; What : Maritimes-based fried chicken &lt;br&gt; Where : 80 Marketplace Ave . ( Barrhaven ) , Unit 6B Cineplex Coliseum , 400 Earl Grey Dr. , Unit B-7 &lt;br&gt; Online : marybrowns.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bangkok Buri &lt;br&gt; What : Thai noodle dishes and curries &lt;br&gt; Where : 300 Earl Grey Dr. , another location coming to Barrhaven &lt;br&gt; Online : bangkokburi.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; La Belle &amp;amp ; La Boeuf &lt;br&gt; What : Quebec-based burger joint &lt;br&gt; Where : 1100 Boul . Maloney O. , Unit 401 &lt;br&gt; Online : belleetboeuf.com &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 20 Feb 2020 12:00:04 **45;19736;TOOLONG ... Out : Bukhari , one of Ottawa 's few Yemeni restaurants , serves appealing , affordable disheshttp : **34;19783;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;19819;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb mandi , lamb broth , salad , spicy dip at Bukhari " alt= " Lamb mandi , lamb broth , salad , spicy dip at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19855;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1559997 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;19885;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 1846 Carling Ave. , 613-501-6140 , &lt;a href= " http : **34;19923;TOOLONG ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Monday to Thursday 9 a.m. to 9 p.m. , Friday to Sunday 9 a.m. to 10 p.m. &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; main courses up to $20 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; no steps to front door , washrooms &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The website for Bukhari Restaurant , which opened in late 2019 in a Carling Avenue strip mall , refers to it as a health food restaurant . Of course , that&amp;rsquo ; s not a bad thing . However , I&amp;rsquo ; m more keen to dine out when the fare is indulgent or intriguing , in addition to being good for me . I can make my own grain bowls at home , thank you very much . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But if you scroll down the eatery&amp;rsquo ; s home page , the intrigue mounts . There&amp;rsquo ; s a reference in a photo to Bukhari serving &amp;ldquo ; Arabian food . &amp;rdquo ; What would that be ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The only way for me to find out was to step away from the screen and head over to the place . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During my visits to Bukhari , it turned out that its Arabian food was generally as tasty as it was affordable and unpretentious . Also , the kind and friendly staff there were happy to specify that Bukhari served dishes with their roots in Yemen , the country that sits south of Saudi Arabia on the Arabian Peninsula , closer to Africa than it is to , say , Iran . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now , I can affirm that Bukhari serves the best Yemeni food I&amp;rsquo ; ve had , which , it should be said , is also the only Yemeni food I&amp;rsquo ; ve had . Bukhari , which replaced the top-notch casual Middle Eastern restaurant Pita Bell Kabab after its westward move on Carling Avenue , is one of the very few Yemeni restaurants in Ottawa . ( I&amp;rsquo ; ve since learned of House of Mandi on Hunt Club Road as well. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Its food is halal , prepared to Islamic strictures , and lamb and chicken are the featured meats , although a beef dish or two can also be found . The meats can be grilled , fried , steamed or stewed. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some menu items were familiar and less specifically Yemeni , including chicken and beef kebabs , chicken tikka , tabbouleh and Greek salad . But most dishes here were Yemeni &amp;mdash ; lesser known to me and listed with no descriptions . We had to ask about dishes such as chicken mandi and lamb haneed , and we were glad that we did . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before any of our orders at lunch or dinner arrived , we were given small but appetite-whetting bowls of nicely seasoned lamb broth , served with wedges of lime on the side . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s welcome soup , &amp;rdquo ; one of the female staffers told me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Speaking of appetite-whetting , the only appetizer we tried at Bukhari was its baba ghanouj ( $6 ) , which was fresher , more herbal and much less smoky than other renditions I&amp;rsquo ; ve had of that eggplant dip . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;19959;TOOLONG ... title= " Pita and house-made babaghanouj at Bukhari " alt= " Pita and house-made babaghanouj at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;19995;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1558814 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hearty appetites would do well with one of the generous platters that pair a heap of long-grain rice with bone-in lamb or chicken . In chicken bukhari ( $18 ) , the tender half-bird was tucked in a mound of mildly spiced rice with slivers of carrot . Lamb haneed ( $18 ) kept its rice , garnished with fried onions , separate from a foil packet filled with delectable pieces of lamb . Lamb mandi ( $18 ) delivered pieces of shank and rib , both flavourful and toothsome , on top of rice garnished with fried onions and slivers of almonds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20025;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb haneed at Bukhari " alt= " Lamb haneed at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20061;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1558909 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20091;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb mandi , lamb broth , salad , spicy dip at Bukhari " alt= " Lamb mandi , lamb broth , salad , spicy dip at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20127;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1559942 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20157;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken Mandi from Bukhari restaurant " alt= " Chicken Mandi from Bukhari restaurant " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20193;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1560040 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of these dishes came with plates of simply dressed green salad , dusted with tangy sumac , and a spicy , salsa-like condiment also landed on our table . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We didn&amp;rsquo ; t try Bukhari&amp;rsquo ; s biryani rice dishes , which we were told were the spiciest among the menu . Next time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bite-sized pieces of lamb figured in two very literally named and spicier dishes &amp;mdash ; fried lamb meat ( $12 ) and fried lamb liver ( $12 ) . The former was accessibly tasty and quick to disappear when scooped up with pita bread . The latter packed some mild mineral tang with its heat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20223;TOOLONG ... title= " Fried lamb meat at Bukhari restaurant " alt= " Fried lamb meat at Bukhari restaurant " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20259;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1558813 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20289;TOOLONG ... title= " Fried lamb liver at Bukhari " alt= " Fried lamb liver at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20325;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1560047 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of Bukhari&amp;rsquo ; s stews , we tried &lt;img src= " https : **34;20355;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20391;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; ogda chicken ( $10 ) , a pleasant mish-mash of bone-in chicken , potatoes , onions , zucchini and more , and fahsa ( $13 ) , a brothy and deeply savoury concoction packed with fall-apart chunks of lamb . After we dug into the fahsa , a server came with a frothy concoction that I later learned was made with the bitter herb fenugreek . The accompaniment is called holba , and by itself , it was bracingly bitter . If I order fahsa again , I&amp;rsquo ; ll trying blending some of it into the stew for another layer of flavour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20421;TOOLONG ... title= " Fahsa ( lamb stew ) at Bukhari " alt= " Fahsa ( lamb stew ) at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20457;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1560044 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bukhari serves breakfast , too , with a few dishes that are specific to the morning meal and some , such as fried lamb and lamb liver , that are served all day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We ordered shakshouka ( $6 ) , which elsewhere has been a dish of eggs in a spicy tomato sauce , and received instead something closer to a scrambled omelet , which was good and homey just the same . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20487;TOOLONG ... title= " Shakshouka eggs at Bukhari " alt= " Shakshouka eggs at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20523;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1558806 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We also had foul ( $7 ) , which was a savoury kidney bean dip served with pita . To finish , we had a very splittable dessert of arika ( $12 ) , which was a plate filled with a blend of bread , dates , and heavy cream , topped with honey and grated cheese . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20553;TOOLONG ... title= " At Bukhari , foul is a breakfast dish made with kidney beans " alt= " At Bukhari , foul is a breakfast dish made with kidney beans " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20589;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1558807 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20619;TOOLONG ... title= " Arika ( Yemeni dessert of bread , dates , heavy cream , honey and cheese ) at Bukhari " alt= " Arika ( Yemeni dessert of bread , dates , heavy cream , honey and cheese ) at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20655;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1560048 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Very little at Bukhari is ostentatious . The closest thing to fancy here is adeni tea , from Aden , the Yemeni port city , which came in an attractive gold-and-white tea set . The milky tea , lightly spiced with cardamom , ginger and more , I think , was a fine meal-ender. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;20685;TOOLONG ... title= " Adeni tea service at Bukhari " alt= " Adeni tea service at Bukhari " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;20721;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1560478 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurant is not licensed , but it does serve several flavours of Barbican , the non-alcoholic malt beverage popular in the Middle East and North Africa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Clearly , I&amp;rsquo ; m a novice when it comes to Yemeni food . I know just enough to say I look forward to learning more at Bukhari . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;20751;TOOLONG " **32;20778;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN ARTS AND LIFE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;20812;TOOLONG ... Out : Spectacular dinner at Atelier proves it 's still the best in the game&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;20847;TOOLONG ... bets , Feb. 13 to 19 : Sandy Sharkey 's photos , and cellist Rachel Mercer&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;20882;TOOLONG ... preview : Fronteras lights expose the darkness of borders , barriers , surveillance culture&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 14 Feb 2020 14:57:41 **45;20918;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : Spectacular dinner at Atelier proves it 's still the best in the gamehttp : **36;20965;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;21003;TOOLONG ... title= " Vegetarian version of the guinea fowl liver mousse served recently at Atelier on Rochester Street " alt= " Vegetarian version of the guinea fowl liver mousse served recently at Atelier on Rochester Street " data-portal-copyright= " Alison Mah " **28;21039;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1554228 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;21069;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 540 Rochester St. , Ottawa , 613-321-3537 , &lt;a href= " http : //atelierrestaurant.ca " &gt;atelier ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; For dinner Tuesday through Saturday , closed Sunday and Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; $125 for a 12-course tasting menu &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; Steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Not to brag , but so far in 2020 , I&amp;rsquo ; ve already had two fantastic , 12-course , haute-cuisine dinners . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last Saturday night at the Shaw Centre , there was the opulent , gourmet&amp;rsquo ; s race-against-the-clock that was the grand finale of the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships . Completist that I am , I hit every station and ate beautiful plate after beautiful plate , savouring delicacy-rich creations from some of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s leading chefs. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And then there was my other fine-dining meal , which was even better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In mid-January , I had the blind , 12-course menu at Atelier . And as vaunted as the culinary championships&amp;rsquo ; delights were , dinner at chef-owner Marc Lepine&amp;rsquo ; s cutting-edge restaurant on Rochester Street topped them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Perhaps that&amp;rsquo ; s not so surprising . Atelier has been consistently lauded since it opened in late 2008 , landing on various renowned lists of elite restaurants in Canada and North America . Lepine took home the gold at the 2012 and 2016 Canadian Culinary Championships , which were held in Kelowna , B.C. , before the event moved to Ottawa this year . Canada&amp;rsquo ; s principal assessor of restaurants and chefs , the annual Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants magazine , named Lepine the country&amp;rsquo ; s most innovative chef in 2018. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I last had dinner at Atelier in 2011 , when four of us marked some birthdays there . It was an evening of wows and irreproachable service , although these are the recollections of a &amp;ldquo ; civilian&amp;rdquo ; diner and not a pro critic &amp;mdash ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t start this reviewing gig until 2012. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So in 2020 , I was curious to appraise Atelier from my critic&amp;rsquo ; s vantage point , in light of the culinary championships and taking into account developments in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant scene in recent years . Not the least of those was the opening last May of THRU , Lepine&amp;rsquo ; s hyper-exclusive six-seat restaurant ( or second-storey private dining room under Atelier&amp;rsquo ; s roof , if you prefer ) that serves an entirely different and mind-boggling menu , all the while relying on Atelier&amp;rsquo ; s kitchen and staff . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After more than a decade , the specific restaurant experience Atelier and Lepine offer should be well-known to discerning food lovers in Ottawa and elsewhere . ( Lepine even published his lavish and revelatory Atelier : The Cookbook in 2018 , which he wrote with my predecessor , Anne DesBrisay. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21107;TOOLONG ... title= " Renowned local chef Marc Lepine has just released a new cookbook , entitled " Atelier , " after his downtown restaurant . " alt= " Renowned local chef Marc Lepine has just released a new cookbook , entitled " Atelier , " after his downtown restaurant . " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;21143;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1267969 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; DesBrisay herself reviewed Atelier in early 2009 , and her glowing take can be distilled to two short sentences : &amp;ldquo ; This is a brilliantly creative place . Tweak the budget &lt;span class= " marktxzffl3oz " &gt;and&lt;/span&gt; book a table . &amp;rdquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ll note that then , the tasting menu cost $75 , not the $125 of 2020 . I&amp;rsquo ; ll also note , to put that price further in context , that eating at Atelier is in fact a bargain compared to the cost of dining at some not dissimilar Michelin-starred restaurants in the U.S. and abroad . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To achieve his brilliance , Lepine has become a national master of what used to be called molecular gastronomy but is now more usually called modernist cuisine , building on the technological and even chemistry-based innovations of such landmark restaurants as El Bulli in Spain and Alinea in Chicago , where Lepine did an unpaid internship. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One feature of modernist cuisine is the transformation of ingredients into chips , powders , gels and more , maximizing the surprises and the assorted visual and flavour-based balancing acts on the plate . Some dismiss modernist cuisine as pretentious and overly artificial , but I&amp;rsquo ; m OK with it , and prefer to view dinner at Atelier as a parade of highly technical dishes that strive for uniqueness in multiple ways while appealing to a guest&amp;rsquo ; s senses of curiosity and playfulness . Another way of looking at Atelier is that it&amp;rsquo ; s simply Lepine&amp;rsquo ; s forum for showing off what he can do with food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is a bit of a disconnect between the feel of Atelier and the ambience that some would associate with luxurious , expensive dining . Atelier has always been more minimalist and funky rather than posh and oversized , surrounding guests in the cosy , narrow quarters of a former Little Italy house , amid a stark but not uncomfortable atmosphere of grey , black and white . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Perhaps the goal is to have the creativity of Lepine&amp;rsquo ; s food pop more against such neutrality . For a fine-dining experience , Atelier can even seem on the casual side &amp;mdash ; or at least , in January , I saw guests dressed as casually for their groundbreaking dinner as they would have been at a fast-food outlet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Appearances aside , Atelier indisputably operates at a fine-dining level when it comes to service . The tone set by our servers was unfailingly attentive but never intrusive . Lepine serves the kind of novel , at times arcane fare that requires 30-second-long recitations of ingredients and techniques , and our servers were up to those challenges . The flawlessness of service included that extra-mile measure of re-folding a guest&amp;rsquo ; s crumpled napkin when the guest had left the table . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My predecessor&amp;rsquo ; s 2009 review managed the feat of sharing very , very little about what she ate as she didn&amp;rsquo ; t want to give away too many surprises . I will offer some specifics , based on the fact that my friend and I tried not only the night&amp;rsquo ; s tasting menu but also the vegetarian/vegan alternatives . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our dinner began very strongly with a snack course that put three mind-expanding bites on a whimsically rotating platform . Who , apart from Lepine , knew that snail caviar , parsnip pur&amp;eacute ; e and crisp , cedar-tinged puff would be delicious ? So , too , was a seaweed crisp topped with crab salad . Just as great was a cube of wagyu beef short rib in sticky soy caramel . We had vegan options , too , which swapped frozen coconut pearls for snail caviar , artichoke salad for crab salad and sweet potato for beef . They were fine , but the carnivore options struck us as the real deal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21173;TOOLONG ... title= " ( Vegan ) snacks at Atelier " alt= " ( Vegan ) snacks at Atelier " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;21209;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1554296 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A mushroom tom yum dish , garnishing exotic mushrooms with smoked apple dust and more , was fantastic . The vegan version , which left out fish sauce , was just a little less complex and great . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21239;TOOLONG ... title= " Atelier 's mushroom tom yum course " alt= " Atelier 's mushroom tom yum course " data-portal-copyright= " Alison Mah " **28;21275;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1554299 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among other courses , some were very good but short of a wow , especially in this meat-eater&amp;rsquo ; s opinion . But there were highlights enough to make me think the meal was worthwhile , including an elaborate dish finished with perfect cauliflower soup , a scallop croquettes dish , a sablefish course , a quail course , and a strikingly garnished beef tenderloin-and-kimchi course . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Two desserts &amp;mdash ; one made with coffee , cajeta ( Mexican caramel ) and goat-cheese ice cream , the other made with chocolate ganache and passionfruit curd &amp;mdash ; were expertly crafted and stimulating in just the right way after the courses that had preceded them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;21965;TOOLONG ... title= " Coffee cajeta dessert at Atelier " alt= " Coffee cajeta dessert at Atelier " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22001;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1554309 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22031;TOOLONG ... title= " Chocolate passionfruit dessert at Atelier " alt= " Chocolate passionfruit dessert at Atelier " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22067;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1554310 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Guests are told to allot three hours for dinner at Atelier . We were there for a good extra hour and a half , but the food gave us much to talk about . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We didn&amp;rsquo ; t dig into Atelier&amp;rsquo ; s well-regarded wine list , but we heard the restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s sommelier dispensing good information and we each enjoyed a top-notch cocktail , one boozy and the other alcohol-free. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22097;TOOLONG ... title= " Atelier 's yuzu popsicle green tea drink " alt= " Atelier 's yuzu popsicle green tea drink " data-portal-copyright= " Alison Mah " **28;22133;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1554232 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Having been ahead of the curve for so long , Atelier has seen rivals , more so on the national and international dining scenes , make their own modernist inroads . I do think it&amp;rsquo ; s harder than it was in 2008 or 2009 to be cutting-edge . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , based on what I ate at the culinary championships and at Atelier , I&amp;rsquo ; d say Lepine still cooks like a two-time national champion at his restaurant . Only his other restaurant THRU , which somehow doesn&amp;rsquo ; t hamper Atelier&amp;rsquo ; s greatness , and chef-owner Briana Kim&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant Alice are in Atelier&amp;rsquo ; s league , in Ottawa and perhaps even beyond . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;22163;TOOLONG " **32;22190;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;22224;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;22259;TOOLONG ... et Madeleines announces closure after nine years&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;22294;TOOLONG ... bets , Feb. 6-12 : Dreamy alt-pop by Jasmine Trails , a tropical party at Kichesippi Beer&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;22329;TOOLONG ... so many in Ottawa are making art about a lesbian couple 's ' outrageous bravery ' &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 05 Feb 2020 19:32:12 **45;22364;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsVancouver chef Roger Ma wins Canadian Culinary Championships in Ottawahttp : **34;22411;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;22447;TOOLONG ... title= " The Canadian Culinary Championships Grand Finale of Canada 's Great Kitchen Party events was held at the Shaw Centre on Saturday , Feb. 1 .
