" alt= " The Canadian Culinary Championships Grand Finale of Canada 's Great Kitchen Party events was held at the Shaw Centre on Saturday , Feb. 1 . Vancouver chef Roger Ma was the top winner Saturday night . From left : second place Marc-Andr ? Jett ? of Hoogan et Beaufort in Montreal ; first place winner chef Roger Ma of the Boulevard Kitchen and Oyster Bar in Vancouver ; and chef Emily Butcher of deer + almond in Winnipeg . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;22483;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1552405 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vancouver chef Roger Ma prevailed over 11 other top Canadian chefs at this weekend&amp;rsquo ; s Canadian Culinary Championships in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Serving an elevated dish that featured honey mussel &amp;ldquo ; gratin&amp;eacute ; e , &amp;rdquo ; foraged bull kelp , sea urchin custard , Yukon potato and scallion terrine and manila clam emulsion , Ma took top honours at Saturday night&amp;rsquo ; s grand finale at the Shaw Centre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Head judge James Chatto said the dish from Ma , the chef at Boulevard Kitchen and Oyster Bar , was &amp;ldquo ; so meticulous , so thoughtful , yet vivid and alive . The flavours were rich , interesting and clear &amp;mdash ; it was Vancouver on a plate . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22513;TOOLONG ... title= " Marc Andre Jette of Hoogan et Beaufort 's dish ft. seared and wood-smoked oyster mushrooms , pickled mushroom gel , petals of charred pearl onions , smoked bread and finely grated cheese . " alt= " Marc Andre Jette of Hoogan et Beaufort 's dish ft. seared and wood-smoked oyster mushrooms , pickled mushroom gel , petals of charred pearl onions , smoked bread and finely grated cheese . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22549;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1551689 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Taking silver was Montreal chef Marc-Andr&amp;eacute ; Jett&amp;eacute ; of the restaurant Hoogan et Beaufort . For the finale , he prepared a dish in which Blanc de Gris mushroom was the star , sitting on a puddle of matsutake gel with burnt pickled onion , smoked bread and Louis d&amp;rsquo ; or cheese . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chatto called Jette&amp;rsquo ; s entry &amp;ldquo ; simple , comfort food &amp;mdash ; something you would eat every day of your life . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In third place was chef Emily Butcher of the Winnipeg restaurant deer + almond , who presented a cogent , clever dish of glazed sablefish with turnip cake , whitefish roe and turnip roe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chatto added that at Saturday morning&amp;rsquo ; s black box competition at Algonquin College , the medalling chefs placed highly , along with Calgary chef Jenny Kang. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22579;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver chef Roger Ma 's winning dish at the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships " alt= " Vancouver chef Roger Ma 's winning dish at the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22615;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1552313 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; This weekend marked the inaugural staging of the culinary championships in Ottawa . For many years , they were held in Kelowna , B.C. , attracting chefs from across Canada who had won regional qualifying competitions that were first known as Gold Medal Plates events before being rebranded as Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party events . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The sold-out grand finale attracted hundreds of foodies who , as did the 14-person judges&amp;rsquo ; panel , sampled the chef&amp;rsquo ; s dishes at the Shaw Centre . The championship and its regional qualifiers raise funds for three causes benefiting young Canadians &amp;mdash ; B2ten , which bolsters amateur sport , MusiCounts , which supports music education , and Community Food Centres Canada , which assists low-income communities through food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Having sampled all of the entrants ' plates , I can say that the judges ' task must have been a difficult one -- for that matter given the sheer quality of food . I was beginning to feel full after the fifth of the contending 12 plates that I tried . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;That said , had it been just myself eating in judgment , I would have handed out the medals to a somewhat different trio of chefs. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;22645;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef Jenny Kang of Shokunin in Calgary dish ft. king oyster mushroom barbajuan , roasted squash and mountain grana veloute " alt= " Chef Jenny Kang of Shokunin in Calgary dish ft. king oyster mushroom barbajuan , roasted squash and mountain grana veloute " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22681;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549234 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;I would have given the gold to chef Jenny Kang of the Calgary restaurant Shokunin . She served an elegant and lucid dish that brought out the best of ahi tuna , side stripe prawn , cured New Zealand snapper , raw Hokkaido scallop and uni , all complemented by a bright , clean sauce that elevated Vietnamese nuoc cham to new heights . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;I would have given the silver to Winnipeg chef Butcher 's cogent sablefish dish , which even made the humble turnip a witty participant . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;I would have given the bronze to one of the competition 's lamb dishes , namely the lamb creation from chef Matt Pennell of Legends Restaurant in Moncton . His combination of lamb loin , lamb belly , and more complimented by air-dried tuna , was a vibrant , colourful dish . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;22711;TOOLONG " **32;22738;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN ARTS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;22772;TOOLONG ... review : Light propels action in extraordinary production of The Neverending Story&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;22807;TOOLONG ... Strumbellas cancel tour , including NAC concert&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;22842;TOOLONG ... winter jazz preview : Saxophonist Allison Au found musical inspiration in her biracial ancestry&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumSun , 02 Feb 2020 20:06:04 **45;22877;TOOLONG ... Out : There 's celebrity-chef sizzle at Grill 41 , but also disappointing , overpriced foodhttp : **36;22924;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;22962;TOOLONG ... title= " Scallops with Granny Smith apple emulsion at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " alt= " Scallops with Granny Smith apple emulsion at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;22998;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549850 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Grill 41&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 100 Elgin St. ( in the Lord Elgin Hotel ) , 613-569-2126 , &lt;a href= " http : //grill41.ca " &gt;grill41.ca&lt;/a &; ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; daily from 6:30 a.m. to 11 p.m. &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; mains at dinner from $22 to $69 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; fully accessible &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; The veteran Ottawa chef Ren&amp;eacute ; Rodriguez doesn&amp;rsquo ; t need me to sing his praises , but here goes anyway . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Back in the fall of 2012 , when I was just getting settled into this reviewing gig , a dinner at Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant Navarra blew my mind . The Murray Street business served vibrant , elevated dishes energized by the culinary inspirations of Mexico , which is where Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s family is from . After Navarra closed in 2017 , Rodriguez later ran the kitchen at the upscale Italian restaurant Orto in the Glebe . There , we also found the food impressive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even if you haven&amp;rsquo ; t eaten Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s best efforts , you may well have rooted for him during his much-publicized TV cooking escapades . He won Top Chef Canada in 2014 . On the 2017 Food Network show Beat Bobby Flay , Rodriguez did just that , prevailing over the American mega-chef. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So , this week&amp;rsquo ; s disheartening mystery is why the dishes we&amp;rsquo ; ve had at the Lord Elgin Hotel&amp;rsquo ; s Grill 41 , where Rodriguez has been the chef since last fall , have ranged from alright to so-so to disappointing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since at least mid-November , Rodriguez has held that position at the venerable downtown hotel , reporting to the Lord Elgin&amp;rsquo ; s executive chef Neil Mather , while the restaurant itself is owned and operated by food services giant Sodexo Canada . The revamped menu now includes a slew of &amp;ldquo ; Ren&amp;eacute ; Rodriguez signature dishes , &amp;rdquo ; designated with &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; beside them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And yet , during my two lunch visits and one dinner , we too often found those dishes and others were scarcely items of which&amp;nbsp ; an accomplished , famous chef should be proud . Some were too casually made , such as an appetizer of dry , overcooked octopus , or too casually conceived , like the entry-level beet salad awash in fris&amp;eacute ; e . The high prices of dishes compounded our disatisfactions. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rodriguez told me this week of the hours he works at Lord Elgin , which are from 3 p.m. to 9 p.m. , five days a week . ( He also works at Mercadito , the taco-based business in the Queen St. Fare food hall . ) He was not in the kitchen when we had dinner . Nor was he hands-on with our lunches . But how much should that matter ? I know that the dinner at Navarra that wowed me years ago was prepared while Rodriguez was absent from his kitchen , by talented cooks who could assemble Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s dazzling creations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23028;TOOLONG ... title= " Complimentary cornbread with shisha butter at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " alt= " Complimentary cornbread with shisha butter at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23064;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549957 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23094;TOOLONG ... title= " Some more disheveled cornbread and shisha butter at another visit to Grill 41 " alt= " Some more disheveled cornbread and shisha butter at another visit to Grill 41 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23130;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549967 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Grill 41 , we ate best when we had less ambitious fare at our two lunches . Thanks to the heavy-on-the-umami quality of the &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; truffle burger ( eight ounces of ground chuck , bolstered by truffled triple cream brie and mushroom jam ) , its $25 splurge was palatable , even if its fries were mediocre . The &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; watercress salad , which matched the greens with cured tuna gravlax , deviled egg and olive powder ( $21 ) was a simple dish , but all of its components were satisfyingly in place . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23160;TOOLONG ... title= " Truffle burger at Grill 41 , with salad and fries " alt= " Truffle burger at Grill 41 , with salad and fries " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23196;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549892 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23226;TOOLONG ... title= " Watercress salad with tuna gravlax ( half serving ) at Grill 41 " alt= " Watercress salad with tuna gravlax ( half serving ) at Grill 41 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23262;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549854 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At lunch , we also tried the oddly designated &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; Wolfgang Puck pizza ( $24 ) , made with Alfredo sauce , shiitake mushroom , arugula , radicchio and truffle , which was reasonably tasty , but also too crisply cooked . For a non-&amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; dish , we went for the highly recommended &amp;ldquo ; Michael Smith&amp;rdquo ; chowder , which must owe its name to the fact that the P.E.I.-based star chef helped develop Grill 41&amp;rsquo ; s menu in 2011 . While the thickly creamy seafood chowder was expensive ( $19 ) and looked as if it had been doled out haphazardly , at least it was generously stocked with shrimp , lobster and other ingredients that were almost surprisingly toothsome and fresh . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23292;TOOLONG ... title= " Wolfgang Puck pizza ( half-serving ) at Grill 41 " alt= " Wolfgang Puck pizza ( half-serving ) at Grill 41 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23328;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549895 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23358;TOOLONG ... title= " Michael Smith Chowder at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " alt= " Michael Smith Chowder at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23394;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549660 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our dinner at Grill 41 , though , was more uneven and included some &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; dishes that flopped . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of four appetizers , best was the ostentatiously named &amp;ldquo ; RR &amp;mdash ; Grill 41 signature steak tartare&amp;rdquo ; ( $21 ) , which was roughly chopped , adequately seasoned and enjoyable , although I&amp;rsquo ; d say that its ingredients could have sung a little louder . Bear in mind , too , that there are slightly cheaper tartares in town that by comparison make you say &amp;ldquo ; Wow ! &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23424;TOOLONG ... title= " Beef tartare at Grill 41 " alt= " Beef tartare at Grill 41 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23460;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549968 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We were perplexed by the sea of Granny Smith apple emulsion that almost drowned four seared scallops ( $19 ) . While not a dud , the dish lacked the finesse you&amp;rsquo ; d want from a scallops starter . Dry and flavour-deprived &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; grilled octopus was a big letdown ( $18 ) that made us scrape the plate for the meagre consolation of burnt honey . The menu also mentione pork belly being a component of the dish , but it was MIA . The &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; house beet salad ( $18 ) seemed off-puttingly thrown-together , made with slow-roasted squash that felt tired and an alarming amount of fris&amp;eacute ; e. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23490;TOOLONG ... title= " Scallops with Granny Smith apple emulsion at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " alt= " Scallops with Granny Smith apple emulsion at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23526;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549850 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23556;TOOLONG ... title= " Octopus appetizer at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " alt= " Octopus appetizer at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23592;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549855 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Wild mushroom risotto ( $22 and not an &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; dish ) was massively portioned and acceptable , but fell short of the more flavourful and al-dente rice treat I can make for myself . The &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; grilled chicken supreme ( $31 ) looked overcooked and was too dry . The side casserole of vegetables was a nice touch , but the chicken&amp;rsquo ; s morel butter sauce tasted more of salt than of prized mushrooms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23622;TOOLONG ... title= " Mushroom risotto at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " alt= " Mushroom risotto at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23658;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549849 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23688;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken breast supreme at Grill 41 " alt= " Chicken breast supreme at Grill 41 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23724;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549851 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23754;TOOLONG ... title= " Vegetable casserole that goes with chicken supreme at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " alt= " Vegetable casserole that goes with chicken supreme at Grill 41 in the Lord Elgin Hotel " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23790;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549930 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Putting the &amp;ldquo ; grill&amp;rdquo ; in Grill 41 were the menu&amp;rsquo ; s several steak-based dishes . We tried the &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; 10-ounce striploin &amp;ldquo ; steak frites&amp;rdquo ; with Roquefort butter , fries and salad . While it was the best of our three mains , it was also priced high enough ( $39 ) for us to expect perfection . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23820;TOOLONG ... title= " Striploin steak frites at Grill 41 " alt= " Striploin steak frites at Grill 41 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23856;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549952 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Let&amp;rsquo ; s put it this way : These and other dishes did not instill confidence that we should later order the $69 &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; surf-and-turf of filet mignon and butter-poached lobster , Robuchon potatoes , lobster sauce Am&amp;eacute ; ricaine and broccoli gratin , no matter how much it appealed on the menu . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another downer : the choice of desserts consisted that night of a standard issue house-made cr&amp;egrave ; me br&amp;ucirc ; l&amp;eacute ; e and a few brought-in items made by Pasticceria Gelateria Italiana in Little Italy . Perhaps this is the Sodexo way , but it&amp;rsquo ; s hard to square with a $69 surf-and-turf dinner . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;23886;TOOLONG ... title= " Cr ? me br ? l ? e at Grill 41 " alt= " Cr ? me br ? l ? e at Grill 41 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;23922;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1549877 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Had my budget allowed , we would have tried some of Grill 41&amp;rsquo ; s reasonably priced two-ounce cocktails and martinis . But that&amp;rsquo ; s a stone we left unturned. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Service from three different servers ranged from efficient and anonymous to effusive but also somewhat slack and lacking in polish to brusque . Two of three times , we did not feel attended to or cared for . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Reached this week , executive chef Mather responded to a synopsis of my thoughts with : &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re very happy with Ren&amp;eacute ; and since November , when we launched his dishes we have had great feedback and the culinary team is growing with him as they continue to learn more about his style and dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are about to make more changes to the menu and add some other dishes from Ren&amp;eacute ; . Also , we are working on a service training program to constantly improve our guest experience . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When my predecessor , Anne DesBrisay , reviewed Grill 41 in February 2011 , its appearance , following a renovation , didn&amp;rsquo ; t do much for her . She called the bi-level room &amp;ldquo ; remarkably unremarkable &amp;hellip ; beige and brown and bland , filled with composite wood , fake Benjaminas , hotel-issue carpet and Home Depot-ish lights . About the only interesting bit is the long wood-like cupboard of wine , behind which is tucked a private dining room . &amp;rdquo ; I would say that nine years later , very little has changed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Searching for a plus side , I would say I liked Grill 41 more than my predecessor did . She approved of the Michael Smith chowder , but sent back gnocchi that were &amp;ldquo ; hard little floury pellets . &amp;rdquo ; She deemed the macaroni and cheese inedible and found a pork chop so &amp;ldquo ; desperately dry&amp;rdquo ; that it was removed from her bill . In late 2019 and 2020 , we ate better than that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the magic of Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s cooking could scarcely be felt in the dishes we had . Of course , all the &amp;ldquo ; RRs&amp;rdquo ; on the menu were meant to entice and raise expectations . But the marketing rang hollow . Grill 41 , whether Rodriguez was there or not , needed to deliver much less drab , overpriced food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;23952;TOOLONG " **32;23979;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;24013;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24048;TOOLONG ... top chefs are in Ottawa for the Culinary Championships -- and the contest gets harder every year&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24083;TOOLONG ... preview : ' Bickering Pines ' barn inspired Jim Cuddy 's latest solo album&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24118;TOOLONG ... things you need to know about the 2020 TD Ottawa Winter Jazz Festival&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 29 Jan 2020 19:02:39 **45;24153;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24274;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1548218 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Just because Ottawa chef Marc Lepine won the Canadian Culinary Championships twice doesn&amp;rsquo ; t mean he would be cocky about his chances were he competing once more this weekend . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m sure it just gets harder every year , the competition , &amp;rdquo ; says Lepine , chef-owner of Atelier , the cutting-edge tasting-menu restaurant on Rochester Street . After winning qualifying competitions in Ottawa , Lepine took home gold at the national level in 2012 and 2016 , and is the only Canadian chef to have won twice at the championships . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Held in Kelowna for many years , the championships have moved to Ottawa , and its first edition in the capital will be held this weekend . A slate of events will culminate in Saturday night&amp;rsquo ; s grand finale competition at the Shaw Centre . There , a dozen Canadian chefs who won the qualifying competitions in their cities , including Ottawa qualifier Ian Carswell of the Black Tartan Kitchen in Carleton Place , will serve their best dishes to a panel of judges and the public . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The grand finale , which is sold out , raises funds for three causes benefitting young Canadians &amp;mdash ; B2ten , which bolsters amateur sport , MusiCounts , which supports music education , and Community Food Centres Canada , which assists low-income communities through food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thanks to photos on Instagram and detailed descriptions , Lepine was able to size up this year&amp;rsquo ; s grand finale entrants . The edited conversation below is his take on the field and the competition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24304;TOOLONG ... title= " Ian Carswell prepared Milkhouse Lamb at the Ottawa regionals for the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships . " alt= " Ian Carswell prepared Milkhouse Lamb at the Ottawa regionals for the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;24340;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1548219 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What 's the first thing that strikes you about these dishes ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Everything is just more artistic-looking . People are putting a lot more attention to detail in their presentations . There&amp;rsquo ; s flow to some of these plates , and they&amp;rsquo ; re colourful . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s not like it used to be . Previously , people didn&amp;rsquo ; t put quite as much emphasis on the way the plate looked . It was more about making sure all the flavours were working really well together . And then once you had all those ingredients put together , presentation always seemed like the last thing . It was almost more like an afterthought . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : I blame Instagram. &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : It&amp;rsquo ; s possible . That&amp;rsquo ; s definitely a factor . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : The dish from Montreal looks a little more casual and rustic . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : &amp;nbsp ; That&amp;rsquo ; s very Montreal , I guess . That one does stand out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24370;TOOLONG ... title= " Montreal 's Marc Andre Jette of Hoogan et Beaufort 's dish ft. seared and wood-smoked oyster mushrooms , pickled mushroom gel , petals of charred pearl onions , smoked bread and finely grated cheese . " alt= " Montreal 's Marc Andre Jette of Hoogan et Beaufort 's dish ft. seared and wood-smoked oyster mushrooms , pickled mushroom gel , petals of charred pearl onions , smoked bread and finely grated cheese . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24406;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1548221 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What other priorities do you see ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : What struck me was how complex everything is , how complicated and how many techniques and ingredients are being used . It&amp;rsquo ; s just wild . Nobody&amp;rsquo ; s showing up and trying to make just a killer risotto and win the competition . They&amp;rsquo ; re really going for it , from every angle , I think . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Compared to your own food , how do these dishes rank in terms of complexity ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : More so , in some cases , for sure . When we competed , our plates ended up having a lot going on on them . I see that here , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What 's the rationale for being so complex ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : You get so many points for technique , so part of it is , I think , they want to showcase as many techniques as they can . There&amp;rsquo ; s a huge myriad of techniques in all of these plates . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You can show up with a five-ingredient dish , and if those flavours sing perfectly , then there&amp;rsquo ; s nothing wrong with that . But we don&amp;rsquo ; t see that here at all . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Do you see certain trendy components or techniques in this field ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : You see koji ( a mix of cultivated mould with rice , soya , or other foodstuffs , used to make fermented items in Japan ) pop up . Of course , it&amp;rsquo ; s very popular these days . You don&amp;rsquo ; t see quite as much in terms of foraging as you used to a few years ago . Lots of fish and shellfish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24436;TOOLONG ... title= " HALIFAX Barry Mooney of @freshtwentyone 's dish ft. shoyu koji lacquer , sturgeon boudin blanc , smoked sturgeon , kombu poached daikon , apple parsley puree , sturgeon bone marrow crisp , tonkotsu cider jus. " alt= " HALIFAX Barry Mooney of @freshtwentyone 's dish ft. shoyu koji lacquer , sturgeon boudin blanc , smoked sturgeon , kombu poached daikon , apple parsley puree , sturgeon bone marrow crisp , tonkotsu cider jus. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24472;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1548222 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Do you have tips for chefs competing this weekend ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : There&amp;rsquo ; s a few definite ways people screw up in this competition . No. 1 , they&amp;rsquo ; ll try to do something that they&amp;rsquo ; re not used to doing or that&amp;rsquo ; s different from what they do . The reason they&amp;rsquo ; re invited is because they make great food at the restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : So do n't wing it . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Yeah . Do what you do , but try to improve that a little bit . I always tell them to pick a dish at your restaurant , the dish that people come to your restaurant and say , &amp;ldquo ; Oh my gosh , this is amazing . &amp;rdquo ; Take that dish and just refine it a little bit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Was that what you did ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Yeah , both times . We picked a dish that people were fawning over the most at the restaurant and used that as the base and tried to elevate that a little . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are a few ways where people overthink it . Or have lost before they even start . People who try to commit to a concept plate , that will restrict everything they do on that plate . Like breakfast for dinner is an example . It&amp;rsquo ; s limiting what they&amp;rsquo ; re going to be able to ultimately do on that plate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the black-box competition ( where chefs have one hour to prepare a dish with a mystery ingredient received just before ) , i &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;t 's more about creativity than for flavour . So go outside the box as much as you can with the ingredients themselves . A lot of people end up doing the same preparations with the ingredients . &lt;/span&gt; One year they had sweet potato , and everybody made a sweet potato pur&amp;eacute ; e. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24502;TOOLONG ... title= " Dish by Toronto chef Keith Pears of the Delta Hotel . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24538;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1548220 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Do these entries give you a sense of where Canada 's best chefs and restaurants rank internationally ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : &amp;nbsp ; We stack right up there with any country , I think . I think we&amp;rsquo ; re probably ahead in some ways , just in terms of creativity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I ate ( recently ) at Vespertine and Dialogue in Los Angeles . They were fantastic , but you can find food that great in Canada , I think . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Canadian Culinary **28;24568;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; When : Jan. 30 to Feb. 1 &lt;br&gt; Where : Algonquin College , the &lt;b&gt; &lt;/b&gt; Canada Aviation &amp;amp ; Space Museum , the Shaw Centre &lt;br&gt; Info and tickets : greatkitchenparty.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24598;TOOLONG ... discover how to accurately date rare bottles of Scotch whisky and say nearly half are fakes&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24633;TOOLONG ... sandwich war with Popeyes has drained the supply of little chickens&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;24668;TOOLONG ... popularity of Ottawa 's Chinese food is at an apex -- and options have never been more diverse&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 29 Jan 2020 12:25:22 **45;24703;TOOLONG ... NewsNationalIn Los Angeles and Pasadena , restaurants newly touted by Michelin Guide shinehttp : **35;24750;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;24787;TOOLONG ... title= " Spiny lobster curry , a course at Providence in Los Angeles last fall . " alt= " Spiny lobster curry , a course at Providence in Los Angeles last fall . " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;24823;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1540064 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Chef has asked me to ask you if you would like to be cooked for , &amp;rdquo ; our grey-suited server asked us &amp;mdash ; rather euphemistically , it should be added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Had that low-key representative of the much-lauded Los Angeles restaurant Providence been more blunt , he would have said : &amp;ldquo ; Would you like us to pamper you with what will likely be one of the best and most luxurious seafood-centred meals of your lives , with freshly shaved white truffles imported from Italy , sprinklings of caviar and top-grade A5 Wagyu beef helping to make dinner more delicious and memorable ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Either way , the answer from my food-loving friends and me could only have been yes . When we visited the 14-year-old Melrose Avenue fine-dining destination last fall , we wanted nothing less than to have Providence&amp;rsquo ; s chef and co-owner Michael Cimarusti pull out the stops with a 10-course tasting menu that proffered a succession of flawlessly prepared delicacies. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For three days in Los Angeles and nearby Pasadena , our itinerary was geared almost single-mindedly on eating , from tacos to truffles with as much as possible in between , and our final night&amp;rsquo ; s dinner at Providence was practically preordained to be a highlight . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24853;TOOLONG ... title= " Provdence in Los Angeles " alt= " Provdence in Los Angeles " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24889;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535927 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24919;TOOLONG ... title= " The dining room of ProvIdence in Los Angeles " alt= " The dining room of ProvIdence in Los Angeles " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;24955;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1541399 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We had taken many cues and made reservations based on the counsel of the Michelin Guide California 2019 guidebook . After a 10-year hiatus , the globally recognized taste-making publication last year resumed assessing Southern California&amp;rsquo ; s dining scene , complementing its ongoing scrutiny of the San Francisco area&amp;rsquo ; s eateries. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of the 657 restaurants that received either highly coveted stars or lesser shout-outs in the Michelin guide for the state , Providence was one of just six LA restaurants to receive two stars , repeating its achievement in the 2009 Michelin Guide for Los Angeles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last year and in past years , no LA restaurant received Michelin&amp;rsquo ; s supreme accolade of three stars , given to top-notch restaurants that score highest according to the guidebooks&amp;rsquo ; five criteria : quality of ingredients , mastery of flavour and cooking , personality of the chef in his cuisine , value for money and consistency between visits . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Providence , we were delighted by courses such as sashimi of Japanese Amberjack belly with shaved white truffle , morsels of sea urchin and box crab under an feather-light canopy of scallop mousseline and caviar , and poached antenna of Santa Barbara spiny lobster paired with a Thai-style spiny lobster curry . Risotto with truffles and a lozenge of seared , richly marbled Wagyu brought our parade of savoury dishes to an end , before the sweet finish of torchon of dulce de leche and chocolate , accented with macerated grapes and partnered with pistachio-lacquered brioche and brie sorbet . That dessert even played with our heads by cheekily and uncannily evoking foie gras , which is banned in California . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;24985;TOOLONG ... title= " Spiny lobster curry , a course at Providence in Los Angeles last fall . " alt= " Spiny lobster curry , a course at Providence in Los Angeles last fall . " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;25021;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1540064 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25051;TOOLONG ... title= " Amadai ( japanese sweet sea bream ) with kabocha squash , chorizo broth , green onion at Providence in Los Angeles " alt= " Amadai ( japanese sweet sea bream ) with kabocha squash , chorizo broth , green onion at Providence in Los Angeles " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;25087;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1540061 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; As fastidiously crafted as our dishes at Providence were , alternatives were prepared for the vegetarian and gluten-abstainer at our table . The restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s setting was elegant , with soft classic jazz wafting through the air , but the experience was neither intimidating or pretentious . Stretching to about four hours , dinner nonetheless felt perfectly paced , with neither lulls nor scrambles marring its flow . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But then , perfection ought to be the reward when dinner , including wines , tip and taxes , topped $440 U.S. per person . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During our trip , we also enjoyed more affordable but substantial satisfactions at restaurants that had received a single star from Michelin , and at some of the budget-friendly designations from the **34;25117;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Le Comptoir in LA&amp;rsquo ; s Koreatown neighbourhood , chef Gary Menes served an elevated vegetable-inspired tasting menu to a full and cosy house of just 10 people , showcasing impeccable produce grown in his Long Beach garden . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The night that we were at Le Comptoir , which opened five years ago and which has one Michelin star , an additional treat was the understated presence of a certain limelight-shunning celebrity who shall go unnamed , because otherwise John Wick might have to kill me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25153;TOOLONG ... title= " Chefs at work at Le Comptoir in Los Angeles , a 10-seat restaurant with one Michelin star . Photo credit : Emma Choo , @vancouverfoodie " alt= " Chefs at work at Le Comptoir in Los Angeles , a 10-seat restaurant with one Michelin star . Photo credit : Emma Choo , @vancouverfoodie " data-portal-copyright= " Emma Choo , @vancouverfoodie " **28;25189;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535925 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We also ate at the one-Michelin-star restaurant Orsa &amp;amp ; Winston in downtown LA , where chef-owner Joseph Centeno serves Asian-Italian tasting menus at dinner . A tip for gourmets who scrimp and save : you can try the food here and at some other Michelin-starred restaurants at lunch , as we did , and still feel you had something special , such as the pear and apple salad with endive , marcona almonds and nori vinaigrette , which we had , or the chicken katsu with black truffle , which I wish we had had . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In fact , we only grazed at Orsa &amp;amp ; Winston because we visited it as part of a Michelin-themed downtown LA walking tour , led by Avital Food Tours&amp;rsquo ; effervescent guide Penny Truitt. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our tour made additional stops at two more casual places , pasta specialists Maccheroni Republic , for bianchi and neri pasta and spaghetti ai quattro , and the funky Indian eatery Badmaash , for chocolate chai pot de creme , which with our bewitching goodness miraculously made us feel ravenous again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both of these restaurants are so-called &amp;ldquo ; Bib Gourmand&amp;rdquo ; eateries in the eyes of Michelin , meaning that the company&amp;rsquo ; s anonymous inspectors regard them as favourites for delivering good value . Introduced in 1955 , the Bib Gourmand designation is defined as a tip of the hat to worthy restaurants where you can get two courses and a glass of wine or a dessert for $40 or less , excluding tax and tip . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Just last year , Michelin launched a new designation called the Michelin Plate , which sits between stars and Bib Gourmand kudos and acknowledges restaurants that serve very good food and might well move up to receiving a star in following years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Pasadena , we enjoyed ourselves immensely at the fun and un-fussy Michelin-Plated restaurant Union . Persimmon salad and endive salad kicked off our epic dinner , pastas such as pork ragu and squid ink lumache with lobster continued the charge and milk-braised lamb shoulder delivered the final savoury blow . Our desserts , olive oil cake and luscious tiramisu , disappeared quickly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25219;TOOLONG ... title= " Milk-braised lamb shoulder at Union in Pasadena " alt= " Milk-braised lamb shoulder at Union in Pasadena " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;25255;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535984 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We even started off each day with Michelin-Plated breakfasts. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the Raymond 1886 , a former 1930s cottage converted into an outstanding bar and homey restaurant in Pasadena , we fortified ourselves with fried chicken and waffles with red eye gravy , lobster on avocado toast , Nutella French toast and more before heading out on a walking tour of the picturesque City of Roses . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25285;TOOLONG ... title= " Pasadena 's Colorado Bridge . Photo courtesy of Visit Pasadena " alt= " Pasadena 's Colorado Bridge . Photo courtesy of Visit Pasadena " data-portal-copyright= " Jamie Pham " **28;25321;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1541396 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Openaire , a bright and verdant pool-side restaurant in the LINE Los Angeles , a Koreatown hotel that&amp;rsquo ; s a magnet for the young and stylish , we indulged with Cornflakes-crusted French toast topped with caramelized bananas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We had our most revelatory breakfast just hours before we jetted home , at the oh-so-hip restaurant Destroyer , a tranquil , minimalist space of bleached white oak in Culver City that opened in September 2016. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25351;TOOLONG ... title= " The interior of the restaurant Destroyer in Los Angeles . Photo credit : Curtis Pickrell " alt= " The interior of the restaurant Destroyer in Los Angeles . Photo credit : Curtis Pickrell " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;25387;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535923 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; There , we shared vibrantly flavoured , arresting-looking dishes inspired by Scandinavia and Japan , including blueberries with lime , black currant , elderflower , Icelandic bread with cultured butter , spice bread with red currants , and a game-changing serving of avocado , covered with burnt onion powder , with toast . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25417;TOOLONG ... title= " Dishes at Destroyer in Los Angeles " alt= " Dishes at Destroyer in Los Angeles " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;25453;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535930 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; After our previous night&amp;rsquo ; s dinner at Providence , Destroyer was probably only place that could have wowed us. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We were lucky enough to have Jordan Kahn , the visionary chef behind Destroyer , pop by and say hello . If we liked Destroyer , Kahn said , we really needed to dine at Vespertine , his controversial fine-dining restaurant down the street . During highly conceptual , outside-the-box meals at Vespertine , Kahn&amp;rsquo ; s futuristic , edible creations can resemble abstract art more than food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s not a restaurant , it&amp;rsquo ; s a portal , &amp;rdquo ; Kahn , who is famously cryptic , said of Vespertine. &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s very powerful and beautiful and interesting . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For corroboration , we looked to the Michelin Guide . It awarded two stars to Vespertine . That cinched it . Vespertine in 2020 or bust . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;EVEN FINE-DINING FANS NEED TACO BREAKS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I and my fellow gourmands visited Los Angeles , we also went out of our way for tacos &amp;mdash ; and it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t even Tuesday . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In fact , it was Sunday &amp;mdash ; the day of the week when Smorgasburg , a massive open-air food market draws thousands to a recently revitalized district of downtown Los Angeles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25483;TOOLONG ... title= " The scene at Smorgasburg LA , open-air food market in downtown Los Angeles . Photo courtesy of Smorgasburg " alt= " The scene at Smorgasburg LA , open-air food market in downtown Los Angeles . Photo courtesy of Smorgasburg " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;25519;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1541393 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Food vendors of every stripe beckoned and it was hard to choose judiciously , given the allure of smoky ribs and brisket from Moo&amp;rsquo ; s Craft Barbecue , the Taiwanese-style buns from Baohaus , the soup dumplings at Brothecary , the pineapple boats filled with Hawaiian garlic butter shrimp at Shrimp Daddy , and much more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Still , we resolved to save room for tacos from the trendy LA taco-maker Tacos 1986 , whose flavourpacked pork adobada tacos and mushroom tacos served on freshly hand-pressed tortillas met our deep-seated needs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If only we had had room to sample the Middle Eastern tacos , Peruvian tacos and other street-food faves at Smorgasburg that day . A return trip is definitely in order . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;em&gt;Peter Hum visited Los Angeles and Pasadena as a guest of Visit California . &lt;/em&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; *** &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Some Michelin-touted restaurants in Los Angeles and Pasadena&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;The Michelin Guide California 2019 guidebook lauds 657 restaurants in the state , including : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Two Michelin **30;25549;TOOLONG Providence : &lt;a href= " http : //providencela.com " &gt;providencel ... &lt;br&gt; Vespertine : &lt;a href= " http : //vespertine.la " &gt;vespertine.la &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;One Michelin star&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; Le Comptoir : &lt;a href= " http : //lecomptoirla.com " &gt;lecomptoirl ... &lt;br&gt; Orsa &amp;amp ; Winston : &lt;a href= " http : //orsaandwinston.com " &gt;orsaandwi ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Michelin Plate&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; Destroyer : &lt;a href= " http : //destroyer.la " &gt;destroyer.la&lt; ... &lt;br&gt; Openaire : &lt;a href= " http : **34;25581;TOOLONG ... &lt;br&gt; The Raymond 1886 : &lt;a href= " http : //theraymond.com " &gt;theraymond.co ... &lt;br&gt; Union : &lt;a href= " http : //unionpasadena.com " &gt;unionpasad ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bib Gourmand ( good value ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; Badmaash : &lt;a href= " http : //badmaashla.com " &gt;badmaashla.co ... &lt;br&gt; Maccheroni Republic : &lt;a href= " http : //maccheronirepublic.com " &gt;macch ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Winter restaurant week in Los Angeles&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; 2020 Winter dineL.A . Restaurant Week runs from Jan. 17 to 31 this year and features more than 400 restaurants offering special deals at lunch or dinner . For example , Providence will serve a deeply discounted five-course tasting menu dinner for $99 . More info : &lt;a href= " http : //dineLA.com " &gt;dineLA.com&lt;/a &; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;25617;TOOLONG " **32;25644;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 24 Jan 2020 15:24:44 **45;25678;TOOLONG ... popularity of Ottawa 's Chinese food is at an apex -- and options have never been more diversehttp : **33;25725;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;25760;TOOLONG ... title= " Hang Yu of Harbin Restaurant . " alt= " Hang Yu of Harbin Restaurant . " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;25796;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1545923 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Oct. 27 , 1945 , the typewritten menu at the Ontario Cafe at 66 Rideau St. listed such temptations as breaded halibut steak for 40 cents , grilled milk-fed veal chops for 45 cents , and , perhaps most surprisingly 75 years later , several chop suey dishes including chicken mushroom chop suey for 75 cents . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those Chinese-Canadian items likely dated back to the early days of the eatery that stood where the Rideau Centre is now . My grandfather , James Hum , opened the Ontario Cafe with several partners in the early 1920s , having emigrated from southern China to Ottawa in the mid-1910s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One of the first Chinese immigrants who settled in Ottawa , my grandfather died in 1934 . The Ontario Cafe , which thrived when farmers sold their wares at the nearby ByWard Market , remained in my family for some years , although perhaps not into the mid-1940s . But two of my grandfather&amp;rsquo ; s sons , my uncle , Tom , and my father , Joe , went on to open restaurants in Ottawa , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25826;TOOLONG ... title= " In July 1937 , Charles Hum was the soda fountain manager at the Ontario Cafe on Rideau Street , one of the first Chinese-run businesses in Ottawa . The eatery opened in the 1920s at 66 Rideau St. , where the Rideau Centre is now . " alt= " In July 1937 , Charles Hum was the soda fountain manager at the Ontario Cafe on Rideau Street , one of the first Chinese-run businesses in Ottawa . The eatery opened in the 1920s at 66 Rideau St. , where the Rideau Centre is now . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;25862;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1545928 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I did not take over the family restaurant when I had the chance to , decades ago , and I was never asked to . Instead , I now have the privilege of dining at restaurants considerably more frequently than my pay grade should allow , as long as I write about those meals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chinese food and my family are on my mind this week . Chinese New Year , which begins Saturday , encompasses many traditions and superstitions , and perhaps above all , it is a time for families to come together , honour ancestors and feast . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But even if your ancestry isn&amp;rsquo ; t Chinese , you too might feel like celebrating soon with a Chinese meal . Analysis released this month by the website &lt;a href= " http : //chefspencil.com " &gt;chefspencil. ... found that the top &amp;ldquo ; ethnic&amp;rdquo ; cuisine in Canada was Chinese , followed by Italian , Thai , Indian and Mexican . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The international food website generated its rankings through Google Trends analyses . Drilling down into the results , you do find that input from B.C. contributed heavily to Chinese cuisine coming out on top . Still , when I asked Chef&amp;rsquo ; s Pencil for data exclusive to Ottawa , Chinese food again topped the list , followed by Indian , Thai , Italian and Mexican . For all of Quebec , Chinese food was again No. 1 , according to the website. &lt;a href= " https : **25;25892;TOOLONG : //che ... target= " blank " rel= " noopener " &gt;&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That kind of popularity is reason enough to ponder what&amp;rsquo ; s meant by Chinese food in 2020 , particularly in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25919;TOOLONG ... title= " Spicy Chicken Wings . Hang Yu of Harbin Restaurant . " alt= " Spicy Chicken Wings . Hang Yu of Harbin Restaurant . " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;25955;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1545924 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although Chinese-Canadian fare hasn&amp;rsquo ; t died out entirely , we are a long way from chop suey . Waves of immigration from China have brought a succession of new , diverse dishes , served not just to fellow expats but to non-Chinese with curious taste buds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Regional specialties such as Yunnanese &amp;ldquo ; Crossing the Bridge&amp;rdquo ; soup , Lanzhou hand-pulled noodles , the street-food crepes called jianbing and more are now served in Ottawa , the latest arrivals in a century-long evolution of Chinese food that in Ottawa began downtown , moved to Somerset Street West by the 1980s , and appears now in our suburbs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From the Ontario Cafe , my relatives made their entrepreneurial move to Centretown . My uncle Tom held shares in the Arcadia Grill at 249 Bank St. and the Ho Ho Cafe at 248 Albert St. My father , who died in 2005 , wrote in his diary of reminiscences that during the Second World War years , the Arcadia Grill &amp;ldquo ; was extremely busy because it had an attractive store front and was the only restaurant to have air conditioning . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;25985;TOOLONG ... title= " By the early 1940s , the Arcadia Grill was a popular Chinese-run restaurant in Centretown , at 249 Bank St. Owned by the Hum family , it boasted an attractive store front and even air conditioning . " alt= " By the early 1940s , the Arcadia Grill was a popular Chinese-run restaurant in Centretown , at 249 Bank St. Owned by the Hum family , it boasted an attractive store front and even air conditioning . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;26021;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1545931 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; By the early 1960s , my father and his brother-in-law were running the Marco Polo Tavern Restaurant on Bank Street near Heron Road . &amp;ldquo ; There was a lot of construction going on in Alta Vista and we catered to the working class , &amp;rdquo ; my father wrote . The Marco Polo served Canadian and Chinese-Canadian dishes galore &amp;mdash ; hot turkey sandwiches and chicken fried rice , grilled baby beef liver and garlic spare ribs . Now , the Thai restaurant Sweet Basil stands where the Marco Polo was . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among the restaurant pioneers of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Chinatown was the Shanghai , opened by Alan Kwan in 1971 . His children continue to run the restaurant , which they transformed into a hip , new-generation haunt . Still , Alan&amp;rsquo ; s son Edward , known widely in Ottawa as drag queen China Doll , has posted on Facebook that the Shanghai could well close in 2021 when it turns 50. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26051;TOOLONG ... title= " Ed Kwan is co-owner of Shanghai restaurant . " alt= " Ed Kwan is co-owner of Shanghai restaurant . " data-portal-copyright= " Bruno Schlumberger " **28;26087;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1545987 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the mid-1980s , entrepreneurial immigrants from Hong Kong opened the massive , cornerstone Chinatown restaurants Fuliwah ( now Oriental Chu Shing Restaurant ) and the Yangtze . Cart-service dim sum likely touched down in Ottawa at these two competing neighbours on Somerset Street West . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By the early 1990s , modest restaurants such as Jadeland , Ben Ben and Cafe Orient , all still open , brought more casual Hong Kong-style fare to Ottawa . They remain popular with Hong Kong expats , says Grace Xin , executive director of the Chinatown Business Improvement Area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Chinatown is the place that really gives you the feeling of home , &amp;rdquo ; says Xin , who came to Ottawa from southern China about 20 years ago . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A particular Chinatown success story is So Good Restaurant , which Peter So opened in 1994 . So retired in 2018 , selling the restaurant to one of his chefs , who has even gone on to open a second location on Springfield Road in New Edinburgh . Although in new hands , So Good retains its original , staggeringly long and diverse menu , which So says was such a hit because it offered so many vegetarian options . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This week , So and I chatted at Cafe Orient , a tiny Chinatown eatery that opened in 1993 . The humble place is naturally a little tired-looking , but we enjoyed small dishes and snacks that Xin told me are true tastes of Hong Kong . Fried bread was lightly sweet and milk tea was creamy and satisfying . Chiu chow dumplings were plump and fresh . Morsels of steamed rice roll were basic but irresistible , swathed in hoisin and peanut sauces . Shrimp wontons were tender and chunky of filling . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I still do think that Hung Sum , the &amp;agrave ; la carte dim sum specialist across from Plant Recreation Centre , serves Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s best dim sum . But Cafe Orient&amp;rsquo ; s no-fuss dishes definitely hit the spot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26117;TOOLONG ... title= " From inside Yangtze restaurant on Somerset Street in Ottawa . " alt= " From inside Yangtze restaurant on Somerset Street in Ottawa . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;26153;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1546004 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Szechuan&amp;rdquo ; food first appeared in Ottawa in the 1980s . But they were just rough approximations , usually made by Cantonese chefs and restaurateurs keen to jump on a trend . True Sichuanese dishes , many brimming with dried chilies and liberal doses of numbing Sichuan pepper , only arrived in Ottawa in the past five or six years , at a few restaurants that have since closed and the still-open Full House on Carling Avenue . Meanwhile , the Dalhousie Street restaurant Spicy House serves chili-heavy fare from different parts of China . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; What&amp;rsquo ; s the core market for these new businesses ? The 2016 census tallied more than 46,000 Chinese people in Ottawa , among an East and Southeast Asian population of more than 76,000 . Perhaps the most crucial customers are thousands of homesick and hungry international Asian students attending Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s post-secondary institutions . A casual glance inside many of the new and affordable Chinese restaurants seems like instant confirmation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Entrepreneurs have also proven in recent years that Asian night market festivals on Somerset Street West and elsewhere could draw crowds of as many as 25,000 &amp;mdash ; Asian expats and Canadian-born foodies &amp;mdash ; for everything from skewers of lamb , squid or potato to stinky tofu. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26183;TOOLONG ... title= " Jackie Xu BBQ 's chicken skewers are made during the Ottawa Night Market hosted by the Ottawa Asian Festival at the Lansdowne Park in Ottawa . " alt= " Jackie Xu BBQ 's chicken skewers are made during the Ottawa Night Market hosted by the Ottawa Asian Festival at the Lansdowne Park in Ottawa . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;26219;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1546022 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Two franchises of Chinese-based hot pot restaurants have opened in Ottawa &amp;mdash ; Liuyishou Hotpot Ottawa on Merivale Road and the more spacious , fancier Morals Village on Riverside Drive . Both provide sumptuous , fondue-like experiences grounded in a range of piping hot broths. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Crossing the Bridge noodles arrived stealthily in Ottawa a few years ago at the modest Vanier restaurant Yunnan Fusion . The dish , which originated in the southern Chinese province of Yunnan , has an elaborate back story . Legend says a wife who brought lunch to her husband , a studying scholar , split up broth and soup ingredients so they would degrade less during her trip that involved crossing a bridge . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Apocryphal or not , the tale lines up with how the soup is served at Yunnan Fusion , in homespun fashion , and at the franchise operations Dagu Rice Noodle on Riverside Drive and Yunshang Rice Noodle , which opened last year in Centretown. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In particular , the soups at Yunshang , a four-year-old Toronto-based business that also has locations in Vancouver , Montreal and New York , impressed me a lot . Appealing broths , authentic or otherwise , range from the original pork-y based one that&amp;rsquo ; s like a more brusque tonkotsu ramen broth , to a Thai-style curry broth to broths made puckeringly sour by pickled vegetables or scorchingly hot by chilies . Trays of accompaniments and optional add-ons won us over , while popcorn chicken and spicy tofu appetizers were also solidly made . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26249;TOOLONG ... title= " Guilin noodles at Sula Wok. " alt= " Guilin noodles at Sula Wok. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;26285;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1546024 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; But as far as Chinese noodles go , I&amp;rsquo ; m most beguiled by the Guilin noodles served at Sula Wok on Main Street . Xin-Hui Su , best known by her nickname Sula , sells a lot of fusion dishes &amp;mdash ; think Asian tacos . But I crave her Guilin rice noodles , the most popular dish in the region of southern China from where she hails . Slippery noodles mingle with the alternating sournesses of pickled daikon and mustard greens and the pop of fried soybeans . The sauce that Sula makes for her noodles &amp;mdash ; made with 50 dried herbs and spices and cooked for more than a day &amp;mdash ; is truly compelling . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My preferred Chinese dumplings come from a Centrepointe mall eatery that opened nearly two years ago . At Dumpling ? Dumpling ! , pork , beef , chicken and shrimp dumplings were made with quality ingredients and contained big , clean flavours , while funky Chinese mushrooms , asparagus , coriander , fennel and curry spoke clearly too in preparations . We&amp;rsquo ; ve preferred our orders pan-fried , to achieve a nice , crisp sear on one side . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26315;TOOLONG ... title= " Crystal dumplings at Dumpling ? Dumpling ! . " alt= " Crystal dumplings at Dumpling ? Dumpling ! . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;26351;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1546026 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Many of these restaurants are essentially specialists . Indeed , paring down menus does require less of a kitchen . So , when I&amp;rsquo ; m asked for my favourite Chinese restaurant in Ottawa , one with a broad menu and not just efficiency and expertise in serving soup or street foods , I respond : Harbin Restaurant , located in a March Road strip mall in Kanata. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Harbin takes its name from northeastern China&amp;rsquo ; s second largest city , and was opened in the fall of 2018 by Harbin native Hang Yu . He and his wife moved to Ottawa in 2012 to study engineering at the graduate level . But Yu , now 31 and a Kanata resident , went instead into the restaurant business , as hard as he says it is. &amp;ldquo ; I just want to share my hometown&amp;rsquo ; s food , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yu says he always wanted to be a chef and has relatives who run restaurants in Harbin and Beijing . His restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s chef is from Shanghai , but can cook Harbin specialties including a range of casseroles , other Northern Chinese dishes , authentically fiery and complex Sichuanese dishes and more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In my experience , whatever he cooks , he cooks well , from scrumptiously spicy Sichuan-style chicken wings to Harbin casseroles of tender meatballs , spinach and vermicelli to punchily flavoured stir-fries of eggplant or beef . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;26381;TOOLONG ... title= " Stir-Fried Potato Green Pepper and Eggplant of Harbin Restaurant . " alt= " Stir-Fried Potato Green Pepper and Eggplant of Harbin Restaurant . " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;26417;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1545925 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; About 80 per cent of Harbin&amp;rsquo ; s customers are Chinese expats , Yu says , before adding : &amp;ldquo ; Chinese , after staying in Canada a couple of years , their taste actually changes . You eat more sweet stuff , you eat more sugar . &amp;rdquo ; Harbin&amp;rsquo ; s recipes have been tweaked ever so slightly as a result , he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We actually teach lots of Canadian people to eat Chinese food , &amp;rdquo ; Yu continues . Family-style dining still seems novel to some of his guests , he says . And yet , sharing dishes at the table has been a convention of Chinese dining in Ottawa since at least the days of the Marco Polo Tavern Restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Yu , who plans to open another Harbin later this year on Merivale Road , makes me think that in a century , Chinese food in Ottawa has travelled very far from Rideau Street to Centretown to Alta Vista to Somerset Street West to March Road . It has changed radically , too , as have its purveyors . But at least two constants &amp;mdash ; deliciousness and a coming together of cultures through food &amp;mdash ; have endured . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;26447;TOOLONG " **32;26474;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; **32;26508;TOOLONG Chinese restaurants in **32;26542;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Cafe Orient&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 808 Somerset St. W. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Dumpling ? Dumpling&lt;/strong&gt; ! &lt;br&gt; 261 Centrepointe Dr. , &lt;a href= " https : **33;26576;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Harbin **25;26611;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 591 March Rd. , &lt;a href= " http : **28;26638;TOOLONG ! / " &gt; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Hung Sum&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 939 Somerset St. W. , &lt;a href= " https : **33;26668;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Morals Village Hot Pot&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 3987 Riverside Dr. , Unit 1 &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;So Good **25;26703;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 717 Somerset St. W. , &lt;a href= " http : **34;26730;TOOLONG ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Sula Wok&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 184 Main St. , &lt;a href= " https : **27;26766;TOOLONG " &gt;f ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Yunshang Rice Noodle&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 275 Bank St. , Unit 101 , &lt;a href= " http : //yunshang.ca/ " &gt;yunshang.ca&lt; ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26795;TOOLONG ... this : Traditional-ish pork dumplings from Double Awesome Chinese Food&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26830;TOOLONG ... slow death of plastic : Why the path to a plastic-less future is so complicated&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;26865;TOOLONG ... decade of deliciousness : The Citizen 's favourite dishes of the 2010s&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 24 Jan 2020 12:41:56 **45;26900;TOOLONG ... " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;27017;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1544284 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; After the long-standing and much-loved Holland Avenue restaurant Allium was devastated by a fire last March , its staff had to scatter and find work across the Ottawa area&amp;rsquo ; s dining scene . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By last fall , Arup Jana , Allium&amp;rsquo ; s chef-owner , was working at the Third , an eatery in Hintonburg . His wife Maggie , who ran the front of house at Allium , went to work at North and Navy and the Manx . Other members of the Allium team wound up at the fine-dining restaurants Atelier and Stofa , or across the Ottawa River , at Gatineau brunch hotspot Edgar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But they were determined to work again under the same roof , ideally when Allium re-opens , whenever that would be . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; re all really really close , &amp;rdquo ; Jana says . &amp;ldquo ; Everyone really had expressed the fact they wanted to get back together . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Allium , which Jana launched in 2004 , is still months away from re-opening . But he&amp;rsquo ; s getting the band back together this month , when the ex-Allium members reunite at Jana&amp;rsquo ; s new restaurant , Brassica. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27047;TOOLONG ... title= " Pork Belly Pancakes Pickled Red Onion/Pickled Chili/Herb Dressing/Chili Syrup/Sunny Egg . " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;27083;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1544290 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The project , at 309 Richmond Rd. in Westboro Village , will begin serving customers this coming Saturday . At that address until last Saturday was Vittoria in the Village , one of three Vittoria Trattoria eateries in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jana says he has , in fact , partnered with the Vittoria Trattoria group&amp;rsquo ; s owners at their invitation . &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re going to be totally hands off , we&amp;rsquo ; re going to run it as we see fit , &amp;rdquo ; Jana says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The switch from one restaurant to another will just involve a bit of decoration while the Italian restaurants tables , chairs and layout are retained . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a little bit stark right now , &amp;rdquo ; Jana says . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; d like to just warm it up a little bit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Vittoria Trattoria group has one restaurant on Riverside Drive , and has to rebuild its ByWard Market location , which itself was forced to close by a four-alarm fire last April . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jana says that Brassica , which takes its name from the genus of humble cruciferous vegetables that includes broccoli , cauliflower , cabbage and more , will offer the kind of menu that Allium , one of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s early advocates for small plates , did on Monday nights . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So , Brassica , which seats 64 , will serve small plates and some larger plates to allow sharing of multiple courses at tables , Jana says . Weekend brunches will also be served . &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ll have to feel out what the clientele is like , &amp;rdquo ; he adds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last March , fire decimated Allium , which is in a building that Jana co-owns , while he was in Chicago for a short vacation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27113;TOOLONG ... title= " Ottawa firefighters still working at Allium restaurant on Holland Ave . in Ottawa Friday March 1 , 2019. Jana told this newspaper then that he was enraged by&amp;nbsp ; the idea of the fire having been deliberately set . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t make any sense , &amp;rdquo ; Jana said then . &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s people living around there . My friend&amp;rsquo ; s daughter lives upstairs next to the restaurant &amp;hellip ; What if the wind changed ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Allium &amp;ldquo ; has been my whole life for the last 15 years . It&amp;rsquo ; s all I do , basically , &amp;rdquo ; said Jana , who is well-known in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s tight-knit restaurant community and a past participant in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Gold Medal Plates cooking competitions . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After the Allium fire , Jana had expected to re-open his first restaurant before the end of 2019 . The delays , he says , have been &amp;ldquo ; kind of ridiculous . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27179;TOOLONG ... title= " Dill and tuna dish at Brassica. He estimates that Allium could be open in half a year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As for Allium&amp;rsquo ; s famed banoffee pie dessert , Jana says it will be available at Brassica. &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s probably the No. 1 question we&amp;rsquo ; ve been getting , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27311;TOOLONG ... title= " Crispy Lemon Pancakes Caramel Apple/Vanilla Sauce/Spiced Maple Syrup/Whipped Cream .
