Out : Hearty , appealing Latin American meal at La Fiesta Latina made up for past disappointmenthttp : **26;27529;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;27557;TOOLONG ... title= " Chorizo , pork , plantains , beans and rice at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Chorizo , pork , plantains , beans and rice at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27593;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1540552 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;La Fiesta Latina&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 565 Somerset St. W. , 613-712-1717 , &lt;a href= " http : //lafiestalatina.ca " &gt;lafiestala ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Saturday 11 a.m. to 8 p.m. , Sunday 11 a.m. to 6 p.m. , closed Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; up to $17 for dishes &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; Steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At La Fiesta Latina , our third time was a charm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In December , we went twice to the Mexican-Latino restaurant that had opened in early November on Somerset Street West just west of Bay Street . At our initial try , the food was promising . But when we visited again , the bland food and slack service left us quite unimpressed. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Happily for us , and the business , a lunch last week was much more enjoyable . Tasty , homey , heaping plates and lively , engaged service were much more on point . We left wanting to return to explore the breadth of this unpretentious place&amp;rsquo ; s picture-filled menus ( all-day breakfast and all-day other dishes ) . Plus , where else can we go for food from Mexico , Colombia , Cuba , Ecuador and elsewhere in the Americas , a good percentage of which are vegetarian . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The culinary diversity corresponds to what our charming server told us last week . The kitchen team is made up of &amp;ldquo ; all kinds of brown people , &amp;rdquo ; he said . The restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s website shows photos of aproned women in the mostly-from-scratch kitchen , from which emerges not just tacos and enchiladas , but also &amp;mdash ; in our best experience &amp;mdash ; chicken enchiladas in a sumptuous mole sauce , appealing Colombian arepitas ( unleavened patties of maize dough ) and assorted plates heavily laden with seasoned rice , plantains , savoury black beans and proteins. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On our first go , we tried a selection of the restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s tacos , all of which had the ring of authenticity . Braised meats had been steeped in bonafide , big-flavoured sauces before meeting their store-bought tortillas , and while some looked similar , they presented us with different flavours . These were not the more chef-y tacos that reign elsewhere , beautifully garnished and made with freshly pressed tortillas . But they provided the fundamental taco satisfactions , and an array of hot sauces will help if extra heat is your thing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27623;TOOLONG ... title= " Tacos at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Tacos at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27659;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539208 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; ( I will note that a scan of La Fiesta Latina&amp;rsquo ; s Facebook page shows more heavily garnished and even interesting tacos , including one made with stewed lamb. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Venezuelan platter came with white rice that had additional flavours cooked in , thick-cut slices of plantain and good , garlicky black beans , although the shredded pork was awfully dry , whether by design or as a result of being reheated or kept heated for a long time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27689;TOOLONG ... title= " Mi Bello Venezuelana platter at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Mi Bello Venezuelana platter at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27725;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539203 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; About the second visit , perhaps the less said , the better . I will mention that some egg-y breakfast items were lacklustre , and our generally inattentive and perfunctory server &amp;mdash ; he was , to be fair , beleaguered during a weekend brunch rush &amp;mdash ; deprived us of water , which we had ask for , and also chips and salsa on the house , which other tables received but we never did . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27755;TOOLONG ... title= " Huevos Mexicana at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Huevos Mexicana at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27791;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539213 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Closest to the plus side of the ledger at that brunch were the rustically tasty chicharrons ( nuggets of fried pork ) , which were crunchy , meaty and fatty all in one and garnished with wedges of lime . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27821;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicharrons at La Fiesta Latina , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Chicharrons at La Fiesta Latina , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27857;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539199 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; So let&amp;rsquo ; s skip ahead to last week&amp;rsquo ; s lunch . Redemption ! &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First , our spirits rose with the immediate arrival of complimentary corn chips , fresh house-made pico de gallo and salsa , courtesy of our lively , new-to-us server whose fun demeanour rubbed off on us. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27887;TOOLONG ... title= " Chips and salsa at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Chips and salsa at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27923;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539211 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then , that chicken enchilada with mole sauce ? A savoury , sweet , spicy , chocolate-y winner . You could have served me cardboard with that sauce and I would have enjoyed it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;27953;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken enchiladas with mole at La Fiesta Latina on Somerset Street West " alt= " Chicken enchiladas with mole at La Fiesta Latina on Somerset Street West " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;27989;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539197 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chunks of pork shoulder and house-made , somewhat spicy chorizo sausages were stars on two fully loaded plates . The La Fiesta Latina platter pleased , too , with chunky fresh guacamole , among its many components . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28019;TOOLONG ... title= " Chorizo , pork , plantains , beans and rice at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Chorizo , pork , plantains , beans and rice at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28055;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1540552 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28085;TOOLONG ... title= " La Fiesta Latina platter at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " La Fiesta Latina platter at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28121;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539212 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Arepitas filled with pork were toothsome . At the previous lunch , the egg-filled arepitas were tough &amp;mdash ; one , almost tooth-breakingly so . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28151;TOOLONG ... title= " Arepitas at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Arepitas at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28187;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1540553 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28217;TOOLONG ... title= " Breakfast arepitas and black beans at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Breakfast arepitas and black beans at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28253;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539200 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A small bowl of Ajiaco soup &amp;mdash ; an appealing Colombian soup starring chicken and corn &amp;mdash ; was simple but delicious . It came with capers and cream on the side , which the server said were necessary to make the soup really sing . Funny , those accompaniments were absent when one of us ordered Ajiaco at our second visit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28283;TOOLONG ... title= " Ajiaco soup at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Ajiaco soup at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28319;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1540551 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; After our third visit , I can recommend going to La Fiesta Latina for the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s desserts alone . A tall slab of tres leches cake was supremely moist , indulgent and sweet . Big pieces of chocoflan cake hit the spot with their fudge-y bottom and creamy top . Arroz con leche was a reliable pleasure for fans of rice pudding . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28349;TOOLONG ... title= " Tres leches cake at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Tres leches cake at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28385;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539201 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28415;TOOLONG ... title= " Chocoflan cake at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Chocoflan cake at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28451;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539209 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;28481;TOOLONG ... title= " Arroz con leche ( rice pudding ) at La Fiesta Latina " alt= " Arroz con leche ( rice pudding ) at La Fiesta Latina " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;28517;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539196 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurant is a bright , narrow , welcoming space of 26 seats split between chairs and a hard banquette flanking tables . Its white walls are decorated with a mish-mash of framed photos , evocative art pieces and sombreros . The eatery is also a mini-grocery with some canned and bottled Latin-American staples for sale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Recently licensed , La Fiesta Latina serves beer as well as imported soft drinks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m partial to humble , friendly , unique places that serve consistently good food representing faraway homelands . Until last week , I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t convinced that La Fiesta Latina was one of them . After that pleasing lunch , I have my fingers crossed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;28547;TOOLONG " **32;28574;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;28608;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " mailto:https : **26;28643;TOOLONG ... Jay hosts carbon-conscious dinner : ' As the national capital , we need to set a standard ' &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28671;TOOLONG ... bets , Jan. 16 to 22 : Theatre from the North , and a Bob Marley celebration&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28706;TOOLONG ... Evan Hansen , Mean Girls , Wicked coming to Ottawa as part of 2020-21 Broadway Across Canada season&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 15 Jan 2020 16:33:25 **45;28741;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsGray Jay hosts carbon-conscious dinner : ' As the national capital , we need to set a standard'http : **31;28788;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;28821;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef Dominique Dufour poses for a photo at Gray Jay in Ottawa Tuesday Jan 14 , 2020. " alt= " Chef Dominique Dufour poses for a photo at Gray Jay in Ottawa Tuesday Jan 14 , 2020. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;28857;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1539927 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; For almost 15 years , Dominique Dufour has been concerned about climate change . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Now , as chef and co-owner of Gray Jay Hospitality , she has committed to reducing her six-month-old Preston Street restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s carbon footprint. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dufour will serve her most eco-conscious dinner yet on Sunday , relying solely on produce from Quebec and Ontario , and also restricting her &amp;agrave ; la carte menu to vegetarian dishes . At her &amp;ldquo ; carbon-conscious pop-up , &amp;rdquo ; Dufour will offer five dishes working in her open kitchen with Montreal chef Maur&amp;iacute ; n Arellano. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below , Dufour , who came to Ottawa from Montreal in 2018 to open Norca in the Le Germain Ottawa Hotel , discusses why locavorism is better for the planet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Why will you serve only Quebec and Ontario produce on Sunday ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : We are only using Quebec and Ontario produce to reduce our carbon footprint due to fossil fuel used in the transport . When we use imported produce or goods such as salt , sugar , coffee , most of them are brought to us by cargo ships . Cargo ships run on bunker fuel or waste oil , which is the dirty byproduct of oil refining . It is so thick that when it is cold outside , it can be walked upon . Bunker fuel contains 2,000 times more sulphur than diesel and one cargo ship pollutes to the equivalent of 50 million cars . At the moment , global trades and cargo shipping pollute more than the 760 million cars we have on earth . Which is one of the big reasons why in my restaurant , every day we only use fruit , vegetables , proteins from Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , Canada is still one of the biggest countries in the world , so transport by trucks when a product comes from Vancouver or Saint John&amp;rsquo ; s is still a polluting factor . So we decided to try and reduce our footprint even further and stick to produce from our immediate surroundings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Why will Sunday 's dinner be vegetarian ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : We want people to realize that meat is so costly . Not only does industrial meat farming contribute hugely to deforestation , carbon production and pollution , it is also a huge burden on our ever-so-depleting fresh water supply . As a conscious consumer , I can choose small-farm , eco-responsible meat producers who mitigate their impact on deforestation , carbon and pollution . However , it is impossible to reduce the fresh water use . Vegetables need about 322 litres of water per kilogram and fruits 964 litres of water per kg . In comparison , chickens require 43,25 litres , pork requires 5,988 litres and beef has the highest cost at some 15,000 litres per kg. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What was the genesis for your eco-awareness ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Over the years , I have always been very concerned about climate change . When I first heard of it and started researching how to do my part , 14 years ago , I became vegetarian , then vegan . I spent five years eating an exclusively plant-based diet . That&amp;rsquo ; s also when I started cooking seriously and over time I started working in vegan kitchens , which is where I realized that the way we were eating plant-based made no sense to me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Basing a diet on coconut milk , cashews and avocados ( this is still nine to 10 years ago &amp;mdash ; the options were far more limited ) seemed counter-productive to reducing harm for the transport reasons I mentioned . So I started researching locavorism and found a philosophy I could get behind . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How does Gray Jay operate accordingly ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I started creating relationships with our farmers and the idea of encouraging them to the fullest extent &amp;mdash ; without resorting to ordering California strawberries or Chilean tomatoes when we had a bad season &amp;mdash ; seemed like a goal that would also drive my creativity . I met some farmers who raised meat very wholesomely and organically and realized that if I wanted to offer that , the best way would be to learn how to butcher and only consume whole animals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the moment , we are using a whole wild boar that we purchased in November , taken down and used piece by piece . Compare that to a restaurant that makes the decision to serve hanger steak . There are only two pieces of hanger steak per cow . Order a few hundred and you&amp;rsquo ; ve created a disproportionate demand to what the market can offer and people need to slaughter more to meet certain demand and sell other parts for pennies on the dollar . The bacon industry is killing pork farmers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the restaurant at the moment , we only serve two large plates that are animal-based , one large plate of fish . All the proteins come from Quebec or Ontario at the moment . Everything else on the menu is plant-based and we make charcuterie from all of the off-cuts and trims from all the animals we get . We only use Canadian fruit and vegetables as well as proteins . We also forage lots of wild foods as well as dehydrate and ferment vegetable scraps to create our own powders and garnishes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So , these principles have become the building blocks of my food philosophy over time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In a smaller food city , it is harder to get a lot of products in Ottawa . It requires a lot more work to be eco-conscious . But as the national capital , we need to set a standard . We need to be the change we want to see . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And now , it seems like things are getting worse exponentially faster . While building the restaurant , we got displaced by the Gatineau flood that made our house inaccessible for a month . Then there&amp;rsquo ; s this incredibly warm winter . The Australia forest fire . Wine harvest in the Niagara Valley this year extended until November ! &amp;nbsp ; With greater impact , I believe we need to take greater actions . Clearly we are not doing enough . That&amp;rsquo ; s where our inspiration came from . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Outside of the restaurant , how do you live carbon-consciously ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I have pretty much the same values as in my professional life . I don&amp;rsquo ; t buy bananas and avocados or fruit , vegetables , proteins from outside of Canada &amp;mdash ; although I haven&amp;rsquo ; t been able to give up my morning coffee yet . I eat very little meat and Devon , my partner , and I only have one small car , a Toyota Matrix . We cycle to work in the summer , fall and spring and when we can afford to , we will replace our car with an electric one . We do travel to expand our horizons and I only recently heard of the initiative to plant trees to offset your flight but I would love to do that next time I fly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Why are you cooking Sunday with Maur ? n Arellano ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Maurin is an awesome woman who is originally from Mexico but who I met in Montreal . Being from Mexico , locavorism was like second nature to her and we connected through the Montreal women chefs association that I created in 2017 . In the summer , she does a series of urban green roof garden pop-ups and cooks on farms regularly . She also owns a community kitchen in Montreal where young cooks can rent space to start their own food business , create events , teach classes . She is a very inspiring , community-driven woman . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Carbon Conscious Pop-up&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; When : Sunday , Jan. 19 &lt;br&gt; Where : Gray Jay Hospitality , 300 Preston St. &lt;br&gt; On the &amp;agrave ; la carte menu : Hydroponic salad with vegetable lace , flowers and cranberry spritz ; soy &amp;ldquo ; burrata , &amp;rdquo ; Quebec strawberries , peppery sprouts , sweet clover gastrique , Ontario walnuts , plant salumi ; Naked oat and flax tart , duck egg , roasted Quebec tomatoes , fermented green Ontario daikon , sour mustard seed and chlorophyll salt ; laminated beens and t&amp;eacute ; n&amp;eacute ; brion pasta , soy whey cacio e pepe , brassica ; candy cat and chanterelle layered torta &lt;br&gt; Plus : carbon-conscious wines &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28887;TOOLONG ... hidden cost of almond milk : ' Exploited and disrespected ' bees are dying by the billions&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;28922;TOOLONG ... Out : Hey Kitchen 's piping hot fusion fare presents Japanese takes on simple Western **26;29738;TOOLONG &lt;/div&gt; Peter HumThu , 09 Jan 2020 19:35:00 **45;29766;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : Hey Kitchen 's piping hot fusion fare presents Japanese takes on simple Western disheshttp : **34;29813;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;29849;TOOLONG ... title= " Smoked duck breast and mushrooms with rice at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Smoked duck breast and mushrooms with rice at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;29885;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535240 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Hey Kitchen&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 710 Somerset St. W. , 613- &lt;span class= " LrzXr zdqRlf kno-fv " **25;29915;TOOLONG , &lt;a href= " http : **27;29942;TOOLONG " &gt;he ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; 11 a.m. to 10 p.m. daily &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; dishes up to $16.99 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Japanese have a word for it &amp;mdash ; yoshoku. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Japanese cuisine , yoshoku refers to fusion dishes that are basically Japanized forms of European or otherwise foreign dishes . Japanese curries that are much more mellow than their Indian inspirations are a famous example . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Although&amp;nbsp ; the generically named Hey Kitchen , which opened in early December where Korean restaurant Owl of Minerva had been in Chinatown , does not come out and announce its indebtedness to yoshoku , that kind of food enjoys pride of place on its menu . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This bright and affordable spot , already popular with young Asian customers , numbers among the latest wave of cozy and specialized restaurants to have opened in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most significant in terms of Hey Kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s distinctiveness is a menu that is taut , yet still roams across Asia , and even bewilders a bit with its novelties , which are presented matter-of-fact and without the kind of context or back story I&amp;rsquo ; m providing now with the help of Wikipedia. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Leaving aside Hey Kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s appetizers , which can be as familiar as french fries or as outr&amp;eacute ; as chicken feet with pickled peppers , the larger offerings here head in two similarly contrasting directions . There are the rice- or pasta-based yoshoku dishes , and there are noodle bowls and soups that are definitely Chinese . ( In fact , the restaurant is run by Chinese expats. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A big chunk of Hey Kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s menu can be traced back to the innovations of a Japanese fast-food franchise called Pepper Lunch . Its hallmark dish , which dates to the mid-1990s , places a mound of cooked rice surrounded by thinly sliced beef on an iron plate that&amp;rsquo ; s been electromagnetically heated until it&amp;rsquo ; s screaming hot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hey Kitchen serves eight similarly made &amp;ldquo ; special teppan pepper rice&amp;rdquo ; dishes . Of course , a beefy version is offered . But so , too , are versions with eel , tiger shrimp , smoked duck breast , smoked chicken , salmon , ox tongue or pork neck , some of which might be influenced by Chinese palates . All of these iron plates arrive at your table on wooden platters and are ringed by a red paper sash warning that the iron plate was heated to 200 C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With these dishes come gravy boats filled with the brown sauce that puts the &amp;ldquo ; pepper&amp;rdquo ; in &amp;ldquo ; pepper rice , &amp;rdquo ; although as per Japanese tastes , it is not that peppery and is more savoury and sweet . The idea is that the gravy hits the hot plate and &amp;mdash ; voila ! A cloud of aromatic steam wafts. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We tried the beef ( $11.99 ) and salmon ( $12.99 ) versions and both were hearty and enjoyable , with the one proviso that the meat , or fish in particular , will become overcooked if left too long on the plate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;29971;TOOLONG ... title= " Beef with rice at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Beef with rice at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30007;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535476 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30037;TOOLONG ... title= " Salmon with rice at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Salmon with rice at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30073;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535241 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30103;TOOLONG ... title= " Smoked duck breast and mushrooms with rice at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Smoked duck breast and mushrooms with rice at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30139;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535240 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hey Kitchen also serves heaps of spaghetti or fettuccine on its blisteringly hot , warning-encircled iron plates . The same protein choices apply . Pepper sauce is available , but not exclusively so . Diners can also opt for tomato , cream or curry sauces , and even additional toppings such as mushrooms , bacon , fried or marinated egg , kimchi and corn are available for customized eating . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30169;TOOLONG ... title= " Fettucine with shrimp , fried egg , cream sauce at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Fettucine with shrimp , fried egg , cream sauce at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30205;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535294 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30235;TOOLONG ... title= " Fettucine with smoked chicken , fried egg , mushrooms , tomato sauce at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Fettucine with smoked chicken , fried egg , mushrooms , tomato sauce at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30271;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535293 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Call me a pasta purist , but I preferred the pepper rice dishes to the fettuccine-based dishes I tried . Above all , the tomato and cream sauces did little for me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another Japanese dish served here is omurice , a fusion dish dating back a century or so that involves thin omelettes stuffed with well-sauced rice . At Hey Kitchen , heavily sauced omurice dishes come with proteins on the side or on top . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Overall , I preferred the omurice to the pasta and rice dishes . I can vouch for steak omurice with black pepper sauce ( $15.99 ) , and especially fried chicken cutlet omurice with not-so-spicy Japanese curry sauce ( $12.99 ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30301;TOOLONG ... title= " Striploin steak omurice at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Striploin steak omurice at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30337;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535467 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30367;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken cutlet omurice at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Chicken cutlet omurice at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30403;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535292 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The eatery&amp;rsquo ; s nine noodle choices are basically Sichuan-style bowls made with your choice of noodles and protein in a soupy broth that&amp;rsquo ; s as spicy and even numbing as you like , from not spicy or numbing to extra spicy or numbing. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I had Chong Qing noodles &amp;ldquo ; with minced pork and peas&amp;rdquo ; ( $11.99 ) , ordered &amp;ldquo ; regular spicy&amp;rdquo ; and &amp;ldquo ; regular numbing , &amp;rdquo ; which was more than intense enough on both counts thanks to ample quantities of chilies and Sichuan pepper , respectively . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30433;TOOLONG ... title= " Chongqing noodles at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Chongqing noodles at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30469;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535244 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Its minced pork turned out to be morsels of pork belly and its peas turned out to be chickpeas . Surprises aside , it was a big , satisfying lunch , with components and garnishes including peanuts , sesame paste , cilantro , green onions , half a marinated egg and pickled vegetables making themselves felt . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The menu&amp;rsquo ; s four stewed soups are for , I think , enthusiasts of long-simmered Chinese broths that I think of as health tonics rather than meals . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My wife ordered the coconut and chicken soup ( $8.99 ) , thinking from its minimal description that she would receive something like Thai tom kha gai . But when she lifted the lid , she saw a brownish broth , chunks of coconut and bony chunks of chicken . I can guess that herbal black chicken soup and silk chicken with conch and ginseng soup would equally bewilder those unaccustomed to their authentic qualities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30499;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken coconut soup at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Chicken coconut soup at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30535;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535291 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Service here was fast and casual , with servers bringing carafes of acidulated hot and cold water and then whatever dishes are ready first , which could be your sizzling plate of pasta followed by your appetizer. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We&amp;rsquo ; ve had takoyaki ( Japanese deep-fried dough balls with bits of octopus in the middle , $7.99 ) which were suitably crisp and savoury , and popcorn chicken ( $7.99 ) , which was just so-so , lacking in the crunch I prefer in that guilty pleasure . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30565;TOOLONG ... title= " Takoyaki at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Takoyaki at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30601;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535243 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;30631;TOOLONG ... title= " Popcorn chicken at Hey Kitchen " alt= " Popcorn chicken at Hey Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;30667;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1535242 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurant is not licensed and serves no dessert . If you need something sweet , there are nearby bubble tea shops . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div class= " 3U2q6dcdZCrTrR42Nxby JWNdg1hee9Rz6bIGvG1c allowTextSelection " &gt; &lt;div class= " rpse361 " &gt; &lt;div dir= " auto " &gt; &lt;div class= " 3U2q6dcdZCrTrR42Nxby JWNdg1hee9Rz6bIGvG1c allowTextSelection " &gt; &lt;div class= " rpse361 " &gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;div dir= " auto " &gt; &lt;p&gt;So , there 's a lot to try and even figure out at Hey Kitchen , which in my imagination anyway is like a diner on the other side of the world . None of it is fancy , but there are still some small thrills and larger , if basic , satisfactions. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div class= " container-fluid bg-primary text-center footer-copyright p-3 " &gt;&lt;a href= " **25;30697;TOOLONG " **44;30724;TOOLONG &lt;div&gt;&lt;/div&gt; **29;30770;TOOLONG IN THE **31;30801;TOOLONG &lt;div&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;30834;TOOLONG ... decade of deliciousness : The Citizen 's favourite dishes of the **25;30869;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;30896;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : At Maht in Chinatown , spicy Korean stews ward off winterhttp : **34;31075;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;31111;TOOLONG ... title= " Sizzling spicy squid , purple rice and kimchi at Maht " alt= " Sizzling spicy squid , purple rice and kimchi at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31147;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1527892 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;31177;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 726 Somerset St. W. , 613-680-7268 , &lt;a href= " http : //themaht.com " &gt;themaht.com&lt;/ ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Thursday , 11:30 a.m. to 2:30 p.m. and 5 to 8:30 p.m. , Saturday and Sunday 11:30 a.m. to 2:30 p.m. and 5 to 9 p.m. , closed Monday &lt;br&gt; **35;31212;TOOLONG : most dishes up to $17.95 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Late last month , we bundled up in our puffiest parkas and ventured into this winter&amp;rsquo ; s first heavy-duty cold snap , wishing for a dinner that would take the chill off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We were the only people hardy enough to eat that night at Maht , a 20-seat Korean restaurant that opened in Chinatown last spring where Wei&amp;rsquo ; s Noodle House had been . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since the early 1990s , that hole-in-the-wall address on Somerset Street West has housed a succession of tiny Vietnamese restaurants that each served appealing , affordable dishes despite their cramped quarters . We crossed our fingers for similar satisfactions at Maht , which in Korean simply means &amp;ldquo ; taste . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cutting wind and -20 C temperatures outside called , we thought , for spicy , substantial Korean stews &amp;mdash ; robust , rugged bowls flavoured with funky gochujang paste , pepper and finely ground chiles , perhaps with bony chunks of pork or chicken adding meaty heft . We were not disappointed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A sign on the wall announced that Maht&amp;rsquo ; s open kitchen was serving gamjatang , a rustic soup made with pork neck bones . Having been cooked directly on the stove&amp;rsquo ; s gas element , the bowl landed on our table while still bubbling ominously . We divvied up its spicy , porky contents , fortified ourselves with broth that set our mouths thrumming and plucked bits of long-simmered meat , soft but leached somewhat of flavour , from their bones . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31249;TOOLONG ... title= " Gamjatang ( pork bone stew ) at Maht " alt= " Gamjatang ( pork bone stew ) at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31285;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1532008 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Less effort to eat , and perhaps even more comforting and satisfying , was Maht&amp;rsquo ; s pork and kimchi soondubu , another potently spicy stew in which soft tofu , kimchi of middling pungency and slices of pork provided contrasts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31315;TOOLONG ... title= " pork kimchi soondubu at Maht " alt= " pork kimchi soondubu at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31351;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1527896 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While that dish and jjimdak , a hearty chicken stew of jolting and long-lasting pepperiness , have been on Maht&amp;rsquo ; s menu for months , they seemed especially suited to warding off winter . That stew , which was meant for two and was Maht&amp;rsquo ; s priciest item by a factor of two at $34.95 , teemed with pieces of bone-in chicken and potatoes , slippery glass noodles , bits of mushrooms , carrots , green onions and more in a sauce that was peppery above all but also soy-salty and a touch sweet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31381;TOOLONG ... title= " Jjim dack ( spicy braised chicken stew ) at Maht " alt= " Jjim dack ( spicy braised chicken stew ) at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31417;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1529104 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; With all of the stews came bowls of nutty purple rice . We&amp;rsquo ; ve also received small complimentary servings of kimchi and various pickled vegetables whose funky sourness offset the heat and richness of our dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;We proceeded our stews with some contrasting appetizers that hit the spot . Tteokkochi were skewers of crisp-fried rice cakes topped with squiggles of gochujang ( savoury fermented chili paste ) and pumpkin seeds , while the japchae , stir-fried glass noodles and vegetables , was salty and sweet . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31447;TOOLONG ... title= " Tteokkochi ( fried rice cakes ) at Maht " alt= " Tteokkochi ( fried rice cakes ) at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31483;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528991 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31513;TOOLONG ... title= " Japchae at Maht " alt= " Japchae at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31549;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528990 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At an earlier lunch visit , we began with the seafood pajeon pancake , whose tender shrimp and squid and overall tastiness made a good impression . We also had a plate of tteokbokki &amp;mdash ; rice cakes stir-fried in a spicy mish-mash with slices of fish cake and cabbage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31579;TOOLONG ... title= " Seafood pancake at Maht " alt= " Seafood pancake at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31615;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528995 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31645;TOOLONG ... title= " Stir-fried rice cakes ( Tteokbokki ) at Maht " alt= " Stir-fried rice cakes ( Tteokbokki ) at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31681;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528997 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At that lunch , a three-chili spicy stir-fry of squid raised the bar for heat tolerance . The bulgogi- and pork-based rice bowls at lunch were more lacklustre though , especially compared to the roaringly flavourful squid dish but also because their purple rice was overcooked. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31711;TOOLONG ... title= " Sizzling spicy squid at Maht " alt= " Sizzling spicy squid at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31747;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528996 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31777;TOOLONG ... title= " Spicy pork bowl at Maht " alt= " Spicy pork bowl at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31813;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1532009 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31843;TOOLONG ... title= " Bulgogi bowl at Maht , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Bulgogi bowl at Maht , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31879;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1528992 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dessert here was limited to ice creams &amp;mdash ; black sesame , red bean and ginger &amp;mdash ; which were nicely garnished and cooled our palates after those zippy stews. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;31909;TOOLONG ... title= " Ginger , black sesame and red bean ice cream at Maht " alt= " Ginger , black sesame and red bean ice cream at Maht " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;31945;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1527895 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Maht is licensed and serves a very small section of beers and alcohol . Service was attentive and keen to please . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Maht , we hope , has what it takes to be more than a refuge from the polar vortex . The women that run it apply good standards preparing food that&amp;rsquo ; s in keeping with the wee kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s size . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And yet , on social media the restaurant has teased us with glimpses of less common dishes that we hope might one day be offered . We&amp;rsquo ; ll be interested to see if Maht can enlarge its menu and serve more tastes of Korea . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;31975;TOOLONG " **32;32002;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumTue , 31 Dec 2019 18:36:11 **45;32036;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsA decade of deliciousness : The Citizen 's favourite dishes of the 2010shttp : **35;32083;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;32120;TOOLONG ... title= " Shan-style noodles at Rangoon Restaurant . October 19 , 2018. " alt= " Shan-style noodles at Rangoon Restaurant . October 19 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;32156;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526240 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; From my run in the 2010s as a restaurant critic , here are the dozen dishes that burn most brightly in my memory . That goes double for the atomic jerk chicken . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve compiled my favourites as a grand , unfurling , tasting menu &amp;mdash ; something epic to see out the decade and welcome in 2020. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If only all of these dishes were still available . In the spirit of looking back , I give special nods to several restaurants that shut in recent years , leaving me and no doubt others still feeling deprived . Should old acquaintances be forgot , and all that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32186;TOOLONG ... title= " Cheese Sunflower at Semsem restaurant . August 16 , 2018. " alt= " Cheese Sunflower at Semsem restaurant . August 16 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;32222;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526231 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 ) Bread at Semsem ( 2018 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; What to pick from the hidden-gem bakery and coffee shop in a South Keys mall , where savoury Arabic breads are baked to order ? It&amp;rsquo ; s a very tough choice , given that many appeal . I most fondly recall the &amp;ldquo ; cheese sunflower&amp;rdquo ; pastry , a wreath-shaped loaf that wraps bread , dotted with sesame seeds and Persian thyme , around the salty punch of halloumi cheese . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32252;TOOLONG ... title= " Crab salad at Navarra. " alt= " Crab salad at Navarra. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32288;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526233 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;2 ) Crab salad at Navarra ( 2012 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; It was a big blow to Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s dining scene in 2017 when chef-owner Ren&amp;eacute ; Rodriguez closed Navarra on William Street . While the 2014 Top Chef Canada winner now runs the kitchen at the Lord Elgin Hotel&amp;rsquo ; s Grill 41 , Navarra was Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s personal culinary playground , with a menu that drew upon his Mexican roots and Spanish inspirations . I was wowed by his salad of sweet crabmeat , avocado and even dried mango , nestled in a puddle of grapefruit , serrano chili and vanilla aguachile , and topped with black sesame ash . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32318;TOOLONG ... title= " Tomatillo Gazpacho , with cracker , compressed vodka watermelon , cured Arctic char , shiso leaf , lumpfish roll , cucumber rolls and Thai chili at Carben. " alt= " Tomatillo Gazpacho , with cracker , compressed vodka watermelon , cured Arctic char , shiso leaf , lumpfish roll , cucumber rolls and Thai chili at Carben. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32354;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526234 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;3 ) Tomatillo gazpacho at Carben ( 2015 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; At his Hintonburg restaurant , chef-owner Kevin Benes had a summery hit several years ago with this perfectly balanced soup that was topped with a long almond cracker , in turn garnished with ribbons of cucumber , compressed watermelon and house-cured Arctic char . It was a beautiful dish that tasted as good as it looked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32384;TOOLONG ... title= " " Summer bouquet " salad at Alice . " alt= " " Summer bouquet " salad at Alice . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32420;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526235 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;4 ) Salad at Alice ( 2019 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; Only the most hardened carnivore could fail to be delighted by the flavours and ingenuity at chef-owner Briana Kim&amp;rsquo ; s new vegetable-focused fine-dining restaurant . Her mini-bouquet of greens from Juniper Farms , garnished with flaxseed , offset by a ring of kohlrabi enclosing sea buckthorn jelly and dressed with fermented almond milk , set a new standard for salads . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32450;TOOLONG ... title= " Tuna crudo at Supply and Demand . " alt= " Tuna crudo at Supply and Demand . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32486;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526236 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 ) Tuna crudo at Supply and Demand ( 2012 , 2019 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; At the Wellington Street West restaurant , this perfectly calibrated , big-flavoured dish doesn&amp;rsquo ; t budge from chef-owner Steve Wall&amp;rsquo ; s menu for good reason . Raw fish dishes in Ottawa don&amp;rsquo ; t get any better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32516;TOOLONG ... title= " Shan-style noodles at Rangoon Restaurant . October 19 , 2018. " alt= " Shan-style noodles at Rangoon Restaurant . October 19 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;32552;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526240 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;6 ) Shan noodles at Rangoon ( 2018 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; So humble , yet so complex . At Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s only Burmese restaurant , rice noodles came blanketed in succulent chunks of chicken and bolstered by a savoury sauce , chilies , pickled mustard leaves that added an enticing amount of sourness , coriander and sesame seeds . &amp;ldquo ; Mix , mix , &amp;rdquo ; chef-owner Ngun Tial said when she served us . We were glad we did . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32582;TOOLONG ... title= " Atomic Jerk chicken at Flavours of the Caribbean . " alt= " Atomic Jerk chicken at Flavours of the Caribbean . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32618;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526246 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;7 ) " Atomic " jerk chicken at Flavours of the Caribbean ( 2015 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; Confession : I could only handle the intoxicatingly delicious jerk chicken at the now-shuttered Flavours of the Caribbean at its medium level of spiciness . But I cite chef Frederick White&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; atomic&amp;rdquo ; jerk chicken because I remember so well the agonies that its maximum-level heat inflicted on a co-worker/consenting victim . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 640 " height= " 360 " src= " https : **34;32648;TOOLONG ... frameborder= " 0 " allow= " accelerometer ; autoplay ; encrypted-media ; gyroscope ; picture-in-picture " **34;32684;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Curry at Sam&amp;rsquo ; s Cafe . &lt;strong&gt;8 ) Massaman curry at Sam 's Cafe ( 2014 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; In the back of his Hintonburg corner store , Sam Souryavong made bold yet exquisite Thai curries that shamed their more North Americanized , homogenized peers in Ottawa . I loved most the Massaman curry , that relied on Souryavong&amp;rsquo ; s house-made , thrillingly complex curry paste and as a result teemed with heat and flavour , including hints of nutmeg and cardamom . Sadly , Sam&amp;rsquo ; s Cafe closed , and is now the Merry Dairy ice cream outpost . I like its frozen treats , but visits there are still bittersweet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32720;TOOLONG ... title= " Duck sampling at feast + revel within the Andaz Ottawa hotel in the ByWard Market . " alt= " Duck sampling at feast + revel within the Andaz Ottawa hotel in the ByWard Market . " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;32756;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526251 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;9 ) Duck platter at feast + revel ( 2016 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; Soon after feast + revel opened in the Andaz Ottawa ByWard Market , executive chef Stephen La Salle knocked us out with his duck platter that was fully loaded with excellent components that celebrated the best of the bird &amp;mdash ; roasted duck breast , smoked and cured duck &amp;ldquo ; pastrami , &amp;rdquo ; duck confit , fried duck wings and plenty of rich duck liver aioli. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32786;TOOLONG ... title= " Guinea fowl at Bar Lupulus in Ottawa Monday March 18 , 2019. " alt= " Guinea fowl at Bar Lupulus in Ottawa Monday March 18 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;32822;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526288 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;10 ) Guinea fowl at Bar Lupulus ( 2019 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; My favourite savoury dish of 2019 was chef Justin Champagne&amp;rsquo ; s guinea fowl , which had its funkiness amped up with a koji rice marinade and umami-rich , snail- and truffle-enriched wheatberry risotto . Come to think of it , it&amp;rsquo ; s one of my very favourite mains of the decade . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32852;TOOLONG ... title= " Dry-aged ribeye at Bar Laurel . " alt= " Dry-aged ribeye at Bar Laurel . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32888;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526254 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;11 ) Dry-aged ribeye at Bar Laurel ( 2016 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; Chef-owner Jon Svazas&amp;rsquo ; go-big-or-go-home , 20-ounce ribeye that had been dry-aged for 80 days delivered concentrated beefiness with nuanced side notes and fat that was too tasty to squander . What&amp;rsquo ; s more , the meat was made better with demi-glace , salsa verde , fingerlings , enoki mushrooms and broccolini. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;32918;TOOLONG ... title= " Ice cream at Odile. " alt= " Ice cream at Odile. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;32954;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526263 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;12 ) Ice cream , almonds , blueberries , chocolate and espresso at Odile ( 2012 ) &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; At Odile , the second business opened in Hull by chef-restaurateur Marysol Foucault , served a dessert that seven years later still seems beguilingly spot-on . The sophisticated yet unfussy triumph consisted of vanilla and praline ice cream , roasted almonds and clusters of frozen wild blueberries enrobed in dark chocolate . A server emptied a small cup of espresso into the bowl , and voila &amp;mdash ; a deluxe , melting , caffeinated , nutty mud pie . Sadly , Odile closed after 15 months . Fortunately , Foucault&amp;rsquo ; s brunch-lunch spot , Edgar , remains a vital , if non-reservable , table for Ottawa-area food-lovers craving her signature Dutch baby pancakes and more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;32984;TOOLONG ... Bieber is coming to Ottawa in 2020&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33019;TOOLONG ... in review : Top highlights of Ottawa 's arts scene in 2019&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;33054;TOOLONG ... review : Alice conquers whimsical wonderland with her charm&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 27 Dec 2019 10:27:41 **45;33089;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsFrom sea urchin to take-out pasta , the Citizen 's favourite dishes of 2019http : **36;33136;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;33174;TOOLONG ... title= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The dish seen here is Summer Bouquet . Photo by Wayne Cuddington/ Postmedia " alt= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The dish seen here is Summer Bouquet . Photo by Wayne Cuddington/ Postmedia " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;33210;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480828 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Looking back at my eighth year of reviewing restaurants , I see a 2019 that was filled with an even bigger glut than usual of great dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s due to a banner year of especially noteworthy new restaurants , serving indulgences ranging from Farinella&amp;rsquo ; s Roman-bakery-style pizza by the slice to THRU&amp;rsquo ; s grand and unique spurge of 50 or so bite-sized delicacies. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; No wonder my annual list of favourites feels a little more diverse this time out , from sea urchin to take-out pasta , from fancy and fantastic dishes featuring rabbit to beef brisket smoked low-and-slow. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Newcomers such as Alice , chef Briana Kim&amp;rsquo ; s modernist vegetable-based restaurant , chef Dominique Dufour&amp;rsquo ; s highly personal and all-Canadian spot Gray Jay Hospitality and many others delivered . So , too , did more established eateries such as Bar Lupulus , Fauna Food + Bar and Supply and Demand , which I thought merited revisiting after some years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before the kudos , a few caveats are in order . The picks below are not the best in their categories bar none in the Ottawa area . They&amp;rsquo ; re just faves compiled after more than 100 meals at more than 50 eateries in 2019 . Nor are they wholesale endorsements of all the dishes at the restaurants that made them . And while some dishes can still be had , others were seasonal or short-run creations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Foodies might notice one conspicuous absence on my list , namely anything from THRU , chef Marc Lepine&amp;rsquo ; s fabulously creative six-seat **30;33240;TOOLONG on the second floor of his much-lauded , cutting-edge place Atelier . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; m afraid my notes about the barrage of items we had at THRU in May are less than clear . But in any event , the experience there was not about any single wow-inducing dish . Rather , it was more like a steady succession of outre treats , the point of which was something like gourmet overload in the best possible way . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But if THRU provided my meal of the year in Ottawa , the individual dishes below nonetheless satisfied mightily. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best **34;33272;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33308;TOOLONG ... title= " Uni with miso butter , English muffins and uni mayo at Gitanes " alt= " Uni with miso butter , English muffins and uni mayo at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;33344;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496550 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Gitanes in October , freshly shelled uni sourced from Rimouski , Que. were potently and pristinely flavourful , as rich and briny as any sea urchin I&amp;rsquo ; ve had and much , much better than the occasional iodine-y specimen . The impeccable ingredient from Gitanes&amp;rsquo ; raw bar was bolstered by miniature miso-buttered English muffins and uni mayo , which by itself was some kind of delicious . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : tempura soft-shell crab at Fauna Food + Bar &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best salad&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33374;TOOLONG ... title= " The summer bouquet salad at Alice " alt= " The summer bouquet salad at Alice " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;33410;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480828 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In September , the salad served at Alice during its tasting-menu dinner was as elegant and visually striking as it was tasty and novel . Assorted greens from Juniper Farms were arranged in a mini-bouquet , topped with toasted flaxseed and other small , good things and offset with a ring of kohlrabi filled with sea buckthorn jelly . The salad&amp;rsquo ; s revelatory dressing was house-made fermented almond milk , added table-side. &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : &amp;ldquo ; spring has sprung&amp;rdquo ; salad at Le St Laurent &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best spicy dish&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33440;TOOLONG ... title= " Hyderabadi Chicken dum Biryani at NH 44 Indian Bistro " alt= " Hyderabadi Chicken dum Biryani at NH 44 Indian Bistro " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;33476;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1384519 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **39;33506;TOOLONG In April , the most compelling of several spicy reasons to seek out NH 44 in an east-end Ottawa industrial park was chef-owner Teegaavarapu &lt;span class= " st " &gt;Sarath Mohan&lt;/span&gt; &amp;lsquo ; s Hyderabadi chicken dum biryani , a vibrant dish that tucked tender , marinated chicken legs into boldly flavoured , fresh and fluffy basmati rice . While the dish can be a half-heartedly made letdown elsewhere , at NH 44 , it was an instant party . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : chili chicken at Harbin Restaurant &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best pasta&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33547;TOOLONG ... title= " Meat lasagna at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Meat lasagna at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;33583;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1515877 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In December , the lasagna at the no-frills take-out and lunch spot Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Boys on Greenbank Road was served on a paper plate . But it was truly superior , with fresh , tender flat noodles and substantial , zesty , meaty sauce that provided bite after bite of perfect comfort . &lt;br&gt; Honourable mentions : Braised beef caramele with red wine , blue cheese and gooseberries at Supply and Demand , p&amp;acirc ; te &amp;agrave ; choux gnocchi at Grunt &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best sandwich&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33613;TOOLONG ... title= " Porchetta sandwich , apple sauce , crushed root veg , mushroom demi glace , Yorkshire pudding , at Grunt " alt= " Porchetta sandwich , apple sauce , crushed root veg , mushroom demi glace , Yorkshire pudding , at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;33649;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1444931 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If there&amp;rsquo ; s a signature dish at Grunt , the cosy eatery on a Mechanicsville side street , it&amp;rsquo ; s the roast porchetta sandwich , which fortunately is a staple on the frequently changing menu . While chef-owner Jason McLelland has sometimes tinkered with his pork belly treat since I tried it in the spring , his original iteration , which evokes an English Sunday dinner with Yorkshire pudding , mushroom demi-glace , crushed root vegetables and apple sauce , is hard to beat . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : chicken parm at Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Boys &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best soup&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33679;TOOLONG ... title= " Tonkotsu ramen at J:unique Kitchen " alt= " Tonkotsu ramen at J:unique Kitchen " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;33715;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1340172 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The flashiest items at J:unique Kitchen in Centretown are the over-the-top &amp;ldquo ; **25;33745;TOOLONG ; sushi rolls , some of which arrive at your table with flaming garnishes . But chef-owner James Park&amp;rsquo ; s tonkotsu ramen also rewarded . Its broth was hefty and clean , its noodles had some spring , and its pork belly and generous garnishes had been prepared with evident care . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : boat noodle soup at Nana Thai &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best pizza &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33772;TOOLONG ... title= " Assorted pizza ( including rosso , potato , zucchini and salami and olive ) at Farinella " alt= " Assorted pizza ( including rosso , potato , zucchini and salami and olive ) at Farinella " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;33808;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1429742 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33838;TOOLONG ... title= " Margherita pizza at Pizza AllAntica " alt= " Margherita pizza at Pizza AllAntica " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;33874;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1526327 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tie : Farinella&amp;rsquo ; s potato pizza , flecked with rosemary and markedly peppery , stood out among the constantly changing offerings from the Little Italy eatery that makes excellent , oven-fresh , foccacia-like pizza . Just as good but entirely different was the quintessentially Neapolitan margherita pizza at Pizza All&amp;rsquo ; Antica in Manotick. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best **29;33904;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;33935;TOOLONG ... title= " Plaa Neung Manao ( steamed tilapia with lime and chilies ) at Nana Thai Cuisine " alt= " Plaa Neung Manao ( steamed tilapia with lime and chilies ) at Nana Thai Cuisine " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;33971;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1410798 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rare is the Thai restaurant in Ottawa that departs from the tried-and-true dishes that North Americans know and love . But don&amp;rsquo ; t let that dissuade you from trying the pla neung manao at Nana Thai on Preston Street . The dish consisted of a steamed whole tilapia , opened like a book and dressed with a thrilling mix of chilies , garlic and lime . The dish&amp;rsquo ; s bright savoury broth was an appealing bonus . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : fillet of Atlantic cod with olive oil-poached fingerling potatoes , broad beans , horseradish and hollandaise sauce at Supply and Demand &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best poultry&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34001;TOOLONG ... title= " Guinea fowl main course at Bar Lupulus " alt= " Guinea fowl main course at Bar Lupulus " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;34037;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1372072 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Bar Lupulus this past spring , guinea fowl that received an esoteric multi-day marinade in koji rice ( cooked rice that&amp;rsquo ; s been inoculated with Aspergillus oryzae mold ) gained new , funky flavour before an expert preparation . Chef Justin Champagne presented the bird&amp;rsquo ; s breast tender and crisp-skinned , while its dark meat was fashioned into a deep-fried croquette . The rest of the plate brimmed with treats , including saucy , umami-rich wheatberry risotto garnished with snails and truffled cheese . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : flash-fried duck with broccolini , chanterelles , corn , morcilla and blueberry jus at Fauna Food + Bar &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best rabbit&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34067;TOOLONG ... title= " Prosciutto wrapped rabbit saddle stuffed with mushroom/ braised leg croquette/ pickled carrots/ roasted carrots and carrot puree/ lemon aioli at Le St. Laurent " alt= " Prosciutto wrapped rabbit saddle stuffed with mushroom/ braised leg croquette/ pickled carrots/ roasted carrots and carrot puree/ lemon aioli at Le St. Laurent " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;34103;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1418674 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Le St Laurent , chef Ryan Edwards combined prosciutto-wrapped , mushroom-stuffed medallions of rabbit with a crisp croquette of succulent braised leg meat , bolstered by lemon aioli or offset by some wild garlic . Pickled and roasted carrots rounded out the dish . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : rabbit dumplings with bone broth at Gray Jay &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best red **29;34133;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34164;TOOLONG ... title= " Brisket at Moes BBQ " alt= " Brisket at Moes BBQ " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34200;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1353761 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Moe&amp;rsquo ; s BBQ in a South Keys mall , pitmaster Mobeen Butt turns out formidable southern-style smoked meats , despite never having tasted the food that inspired him . Why ? Because Butt&amp;rsquo ; s food is halal , while typical U.S. barbecue never follows that stricture . Nonetheless , Butt&amp;rsquo ; s smoked brisket was a thing of beauty , moist , thick-cut and beef-forward in flavour rather than heavily spiced or salted . &lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Honourable mention&lt;/em&gt; : Oak-brined carpaccio with nori mayo and foie gras-spiked caramel corn at Bar Lupulus &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Best **32;34230;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34264;TOOLONG ... title= " Chocolate olive oil cake with carrot sorbet at Bar Lupulus " alt= " Chocolate olive oil cake with carrot sorbet at Bar Lupulus " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;34300;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1372038 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34330;TOOLONG ... title= " " ice cream sandwich " dessert at Alice " alt= " " ice cream sandwich " dessert at Alice " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34366;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462226 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34396;TOOLONG ... title= " Maple fondant , caramelized yogourt semifreddo , chanterelles at Gray Jay " alt= " Maple fondant , caramelized yogourt semifreddo , chanterelles at Gray Jay " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;34432;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1483842 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tie : I used to say Ottawa restaurants frequently fizzle when it comes to dessert . But this year , some ravishing , sophisticated creations ended a few of my meals . At Bar Lupulus , there was chocolate olive oil cake with pandan meringue inside , plus carrot sorbet with special fermented zip . At Alice , the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s riff on an ice cream sandwich placed a quenelle of fermented rice-based &amp;ldquo ; ice cream&amp;rdquo ; between shards of hyper-crisp oat cake , buttressed by berries in a charred pine cone syrup . At Gray Jay , a startling dessert paired a molten ( but not overly sweet ) maple cake with a semifreddo made with sugar and dehydrated chanterelles and topped with mushroom morsels . Surprising ? Yes , but also very good . &lt;/p&gt; **27;34462;TOOLONG **37;34491;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Alice , 40 Adeline St. , &lt;a href= " http : //alicerestaurant.ca " &gt;alicerest ... &lt;br&gt; Bar Lupulus , 1242 Wellington St. W. , &lt;a href= " http : //barlupulus.ca " &gt;barlupulus.ca &; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;34618;TOOLONG " **32;34645;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 19 Dec 2019 14:48:10 **45;34679;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : At Cumin Indian Grill in Centretown , street-food items satisfied mosthttp : **36;34726;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;34764;TOOLONG ... title= " Hariyali chicken at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Hariyali chicken at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34800;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521182 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Cumin Indian Grill&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 373 Somerset St. W. , 613-695-6969 , &lt;a href= " http : **26;34830;TOOLONG " class= " fl " **33;34858;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Monday to Saturday 11:30 a.m. to 2 p.m. , and 5 to 9 p.m. , closed Sunday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; main dishes up to $15.99 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; no steps to front door , washrooms on ground floor and downstairs &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For most of the last decade , if not longer , the hole-in-the-wall at 373 Somerset St. W. was home to a modest Indian eatery called Basmati . Then , last year , it became Caf&amp;eacute ; Delish , which served crepes and waffles , but also Indian food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; About eight months ago , the address reverted back to an exclusively Indian restaurant , an affordable place called Cumin Indian Grill . It recently caught my attention because its colourful renovations and menus with less-frequently-seen dishes suggested ambitions to raise the dining experience up a notch or two from what the previous tenant had offered . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Cumin , the two-storey space , which seats about 25 and is practically as tall as it is deep , now has violet walls and cyan tables , while a white , faux barn board wall now hides the sight of , if not the sounds of , the modest kitchen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those cyan tables are adorned with cloth napkins held snug with &lt;span class= " marko2zukmsj7 " &gt;Cumin&lt;/span&gt; -branded napkin rings . Edison lights hang on long strings , and the walls are adorned with stylized art . The place has a youthful feel its groovy South Asian pop soundtrack that comes with the music videos project overhead , on the eatery&amp;rsquo ; s back wall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurants lunch and dinner menus are not extensive , but they are still wide-ranging and not as curry-centric as more established Indian restaurants often are . In particular , I was glad to see some Indian street-food dishes and Hakka ( Indo-Chinese fusion ) dishes . Also , Cumin&amp;rsquo ; s meats confirm to halal practices . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dishes that most hit the spot were hearty , rugged street-food items on Cumin&amp;rsquo ; s lunch menu . The Punjabi dish of chole bhature ( $13.99 ) , a combination of potently spiced , tomato-y chickpea curry and puffy , freshly fried flatbread , was a favourite at our table . Almost as good was the pav bhaji ( $7.99 ) , which served its thick potato-and-peas curry with soft buns . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34893;TOOLONG ... title= " Chole bhature ( Chickpea curry and fried flatbread ) at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Chole bhature ( Chickpea curry and fried flatbread ) at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34929;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521734 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;34959;TOOLONG ... title= " Pav bhaji , a thick vegetable curry with a soft bread roll , at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Pav bhaji , a thick vegetable curry with a soft bread roll , at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;34995;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521712 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We thought less of the aloo paratha ( $7.99 ) , because the flatbread&amp;rsquo ; s spiced potato filling was a little meagre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35025;TOOLONG ... title= " Allo Paratha at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Allo Paratha at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35061;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521733 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was surprised but happy to see chicken dum biryani ( $15.99 ) offered at lunch , because I think of it as a labour-intensive rice dish fit for a evening&amp;rsquo ; s feast . Cumin&amp;rsquo ; s rendition delivered big flavours but felt more dry ( especially in the chicken department ) than the sublime chicken dum biryani that I&amp;rsquo ; ve had at NH44 in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s east end . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35091;TOOLONG ... title= " Hyderabadi chicken dum biryani ) at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum ' " alt= " Hyderabadi chicken dum biryani ) at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum ' " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35127;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521742 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of the Hakka dishes , I was most curious to see how an Indian kitchen would make hot and sour soup ( $4.99 ) . It turned out the soup&amp;rsquo ; s broth had the look , gloss and savouriness of the Chinese-restaurant staple , but frozen vegetables stood in for meat , tofu and mushrooms . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35157;TOOLONG ... title= " Hot and sour soup at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Hot and sour soup at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35193;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521716 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also from the Hakka selection , we liked the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s versions of chicken 65 ( $14.99 ) and chilli chicken ( $14.99 ) , two dishes in which breaded , tender chicken morsels starred and which both had welcome spicy , sour stings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35223;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken 65 at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Chicken 65 at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35259;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521743 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35289;TOOLONG ... title= " Chilli chicken at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Chilli chicken at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35325;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521739 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Given that the eatery stresses its grill in its name , I had high hopes for its lamb chops ( $15.99 for two ) . They were more than fine &amp;mdash ; tender , highly seasoned and covered in a green , herbal sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35355;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb chops at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Lamb chops at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35391;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521715 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; With the lamb chops and other dishes came massive quantities of basmati rice and some stir fried onions and peppers . This approach might suit single diners , but tables wishing for family-style sharing might want to ask for their food to be partitioned accordingly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also grilled and good was the hariyali chicken ( $13.99 ) , in which chunks of white meat were stained green by their herb-and-spice marinade and sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35421;TOOLONG ... title= " Hariyali chicken at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Hariyali chicken at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35457;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521717 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The lamb ( $13.99 ) and butter paneer curries ( $13.99 ) that we tried at lunch this week were substantially portioned but a little shallow of flavour . More rounded and complex was a beef Madras curry ( $14,99 ) that we had at dinner last week . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35487;TOOLONG ... title= " Butter paneer curry at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Butter paneer curry at Cumin Indian Grill , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35523;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521713 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35553;TOOLONG ... title= " Madras beef curry at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Madras beef curry at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35589;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521751 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Garlic naan bread ( $3.99 ) was a letdown because it seemed more like an unleavened flatbread that the leavened , charred treat that we had wanted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;div dir= " auto " &gt; &lt;p&gt;The restaurant is licensed and serves a small selection of beers , affordable Ontario wines and some spirits . We had to try our first " dirty lassis " ( $7 ) , to see what a rum-spiked mango lassi was like . It turned out that our dirty lassi was more fun to say than to drink -- I prefer my lassis , dirty or not , to be colder and more sweet and creamy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;A more appealing drink that was some meal-ending pink chai . The uncommon-in-Ottawa tea-based drink -- " You get this in Toronto , " our server said -- was sweet and pistachio-studded , even if it was not pink . I 'll be seeking it out again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;35619;TOOLONG ... title= " Pink chai at Cumin Indian Grill " alt= " Pink chai at Cumin Indian Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;35655;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1521714 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt;The round of pink chai was on the house at our dinner because of a serving mix-up , compensating us because we did not receive the coconut rice or biryani rice we had asked for with our curries.Service here has varied with personnel . Two servers have been on-point , while a third , while pleasant , was clearly inexperienced , unable to answer questions about dishes and not mindful about refilling water to help us assuage our spice-covered palates . At one visit , the un-busy kitchen fired out food quickly , but it was more slow to produce when the restaurant was more than half-full. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;In the end , although both service and food were hit-and-miss at Cumin Indian Grill . I 'm likely to return , given its best dishes and visible efforts . Plus , a server told me that a crab dish , I 'm guessing a South Indian spicy crab curry -- is bound for the menu in early 2020 . Who could resist that from a kitchen , however humble , that tries hard ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " **25;35685;TOOLONG " **42;35712;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;/p&gt; **27;35756;TOOLONG IN ARTS AND **29;35785;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35816;TOOLONG ... the Nutcracker : A guide to Christmas concerts at the NAC&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;35851;TOOLONG ...
