Food ReviewsDining Out : Bella 's Boys food shop keeps legacy and dishes of their mother 's bistro alivehttp : **35;36027;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;36064;TOOLONG ... title= " Bella 's Boys Italian Kitchen in Ottawa Monday Dec 2 , 2019 . Eugenio , Andrew , Rob , Bella , and Nick Milito pose for a photo at Bella's. " alt= " Bella 's Boys Italian Kitchen in Ottawa Monday Dec 2 , 2019 . Eugenio , Andrew , Rob , Bella , and Nick Milito pose for a photo at Bella's. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;36100;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1517340 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bella 's Boys Italian Kitchen and Food Shop&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 250 Greenbank Rd. , Unit 12 , 613-695-1900 , &lt;a href= " http : //bellas.ca " &gt;bellas.ca&lt;/a&gt; ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Monday to Thursday 10 a.m. to 6 p.m. , Friday 10 a.m. to 7 p.m. , Saturday 10 a.m. to&amp;nbsp ; 4 p.m. , closed Sunday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; pastas $12 to $14 , paninis $8 to $11.50 , mains $16 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; no steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A year ago , I was all set to write a glowing review of Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Bistro on Wellington Street West , which after two decades was still going strong and serving its much-liked and comforting Italian food to packed houses . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But the review , which would have included special kudos for chef-owner Raffaela &amp;ldquo ; Bella&amp;rdquo ; Milito&amp;rsquo ; s gnocchi with prosciutto and mushroom cream sauce as well as her classic tiramisu , never made it into print . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I had missed the memo that Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Bistro needed to wind down its business . After 23 years , the restaurant closed last Dec. 31 , in advance of condo construction at Mizrahi Developments&amp;rsquo ; 1451 Wellington , which one day will go up where Bella&amp;rsquo ; s stood . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of this is backstory to a fine lunch I had last week . It consisted of gnocchi with prosciutto and mushroom cream sauce , followed by a block of tiramisu , all as good as what I&amp;rsquo ; d had at Bella&amp;rsquo ; s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But of course it was . I had just lunched at Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Boys on Greenbank Road , which is run by Milito&amp;rsquo ; s three sons , Rob , Nick and Andrew . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Militos opened their eatery , which focuses on take-home foods , catering jobs and eat-in lunches , in early September , not far from where they grew up . Their mother herself , who launched her own restaurant only after cooking in the 1980s at the Ritz on Elgin Street , still toils away in her boys&amp;rsquo ; large open kitchen , as does her husband , Gino. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rob says that after his mother&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant closed , she &amp;ldquo ; was bored out of her mind . She was just going stir-crazy. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is what they do . This is what they love doing , &amp;rdquo ; he says of his parents , who are in their 70s and work as much as they want to with their sons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The second generation&amp;rsquo ; s business lacks the intimate , old-house atmosphere that made Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Bistro date night central for its regulars . However , the Bella&amp;rsquo ; s connection is visible , with the bistro&amp;rsquo ; s front door hanging on the food shop&amp;rsquo ; s wall and the sign from outside of the bistro , also salvaged before it was torn down , mounted above the fridges at the back of the shop . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those keepsakes aside , the sons&amp;rsquo ; place is bright and utilitarian , a newly equipped commercial kitchen with just a few more than a dozen seats at small tables for its lunch crowd . The Militos and their staff are clad in black ball caps and T-shirts . While dine-in customers order at the front cash , their food , on paper plates , will be brought to them at their tables . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On my first few visits to Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Boys , I was wowed by its panini sandwiches , which are not of the grilled variety but instead are distinguished by perfectly fresh and fluffy rolls that you wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t want to ruin by compressing them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The bread that Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Boys uses is from the Bakery , a matter-of-factly named business a few stores away in the same Greenbank Road strip mall . These rolls were perfect for receiving tender , fresh meatballs ( $8.50 ) or chicken parm ( $9 ) , both bathed in fine red sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36130;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken Parm sandwich at Bella 's Boys , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Chicken Parm sandwich at Bella 's Boys , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36166;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1517338 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36196;TOOLONG ... title= " Veal parm sandwich at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Veal parm sandwich at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36232;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1515872 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36262;TOOLONG ... title= " Grilled vegetable sandwich at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Grilled vegetable sandwich at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36298;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1515876 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Boys , everything but the bread , Rob said this week , is made from scratch on the premises . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s pastas have consistently won me over with their freshness and balance of clear , clean flavours . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve always opted for stuffed pastas here , and found the spinach and ricotta manicotti ( $14 ) , beef and veal ravioli ( $14 ) and beef and veal cannelloni ( $14 ) to be well-made , toothsome winners that delivered all of the necessary comforts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36328;TOOLONG ... title= " Manicotti at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Manicotti at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36364;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1517353 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36394;TOOLONG ... title= " Cannelloni at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Cannelloni at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36430;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1516389 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; When it comes to lasagna , I have something approaching a phobia due to too many shoddy , self-destructing lasagnas I ate at my university&amp;rsquo ; s cafeteria years ago . The meat lasagna at Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Boys ( $12 ) eradicated that horrible memory with its tender flat noodles and substantial , zesty , meaty sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36460;TOOLONG ... title= " Meat lasagna at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Meat lasagna at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36496;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1515877 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gnocchi ( $13 ) was as fresh and tender as expected , and its enjoyable sauce was sufficiently rich and creamy that I was scraping every last bit of it off its paper plate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36526;TOOLONG ... title= " Gnocchi with proscuitto and mushroom cream sauce at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Gnocchi with proscuitto and mushroom cream sauce at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36562;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1515875 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of these pastas were favourites at Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Bistro , Rob says . The same goes for the food shop&amp;rsquo ; s two chicken-based main courses , he adds . The &amp;ldquo ; chicken honey&amp;rdquo ; ( $16 ) was a perfectly cooked piece of chicken breast served with a creamy honey-mustard sauce of smack-you-in-the-face quality . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36592;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken Honey at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Chicken Honey at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36628;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1516390 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s a dessert counter beside the cash , and its contents also cast back to Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Bistro . That tiramisu with all of its components in the proper proportion and a slight boozy tingle was best , while coconut-y Hello Dolly bars packed a smaller but sweeter punch . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;36658;TOOLONG ... title= " Tiramisu at Bella 's Boys " alt= " Tiramisu at Bella 's Boys " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;36694;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1515873 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rob says that when the Mizrahi condo building opens , a Bella&amp;rsquo ; s business will be in it , although he can&amp;rsquo ; t say whether it will be Bella&amp;rsquo ; s Bistro 2.0 , as the developer has mentioned , or a take-home shop along the lines of his Greenbank store . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But until Bella&amp;rsquo ; s in some form returns to Wellington Street West , its old and new fans know that the trip to Greenbank Road will be well worth it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;36724;TOOLONG " **32;36751;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;36785;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;36820;TOOLONG ... Out : Fauna a contender for best restaurant dinner in Ottawa this year&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;36855;TOOLONG ... Out : In praise of Afghan kebab houses serving filling , casual , meaty meals&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;36890;TOOLONG ... the Nutcracker : A guide to Christmas concerts at the NAC&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 04 Dec 2019 16:56:23 **45;36925;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : In praise of Afghan kebab houses serving filling , casual , meaty mealshttp : **35;36972;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;37009;TOOLONG ... title= " chicken , beef and shami ( ground beef ) kebabs at Harir Kabab House , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " chicken , beef and shami ( ground beef ) kebabs at Harir Kabab House , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37045;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512446 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;37075;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 3098 Carling Ave. , Unit 8 , &lt;a href= " http : //mezbaan.restaurant " &gt;mezbaan.r ... &lt;br&gt; Open : Daily from 11 a.m. to 11 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Harir Kabab House&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 2900 Woodroffe Ave. , Unit 9 , &lt;a href= " http : //harirkababhouse.com " &gt;harirkab ... &lt;br&gt; Open : Monday to Friday 10 a.m. to 10:30pm , ? Saturday 10:30 a.m. to 10:30 p.m. , Sunday 11 a.m. to&amp;nbsp ; 10:30 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Samar Kabab House&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 1-1729 Walkley Rd. , Unit 1 , &lt;a href= " http : //samarkabab.com " &gt;samarkabab.co ... &lt;br&gt; Open : Daily from 11 a.m. to 10:30 p.m. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I&amp;rsquo ; m asked for dining-out recommendations , the question usually has to do where to go on date night , or to mark an anniversary , or where to take visitors from out of town . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But this time , it was a more plebeian query that stumped me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; What restaurant would you recommend for a good working person&amp;rsquo ; s dinner ? &amp;rdquo ; I was asked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; By that , she meant a restaurant she could pop into after her shift ended , a casual place that wasn&amp;rsquo ; t too expensive , that was fast , and that served good and generously portioned food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It took me a good week to come up with what I think is a smart answer to share with my questioner , and now with you . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; How about an Afghan kebab house ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For some , this answer might be a little on the vague side . Which place do I mean ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Well , this fall I&amp;rsquo ; ve tried three of the newer Afghan eateries that have opened in suburban Ottawa strip malls and found them to be quite comparable , and good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For a no-fuss , working-person&amp;rsquo ; s meal , I can recommend Mezbaan on Carling Avenue , in the parking lot of Cineplex Cinemas Ottawa ; Harir Kabab House , which is in Barrhaven about half a kilometre from the SNMC Mosque ; and Samar Kabab House , which is on Walkley Road . Samar is the oldest of the three , having opened roughly two years ago , while the Barrhaven and west-end kebab houses have been open roughly six months or less . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; They join a handful of other Afghan restaurants in Ottawa , which have opened in roughly the last seven or eight years . Frankly , the three I recently tried and several others I&amp;rsquo ; ve reviewed over the years have been remarkably similar , in terms of what their menus offer and the flavour profiles and quality of the food on their all-day menus . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The specialties at any of these restaurants is , of course , grilled halal-butchered meats , including lamb , beef , both ground or in chunks , and chicken , both ground or in chunks . The prices for kebab platters can range from $15 or so up to about $25 , depending on the kind and quantity of meat you want . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve not received a sub-par kebab at any of my recent visits . On my kebab honour roll were exceptionally juicy kebabs during a visit to Samar in the east end , when we were the only customers at lunch . At a recent lunch visit to Mezbaan in the west end , while the chicken thigh kebab offered on the menu wasn&amp;rsquo ; t available , the chicken breast kebab was seconds-off-the-grill moist , and blessed with fine tandoori seasoning . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37113;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb kebab at Mezbaan " alt= " Lamb kebab at Mezbaan " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37149;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512450 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37179;TOOLONG ... title= " Kebab combo at Mezbaan " alt= " Kebab combo at Mezbaan " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37215;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512451 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37245;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken , beef and shami ( ground beef ) kebabs at Harir Kabab House " alt= " Chicken , beef and shami ( ground beef ) kebabs at Harir Kabab House " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37281;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512446 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At one visit to Harir in Barrhaven , the kebabs were uniformly more spicy , but given the more temperate meats I later tried , those more potent kebabs might have been a one-off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Harir and Samar also offer torsh kebabs , a more expensive preparation I think of as Persian , in which chunks of beef are made tangy and sweet with a pomegranate-walnut marinade . If I had a craving for them , I&amp;rsquo ; d go out of my way for Samar&amp;rsquo ; s torsh kebabs. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37311;TOOLONG ... title= " Toorsh kabab at Harir Kabab House " alt= " Toorsh kabab at Harir Kabab House " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37347;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512536 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In generally , the kebabs at Samar , which is the largest and fanciest of these three restaurants , are a little bit pricier , but not unfairly so . Harir seems to have the lowest prices . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We&amp;rsquo ; ve always ordered our kebabs with seasoned , fluffy basmati rice and fresh , simple salad , dressed with a lightly creamy dressing , and found our plates brimming with food . A cheaper , less filling option would be to order kebabs in wraps. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Smaller appetites might also go for one of the stewed dishes ( usually around $8 or $9 ) offered at each of the restaurants that attest to Afghanistan&amp;rsquo ; s proximity to northern India &amp;mdash ; mellow butter chicken or runnier , more tomato-y chicken , beef or eggplant qorma. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Side dishes ( usually between $7 and $9 ) that always appealed included sabzi , a stew of spinach with dill , coriander and cilantro , qabelli rice , made more festive with the addition of nuts , carrots and raisins , and steamed mantu dumplings , filled with well-fried ground beef and lamb . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37377;TOOLONG ... title= " Sabzi at Mezbaan " alt= " Sabzi at Mezbaan " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37413;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512533 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37443;TOOLONG ... title= " Sabzi at Harir Kabab House " alt= " Sabzi at Harir Kabab House " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37479;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512537 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37509;TOOLONG ... title= " Mantu dumplings at Harir Kabab House " alt= " Mantu dumplings at Harir Kabab House " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37545;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512675 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37575;TOOLONG ... title= " Qabelli rice at Mezbaan " alt= " Qabelli rice at Mezbaan " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37611;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512684 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Freshly made Afghan naan bread &amp;mdash ; ovals with a coloured , crisp exterior and soft , yielding crumb &amp;mdash ; came with every meal . In particular , Mezbaan&amp;rsquo ; s bread emerged piping hot . Bolani ( between $6.49 and $8 ) , which I&amp;rsquo ; ve tried and enjoyed at Mezbaan and Harir , is a thinner , softer bread stuffed with a potato filling of varying pepperiness. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;37641;TOOLONG ... title= " Bolani at Harir Kabab House " alt= " Bolani at Harir Kabab House " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;37677;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1512664 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve had just a few small quibbles . At Samar , the ash-e-reshteh&amp;nbsp ; soup of noodles , beans , chickpeas and herbs seemed muted of flavour and limp-noodled . At Harir , we wanted to try the chicken karahi , which from the photo in the menu involved a well-marinated and spiced bird&amp;rsquo ; s meaningful encounter with a wok-like vessel . However , we were told the deluxe dish ( $28.99 ) that I associate with Pakistan and India wasn&amp;rsquo ; t available because the chef who knew the recipe had the day off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , the quality of Harir&amp;rsquo ; s tender and well-seasoned lamb chops was more than enough of a consolation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurants are not licensed but there are thirst-quenching drinks such as the massive , well-chilled mango lassis at Harir or the alcohol-free , flavoured Barbican beer at Samar. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Harir and Samar both offer children&amp;rsquo ; s menus , and all three restaurants can scale up their output to serve kebabs en masse in family packs for as many as 20 people . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Desserts at these restaurants are simple , ranging from firnee , a small serving of milk pudding sprinkled with cardamom , to the ice creams and cheesecake at Samar. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But these sweet meal-enders have struck me as optional , and I&amp;rsquo ; ve rarely had enough appetite left to try them . If you , or any other working person , leave an Afghan restaurant hungry or unsatisfied , then you&amp;rsquo ; ve probably done something wrong . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;37707;TOOLONG " **32;37734;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE ARTS AND LIFE &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;37768;TOOLONG ... Preview : Trailer Park Boys roll into Ottawa with Sunnyvale Christmas show&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;37803;TOOLONG ... Perfect YouTube stars coming to Ottawa in July&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;37838;TOOLONG ... bets , Nov. 28 to Dec. 4 : A monstrous exhibit at the gallery , Current Swell , Blind Boys of Alabama&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 27 Nov 2019 15:59:45 **45;37873;TOOLONG ... sous chef Kathryn Ferries wins award at New York competitionhttp : **29;37920;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;37951;TOOLONG ... title= " Sous-chef Kathryn Ferries poses for a photo at Stofa restaurant in Ottawa Wednesday Nov 20 , 2019. " alt= " Sous-chef Kathryn Ferries poses for a photo at Stofa restaurant in Ottawa Wednesday Nov 20 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;37987;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1510045 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kathryn Ferries , the 27-year-old sous chef at Stofa Restaurant on Wellington Street West , came home a winner after competing at the recent S.Pellegrino Young Chef Competition in New York . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Ferries didn&amp;rsquo ; t come first outright among the 10 North American competitors invited to cook and serve their signature dishes to judges , she did win the social responsibility award , and with it , a trip to the competition&amp;rsquo ; s Grand Finale in Milan , Italy next year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Below , Ferries , a Winnipeg native who has lived in Ottawa for 15 years and who graduated from Le Cordon Bleu Ottawa Culinary Institute , discusses why she cooks and competes and what her long-term ambitions are . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What prompted you to take part in the competition ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : I enjoy the way competitions give chefs new challenges and a change of pace from the regular restaurant hustle . It&amp;rsquo ; s appealing to create a dish that&amp;rsquo ; s truly representative of your beliefs and tastes and then walk into an unknown kitchen and produce that dish at the highest level . It&amp;rsquo ; s cooking solely off your knowledge and talent , using the ability to manage stress and nerves while also engaging with the guests who are lucky enough to watch you cook for five hours . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I also follow San Pellegrino&amp;rsquo ; s 50 Best Lists every year , and the exceptional talent that makes those lists plays a part in how they approach their standards for the young chef competition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38017;TOOLONG ... title= " The dish is the Forest Glazed Rabbit Ballotine with Fennel , Chanterelles , Coco Blanco and Mustard Greens , created by the young Ottawa chef Kathryn Ferries . " alt= " The dish is the Forest Glazed Rabbit Ballotine with Fennel , Chanterelles , Coco Blanco and Mustard Greens , created by the young Ottawa chef Kathryn Ferries . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38053;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1510065 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : Tell me about your signature dish . &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : My dish for the competition was black garlic and herb rabbit ballotine glazed with forest honey , served with fennel sauerkraut , chanterelle conserva , coco blanco puree , pomelo preserve and treviso radicchio . It&amp;rsquo ; s garnished with toasted almonds , pumpernickel crisps , mustard greens and anise hyssop and served with a rabbit jus made with marsala wine and warm spices , split with a bright green chive oil . We commissioned plates and jus pourers to complement the style of the dish , made by a friend of chef Jason Sawision , who also created some of the plateware we have at ( his restaurant ) Stofa. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I don&amp;rsquo ; t see rabbit used on many menus , at least in Ottawa , which is unfortunate since it is such a versatile protein that can take on many different flavours and cuisines . This dish is reflective of my French training but updated to showcase the style of plating I currently enjoy . It&amp;rsquo ; s classic in ingredient pairings , but with small twists to add interest . For example , using pomelo instead of lemon in the preserve , creating sauerkraut out of fennel instead of cabbage , and using black garlic to not only create visual contrast on the inside of the ballotine but to also give the rabbit an earthy flavour that plays between the elements on the dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I knew I wanted to showcase techniques that usually you would shy away from in a five-hour time window , as well as take advantage of the new rule allowing preserves , ferments , and dehydrated products to be brought to the competition . You can really layer a dish with flavour instead of being confined to what can be created solely within the competition time . I am happy with the result , but am looking forward to getting started on next year&amp;rsquo ; s submission . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : How did the competition go ? Were you nervous ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : Lots of practice , preparation and organization went into this competition in the months and weeks leading up to New York , so most of my nerves were non-existent , which was fantastic because I got to really enjoy the event . As with all competitions , not everything goes according to plan , but as a first-time competitor , it was a fantastic experience that I hope to get the chance to experience again . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;5 ) What did it mean for you to win the social responsibility award ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a great award to have won for this dish , as it really follows the principles of not wasting food , cooking within the seasons and using ingredients that don&amp;rsquo ; t create harm to our planet . This dish highlights whole-animal butchery and is intentionally low-waste , ensuring any leftover ingredients made their way into the stock or were reused on the plate ( mirepoix for stock , chives trimmings into oil , rabbit bones for stock ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There are many more steps to take to reduce waste in our industry , but to be recognized for making the effort to create change is a huge honour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Q : What other culinary ambitions do you have ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A : It&amp;rsquo ; s still early in my career , so I seem to keep collecting more ideas . But for now , ( my ) long term ( goal ) is to have a restaurant , or a few , of my own one day . In the meantime , I would love to continue to travel and learn from the many talented chefs in the world , as well as work closer with chefs in Ottawa to showcase all the talent we have here at home . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa shouldn&amp;rsquo ; t be looked over as much as it is . As we&amp;rsquo ; ve shown time and time again , we are much more than our stereotype of the sleepy , government town . There are so many amazing chefs and restaurants in this city who deserve recognition on a larger stage . I look forward to the day that an Ottawa restaurant is No. 1 on all the lists , in this country and others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;38083;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;38118;TOOLONG ... Ian Carswell of Black Tartan Kitchen wins Ottawa qualifier for 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 21 Nov 2019 20:50:08 **45;38223;TOOLONG ... Out : Bistro Ristoro in the Market serves appealing pizzas and hearty , uncommon Balkan farehttp : **36;38270;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;38308;TOOLONG ... title= " Plieskavitza ( XL burger ) at BIstro Ristoro , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Plieskavitza ( XL burger ) at BIstro Ristoro , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38344;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507625 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Bistro Ristoro&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 17 Clarence St. , 343-984-6080 , &lt;a href= " http : //bistroristoro.ca " &gt;bistroristo ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Saturday , 11:30 a.m. to 10 p.m. , Sunday 11:30 a.m. to 4 p.m. , closed Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; appetizers $6 to $24 , mains $16 to $26 , pizzas $17.50 to $22 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; no steps to front door or washrooms &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa has lots of eateries that describe their fare as Mediterranean . But what do they really mean by that ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dig a little deeper into their menus and you&amp;rsquo ; ll usually see Lebanese dips , pies or platters . At other restaurants , Greek salads and appetizers prevail , or perhaps pizzas and pastas. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And then there&amp;rsquo ; s Bistro Ristoro , which has been open for more than a year in the ByWard Market , near the quieter , western end of Clarence Street . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Its all-day menu , which I&amp;rsquo ; ve sampled from twice this fall , takes its own tour of Mediterranean countries , offering an assortment of thin-crust pizzas and bread-y items from its visible brick oven imported from Italy , plus a smattering of Greek dishes , pastas and salads . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Plus , because the restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s owner-operators are from North Macedonia , the landlocked Balkan country that doesn&amp;rsquo ; t touch the Mediterranean Sea but is just north of Greece , the bistro &amp;mdash ; perhaps most interestingly &amp;mdash ; serves some Balkan dishes that you&amp;rsquo ; ll be hard-pressed to find elsewhere in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At least , if someone knows another Ottawa eatery that serves plieskavitza ( a spiced , half-pound beef patty that&amp;rsquo ; s widely&amp;nbsp ; cherished in the Balkans ) , could you please let me know , as I would then have something with which to compare Bistro Ristoro&amp;rsquo ; s rendition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We first visited Bistro Ristoro for lunch , when we found its dishes simple , generously portioned and a little hit and miss . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One carnivore at my table was pleased with his baked pork tenderloin ( $24 ) , which was a touch overdone but nonetheless tasty thanks to its white wine mushroom cream sauce . With the hunk of pork came fingerling potatoes and salad , making for a hefty , plate-filling lunch . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38374;TOOLONG ... title= " Baker 's tenderloin at Bistro Ristoro " alt= " Baker 's tenderloin at Bistro Ristoro " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38410;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508508 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The other meat-lover opted for the Balkan kobasitza sausage ( $18 ) , a long , somewhat spicy link that we were told had been made by a Montreal-based supplier . Again , meat , potatoes and salad filled the plate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38440;TOOLONG ... title= " Kobasitza sausage at Bistro Ristoro " alt= " Kobasitza sausage at Bistro Ristoro " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38476;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508507 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A friend who had worked as a pizza-maker thought highly of his pastrmaylia ( $21 ) , described by the menu as &amp;ldquo ; a Macedonian traditional canoe-shaped pizza . &amp;rdquo ; When it arrived on our table , it made me think of a larger version of Turkey&amp;rsquo ; s pide flatbreads , albeit with cubes of pork as a topping . My take was that the dough outshone the meat on this no-frills dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38506;TOOLONG ... title= " Traditional Macedonian pizza with pork at Bistro Ristoro " alt= " Traditional Macedonian pizza with pork at Bistro Ristoro " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38542;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508509 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; My mushroom risotto ( $16 ) was the letdown of our lunch . Its rice was overly al dente and the too-dry risotto lacked the kind of creamy luxuriousness I prefer in the classic Italian indulgence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38572;TOOLONG ... title= " " alt= " " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38608;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our choices at dinner last week were more satisfying . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From the menu&amp;rsquo ; s dozen pizzas , we opted for the &amp;ldquo ; flamb&amp;eacute ; e&amp;rdquo ; ( $19 ) , which was a circular take on the usually oblong , Alsatian tarte flamb&amp;eacute ; e . Its blend of thick-cut bacon , cheese , red onions and sour cream was on point , and the thin-crusted pie was toothsome and sturdy &amp;mdash ; thicker and more dry than a Napolitan pizza , and also nicely coloured but devoid of charring. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38638;TOOLONG ... title= " Flambee pizza at Bistro Ristoro , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Flambee pizza at Bistro Ristoro , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38674;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507623 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of four pastas , we tried the spaghetti carbonara ( $18 ) and while the pasta was just a touch past al dente , the dish admirably delivered the yolky richness and bacon-y , cheesy satisfaction of a respectable carbonara. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38704;TOOLONG ... title= " Spaghetti carbonara at Bistro Ristoro " alt= " Spaghetti carbonara at Bistro Ristoro " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38740;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508516 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of two Greek dishes , the no-nonsense but well-made grilled halloumi salad ( $16.50 ) topped the more pedestrian pork souvlaki ( $16 for one kebab with fries and salad , $21 for two kebabs ) , which was made with chopped pork that could have been more assertively flavoured and whose fries were soggy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38770;TOOLONG ... title= " Halloumi salad at BIstro Ristoro , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Halloumi salad at BIstro Ristoro , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38806;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507624 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our survey of Bistro Ristoro would not have been complete without the massive plieskavitza ( $20 ) , also described on the menu as an &amp;ldquo ; XL beef burger . &amp;rdquo ; Surrounded by dough , the plieskavitza struck me to be equal parts pie and burger . The meat , which had been grilled separately , was well-seasoned and had a certain springiness to it . A dollop of a tangy dairy product called kaymak added a bit of sour richness . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38836;TOOLONG ... title= " Plieskavitza ( XL burger ) at BIstro Ristoro " alt= " Plieskavitza ( XL burger ) at BIstro Ristoro " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38872;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507625 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The only dessert available here is a two-scoop serving of chocolate mousse ( $8 ) , served on a slate slab with whipped cream . It did the trick for the three of us. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;38902;TOOLONG ... title= " Chocolate mousse at Bistro Ristoro " alt= " Chocolate mousse at Bistro Ristoro " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;38938;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508513 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Were I to return and have some wine with dinner , which would most likely be pizza , I might choose one of the Croatian options from the affordable , all-European wine list . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While I wasn&amp;rsquo ; t out-and-out wowed by the food here , I nonetheless like this spacious , unpretentious , new-feeling place that seats about 40 , where bouzouki and flamenco music can play on the sound system and Balkan musicians sometimes play on weekends . Bistro Ristoro appeals to me for its uniqueness in town and its willingness to build a bridge between Balkan dishes and palates unfamiliar with them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;38968;TOOLONG " **32;38995;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;39029;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;39064;TOOLONG ... Out : Fauna a contender for best restaurant dinner in Ottawa this year&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;39134;TOOLONG ... review : Jeff Ho 's insightful and moving tale of family trauma in trace&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 20 Nov 2019 16:23:10 **45;39169;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsChef Ian Carswell of Black Tartan Kitchen wins Ottawa qualifier for the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championshipshttp : **27;39216;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;39245;TOOLONG ... title= " Ian Carswell prepared Milkhouse Lamb as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party " to represent Ottawa in the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships . " alt= " Ian Carswell prepared Milkhouse Lamb as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party " to represent Ottawa in the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39281;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508875 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;39311;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chef Ian Carswell of Black Tartan Kitchen prevailed over seven other Ottawa-area chefs Tuesday night at the Ottawa edition of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party , the event known in past years as Gold Medal Plates . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Held at Signatures , the restaurant of Le Cordon Bleu Ottawa Culinary Arts Institute in Sandy Hill , Tuesday&amp;rsquo ; s qualifier will send Carswell to compete at the 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships . That multi-day event will be held in late January next year in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In second place was chef Ben Landreville of Sidedoor , the upscale Asian-themed restaurant in the ByWard Market . He served his version of banh khoai , a Vietnamese pancake , with spot prawns , pork sausage and fungus , nuoc mam cham , preserved vegetables and fresh herbs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In third place was Daniela Manrique of Soca Kitchen on Holland Avenue . She served chile-rubbed and smoked beef tenderloin with hazelnut romesco sauce , shaved raw artichoke , fermented black garlic and white soy vinaigrette and uni , port , pear p&amp;acirc ; t&amp;eacute ; br&amp;ucirc ; l&amp;eacute ; e . Her dish also won the event&amp;rsquo ; s people&amp;rsquo ; s choice award . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The other chefs competing were Shane Brown of Beckta Dining and Wine on Elgin Street , Justin Champagne of Bar Lupulus on Wellington Street West , Billy Khoo of Fauna Food + Bar in Centretown , Razmon Poisson of Orto Trattoria in the Glebe and Jason Sawision of Stofa on Wellington Street West . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At last year&amp;rsquo ; s Ottawa event , Manrique came in second while Sawision finished third . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As in past years , I took it upon myself to pit my palate against the assessments of the judges , who , to be fair , would be more rigorous and less gut-driven than I was . Below are my takes on the dishes , beginning with my least favourite and ending with my favourite . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39347;TOOLONG ... title= " Razmon Poisson prepared Smoked Sea Urchin as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Razmon Poisson prepared Smoked Sea Urchin as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39383;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508844 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Razmon Poisson of Orto Trattoria in the Glebe served smoked sea urchin with tamarind maple glaze , &amp;ldquo ; tom yum&amp;rdquo ; sorbet , chicken skin shard , duck fat snow and umami sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I love the bracing , briny delicacy of sea urchin and was looking forward to this dish . However , I felt there was just too much going on , and the tom yum sorbet overshadowed the uni . This dish was creative and had some well-executed components , including the crispy chicken skin and duck fat snow . But I didn&amp;rsquo ; t get a sense of perfect harmony from all of the components . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39413;TOOLONG ... title= " Billy Khoo prepared Smoked Sablefish as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Billy Khoo prepared Smoked Sablefish as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39449;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508845 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Billy Khoo of Fauna Food + Bar in Centretown served smoked sablefish rolled in green strawberry ash and served with sablefish consomm&amp;eacute ; pearls , salsify and house-made miso pur&amp;eacute ; e , green strawberry chutney , preserved lemon foam , cured sablefish skin , and fried salsify nest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I like sablefish as much as I like uni , but again , I thought the star of the plate arrived over-dressed . This entry had technique and creativity in its favour , but not all of its components felt essential . While I admired the concept of making maximum use of sablefish , strawberry and salsify , the smoking of the fish and bitterness from the strawberry ash obscured the beautiful natural flavour of the fish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39479;TOOLONG ... title= " Ben Landreville prepares Banh Khoai " Vietnames Happy Pancake " as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Ben Landreville prepares Banh Khoai " Vietnames Happy Pancake " as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39515;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508838 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Ben Landreville of Sidedoor , the upscale Asian-themed restaurant in the ByWard Market , served his version of banh khoai , a Vietnamese pancake , with spot prawns , pork sausage and fungus , nuoc mam cham , preserved vegetables and fresh herbs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This entry was pleasant , punchy of flavour and did have the ring of truth in terms of Vietnamese flavour profiles . But I thought it needed to be a bit more elevated , and the crepe would have been much more satisfying if it somehow could have been fresh and warm from the kitchen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39545;TOOLONG ... title= " Daniela Manrique prepared Chiles Secos and smoke lomito , as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Daniela Manrique prepared Chiles Secos and smoke lomito , as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39581;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508843 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Venezuelan-born chef Daniela Manrique of Soca Kitchen on Holland Avenue served chile-rubbed and smoked lomito ( beef tenderloin ) with hazelnut romesco sauce , shaved raw artichoke , fermented black garlic and white soy vinaigrette and uni , port , pear p&amp;acirc ; t&amp;eacute ; br&amp;ucirc ; l&amp;eacute ; e. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The cooking of the beef was impeccable , so much so that I caught myself wishing I could have a full portion of tenderloin with all of the accoutrements that came with this one-bite version . Miniaturized , the dish struck me as less successful , as its complexities seemed muddled on a smaller scale . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39611;TOOLONG ... title= " Ian Carswell prepared Milkhouse Lamb as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Ian Carswell prepared Milkhouse Lamb as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39647;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508846 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Ian Carswell of Black Tartan Kitchen in Carleton Place stressed his restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s Scottish roots by serving Milkhouse Farm lamb with &amp;ldquo ; Neeps and Tatties&amp;rdquo ; ( turnips and potatoes ) , buckwheat-crusted lamb loin , roasted neep accents , braised lamb pierogi , and &amp;ldquo ; Scott-ish&amp;rdquo ; p&amp;acirc ; t&amp;eacute ; . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This was a really solidly made and enjoyable entry , and all of its components worked for me . I&amp;rsquo ; d only say that the dishes I enjoyed more had a few more bells and whistles ringing on them , and were a little edgier artistically. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39677;TOOLONG ... title= " Shane Brown prepared a Ballotine of New Brunswick Striped Bass as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Shane Brown prepared a Ballotine of New Brunswick Striped Bass as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39713;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508847 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Shane Brown of Beckta Dining and Wine on Elgin Street served a ballotine of New Brunswick striped bass with black trumpet mushroom powder , Fogo Island snow crab remoulade , sea fennel &amp;amp ; sea grass salad , pickled Saskatchewan chanterelle mushrooms , celeriac and spruce pur&amp;eacute ; e and caribou moss crumble . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Between this entry and the one which I ranked just a bit more highly , I could really go either way . This very enjoyable dish was very Beckta &amp;mdash ; classic technique with contemporary , Canadian touches . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39743;TOOLONG ... title= " Justin Champagne prepared Koji Aged Rabbit Loin as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Justin Champagne prepared Koji Aged Rabbit Loin as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39779;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508839 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Justin Champagne of Bar Lupulus on Wellington Street West served koji-aged rabbit loin , wheatberry risotto with rabbit kidney lardons , mole jus , lacto-potato chip , carrot coated in quince juice and puffed amaranth , red cabbage terrine with cedar capers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For creativity and au courant techniques , this dish was unsurpassed . It packed all kinds of novel stimulations onto its small plate . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;39809;TOOLONG ... title= " Jason Sawision prepared Dungeness Crab , Pork and Truffle Soup , as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " alt= " Jason Sawision prepared Dungeness Crab , Pork and Truffle Soup , as eight local chefs compete at the Ottawa edition of the 2019 " Canada 's Great Kitchen Party . " " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;39845;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1508834 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; * Jason Sawision of Stofa on Wellington Street West served Dungeness crab , pork and truffle soup dumplings with corn &amp;ldquo ; XO&amp;rdquo ; chowder , smoked ham hock , braised cabbage and crab crisp . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This dish totally worked for me and was like a Michelin-starred elevation of a humble dim sum staple . It felt generous , substantial , lucid and luxurious , and the warmth of its flavourful , aromatic broth was a big plus . After trying all eight dishes , Sawision&amp;rsquo ; s dish was the one I clearly was craving to sample again , and I happily had seconds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The eight chefs served a panel of judges that included Kitchen Parry head judge James Chatto , cookbook author and former Citizen restaurant critic Anne Desbrisay , Algonquin College culinary program professor and chef Cory Haskins , Thyme &amp;amp ; Again owner Sheila Whyte , culinary historian Janet Boileau , chef Marc Lepine of Atelier , a two-time winner of the Canadian Culinary Championships , and chef Yannick LaSalle of Les Foug&amp;egrave ; res in Chelsea , who won in Ottawa last year and then at the national championships this year . Lepine was not allowed to judge the entries from chefs Sawision and Champagne , who had previously worked for him at Atelier . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The event attracted a sold-out crowd of about 150 food-lovers who scuttled from room to room in the restaurant of Le Cordon Bleu Ottawa Culinary Arts Institute on Laurier Avenue East , which was hosting the annual event for the first time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Compared to what was available in past years at the Shaw Centre&amp;rsquo ; s expansive ballroom , the working and serving space for chefs was more limited , too , which likely heightened the challenge of assembling refined creations with just a few hot plates available for last-minute cooking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Held this fall in a dozen cities across the country , the Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party events are successors to the long-running Gold Medal Plates competitions , which were fundraisers for Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Olympic athletes as well as celebrations of chefs and haute cuisine . The Kitchen Party events continue to support young Canadian athletes through the charity B2ten , as well as Community Food Centres Canada and MusiCounts , which also support youth . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;39875;TOOLONG ... finding retirement is not all it 's cracked up to be : survey&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;39910;TOOLONG ... sues Burger King , claiming Impossible Whoppers are contaminated by meat&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;39945;TOOLONG ... moderate drinking by parents may impact kids&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 20 Nov 2019 14:28:07 **45;39980;TOOLONG ... NewsDining Out : Fauna a contender for best restaurant dinner in Ottawa this yearhttp : **36;40027;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;40065;TOOLONG ... title= " Half duck for two at Fauna " alt= " Half duck for two at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40101;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507036 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Fauna Food + Bar&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 425 Bank St. , 613-563-2862 , &lt;a href= " http : //faunaottawa.ca " &gt;faunaottawa.c ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; for lunch Monday to Friday 11:30 a.m. to 2 p.m. , for dinner Sunday to Thursday 5:30 to 10 p.m. ( last seating 9 p.m . ) , Friday and Saturday 5:30 to 11 p.m. ( last seating 10:30 p.m. ) , bar open late &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; small plates $14 to $20 , mains $30 to $37 , plates for two $85 and $110 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; washrooms in basement &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We went recently to Fauna Food + Bar on a whim , after a last-minute need for a nice dinner out caught us by surprise . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That Saturday morning , we tried to go to two other upscale and well-established Ottawa restaurants , but they were booked solid . Fortunately , Fauna in Centretown could still take us for an early evening reservation . Luckier still , our meal turned out to be a contender for my best restaurant dinner in Ottawa this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We did go to Fauna with high hopes , although the last time I&amp;rsquo ; d eaten there was in late 2014 , soon after its chef-owner Jon Svazas opened it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since 2014 , there have been accolades and tweaks at Fauna . It cracked the Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants list in 2016 , 2017 and this year . Given that Svazas opened Bar Laurel in Hintonburg in 2016 , Fauna&amp;rsquo ; s chef de cuisine Billy Khoo plays a greater role now , and next Tuesday , it will be Khoo rather than Svazas who will lead the kitchen crew from Fauna next Tuesday at Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s edition of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party , the regional qualifier for next year&amp;rsquo ; s Canadian Culinary Championships . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 2014 , Fauna was all about small plates . Today , it serves mains and even big dishes for two or more , and I was curious to see what the restaurant would do with dishes of that scale . Ultimately , regardless of plate size , the way to go seems to be to share . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our meal&amp;rsquo ; s strong , cool start consisted of two raw dishes , although they arrived so quickly from the kitchen that they beat the well-made but lagging cocktails to our table . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Bison tartare ( $19 ) wowed us with the quality of its finely chopped meat , its different gradations of acid , fat , sweetness and umami , its crisp potato chips and its striking plating . &amp;ldquo ; I could eat that all night , &amp;rdquo ; said one of my friends . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40131;TOOLONG ... title= " Bison tartare at Fauna " alt= " Bison tartare at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40167;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1505715 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; More delicate was the swordfish ceviche ( $19 ) , a distinct departure from the rustic , boldly flavoured , chunky , Peruvian-style ceviches that typically win me over . Fauna&amp;rsquo ; s ceviche impressed with its finesse and calibration , its mild fish artfully wrapped in cucumber and nestled in a flavourful pool of coconut milk , chili and herb oil . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40197;TOOLONG ... title= " Swordfish ceviche at Fauna " alt= " Swordfish ceviche at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40233;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507039 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Two warm small plates continued the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s streak . Shishito peppers ( $20 ) stuffed with scrumptious lamb played on chile rellenos , and came with a complex mole sauce that delivered lingering heat . A plump , whole soft shell crab ( $20 ) received a marvellously crisp tempura treatment , while the seaweed aioli and a sprinkling of spice blend added punch to the crustacean&amp;rsquo ; s already intense flavour . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40263;TOOLONG ... title= " Stuffed shishito peppers at Fauna " alt= " Stuffed shishito peppers at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40299;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507034 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40329;TOOLONG ... title= " Tempura soft shell crab at Fauna " alt= " Tempura soft shell crab at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40365;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507035 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then , the four of us tackled mains that brimmed with concentrated flavours that made us want to gnaw on bones and scrape the plates for every speck of goodness . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Beef cheeks ( $37 ) were braised to fall-apart tenderness , and were part of a cohesive plate that also included matsutake mushrooms , kale and squash . &lt;/p&gt;
