&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div class= " menu-item-description " &gt;&lt;span class= " currency-sign " style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;An admittedly thin chop of suckling pig ( $&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;37 ) , supported by cauliflower pur ? e , mushrooms , corn and beans , was succulent , porky perfection , blessed with mild but lucid flavour and some much-appreciated crackling . **25;40461;TOOLONG &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40488;TOOLONG ... title= " Suckling pig at Fauna " alt= " Suckling pig at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40524;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1505716 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div class= " menu-section " &gt; &lt;div class= " menu-items " &gt; &lt;div class= " menu-item " &gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span class= " menu-item-price-top " &gt;&lt;span class= " currency-sign " &gt;A flash-fried half duck ( $&lt;/span&gt;85 ) -- meaty , succulent , crisp of skin , and perked by a blueberry jus -- was our meal 's star , although its fine vegetables ( broccolini , chanterelles , peppers ) reminded us to enjoy that other food group . &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40554;TOOLONG ... title= " Half duck for two at Fauna " alt= " Half duck for two at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40590;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1507036 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;p&gt; For dessert , we had the contrasting pleasures of a unique chocolate cake ( $14 ) accented with violet and confit fennel , and brown-butter cake ( $12 ) offset by cherry sorbet , poached cherry and brown sugar crackers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40620;TOOLONG ... title= " Violet chocolate bar : flourless chocolate cake , chocolate ganache , violet and wildberry compote , confit fennel and cedar oil at Fauna " alt= " Violet chocolate bar : flourless chocolate cake , chocolate ganache , violet and wildberry compote , confit fennel and cedar oil at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40656;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1505717 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;40686;TOOLONG ... title= " Cherry dessert at Fauna " alt= " Cherry dessert at Fauna " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;40722;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1505719 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Five years ago , I called Fauna , which seats about 60 at its woody tables and wraparound bar , the loveliest new dining room in town . It still feels vibrant and au courant , with its textured walls , massive and funky art and fun lighting fixtures . Downstairs , a private dining room provides an alternate setting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I also noted in 2014 that Fauna is a little darker than I like , and grows louder than I like , and these aspects of the ambience have remained constant . Being seated beside one of the huge windows looking onto Bank Street at my recent dinner did make for a slightly brighter and more isolated experience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The bar&amp;rsquo ; s offering of 10 craft cocktails is wide and intriguing , while the lengthy and discerning wine list here favours natural , biodynamic and organic bottles . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Overall , Fauna at five feels like a restaurant that can execute and serve its many strengths with easy , knowing confidence . The level it attained , in particular with its mains and big duck plate , made me feel like we&amp;rsquo ; d hit the jackpot &amp;mdash ; and maybe even as if I&amp;rsquo ; ve been a wee bit too kind to its competition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;40752;TOOLONG " **32;40779;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;40813;TOOLONG ... Hum 's restaurant reviews&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumSat , 16 Nov 2019 16:07:42 **45;40849;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsRen ? Rodriguez becomes chef of the Grill 41 restaurant in the Lord Elgin Hotelhttp : **35;40896;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;40933;TOOLONG ... title= " Top Chef Canada 's 2014 winner , Rene Rodriguez. " alt= " Top Chef Canada 's 2014 winner , Rene Rodriguez. " data-portal-copyright= " Sabrina Bedford " **28;40969;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1500904 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The venerable Lord Elgin Hotel has hired veteran Ottawa chef Ren&amp;eacute ; Rodriguez to helm the kitchen of its Grill 41 restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Rodriquez is known nationally for winning the 2014 season of the Top Chef Canada TV series . He has cooked in Ottawa since at least the early 2000s at many of the city&amp;rsquo ; s leading restaurants , and most notably he owned and operated Navarra , which drew upon the cuisine of Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s Mexican heritage , for nearly a decade in the ByWard Market until it closed in the spring of 2017. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After Navarra closed , Rodriguez was hired as the chef who opened the Italian restaurant Orto Trattoria in the Glebe . He then moved on to help open Mercadito , the Mexican street food eatery in the Queen St. Fare food hall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; According to a release , Rodriguez has been working for the past several few months with the Grill 41 team to create new menu items . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m thrilled to be leading a kitchen of hard-working pros whose ambition is to amaze every guest , &amp;rdquo ; Rodriguez says in the release . &amp;ldquo ; One of the many things I&amp;rsquo ; ve learned in my career is the kitchen staff must work together like a professional team . We must all bring our strengths to the table , and my job is to bring out the very best in each of us. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some of Rodriguez&amp;rsquo ; s dishes , which will be designated with &amp;ldquo ; RR&amp;rdquo ; on Grill 41&amp;rsquo ; s menu , will include watercress salad with cured albacore tuna gravlax , with Spanish paprika deviled egg and olive powder ; Grill 41 Signature Steak Tartare with pearl onion marmalade , crostini , sorrel and celeriac tuile ; seared sea scallops with Granny Smith apple emulsion , parsnip leather and lovage ; and steak frites with Roquefort butter , house fries , espelette aioli and herb salad . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Located close to Confederation Park and the Rideau Canal , the Lord Elgin Hotel was built in 1941. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN ARTS AND LIFE&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;40999;TOOLONG ... things you ca n't miss in Abadakone , the National Gallery 's exhibition of contemporary Indigenous art&lt;/a&gt; &lt;strong&gt; &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;41034;TOOLONG ... Ottawa studio for artists addresses ' dire ' need for more creative workspaces&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;41069;TOOLONG ... Out : Luxurious , hedonistic eating at Gitanes on Elgin Street&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 07 Nov 2019 20:38:43 **45;41104;TOOLONG ... Out : Luxurious , hedonistic eating at Gitanes on Elgin Streethttp : **34;41151;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;41187;TOOLONG ... title= " Mitch Lacombe is the Chef de Cuisine at Gitanes on Elgin Street . Julie Oliver/Postmedia " alt= " Mitch Lacombe is the Chef de Cuisine at Gitanes on Elgin Street . Julie Oliver/Postmedia " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;41223;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496428 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **36;41253;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 361-6 Elgin St. , 613-562-0699 , &lt;a href= " http : //gitanes.co " &gt;gitanes.co&lt;/a &; ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Saturday 5 p.m. to 2 a.m. , closed Sunday and Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; appetizers $7 to $45 , mains $22 to $70 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; restaurant is below street level , steps or long ramp to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As the cool kids like to say , the menu at Gitanes was lit. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Or rather , as a more literal adult would say , the menu , a large paper card filled with beguiling choices , was lit on fire last week after my dining companion allowed it to hover too close to our table&amp;rsquo ; s tiny candle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our server was sympathetic . She told us this was roughly the eighth case of a menu catching fire since Gitanes opened in mid-September on Elgin Street , in a much renovated space where Oz Kafe , now in the ByWard Market , had been . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s the first warning I will share about Gitanes . Don&amp;rsquo ; t set your menu on fire . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The second warning : At Gitanes , which broadly speaking is a French-inspired restaurant , the food can be very rich . Both the French onion soup and the bordelaise sauce that comes with the massive O&amp;rsquo ; Brien Farms ribeye are enriched with bone marrow . There&amp;rsquo ; s a puddle of foie gras mousse beside the truffle-sauced chicken and even foie gras incorporated , quite subtly , into the ice cream sundae . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41291;TOOLONG ... title= " French onion soup with bone marrow at Gitanes on Elgin Street " alt= " French onion soup with bone marrow at Gitanes on Elgin Street " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;41327;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496549 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , foie gras and truffles aren&amp;rsquo ; t cheap . Accordingly , my third warning is that it will help you at Gitanes if you are rich or at least enjoy feeling rich . It is a youthful , casual and fun place , but also a splurge if you want to do more than nibble on some shrimp tartare ( $10 ) and impeccably crisp , salted and herb-enhanced fries with aioli and ketchup ( $8 for a generous portion ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41357;TOOLONG ... title= " French fries with aioli and ketchup at Gitanes " alt= " French fries with aioli and ketchup at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;41393;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496417 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Main courses can easily top $40 here . Seafood towers from the cold bar are $90 and $125 , with a $90 supplement for caviar . For the most celebratory guests , Gitanes stocks 11 champagnes , including 2011 Pierre Peters &amp;ldquo ; Les Ch&amp;eacute ; tillons&amp;rdquo ; Blanc de Blancs Grand Cru Brut at $355 a bottle . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But after all these caveats , I still recommend Gitanes , as long as you know what you&amp;rsquo ; re in for . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After Thru , Alice and Gray Jay , it is the fourth very notable restaurant to open in Ottawa since the spring of this year . They are all different . Gitanes , perhaps apropos of being on Elgin Street , is less high-minded than the others . But , at its best , Gitanes is more about food as a direct , carnal pleasure as well as a sophisticated indulgence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That was our conclusion as we extracted briny , potent Rimouski-raised sea urchin from their shells ( two for $25 ) and smeared the orange ambrosia ( gonads , yes I know ) on some miniature miso-buttered English muffins . For umami overload , the iced platter also contained some uni mayo , which by itself made our eyes widen . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41423;TOOLONG ... title= " Sea urchin with miso butter , uni mayo and English muffins at Gitanes " alt= " Sea urchin with miso butter , uni mayo and English muffins at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;41459;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496550 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stored wrong or out of the water too long , sea urchin is really off-putting and tastes of iodine . I&amp;rsquo ; ve had several bad uni experiences at restaurants , but Gitanes&amp;rsquo ; uni could not have been more fresh and mind-altering. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I don&amp;rsquo ; t have the budget to have tried all 11 items from the cold seafood bar , which is out front at Gitanes , separate from the kitchen , but I&amp;rsquo ; ll be saving up for snow crab and lobster . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I did try some of the more composed raw-fish items and they were very good , if not as transcendental as the admittedly acquired taste of uni . Slabs of mackerel ( $14 ) were unctuous and earthy , perked by fermented rhubarb , hazelnut and serrano chiles and sitting in a bright sauce of fermented plum and ponzu . Tuna tartare ( $20 ) was well-made &amp;mdash ; finely chopped , clean and mild of taste and elevated by the right amount of plum gel&amp;eacute ; e , cr&amp;egrave ; me fra&amp;icirc ; che and bits of crisped sunchoke. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41489;TOOLONG ... title= " Mackerel with fermented rhubarb , toasted hazelnut , serrano and shiso " alt= " Mackerel with fermented rhubarb , toasted hazelnut , serrano and shiso " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;41525;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496531 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41555;TOOLONG ... title= " Tuna tartare at Gitanes " alt= " Tuna tartare at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;41591;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496561 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Beef tartare ( $20 ) , while not so appetizing-looking , was simple and fine , its meat tucked under a layer of bigger sunchoke crisps . I&amp;rsquo ; m used to a bit more acid in my tartares , but in my experience , that&amp;rsquo ; s not how Gitanes rolls . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41621;TOOLONG ... title= " Beef tartare with sunchokes at Gitanes " alt= " Beef tartare with sunchokes at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;41657;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496432 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Celery root and apple &amp;mdash ; a classic pairing &amp;mdash ; starred in a lower-key but satisfying salad ( $16 ) . The vegetable and fruit were crisp and thinly sliced , bolstered by a creamy dressing that had the nuttiness of brown butter going for it , while bits of cheddar added their pop to the dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41687;TOOLONG ... title= " Apple celery root salad at Gitanes " alt= " Apple celery root salad at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;41723;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496430 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; That menu that nearly went up in flames boasts 16 appetizers and vegetable dishes , but we skipped intriguing options such as escargots , that French onion soup and duck boudin sausage to tuck into some of the big-money mains ( as opposed to the cheaper burger or steak frites ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A massive chicken breast received a deluxe treatment ( $47 ) . It was blissfully moist after being cooked sous vide and then roasted , and its truffled sauce , cinnamon cap mushrooms and foie gras mousse made for an opulently rich main , offset only by the tangy hint of fermented elderberry. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41753;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken with mushrooms , foie gras , perigord sauce at Gitanes " alt= " Chicken with mushrooms , foie gras , perigord sauce at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;41789;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496536 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; On my second visit , a buddy and I split the uber-beefy O&amp;rsquo ; Brien Farms 16-ounce ribeye ( $70 ) , also showered with mushrooms plus some cippolinis , and served with the dark , beefy-in-its-own right , marrow-infused bordelaise . We felt like the world&amp;rsquo ; s most decadent carnivores. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41819;TOOLONG ... title= " O'Brien Farms ribeye for two with mushrooms and onions at Gitanes " alt= " O'Brien Farms ribeye for two with mushrooms and onions at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;41855;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496424 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Even more tongue-catching and pleasing was the **25;41885;TOOLONG sauce with Gitanes&amp;rsquo ; dry-aged duck breast ( $40 ) that had a bit of extra funk to it , and was tender , if a touch overdone . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41912;TOOLONG ... title= " Dry-aged duck breast at Gitanes " alt= " Dry-aged duck breast at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;41948;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1495845 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Flawless was the big chunk of roasted halibut ( $47 ) with stewed beans , a quenelle of herby sauce , with clams and chanterelles along for the ride . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;41978;TOOLONG ... title= " Roasted halibut with beans , chanterelles and green sauce at Gitanes " alt= " Roasted halibut with beans , chanterelles and green sauce at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42014;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496532 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The sundae made with foie gras ice cream ( $19 ) was very creamy and barely liver-y , but it delivered a wave of sweet , boozy excess , with its rum-spiked bananas Foster sauce and bourbon-soaked cherry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42044;TOOLONG ... title= " Foie gras ice cream sundae at Gitanes , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Foie gras ice cream sundae at Gitanes , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42080;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496418 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Restrained by comparison was a dark , smooth slice of chocolate tart ( $10 ) covered with ground cherries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42110;TOOLONG ... title= " Chocolate ground cherry tart at Gitanes " alt= " Chocolate ground cherry tart at Gitanes " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42146;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1496433 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Service was always personable , assured and knowledgeable about the food and beverages , significantly adding to our enjoyment . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurant seats about 50 people in distinctly different spaces . I&amp;rsquo ; ve sat twice at the back , near the kitchen , in an area with a cellar-y feel under exposed ducts where we wished the very diverse piped-in music was somewhat quieter and the lights were a little brighter . That said , our space was softened by an area graced by sofa seating for about eight , not that we were that uncomfortable in our curvy moulded plastic chairs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I envied the people at the front of the restaurant , who could watch the cook at the raw bar station . I really envied the folks at the chef&amp;rsquo ; s table right in the kitchen , treating themselves to the $95-per-person tasting menu . There&amp;rsquo ; s also a darker lounge-y bar area with a few tables . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gitanes is co-owned by Nader Salib , who also has a stake in Common Eatery , a block south on Elgin Street . His co-owner is American chef Luke Reyes , who splits his time between Los Angeles and Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chef de cuisine Mitch Lacombe definitely deserves kudos . Having been the chef de cuisine at Restaurant E18hteen and sous chef at Riviera , Lacombe is no stranger to high-end dishes that smack of luxury . He is definitely helping to rocket Gitanes into the elite company of his previous kitchens . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;42176;TOOLONG " **32;42203;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;42237;TOOLONG ... Out : Last Train to Delhi serves thoughtful Indian food for the spicy-averse&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;42272;TOOLONG ... Out : All-Canadian menu at Gray Jay pushes boundaries of creativity&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;42307;TOOLONG ... foodies come to aid popular Vanier taco spot trashed by vandals&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 01 Nov 2019 14:55:51 **45;42342;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : Last Train to Delhi serves thoughtful Indian food for the spicy-aversehttp : **28;42389;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42419;TOOLONG ... title= " Vegetarian curry and eggplant , at Last Train to Delhi , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Vegetarian curry and eggplant , at Last Train to Delhi , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42455;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463512 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Last Train to Delhi&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 103 Fourth Ave. , 613-882-0035 , &lt;a href= " http : //lasttraintodelhi.com " &gt;lasttra ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Sunday 5:30 to 9:30 p.m. , closed Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; starters $10 to $15 , mains $16 to $25 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During my two dinners at Last Train To Delhi , the question I&amp;rsquo ; d expected to hear just never came up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At other Indian restaurants , and generally at eateries where the chilli-based heat of dishes might range from mild to tongue-searing , I&amp;rsquo ; m used to servers asking : &amp;ldquo ; How spicy would you like it ? &amp;rdquo ; But at this tiny five-month-old place in the Glebe , the chef and owner , Surinder Singh , knows what his dishes should taste like , and they won&amp;rsquo ; t be customized to be more tepid or fiery . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , in the end , tepidness and fieriness are relative . There are Indian restaurants in Ottawa where a &amp;ldquo ; mild&amp;rdquo ; is more scorching than a &amp;ldquo ; spicy&amp;rdquo ; dish at one of its rivals . Where a restaurant falls on the heat spectrum might depend on whether they&amp;rsquo ; re trying to please spice-craving expat tastebuds or less seasoned palates that are content with less seasoning . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At his restaurant , Singh , who formerly ran the now-closed Tea Party Cafe in the ByWard Market , serves what he calls &amp;ldquo ; progressive Indian cuisine . &amp;rdquo ; His menu , which concentrates on North Indian-inspired dishes , is smaller and more curated . At my first meal here , a server additionally told me that the food is &amp;ldquo ; spiced rather than spicy &amp;hellip ; We&amp;rsquo ; re not trying to blow people&amp;rsquo ; s heads off . &amp;rdquo ; Indeed , some dishes , I thought , could have used more heat and spicy punch , even if Singh grinds his own spices . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But I was more keen on Last Train to Delhi after my second meal there , which involved , I think , better choices . In all , I&amp;rsquo ; d recommend this restaurant to those who like their Indian food prettily plated , well-composed and crafted , but not roaringly hot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last Train to Delhi replaces the tiny Filipino restaurant Tamis Caf&amp;eacute ; , which moved to Centretown nearly a year ago . Singh has renovated his Fourth Avenue space and made it more upscale . The dining room&amp;rsquo ; s brick wall remains , but the narrow , contemporary dining area , which seats about 18 , is dressed up by a bevy of plants , shelving filled with wines and spirits , and the wine glasses that dot the blond wood tables . Singh cooks away in the small kitchen visible from the dining area . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our meals have begun with complimentary bowls of curried popcorn , which we thought could have used more zip . At my first dinner , things picked up with a nice tandoori shrimp appetizer ( $15 ) , in which the plump shellfish were sheathed in crispy exteriors of fried , shredded potato and bolstered by a pineapple chutney . But the Amritsari fish fry ( $22 ) , made with pickerel and accompanied by mint chutney , seemed more casually cooked and made less of an impression . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42485;TOOLONG ... title= " Crispy tandoori shrimp appetizer at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Crispy tandoori shrimp appetizer at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42521;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463514 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42551;TOOLONG ... title= " Amritsari fish fry and potatoes at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Amritsari fish fry and potatoes at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42587;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1490288 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another aspect of the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s progressiveness is its slight tilt toward vegetables over meat . &amp;ldquo ; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;We ... do not have a lot of meat options and want people to enjoy eating more plants , " the restaurant 's website says . The restaurant is also environmentally minded , and it composts and recycles . " We ... try our very best to lower the amount of waste we put to the curb , " its website continues . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At our first meal , the top vegetarian dish was Singh&amp;rsquo ; s kofta curry ( $20 ) made with pleasingly flavourful balls of bottleneck gourd and potatoes in a cashew coconut sauce that did have some lingering but mellow heat . The kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s version of baigan bartha ( $17 ) , a dish of meltingly soft eggplant supported by onions and peas ( $17 ) , was more restrained flavour-wise than we liked . That night , I also thought the rice needed salt . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42617;TOOLONG ... title= " Vegetarian kofta curry and baigan bartha eggplant dish at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Vegetarian kofta curry and baigan bartha eggplant dish at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42653;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463512 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At my second visit , we happily ate more dishes that packed some vibrancy and intensity . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of the chicken dishes appealed . Hariyali chicken kebabs ( $14 ) made with breast meat were sufficiently moist and tender , and their coriander mint sauce delivered a welcome thrill . The Kashmiri chicken korma ( $21 ) appealed too , with exceptionally tender chicken and a full-bodied , nut-enriched sauce . Butter chicken ( a.k.a. murgh makhini , $22 ) was solidly made , too . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42683;TOOLONG ... title= " Hariyali chicken at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Hariyali chicken at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42719;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1490289 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42749;TOOLONG ... title= " Kashmiri chicken korma at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Kashmiri chicken korma at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42785;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463513 " /&gt; &lt;div&gt;&lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42815;TOOLONG ... title= " Butter chicken at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Butter chicken at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42851;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463508 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt;The menu described the lamb in the lamb rogan josh as spicy ( $23 ) , which it was , relative to other dishes . We gave it a thumbs-up above all for the tenderness of its meat . In general , in spite of the kitchen 's professed fondness for vegetables , its meat dishes were winners with a lot of intention and high standards going for them . &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " **33;42881;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;42916;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb rogan josh at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Lamb rogan josh at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;42952;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463515 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Our server , who that night was Singh 's wife , pointed out that the restaurant 's saag paneer ( $20 ) was made authentically , with not just the usual spinach , but also rapini , which added a hint of bitterness , and house-made Indian cheese . It did do more for me than the saag paneers I 've had elsewhere . We liked just as much Singh 's okra ( $17 ) , which was nicely textured and savoury . &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;42982;TOOLONG ... title= " Saag paneer at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Saag paneer at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43018;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463516 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;br&gt; Desserts here depart from the usual Indian sweets and offer Western touches . We preferred the cardamom chocolate tart ( $9 ) to the passionfruit pavlova ( $9 ) with a hard meringue . **26;43048;TOOLONG &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43076;TOOLONG ... title= " Chocolate tart at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Chocolate tart at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43112;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463517 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div dir= " ltr " &gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43142;TOOLONG ... title= " Pavlova at Last Train to Delhi " alt= " Pavlova at Last Train to Delhi " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43178;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1463507 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt;The range of non-alcoholic drinks at Last Train to Delhi is admirable , including both rose and mango lassis ( $5 ) , plus lime soda and lavender lemonade for the yogurt drink-averse . Most of the restaurant 's half-dozen cocktails skew in a tropical direction . The wine list includes bottles between $41 and $65 , plus a few by-the-glass choices , but I 'd lean more to one of the Canadian craft beers or even one of six whiskeys , as that spirit is popular with food in India . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;Perhaps it 's paradoxical , or even faint praise , to suggest that Last Train to Delhi serves Indian food for people who do n't like really spicy food . Better , I think , to focus on the thoughtful , from-scratch feeling of the food here and the hospitality and vibe that 's as mellow and sustaining as Singh 's best dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;div class= " css-1dbjc4n r-1iusvr4 r-46vdb2 r-1777fci r-5f2r5o r-bcqeeo r-1mi0q7o " &gt; &lt;div class= " css-1dbjc4n r-18u37iz r-1wtj0ep r-zl2h9q " &gt; &lt;div class= " css-1dbjc4n r-1d09ksm r-18u37iz r-1wbh5a2 " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;a href= " **25;43208;TOOLONG " **55;43235;TOOLONG ... href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;&lt;spa ... style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " **33;43292;TOOLONG ... &lt;div&gt; &lt;hr&gt;&lt;/div&gt; **29;43327;TOOLONG IN FOOD AND **31;43358;TOOLONG &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;43391;TOOLONG ... Food and Wine Festival postponed again , still owes more than **28;43426;TOOLONG &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;43456;TOOLONG ... Out : All-Canadian menu at Gray Jay pushes boundaries of **30;43554;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;/div&gt; Peter HumWed , 23 Oct 2019 15:36:20 **45;43586;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsBeckta serves up refined dinner in support of Mealshare program to combat youth hungerhttp : **34;43633;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;43669;TOOLONG ... title= " Fried green tomatoes with ch ? vre , pickled Matane shrimp , cherry tomato confit , sheep milk and herb vinaigrette served at Beckta. " alt= " Fried green tomatoes with ch ? vre , pickled Matane shrimp , cherry tomato confit , sheep milk and herb vinaigrette served at Beckta. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43705;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1487436 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We were just 17 people around the table on Thursday night , but between us we contributed more than 400 meals to help combat youth hunger in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We were gathered upstairs at Beckta Dining &amp;amp ; Wine on Elgin Street for a dinner in support of Mealshare , the national non-profit initiative that partners with Canadian restaurants so that portions of revenue from the sales of designated dishes go to charities that help feed youth . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the four-course dinner prepared by Beckta chef Shane Brown , each guest , who had paid $100 , was contributing 25 meals through Mealshare . Outside of special events , at each of restaurateur Stephen Beckta&amp;rsquo ; s three Ottawa restaurants , ordering bread means that a guest contributes a meal via Mealshare. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brown&amp;rsquo ; s menu shone a spotlight on produce from Terramor Farm , a small-scale organic farm in Burnstown that supplies Beckta , among other Ottawa restaurants , and sells to the public at the Carp Farmers&amp;rsquo ; Market and through CSA ( community supported agriculture ) boxes . The five-year-old business operated by Luke Sheldrick and Dana Moores , who were previously servers on Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant scene , supplied the kale , carrots , watermelon and green tomatoes that wound up being stars on their respective plates at Beckta. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brown&amp;rsquo ; s dishes included : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43735;TOOLONG ... title= " Watermelon and porchetta salad , mizuna , pickled rhubarb , hazelnut-crusted bocconcini , and rhubarb butter at Beckta Dining &amp; Wine " alt= " Watermelon and porchetta salad , mizuna , pickled rhubarb , hazelnut-crusted bocconcini , and rhubarb butter at Beckta Dining &amp; Wine " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43771;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1487430 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43801;TOOLONG ... title= " Fried green tomatoes with ch ? vre , pickled Matane shrimp , cherry tomato confit , sheep milk and herb vinaigrette " alt= " Fried green tomatoes with ch ? vre , pickled Matane shrimp , cherry tomato confit , sheep milk and herb vinaigrette " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43837;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1487436 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43867;TOOLONG ... title= " Duck magret with potato pav ? , creamed kale , chanterelles , caramelized onion demi-glace " alt= " Duck magret with potato pav ? , creamed kale , chanterelles , caramelized onion demi-glace " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43903;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1487438 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;43933;TOOLONG ... title= " Carrot cake with sea buckthorn cheesecake ganache , port-reduced candied carrots , pecan caramel crumble , citrus ice cream " alt= " Carrot cake with sea buckthorn cheesecake ganache , port-reduced candied carrots , pecan caramel crumble , citrus ice cream " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;43969;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1487437 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dinner was refined and classic , with more than a few highlights . Most memorably , the fried green tomatoes course struck me as delicious , complex and harmonious , and the pop of its cherry tomatoes and vinaigrette were especially pleasing . The duck breast was practically beefy given the heft of its flavour and chew , and its accompaniments brought an especially indulgent note to the table . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Katie Hession , a Ottawa community leader for Mealshare , said Operation Come Home , the Parkdale Food Centre and the Boys and Girls Club of Ottawa ( Beckta is the chair of its board of directors ) would receive support through the dinner . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of the roughly 500 restaurants in 10 major Canadian cities and 50-plus smaller communities that have partnered with Mealshare , almost 100 are in Ottawa , Hession said . Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s contributions put it in the top three of Mealshare&amp;rsquo ; s cities , she continued , adding that she hopes to see the city rise even higher in the ranking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;43999;TOOLONG " **32;44026;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //instagram.com/peterhum " &gt;insta ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN LIFE AND **28;44060;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;44090;TOOLONG ... preview : Rent choreographer Marlies Yearby still finds relevance in acclaimed musical&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;44125;TOOLONG ... Ottawa YouTubers up for prestigious Streamy Awards&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;44160;TOOLONG ... Out : All-Canadian menu at Gray Jay pushes boundaries of creativity&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 18 Oct 2019 18:41:27 **45;44195;TOOLONG ... Out : All-Canadian menu at Gray Jay pushes boundaries of creativityhttp : **30;44242;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;44274;TOOLONG ... title= " Gray Jay partners Dominique Dufour and Devon Bionda . October 11 , 2019 . Errol McGihon/Postmedia " alt= " Gray Jay partners Dominique Dufour and Devon Bionda . October 11 , 2019 . Errol McGihon/Postmedia " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;44310;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1483841 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Gray Jay&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 300 Preston St. , 613-680-0380 , &lt;a href= " http : //grayjayhospitality.ca " &gt;grayja ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday and Wednesday 5:30 to 10 p.m. , Thursday to Saturday 5:30 p.m. till closing , closed Sunday and Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; smaller plates $14 to $24 , larger plates $25 to $50 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; two steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The second time I ate at Gray Jay , I knew better than to ask for coffee . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chef and co-owner Dominique Dufour&amp;rsquo ; s cosy restaurant on Preston Street is as principled as it is accomplished , friendly and even intriguing . At this four-month-old restaurant named after Canada&amp;rsquo ; s national bird , Canadian ingredients are a must . Because coffee beans don&amp;rsquo ; t grow in Canada , Gray Jay does not serve coffee . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For the same reason , don&amp;rsquo ; t expect any tropical fruit at Gray Jay , or any Canadian produce out of season unless it&amp;rsquo ; s been preserved . Do expect Dufour to exercise great creativity within her constraints . She , like a growing number of au courant chefs , is pushing a vision of elevated Canadian dining aligned with the precepts and expanded bounties of new Nordic cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s why Dufour tops her lightly cold-smoked oysters with a sprig of foraged and then fried reindeer moss . She indulges in fermentation , following the trickle-down influence of culinary trailblazer Noma in Copenhagen . Dufour marries seaweed with roasted cod or potatoes , adds rye and oats to her savoury dishes , and favours mushrooms to the point of working them into a persuasively tasty dessert . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before launching Gray Jay , Dufour came to Ottawa from Montreal last year to be the executive chef at the Le Germain Hotel Ottawa . She opened Norca , the boutique hotel&amp;rsquo ; s sleek restaurant whose name refers &amp;mdash ; and this will sound familiar &amp;mdash ; to &amp;ldquo ; Northern cuisine , Canadian ingredients . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But if Dufour is working from a similar playbook at Gray Jay , her food here , which I enjoyed twice this month , strikes me as more personal , interesting and consistent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gray Jay&amp;rsquo ; s taut fall menu consists of 11 items . About half of them are smaller , very well crafted , novel and vegetable-forward . Three meatier large plates round out the savoury selections and there are two desserts . Sharing is very much encouraged . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You can also order a chef&amp;rsquo ; s-choice tasting menu , served family-style for $65 a person , or graze from a selection of cheeses and house-made charcuterie , which befits a place with a sophisticated and user-friendly wine list ( that is , surprisingly , not exclusively Canadian ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s a great deal of thought and technique involved in Dufour&amp;rsquo ; s dishes , all the more to deliver something complex and stimulating that a guest hasn&amp;rsquo ; t eaten before . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those succulent , hard-to-pass-up oysters ( $24 for six ) sat on beds of pur&amp;eacute ; ed cauliflower and cucumber , emulsified with hemp seeds , and topped with dollops of a zippy fennel , roasted red pepper and Tokyo turnip mignonette , not to mention the fried moss . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44340;TOOLONG ... title= " smoked oysters with cauliflower and cucumber mousse , mignonette , fried reindeer moss at Gray Jay " alt= " smoked oysters with cauliflower and cucumber mousse , mignonette , fried reindeer moss at Gray Jay " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;44376;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1483851 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ll note here that these and a few dishes were brought to our table from the long open kitchen facing the dining area by Dufour herself , who likes to break down the distinction between front and back of house . She&amp;rsquo ; s generous with the details and stories of her creations , and will tell you , for example , that she favours wild , hand-harvested P.E.I. oysters because choosing them helps to minimize damage to the oysters&amp;rsquo ; habitat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Indeed , between them , Dufour and sommelier/server Alex Nicholson provided the most engaging and knowledgeable service that I&amp;rsquo ; ve experienced this year in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A dish that wowed us as a distinct indulgence consisted of thinly sliced and marinated pumpkin , tossed with roasted cauliflower and garlic-oil-confit sunchokes in a caramelized onion sauce , with scoops of **29;44406;TOOLONG bone marrow as a finishing touch . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44437;TOOLONG ... title= " Pumpkin , cauliflower and sunchoke with roasted bone marrow at Gray Jay " alt= " Pumpkin , cauliflower and sunchoke with roasted bone marrow at Gray Jay " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;44473;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1483844 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Just as impressive , but more unexpected , was Gray Jay&amp;rsquo ; s deliciously braised Japanese eggplant ( $16 ) with a chickpea-based miso and loose yogurt made with soy milk driving up the umami factor , while beets that had been julienned , fried and dehydrated added crunch and novelty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44503;TOOLONG ... title= " Braised eggplant with soy yogurt , chickpea miso and beets at Gray Jay " alt= " Braised eggplant with soy yogurt , chickpea miso and beets at Gray Jay " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;44539;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1485288 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gray Jay&amp;rsquo ; s menu offers cod collar at two price points ( $25 and $40 ) . We took the smaller cutlet , and were told by Nicholson that either way , Dufour was experimenting with roasting that off-cut of fish on the bone , as if it were red meat . The slab of fish , topped with a gremolata of garlic , butter , lemon thyme and sourdough crumble , was juicy and varied in texture , quite unlike a more demure fillet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44569;TOOLONG ... title= " Roasted cod with gremolata and labneh " alt= " Roasted cod with gremolata and labneh " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;44605;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1483850 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; True red meat lovers should be pleased with the steak of deer or bison , depending on availability ( $50 , for two people ) . We had significantly smoked but richly meaty bison , served with yet another perplexingly good sauce , this time made with chestnuts , fermented and caramelized apples and caramelized onions . Completing the dish was a mound of dill-flecked shoestring potatoes that were dramatically crunchy , possibly to a fault . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44635;TOOLONG ... title= " Bison steak with chestnut-black apple sauce and shoestring potatoes at Gray Jay " alt= " Bison steak with chestnut-black apple sauce and shoestring potatoes at Gray Jay " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;44671;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1485240 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A bowl of rabbit dumplings ( $33 ) was a lucid , comforting dish . Its toothsome , expertly made dumplings sat in a rich bone broth that played its salty notes with pride . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44701;TOOLONG ... title= " Rabbit dumplings , bone broth , mostarda , carrots , onions and celery at Gray Jay . " alt= " Rabbit dumplings , bone broth , mostarda , carrots , onions and celery at Gray Jay . " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;44737;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1483845 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Both of Gray Jay&amp;rsquo ; s desserts were winners . The more conventional ( and vegan-friendly ) of the two was a squash flan ( $8 ) whose sweet , soy-milk-based sauce had notes of hay . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44767;TOOLONG ... title= " Squash flan at Gray Jay " alt= " Squash flan at Gray Jay " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;44803;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1485237 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; More startling was the dessert ( $9 ) that paired a molten ( but not overly sweet ) maple cake with a semifreddo that was topped with morsels of chanterelle mushrooms that had been made more mellow , yet still savoury , by their preparation , and the semifreddo sat on a crumble of yogurt cooked down to caramelized solids . The semifreddo had a streak of tamed mushroom to it as well , thanks to the addition of sugar blended with dehydrated chanterelles. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;44833;TOOLONG ... title= " Maple fondant , caramelized yogourt semifreddo , chanterelles at Gray Jay " alt= " Maple fondant , caramelized yogourt semifreddo , chanterelles at Gray Jay " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;44869;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1483842 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of these experiments happen in what was formerly a Domino&amp;rsquo ; s pizzeria , which Dufour and her co-owner , Gray Jay&amp;rsquo ; s general manager Devon Bionda , renovated on their own . Their restaurant , which seats 27 , is a light-coloured space with concrete tabletops and concrete underfoot , cushioned banquettes and harder chairs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s softer seating at the front of Gray Jay , on an older sofa , while a small bar also appeals . The open kitchen is an eye-grabber and conversation-starter , with its pale green antique fridge and other funky accoutrements . The overall vibe brings to mind some Quebec City restaurants I&amp;rsquo ; ve visited that make fine-dining fun . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;44899;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;44941;TOOLONG ... is Ottawa 's dining scene constantly short-changed by Canada 's restaurant taste-makers ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;44976;TOOLONG ... have Michelin stars evaded Canada 's top restaurants ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gray Jay&amp;rsquo ; s fall menu will be in effect until mid-November , Dufour told me , after which a game-based winter menu will take over . Meanwhile , on Saturdays , Gray Jay serves whole-animal-based menus . Past stars have included smoked goose , a Holstein calf , brook trout , and lamb from Shady Creek Lamb Co. in Kinburn. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dufour is clearly a chef filled with talent and ideas , stretching out successfully at Gray Jay . &amp;ldquo ; This project is very personal and very much a gamble on our side , &amp;rdquo ; she told me. &amp;ldquo ; We are holding on with hopes , dreams and passion . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That gamble by Dufour and her team has paid off handsomely . It&amp;rsquo ; s time now for Ottawa foodies to be passionate in their support of this special place . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;45011;TOOLONG " **32;45038;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;45072;TOOLONG ... Hum 's restaurant reviews&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 16 Oct 2019 15:37:10 **45;45107;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsWhy is Ottawa consistently short-changed in Canada 's best restaurant rankings ? http : **31;45154;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;45187;TOOLONG ... title= " Jim Kyte , the Dean of the School of Hospitality and Tourism at Algonquin College , is also a judge for Canada 's 100 Best Restaurants list for a number of years . " alt= " Jim Kyte , the Dean of the School of Hospitality and Tourism at Algonquin College , is also a judge for Canada 's 100 Best Restaurants list for a number of years . " data-portal-copyright= " JULIE OLIVER " **28;45223;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480818 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;45253;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I&amp;rsquo ; m out of town and dining , I often keep the best of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants in the back of my mind . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Trips this year have taken me to Chicago , Las Vegas , New York , Montreal and Quebec City . I ate very well in each city , frequently at restaurants with the kind of stellar reputations that attract food-focused tourists . But it&amp;rsquo ; s also hit me that I&amp;rsquo ; ve had comparable dinners in Ottawa &amp;mdash ; but at places that are much more unsung , if not locally , then certainly nationally or internationally . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As someone who has dined out roughly twice weekly in Ottawa since the spring of 2012 , here&amp;rsquo ; s how I answer the question , &amp;ldquo ; How good is Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant scene ? &amp;rdquo ; Better , I say , than people , including some influential tastemakers , give it credit for . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I do expect Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s culinary reputation to rise in the foreseeable future . Next year , the Canadian Culinary Championships will take place at the Shaw Centre , and then return in 2021 and 2022 , and perhaps beyond , attracting national attention . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;45289;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;45331;TOOLONG ... have Michelin stars evaded Canada 's top restaurants ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also , since mid-May , several exceptional restaurants have opened in town&amp;nbsp ; &amp;mdash ; namely THRU , Alice and Gray Jay , all coincidentally in Little Italy . Each of these places doles out the kind of ambitious , creative and delicious cuisine that ought to attract the attention of those experts who offer input for the 2020 editions of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants magazine and enRoute magazine&amp;rsquo ; s list of best new Canadian restaurants . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But until then , I and others will feel that Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants have too often been overlooked , although pinning down the reasons why is tricky . &amp;ldquo ; I believe Ottawa is generally under-represented on national ranking lists , certainly not from being undeserving . It&amp;rsquo ; s hard to say why , &amp;rdquo ; says Marc Lepine , the chef-owner of Atelier , which is widely regarded as the city&amp;rsquo ; s finest restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45366;TOOLONG ... title= " Marc Lepine of Atelier . " alt= " Marc Lepine of Atelier . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;45402;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480829 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some say that Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s overall snoozy , government-town reputation obscures an exciting dining scene . Meanwhile , oft-celebrated restaurants in Montreal and Toronto in particular are better marketed and more plugged in to a restaurant industry and tastemakers&amp;rsquo ; network that can make stars of some businesses while ignoring others . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The issue matters not simply in terms of the national capital&amp;rsquo ; s self-respect or bragging rights . Restaurants that operate with razor-thin profit margins must find the going a lot tougher if they&amp;rsquo ; re not lauded . I wonder , for example , if the five-year-old Preston Street restaurant MeNa , which closed in December 2018 , would still be serving refined tasting menus if it had garnered more buzz from the get-go. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Consider the boost Atelier received after it came in fourth on enRoute magazine&amp;rsquo ; s 2009 list of best new Canadian restaurants . That ranking &amp;ldquo ; really helped establish us , &amp;rdquo ; says Lepine. &amp;ldquo ; It was much more important to the business than I would have guessed at the time . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Furthermore , with culinary tourism on the rise , a restaurant scene recognized for its vibrancy and depth makes a city a more appealing destination for wide-roaming food-lovers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Whatever respect Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s dining scene has gained has been hard-won because as recently as 15 years ago , the city had a lousy reputation for dining out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In 2005 , Lesley Chesterman , the Montreal Gazette&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant critic , wrote : &amp;ldquo ; Ottawa isn&amp;rsquo ; t the first city that comes to mind when I&amp;rsquo ; m thinking fine dining . It&amp;rsquo ; s not so much that I&amp;rsquo ; ve sampled enough restaurants in our national capital to come to that conclusion , it&amp;rsquo ; s just that a few ex-Montrealers I know who live there complain endlessly about Ottawa cuisine , or , more specifically the lack of Ottawa cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They don&amp;rsquo ; t like the bread . They say they can&amp;rsquo ; t find good sushi . They&amp;rsquo ; ll even go so far as to drive back to Montreal for birthday dinners and anniversaries , &amp;rdquo ; Chesterman wrote . Despite that , the point of her article was to laud Stephen Beckta&amp;rsquo ; s first eponymous restaurant on Nepean Street , which had opened in 2002. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A dozen years later , Chesterman revised her opinion of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants . Still , she led with this jab : &amp;ldquo ; There&amp;rsquo ; s an old joke regarding the Ottawa food scene that goes something like this : when someone asks , &amp;lsquo ; Where should I go out to eat in Ottawa ? &amp;rsquo ; the quickie comeback is , &amp;lsquo ; Montreal . &amp;rsquo ; Ba-dum-ch ! I know , I know &amp;mdash ; not nice . But for a while there , it was pretty accurate . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chesterman went on to write that the Ottawa area had become &amp;ldquo ; quite a funky dining destination , &amp;rdquo ; thanks to not just the Beckta restaurants ( Play and Gezellig included ) , but also Atelier , Riviera , Pure Kitchen , Union 613 , and , on the Quebec side , Edgar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Restaurant-goers in Ottawa will know this is very much an incomplete list of eateries worth celebrating , and some might even regard &amp;ldquo ; quite a funky dining destination&amp;rdquo ; as faint praise . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45432;TOOLONG ... title= " Jim Kyte , the Dean of the School of Hospitality and Tourism at Algonquin College , is also a judge for Canada 's 100 Best Restaurants list for a number of years . " alt= " Jim Kyte , the Dean of the School of Hospitality and Tourism at Algonquin College , is also a judge for Canada 's 100 Best Restaurants list for a number of years . " data-portal-copyright= " JULIE OLIVER " **28;45468;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480818 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Indeed , Jim Kyte , dean of Algonquin College&amp;rsquo ; s School of Hospitality and Tourism , goes so far as to say &amp;ldquo ; Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s best restaurants are equal to or better than any other top restaurants in Canada that I have experienced . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Starting with high-quality local ingredients combined with its rich multicultural and bilingual diversity , our region is blessed with exceptionally skilled and innovative chefs that truly capture the best of Canada on a plate , &amp;rdquo ; Kyte says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He says he believes Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant scene is under-appreciated by Canadian food-lovers because of &amp;ldquo ; the misconception of ( the city ) only being a government town . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As well , the sheer scale of the restaurant scenes in Toronto and Montreal makes it easy for Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s scene to be overshadowed in the national imagination , Kyte says . &amp;ldquo ; They have more of everything &amp;mdash ; media outlets , restaurants , tourists , customers , critics , bloggers , and foodies &amp;mdash ; so it&amp;rsquo ; s not easy . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chris Knight , CEO of the Ottawa-based food TV channel Gusto Worldwide Media , says that while there&amp;rsquo ; s no shortage of great restaurants and chefs in Ottawa , their counterparts in Montreal , Toronto and Vancouver are better at marketing themselves , often with help from publicists. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re better at the hype , &amp;rdquo ; Knight says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45498;TOOLONG ... title= " Gusto CEO Chris Knight . " alt= " Gusto CEO Chris Knight . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;45534;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480833 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anne DesBrisay , former restaurant critic for the Citizen and then Ottawa Magazine , suggests that restaurant culture in the capital may simply be more low-key . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Are our restaurants as good as those of Montreal and Toronto ? Well , I&amp;rsquo ; d say they&amp;rsquo ; re different , &amp;rdquo ; she says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We don&amp;rsquo ; t have the deep pockets of Toronto to support a ton of really luxurious restaurants and we don&amp;rsquo ; t have nearly as interesting an Asian food scene or a suburban food scene as does the GTA . Neither do we have the rich history of a dining-out culture that Montreal has . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Still , we have a handful of outstanding fine-dining restaurants ( just with less lavish chandeliers ) and a great number of solidly good neighbourhood places , &amp;rdquo ; DesBrisay says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; On the whole , I happen to prefer the atmosphere , the convivial feel , the hospitality offered in this city&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants to those of our bigger neighbours . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since 2015 , the most significant arbiter of Canadian restaurant quality has been Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants magazine . Typically , Toronto and Montreal restaurants dominate its list . While between four and six Ottawa-area restaurants usually crack the list annually , it&amp;rsquo ; s rare for one to break into the Top 50 . Usually that top-ranked Ottawa eatery is Atelier . The highest rank it achieved was 19th in 2015 , followed by 25th last year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s very surprising to me that Marc Lepine&amp;rsquo ; s&amp;nbsp ; Atelier is not in the Top 10 , but this is a very subjective statement , &amp;rdquo ; Kyte says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45564;TOOLONG ... title= " Food from Atelier . " alt= " Food from Atelier . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;45600;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480827 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Restaurateur Beckta has judged in past years for Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants , and says he respects its organizers and consults the magazine when planning his dining while traveling in Canada . But he feels that restaurants in Ottawa and other smaller Canadian cities may be disadvantaged in that they are &amp;ldquo ; less connected to the informal chef network&amp;rdquo ; that makes up part of the judge&amp;rsquo ; s panel . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A contrast to ponder : Beckta&amp;rsquo ; s flagship restaurant received Canadian Automobile Association four-diamond awards for 16 years running , while in the last two years , it did not make the magazine&amp;rsquo ; s list . The CAA/AAA diamonds are awarded after anonymous , paid reviewers assess restaurants , Beckta says . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve seen the criteria and it&amp;rsquo ; s really hard to achieve a top four- or five-diamond rating . Many think of them as the equivalent to Michelin ( stars ) in Canada , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kyte , Knight , DesBrisay and , indeed , Lepine were the four Ottawa-based judges among the 99 who submitted their lists for this year&amp;rsquo ; s Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurant magazine , published in mid-April this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45630;TOOLONG ... title= " Anne DesBrisay. " alt= " Anne DesBrisay. " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;45666;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480826 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; After the magazine&amp;rsquo ; s 2016 list included just two Ottawa-area restaurants , while Calgary , a city of comparable size , had eight listed restaurants , Kyte wrote to question the list&amp;rsquo ; s processes . As a result , he was offered a judging spot , joining the ever-changing slate of food writers , gourmets , restaurateurs and chefs ( none of whom can vote for one of his or her restaurants ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jacob Richler , the editor-in-chief of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants , says its possible that his judges at large lag somewhat when it comes to recognizing Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s most noteworthy restaurants . He calls Toronto , where he lives , &amp;ldquo ; a young , great dining city&amp;rdquo ; compared to Montreal , and adds : &amp;ldquo ; I guess Ottawa is the same compared to Toronto . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It takes people a little time to acclimatize , to catch up with a place&amp;rsquo ; s reputation , &amp;rdquo ; Richler says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; With each iteration , the Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants list involved more judges , from 58 in its first year to 99 this year . The judging panel improves each year in terms of regional representation , Richler says . &amp;ldquo ; At first , the big chore was to make provincial representation fair to their respective populations &amp;hellip ; and then everybody wants the same thing for all the cities in the provinces . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I could always use more judges in different cities around the country , &amp;rdquo ; Richler says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He then asked me if I could refer Ottawa-based candidates for judging , and even invited me to judge . I accepted . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Richler says he seeks out judges who travel . &amp;ldquo ; I like to have people who have perspective nationally , &amp;rdquo ; he says . Each judge is asked to name three of their 10 nominated restaurants from outside of their home region . &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t want people to just vote for restaurants in their hometown , &amp;rdquo ; Richler says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The magazine&amp;rsquo ; s panel has included a lot of Toronto-based judges , Richler says , because he has &amp;ldquo ; a real ease finding Torontonians who can name restaurants around the country , as compared to , say , people in Vancouver . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Judges who nominate five or more of restaurants outside of their home region are lauded as &amp;ldquo ; national&amp;rdquo ; judges . This year , 34 of 99 judges , including Kyte and Knight , were so designated . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For Ottawa Tourism , one goal is to promote the city&amp;rsquo ; s food scene by reaching out to travel and food writers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chesterman , the Montreal Gazette critic , came to Ottawa in 2017 at the invitation of the marketing organization . She visited primarily to take part in Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Table , the 1,000-person outdoor dinner on Wellington Street in front of Parliament Hill , but also followed an itinerary prepared by Ottawa Tourism that covered selected restaurants and other activities , says Jantine Van Kregten , director of communications for Ottawa Tourism . ( My trips this year to Las Vegas , Montreal and Chicago were similarly underwritten and organized by local tourism organizations. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; More recently , 130 travel writers from around the world and 160 Canadian tourism professionals met in Ottawa this summer at the GoMedia Canada conference . The attendees went on day-long tours during their visit , including one of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s food scene , says Catherine Callary , Ottawa Tourism&amp;rsquo ; s vice-president of destination development . &lt;i&gt;&lt;/i&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We expect to see benefits of these day tours coming back to us over the course of the next couple of years , through travel stories on Ottawa , &amp;rdquo ; Callary says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45696;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef Yannick LaSalle of Restaurant Les Foug ? res with a plate of Duck Magret , Black Garlic Mustard , Red Currants , and Beets as ten different chefs compete in Canada 's Great Kitchen Party with their unique dishes at the Shaw Centre . " alt= " Chef Yannick LaSalle of Restaurant Les Foug ? res with a plate of Duck Magret , Black Garlic Mustard , Red Currants , and Beets as ten different chefs compete in Canada 's Great Kitchen Party with their unique dishes at the Shaw Centre . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;45732;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480835 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s telling , she adds , that Ottawa-area chefs have done so well at the Canadian Culinary Championships . Its winner this year was Yannick LaSalle from Les Foug&amp;egrave ; res in Chelsea . Atelier&amp;rsquo ; s Lepine won gold at the championships twice , in 2012 and 2016 &amp;mdash ; he is the only Canadian chef with two national victories . Chefs Joe Thottungal of Coconut Lagoon , and Jamie Stunt , then of Oz Kafe , won silver in 2017 and 2013 , respectively . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I think there&amp;rsquo ; s a general sense amongst Canadian restaurant industry folk that Ottawa punches above its weight when it comes to the quality of our restaurants , the abilities and creative vision of our chefs , &amp;rdquo ; says DesBrisay , noting the city&amp;rsquo ; s culinary medal-winners. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Earlier this year , it was announced that the culinary championships , which were held for many years in Kelowna , B.C. , would move to Ottawa in 2020. &amp;ldquo ; We want to take it so that all of Canada knows about this , and what better place than Ottawa ? &amp;rdquo ; said Karen Blair , co-founder of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party , which is the series of regional culinary competitions that culminate in the national contest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Blair said earlier this year that Ottawa had much going for it&amp;nbsp ; &amp;mdash ; its central location , strong food scene , and the proven success of its Gold Medal Plates events in past years , which attracted hundreds of food-lovers each fall to the Shaw Centre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kyte points out that Algonquin&amp;rsquo ; s Restaurant International , which is staffed by students , will host the &amp;ldquo ; black box&amp;rdquo ; portion of the national competition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , 2019 has been a strong year for ambitious restaurant openings in Ottawa , with the arrival of several that I think should be contenders next year when enRoute magazine compiles its list of best new Canadian restaurants . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The ones I have in mind opened too late to be considered for this year&amp;rsquo ; s enRoute Top 10 list , which will be unveiled Oct. 23 . This summer , enRoute unveiled its long list of 35 restaurants in contention for the Top 10 . None of the 35 is in Ottawa . They would have been culled from a longer list of recommendations from various Canadian experts . The two such experts from the Ottawa area are Pierre Jury of Le Droit and sommelier-restaurateur V&amp;eacute ; ronique Rivest. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Atelier chef Lepine&amp;rsquo ; s **30;45762;TOOLONG THRU was a unique and enthralling gourmet experience when I dined there on its opening night in May . Its almost-giddy tasting menu of roughly 50 bites was unlike anything I&amp;rsquo ; d experienced in a restaurant , and the dinner was all the more out of the ordinary , given the amount of tech-assisted interactivity L&amp;eacute ; pine encourages . He asks that customers bring cellphones , which through his wizardry deliver more information about his food than menus or even servers would . While THRU&amp;rsquo ; s setting is more stark than luxurious , and its approach more playful than snooty , it&amp;rsquo ; s the kind of outside-the-box , creative endeavour that I hope would be recognized by Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants or enRoute magazine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;45794;TOOLONG ... title= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The dishes are Summer Bouquet , Tomato and Ponzu , and Coconut , Cranberry Beans and Coffee . " alt= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The dishes are Summer Bouquet , Tomato and Ponzu , and Coconut , Cranberry Beans and Coffee . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;45830;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480825 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Similarly , at her restaurant Alice , chef-owner Briana Kim is creating wonders of fine-dining that just happen to be vegan . Like Lepine , Kim , who has also represented Ottawa at the Canadian Culinary Championships , is another brave chef blazing a distinctive trail . The tasting menu at Alice I tried a few weeks ago was a parade of imaginative , beautiful dishes that forced us to re-evaluate the refined satisfactions that plant-based cuisine can offer . Again , I hope that tastemakers from beyond Ottawa put Alice on their radar . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Finally , I&amp;rsquo ; ve eaten one dinner at Gray Jay , which chef-owner Dominique Dufour opened this summer on Preston Street . She served some enticing and singular dishes that epitomized her dedication to Canadian ingredients and love of culinary research and techniques . Service here was exceptional , with Dufour and her team breaking down the wall between the so-called front and back of house . As Kim does at Alice , Dufour makes a point of serving customers and sharing her thoughts about special dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During those jaunts to Chicago , New York , Quebec City and Montreal , I ate at several restaurants with one or two Michelin stars , a previous No. 1 restaurant on enRoute magazine&amp;rsquo ; s list and two restaurants in the Top 10 of this year&amp;rsquo ; s Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants list . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , in each instance I was very impressed &amp;mdash ; but not enough to think that the best of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants don&amp;rsquo ; t merit similar accolades. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;br&gt; twitter.com/peterhum &lt;/p&gt; Wilfrid&amp;rsquo ; s Restaurant ( Four Diamonds since 2017 ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;45917;TOOLONG ...
