Out : Alice in Little Italy is a trailblazer , and should be recognized nationally&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;45987;TOOLONG ... d ? ner to tacos , your guide to the newest restaurants in Ottawa&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 10 Oct 2019 18:30:13 **45;46022;TOOLONG ... NewsNationalWhy have Michelin stars evaded Canada 's top restaurants ? http : **28;46069;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;46099;TOOLONG ... title= " Stephen Beckta , owner of Beckta restaurant on Elgin Street in Ottawa Friday Sept 27 , 2019. " alt= " Stephen Beckta , owner of Beckta restaurant on Elgin Street in Ottawa Friday Sept 27 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;46135;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480816 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;46165;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; It rarely happens that the great Canadian chef Normand Laprise of the perennially acclaimed Montreal restaurant Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! cooks for a group of culinary high-achievers , including kitchen superstars even more celebrated than himself . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That unique occasion happened in early 2015 , when he traveled to Lyon , which some regard as the best city in France in which to eat . There , at Lyon&amp;rsquo ; s city hall , Laprise was one of five chefs preparing dishes for a grand , 250-person banquet in honour of the legendary Lyonnais chef Paul Bocuse. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We cooked for all the two- and three-starred chefs in France and Italy , &amp;rdquo ; Laprise recalls , referring to the much-coveted ratings of the Michelin Guide , the time-honoured international reference for gastronomes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It was so stressful . But at the same time , it was so stimulating . You represent yourself , Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! , Canada and Quebec . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;46201;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;46243;TOOLONG ... is Ottawa consistently short-changed in Canada 's best restaurant rankings ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We need this to happen more to other Canadian chefs , so that Canadians get more international recognition , &amp;rdquo ; Laprise told this newspaper in a translated interview from French . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That&amp;rsquo ; s a patriotic sentiment , but also easier for Laprise to say , given his accomplishments that preceded his trip to Lyon . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46278;TOOLONG ... title= " Normand Laprise , chef and co-owner at Toque in Montreal , on Friday , December 7 , 2018. " alt= " Normand Laprise , chef and co-owner at Toque in Montreal , on Friday , December 7 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Allen McInnis " **28;46314;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480819 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Opened in 1993 , Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! rose to become widely regarded as Quebec&amp;rsquo ; s best restaurant and an incubator for the next generation of chefs after Laprise , who is now 58 . The restaurant has been accepted into two distinguished France-based culinary organizations , joining the Relais &amp;amp ; Ch&amp;acirc ; teaux group in 2006 and the Les Grandes Tables du Monde association in 2014 . Laprise says he raised Toque ! &amp;rsquo ; s game to qualify for this prestigious membership in an effort to attract more international diners. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since the annual Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants list debuted in 2015 , Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! has ranked first , second or third in the country . This year , Laprise became the first Canadian chef to crack the World&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Chefs &lt;a href= " https : **33;46344;TOOLONG ... ( in fact , he ranked 100th ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Still , Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! does not have a Michelin star , never mind two or three . While the Michelin Guide assesses restaurants in more than two dozen countries , Canada is not one of them . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Effectively , Canada&amp;rsquo ; s culinary scene is in Michelin&amp;rsquo ; s blind spot , and is similarly out of sight as far as other international culinary rankings are concerned . Laprise is arguably the exception that proves the rule , and the lack of recognition for Canadian restaurants from Michelin&amp;nbsp ; may well mean there&amp;rsquo ; s a hurdle for the country to surmount when it comes to wooing culinary tourists , who make up a growing segment of the tourism sector . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Michelin Guide is not the only reference that globe-trotting gourmets consult . The World&amp;rsquo ; s 50 Best &lt;a href= " https : **33;46379;TOOLONG ... of restaurants , which occasionally mentions a Canadian restaurant , has considerable stature . It relies on a panel of judges &amp;mdash ; I am to join that group this year &amp;mdash ; each of whom submits a list of 10 restaurants . La Liste , which was launched in Paris in 2015 and is sanctioned by France&amp;rsquo ; s Foreign Ministry and tourism board , offers its list of the world&amp;rsquo ; s 1,000 best restaurants , based on an aggregation of pronouncements of hundreds of guidebooks and millions of online reviews . The Opinionated About Dining ( OAD ) lists , which debuted in 2013 , are compiled from the surveys of almost 6,000 elite contributors . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46414;TOOLONG ... title= " Gwendal Poullennec ( R ) , international director of the Michelin Guide , speaks with three-star chef Patrick O'Connell , of the restaurant The Inn at Little Washington , during the unveiling of the new edition of the Michelin gastronomic guide at the French Ambassador 's residence in Washington , DC , on October 1 , 2019. " alt= " Gwendal Poullennec ( R ) , international director of the Michelin Guide , speaks with three-star chef Patrick O'Connell , of the restaurant The Inn at Little Washington , during the unveiling of the new edition of the Michelin gastronomic guide at the French Ambassador 's residence in Washington , DC , on October 1 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " ERIC BARADAT " **28;46450;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480837 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , as far as Canada&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants are concerned , the Michelin Guide is most conspicuous precisely because of its absence . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Asked what consideration , if any , the Michelin Guide has given to assessing Canadian restaurants , a spokeswoman offered a response that did not mention Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are always evaluating exciting new destinations for the Michelin Guide , &amp;rdquo ; said Lauren McClure , a public relations manager for Michelin North America . &amp;ldquo ; However , we have no news to announce at this time . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The No. 1 consideration before we go to any country is the importance of a thriving culinary scene . Globally , we are adding new titles every year , &amp;rdquo ; McClure wrote . Michelin just announced its 2019 guide for California , which joins sister publications in the United States covering New York City , Chicago and Washington , D.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; As a restaurant , your primary objective should be to fill your restaurants with happy customers and to cook for your patrons , not for Michelin . If you do this well and consistently , Michelin inspectors will find you , &amp;rdquo ; McClure wrote . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; McClure does not delve into what the Washington Post &lt;a href= " https : **33;46480;TOOLONG ... in 2017 &amp;mdash ; that Michelin Guides can be commissioned for a price . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Claire Dorland Clauzel , then Michelin&amp;rsquo ; s executive vice-president overseeing the guides , told the Post two years ago : &amp;ldquo ; Some countries and some governments that want to . &amp;thinsp ; . &amp;thinsp ; . attract tourism , they are very interested in having a guide , and so they sponsor a guide to have the ability to communicate around their gastronomic landscape . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Dorland Clauzel told the Post that guides in Seoul , Macau , Hong Kong , Bangkok and Singapore were commissioned . Korea&amp;rsquo ; s Yonhap News reported in the fall of 2017 that the government-run Korea Tourism Organization subsidized the guide for Seoul to the tune of two billion won , or about $2 million CAD , for four years . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Destination Canada , the Crown corporation that was formerly the Canadian Tourism Commission , &amp;ldquo ; is currently not involved in any formal discussions about bringing the Michelin Guide to Canada , &amp;rdquo ; said spokeswoman Emma Slieker. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; James Chatto , the co-founder and head judge of the Canadian Culinary Championships , wonders if Michelin&amp;rsquo ; s focus on fine dining in a classic vein would be &amp;ldquo ; rather old-fashioned and out of sync with the way Canada builds restaurants these days . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Many of our leading restaurateurs prefer a much more casual and idiosyncratic approach that wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t tick Michelin&amp;rsquo ; s boxes for linen , stemware , wine cellar and so on . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chatto says he could see several restaurants in Toronto receiving a single Michelin star , as well as two in Montreal and one or two in Quebec . In Ottawa , though ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I have had stellar meals at Atelier and Beckta , &amp;rdquo ; Chatto says . &amp;ldquo ; But is Michelin open-minded and sophisticated enough to understand what they do ? I&amp;rsquo ; m not sure . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46515;TOOLONG ... title= " Seared Bay of Fundy Scallops with crispy sweetbreads and late summer vegetables at Beckta. " alt= " Seared Bay of Fundy Scallops with crispy sweetbreads and late summer vegetables at Beckta. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;46551;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480817 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , my impression is that Michelin does practise cultural relativism when it visits different destinations . For example , in Chicago , it has awarded a single star to Band of Bohemia , a brew pub , and to Longman &amp;amp ; Eagle , a gastropub . In Singapore , Liao Fan Hong Kong Soya Sauce Chicken Rice &amp;amp ; Noodles , a street-food stall , has a Michelin star . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Stephen Beckta , who before becoming one of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s leading restaurateurs worked at the top-notch , Michelin-starred New York restaurants Caf&amp;eacute ; Boulud and Eleven Madison Park , says that Canada&amp;rsquo ; s top restaurants deliver admirable , and comparable , fine dining and service , if not over-the-top extravagance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; While we don&amp;rsquo ; t have the concentration of wealthy guests that dine out nightly as a city like New York City or Chicago does , I think many of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s best restaurants stack up very well with the best of American destinations , &amp;rdquo ; Beckta says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Where I think we excel is in the depth of our hospitality , even though we might not feature as many luxury products like white truffles and caviar as often , or have 10 unpaid apprentices in the back to prep many of the super-fussy dishes that the top spots in the States do . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Jacob Richler , editor-in-chief of Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants magazine , says that Canadian restaurants are &amp;ldquo ; not playing at the same price point&amp;rdquo ; as their Michelin-starred counterparts abroad . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He cites the example of Alo in Toronto , which has been top-rated in his magazine for the last two years . There , tasting menus cost $135 and $165 per person , before wine . At a two-Michelin-starred restaurant in the U.S. , comparable meals would be twice the price , he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46581;TOOLONG ... title= " Individual servings of Wagyu Beef cooks over a burner at Thru . " alt= " Individual servings of Wagyu Beef cooks over a burner at Thru . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;46617;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480821 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Ottawa , Atelier&amp;rsquo ; s tasting menu is $125 , while the tasting menu at its new sister restaurant THRU is $200 per person , including beverage pairings , taxes and tip . Beckta&amp;rsquo ; s tasting menu is $98 , and at Alice , chef Briana Kim&amp;rsquo ; s new restaurant , the tasting menu is $95 . In Chicago last month , I ate at the two-Michelin-starred restaurant Smyth , where the tasting menu was $155 US and non-alcoholic pairings were $70 US. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Canadian chefs work &amp;ldquo ; really , really , really hard&amp;rdquo ; to keep their prices low , Richler says . He wonders : If Michelin were to rate Canadian restaurants and make them more alluring to international diners , wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t the prices go up ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! &amp;rsquo ; s Laprise , too , says that ostentatious , blow-the-bank meals at three-Michelin-starred restaurants in France or New York don&amp;rsquo ; t jibe with Canada&amp;rsquo ; s fine-dining scene . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If you had a restaurant in Montreal or Toronto and you charged $500 per person , no wine , it wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t work , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We can&amp;rsquo ; t afford the same things . We don&amp;rsquo ; t have old money like in Europe . We can&amp;rsquo ; t offer the same luxury . We have to stay who we are . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Michelin is changing , but they would really have to adapt to our reality , &amp;rdquo ; Laprise says . Further , he asks : If Michelin were to come to Canada , but decline to award three stars to any restaurants , what would that say about fine dining in Canada ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anne DesBrisay , an Ottawa-based retired restaurant critic and cookbook author , says she is very keen to have Canada&amp;rsquo ; s food and drink scene recognized more internationally , but that we need to do a better job of selling it abroad . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It doesn&amp;rsquo ; t help that we don&amp;rsquo ; t toot our own horn of plenty ; that we still write about poutine and BeaverTails and Screech ; that our national airlines are still serving French wine to passengers when Canada has a kick-ass wine industry , &amp;rdquo ; DesBrisay says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46647;TOOLONG ... title= " Server Alison Hussey serves Alex and Erin Anderson at the small dining room called Thru . " alt= " Server Alison Hussey serves Alex and Erin Anderson at the small dining room called Thru . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;46683;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480823 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Laprise points to other countries whose culinary stars are celebrated far more than their Canadian peers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Peru , to name one , is a hot culinary destination now , although no one was talking about its unique cuisine 20 years ago , Laprise says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Indeed , according to Ireland&amp;rsquo ; s Food and Drink Strategy for 2018-2023 , a document meant to bolster its culinary status internationally , Peru is a role model . In the first half of the 2010s , the World Travel Awards selected Peru as the world&amp;rsquo ; s leading culinary destination for five consecutive years , the Irish document notes , while Italy , France , Spain , and Japan were runners-up . Since he opened his restaurant Astrid y Gaston 25 years ago , chef Gast&amp;ograve ; n Acurio has promoted Peruvian cuisine in his country and abroad , with support from his government , Laprise says . Two restaurants in Lima &amp;mdash ; Central and Maido &amp;mdash ; are among the Top 10 in the world , according to the 2019 World&amp;rsquo ; s Best 50 Restaurants list . ( Fun fact : Central&amp;rsquo ; s chef Virgilio Mart&amp;iacute ; nez is an alumnus of the Le Cordon Bleu Ottawa culinary program. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Nordic countries are celebrated for their cuisine and top-rated restaurants such as Noma in Copenhagen , Laprise continues . &amp;ldquo ; They&amp;rsquo ; re really strong when it comes to branding . They created what we know as the famous Nordic cuisine , &amp;rdquo ; Laprise says . &amp;ldquo ; But in Canada , we&amp;rsquo ; ve had Nordic cuisine since the 1950s . We never marketed it or made it trendy , but they did the branding expertly , supported again by their governments , to become known gastronomically. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You can eat as well in Canada as you can eat there , as far as I am concerned , but they knew well how to sell their image to the world and get recognized internationally . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Canada , culinary tourism is &amp;ldquo ; in its infancy , &amp;rdquo ; says Greg Klassen , the Vancouver-based principal at Twenty31 Consulting , which several years ago prepared a report on culinary tourism for Innovation , Science and Economic Development Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Culinary tourism , Klassen says , is one of the most significant areas of tourism growth around the world . It refers to not only the pursuit of fine dining , but rather of good eating and everything from food trucks to farms to picnics . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46713;TOOLONG ... title= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The dish seen here is Summer Bouquet . " alt= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The dish seen here is Summer Bouquet . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;46749;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480828 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; This year , several projects related to culinary tourism have received funding , says Parmar , a media relations officer for Innovation , Science and Economic Development Canada . Grants include $625,000 to the Tourism Industry Association of Canada , as much as $500,000 to the M&amp;eacute ; t is Nation of Alberta to develop a culinary experience at M&amp;eacute ; t is Crossing , and $193,000 to the City of Temiskaming Shores , Ont. to develop a culinary strategy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , Destination Canada this summer partnered with the World&amp;rsquo ; s 50 Best Restaurants to have Ontario featured in a &amp;ldquo ; 50 Best Explores&amp;rdquo ; &lt;a href= " https : **33;46779;TOOLONG ... , in which the global gastronomy brand would feature several Ontario and globally recognized chefs on its social media channels and website . Previously , a &amp;ldquo ; 50 Best Explores&amp;rdquo ; initiative visited Peru . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Richler from Canada&amp;rsquo ; s 100 Best Restaurants feels that in Canada , Montreal is tops when it comes to attracting international fine-dining lovers and American gourmets in particular . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Montreal , having been the longest established , and simultaneously having a more readily identifiable culinary culture than a lot of other places , does attract more culinary tourism , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Under the Tourisme Montr&amp;eacute ; al event called MTL&amp;agrave ; TABLE , 150 restaurants offer discounted set-menu dinners between $23 and $43 during the first two weeks of November . Each winter , the Montr&amp;eacute ; al en Lumi&amp;egrave ; re includes a significant culinary component , in which chefs from Laprise down offer special menus and splashy dinner events . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The prestige of Montreal&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants fits within a larger frame . Overall , Quebec celebrates its culinary successes with the fervour of a distinct society . &amp;nbsp ; Since 2010 , the Radio-Canada TV show Les Chefs ! has crowned culinary champions . ( Of the show&amp;rsquo ; s roughly 100 contestants , none has been from Gatineau . ) Last year , the awards called Les Lauriers de la gastronomie Qu&amp;eacute ; b&amp;eacute ; coise debuted , honouring restaurants , chefs , pastry chefs , sommeliers , mixologists , servers , farms and food trucks . Last year , Laprise won the prize for promoting Quebec&amp;rsquo ; s culinary culture outside of the province . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;46814;TOOLONG ... title= " Taste of the Cayman event on the great lawn of the Ritz Carlton Grand Cayman , at the 2019 Cayman Cookout. " alt= " Taste of the Cayman event on the great lawn of the Ritz Carlton Grand Cayman , at the 2019 Cayman Cookout. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;46850;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480830 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In mid-January next year , the Toque ! chef will continue to wave the flag when he jets to the Caribbean to take part in the Cayman Cookout , which despite its no-frills name is one of world&amp;rsquo ; s most posh and alluring culinary festivals for well-heeled gastronomes. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the pristine white sands of Seven Mile Beach outside of the Ritz-Carlton , Grand Cayman , Laprise will share the story of Toque ! and serve small bites at a cooking demonstration . He is to prepare a fancier Quebecois lunch in one of the Ritz-Carlton&amp;rsquo ; s restaurants . On the event&amp;rsquo ; s final night , Laprise will cook with such world-famous chefs as Dominique Crenn and Clare Smyth at its grand , $950 US-a-seat gala . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; m very excited to cook with all those great chefs , &amp;rdquo ; Laprise says , noting that he knows and has cooked before with New York chef Eric Ripert , the host of the Cayman Cookout. &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s very stimulating &amp;hellip ; I go to learn , to spread my culture , to see a different vision , to compare , always to learn , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Grand Cayman , Laprise will also rub shoulders with such globally renowned chefs and fellow headliners as Jose Andres , Daniel Boulud and Emeril Lagasse . But of course , he will be the only Canadian chef present . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; phum@postmedia.com &lt;br&gt; twitter.com/peterhum &lt;/p&gt; **27;46880;TOOLONG Canadian restaurants rank : **26;46909;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; The World&amp;rsquo ; s 50 Best restaurants lists ( with honourable mentions , the list extends beyond 50 ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alo , Toronto ( 90 in 2019 , 94 in 2018 ) &lt;br&gt; Joe Beef , Montreal ( 81 in 2015 ) &lt;br&gt; Rouge , Calgary ( 60 in 2010 ) &lt;br&gt; Langdon Hall Dining Room &amp;amp ; Terrace , Cambridge ( 77 in 2010 ) &lt;br&gt; Eigensinn Farm , Singhampton ( 28 in 2003 , 9 in 2002 ) &lt;br&gt; Susur , Toronto ( 49 in 2002 ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **28;46937;TOOLONG " &gt; &; ... La Liste 2019 list of &amp;ldquo ; 1,000 remarkable restaurants&amp;rdquo ; ( scores out of 100 ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alo , Toronto ( 98 ) &lt;br&gt; Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! , Montreal ( 84 ) &lt;br&gt; Edulis , Toronto ( 83.25 ) &lt;br&gt; Initiale , Quebec City ( 82.25 ) &lt;br&gt; Langdon Hall Dining Room &amp;amp ; Terrace , Cambridge ( 82 ) &lt;br&gt; Raymonds , St. John&amp;rsquo ; s ( 81.25 ) &lt;br&gt; Buca Osteria &amp;amp ; Bar , Toronto ( 81.25 ) &lt;br&gt; Le Baccara , Gatineau ( 80.5 ) &lt;br&gt; Montr&amp;eacute ; al Plaza , Montreal ( 80.5 ) &lt;br&gt; Le Hatley , North Hatley , QC ( 80.5 ) &lt;br&gt; Canoe Restaurant and Bar , Toronto ( 80.25 ) &lt;br&gt; Caf&amp;eacute ; Boulud , Toronto ( 80 ) &lt;br&gt; Maison Boulud , Montreal ( 80 ) &lt;br&gt; Joe Beef , Montreal ( 80 ) &lt;br&gt; Le Mousso , Montreal ( 80 ) &lt;br&gt; Scaramouche , Toronto ( 80 ) &lt;br&gt; Kissa Tanto , Vancouver ( 80 ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; OAD 2019 of top 100+ North American restaurants &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Raymonds , St. John&amp;rsquo ; s ( 8 ) &lt;br&gt; Alo , Toronto ( 29 ) &lt;br&gt; Atelier , Ottawa ( 80 ) &lt;br&gt; Actinolite , Toronto ( 81 ) &lt;br&gt; Hashimoto , Toronto ( 89 ) &lt;br&gt; Hawksworth , Vancouver ( 98 ) &lt;br&gt; Joe Beef , Montreal ( 107 ) &lt;br&gt; Canoe Restaurant and Bar , Toronto ( 116 ) &lt;br&gt; Edulis , Toronto ( 129 ) &lt;br&gt; Europea , Montreal ( 131 ) &lt;br&gt; Toqu&amp;eacute ; ! , Montreal ( 132 ) &lt;br&gt; Scaramouche , Toronto ( 140 ) &lt;br&gt; Opus Restaurant , Toronto ( 151 ) &lt;br&gt; Eigensinn Farm , Singhampton , ON ( 181 ) &lt;br&gt; Buca Yorkville , Toronto ( 186 ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;46967;TOOLONG ... is Ottawa consistently short-changed in Canada 's best restaurant rankings ? &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;47002;TOOLONG ... end of meat ? Today 's morality advocates eating less animal protein and more sustainable weird stuff&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;47037;TOOLONG ... makes restaurant food ' authentic , ' and who gets to decide ? &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 10 Oct 2019 13:28:38 **45;47072;TOOLONG ... NewsNationalDining Out : 1 Elgin , the revitalized NAC restaurant , scores with Indigenous-inspired fall menuhttp : **36;47119;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;47157;TOOLONG ... title= " Pan-seared duck breast at 1 Elgin , the recently rebranded restaurant in the National Arts Centre " alt= " Pan-seared duck breast at 1 Elgin , the recently rebranded restaurant in the National Arts Centre " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47193;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480545 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;1 Elgin&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; The former Le Caf&amp;eacute ; inside the National Arts Centre , 613 594-5127 , &lt;a href= " http : //nac-cna.ca/en/1elgin " &gt;nac-cna ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Saturday 11:30 a.m. to 2 p.m. and 5 to 9 p.m. , closed Sunday and Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; starters $13 to $19 , mains $28 to $42 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; steps down to entrance &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For four decades , Le Caf&amp;eacute ; , the National Arts Centre&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant , won over innumerable fans . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Its first chef , the late and revered Kurt Waldele , was a pioneer of elevated cuisine that celebrated Canada , years before waving our flag in the kitchen became common . With its unbeatable downtown location and view of the Rideau Canal , Le Caf&amp;eacute ; was a prime destination for locals and tourists alike . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , Le Caf&amp;eacute ; also had its detractors . In 2010 , my predecessor Anne DesBrisay wrote : &amp;ldquo ; For as many years as I&amp;rsquo ; ve been writing about Ottawa restaurants&amp;rdquo ; &amp;mdash ; which was 17 at the time &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; I have found the food at the National Arts Centre&amp;rsquo ; s dining room pretty so-what-ish &amp;hellip ; It seemed to me a restaurant stubbornly determined not to outshine the shows going on above its head . &amp;rdquo ; I was of a similar mind in 2013 , although DesBrisay and I were reviewing restaurants overseen by different chefs. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since then , much has changed . In the summer of 2017 , Kenton Leier came on board as the NAC&amp;rsquo ; s executive chef after helming kitchens at some of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s top downtown hotels . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the summer of 2018 , a first phase of renovations revamped the kitchen and brought in new custom-made tables and chairs , installed circular booths along its back wall , and changed the carpeting . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In early June this year , the restaurant closed for more renovations . The bar that had been near the entrance was moved to the middle of the dining room , cutting its long , narrow space in half . Just as dramatically , the ceiling has been modernized , swapping in a reflective surface broken up energetically by sleek bars of light . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The NAC also bid adieu to the Le Caf&amp;eacute ; name . I&amp;rsquo ; m not sure I like the new name &amp;mdash ; 1 Elgin &amp;mdash ; more , but as a signal of change , it&amp;rsquo ; s meaningful . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most importantly , based on my visits to Le Caf&amp;eacute ; earlier this year and 1 Elgin last week , I can say that recent changes have done the restaurant a lot of good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last Friday , which was not a show night at the NAC , we liked the half-full room&amp;rsquo ; s revitalized ambience . The centralized bar adds more buzz to the place . Over all , the room feels more of today , in line with the NAC&amp;rsquo ; s overall renovation in recent years that coincided with Canada&amp;rsquo ; s sesquicentennial and the centre&amp;rsquo ; s 50th anniversary this year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most dishes we ordered from the menu of six appetizers , six mains and seven desserts were more well made , better presented and even more significant than what I&amp;rsquo ; d eaten at the NAC both years ago and this spring , before Le Caf&amp;eacute ; closed . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The extra significance was due to the fact that Leier last month teamed up with Six Nations chef Rich Francis to design the menu . Francis is the first of four resident chefs at the NAC in 2019-20 . His food debuted in mid-September , coinciding with the launch of the NAC&amp;rsquo ; s new Indigenous Theatre , and will be offered at 1 Elgin until Nov. 13 . Francis has set the bar high for the chefs that will have residencies next January , March and May to complete the NAC&amp;rsquo ; s inaugural resident chefs program . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Francis&amp;rsquo ; s input has added , to varying degrees , Indigenous touches or back stories to some menu dishes , and we leaned in that direction , passing on more conventional items such as beef tartare or beef tenderloin that might have been on Le Caf&amp;eacute ; &amp;rsquo ; s menu . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of three appetizers , we enjoyed most of all the &amp;ldquo ; three sisters soup&amp;rdquo ; ( $13 ) . While I&amp;rsquo ; ve since read that three sisters soup usually includes corn , beans and squash , our soup was a hearty , homey potato-fennel soup , enlivened by tender shreds of smoked duck , black olive tapenade and some hidden but zippy candied lemon zest . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47223;TOOLONG ... title= " Three Sisters Soup at 1 Elgin " alt= " Three Sisters Soup at 1 Elgin " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47259;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480518 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Anishinaabe wild rice and Tuscarora corn salad ( $ &lt;span class= " price " &gt;15 ) was simple but refined , with ribbons of lightly pickled root veg and a creamy , intriguing vinaigrette adding breadth to the dish . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47289;TOOLONG ... title= " wild rice and corn salad at 1 Elgin " alt= " wild rice and corn salad at 1 Elgin " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47325;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480544 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Medicine-cured salmon&amp;rdquo ; ( $19 ) was the night&amp;rsquo ; s only dish that felt like a bit of a letdown . Its slab of fish was pleasant and restrained of flavour , but its greens were more pedestrian . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47355;TOOLONG ... title= " Medicine-cured salmon and greens at 1 Elgin " alt= " Medicine-cured salmon and greens at 1 Elgin " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47391;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480543 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Also , when we asked our server why the dish was &amp;ldquo ; medicine-cured , &amp;rdquo ; his answer was more improvised than informed . He returned later to tell us that the salmon had been cured with juniper and beet juice , which was nice to know , but still didn&amp;rsquo ; t answer the question . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the whole , our servers that night were friendly , attentive and engaged . But they could also have been more knowledgeable about dishes that deserved a bit of explanation and detailed touting. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Main courses were satisfying and attractively plated . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My wife&amp;rsquo ; s Arctic char ( $29 ) was the largest piece of that fish I&amp;rsquo ; d ever seen on a plate , and it was appealingly crisp-skinned and succulent . Wild rice and steel-cut oat risotto struck a comforting note and well-roasted chunky vegetables ( a feature on every plate ) added bulk and visual appeal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47421;TOOLONG ... title= " Arctic char with wild rice and steel-cut oats risotto , at 1 Elgin " alt= " Arctic char with wild rice and steel-cut oats risotto , at 1 Elgin " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47457;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480548 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Elk osso buco ( $42 ) was massive , meaty and duskily-flavoured , and its roasted grapes were a nice accent . However , the marrowbone on the plate was seemingly more for show than enjoyment , as its marrow was dry and didn&amp;rsquo ; t provide the requisite indulgent note . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47487;TOOLONG ... title= " Elk osso bucco with br ? l ? ed marrowbone , at 1 Elgin " alt= " Elk osso bucco with br ? l ? ed marrowbone , at 1 Elgin " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47523;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480547 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pan-seared and impeccably seasoned duck breast ( $32 ) could not have been improved upon . Our table&amp;rsquo ; s vegan was pleased with a colourful mushroom-based main ( $28 ) , supported by surprisingly palatable seitan &amp;ldquo ; prosciutto , &amp;rdquo ; a saffron coconut sauce and pumpkin pur&amp;eacute ; e. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47553;TOOLONG ... title= " Mushrooms with seitan " prosciutto , " saffron coconut sauce and vegetables at 1 Elgin " alt= " Mushrooms with seitan " prosciutto , " saffron coconut sauce and vegetables at 1 Elgin " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47589;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480546 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Because we let 1 Elgin know in advance about birthday celebrants at our table , several appropriately decorated desserts were on the house . Tops was the dark chocolate tart ( $12 ) , especially with its sweet and grassy dollop of sweetgrass ice cream . Maple pumpkin spice cake ( $11 ) did less for us. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47619;TOOLONG ... title= " Dark chocolate tart with sweetgrass ice cream and bannock crumble at 1 Elgin " alt= " Dark chocolate tart with sweetgrass ice cream and bannock crumble at 1 Elgin " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47655;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480550 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47685;TOOLONG ... title= " Maple Pumpkin dessert at One Elgin , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Maple Pumpkin dessert at One Elgin , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47721;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480549 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since we were told when we arrived about those free desserts , we allocated that part of the budget on house cocktails ( $11 ) that used Canadian gin and were suitably complex and stiff . Some craft beers are on tap , and the wine list is lengthy , with most bottles in the $40 to $80 range and many wines available by the glass in five- and eight-ounce pours . To support the menu by Francis and Leier , 1 Elgin is also offering wines from Okanagan Valley&amp;rsquo ; s NK&amp;rsquo ; MIP Cellars , Canada&amp;rsquo ; s first Indigenous winery , although the menu consistently refers to &amp;ldquo ; MK&amp;rsquo ; MIP Cellars . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I won&amp;rsquo ; t dwell on the meals I had at Le Caf&amp;eacute ; this spring , except to say I liked them less because they seemed to have been prepared with less attentiveness and were more old-fashioned in terms of taste and appearance . Better dishes included a veal chop special and the beef tenderloin tartare , and the latter is still on the latest menu . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47751;TOOLONG ... title= " Duck confit at Le Cafe , spring 2019 " alt= " Duck confit at Le Cafe , spring 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47787;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480542 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47817;TOOLONG ... title= " Veal chop special at NAC 's Le Cafe , spring 2019 " alt= " Veal chop special at NAC 's Le Cafe , spring 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47853;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480541 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47883;TOOLONG ... title= " Beef tenderloin tartare at NAC 's Le Caf ? , spring 2019 " alt= " Beef tenderloin tartare at NAC 's Le Caf ? , spring 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47919;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480538 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;47949;TOOLONG ... title= " Scallops and pork belly at Le Caf ? , spring 2019 " alt= " Scallops and pork belly at Le Caf ? , spring 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;47985;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480537 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48015;TOOLONG ... title= " Seafood chowder at Le Cafe , spring 2019 " alt= " Seafood chowder at Le Cafe , spring 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48051;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1480523 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Soon after Leier was hired , he told this newspaper he wanted to raise the level of the food at the NAC&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant , which had received mixed reviews online . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The goal is to be a great restaurant , not an average one , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &amp;ldquo ; It won&amp;rsquo ; t happen overnight , but the effort will be there . &amp;rdquo ; Consistency , he said , was an issue , especially given that on show nights , 150 to 200 guests come for a quick meal before a Southam Hall concert . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Leier is on the right track , I think , with 1 Elgin&amp;rsquo ; s freshened surroundings and the best fare that he and Francis presented persuasively winning us over . We&amp;rsquo ; ll hope that the executive chef and future resident chefs keep up the good work . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;48081;TOOLONG " **32;48108;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;48142;TOOLONG ... Hum 's restaurant reviews&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 09 Oct 2019 15:00:12 **45;48177;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsFrom Michelin-starred plates to hot dogs , Chicago 's food satisfiedhttp : **31;48224;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;48257;TOOLONG ... title= " A fanciful culinary creation at Smyth , a restaurant with two Michelin stars in Chicago " alt= " A fanciful culinary creation at Smyth , a restaurant with two Michelin stars in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " Galdones Photography " **28;48293;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1477946 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the last decade , Michelin-starred fine dining in Chicago has become practically as iconic for food-lovers as the city&amp;rsquo ; s famed hot dogs , Italian beef sandwiches and deep-dish pizza . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Since the Michelin Guide began surveying sleek dishes in Chicago in 2011 , more than 40 restaurants in the city , from the world-famous avant-garde temple of gastronomy Alinea down , have been awarded much-coveted stars . The latest Michelin Guide for the city , released in late September , awards one or more stars to 25 restaurants . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While I didn&amp;rsquo ; t make it to three-starred Alinea on my recent trip to Chicago , I ate terrifically at several fine-dining destinations and neighbourhood restaurants alike . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At the two-Michelin-starred Smyth , a warm and gorgeous three-year-old restaurant in the West Loop that specializes in sleek tasting menus , the open kitchen turned out a succession of fascinating dishes made with ingredients from its dedicated farm . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48323;TOOLONG ... title= " Chefs at work at Smyth , a restaurant in Chicago with two Michelin stars " alt= " Chefs at work at Smyth , a restaurant in Chicago with two Michelin stars " data-portal-copyright= " Eugene ( Huge ) Galdones " **28;48359;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1477967 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Deconstructed gazpacho , caramelized halibut with double cream and toasted crab and aged lamb with grapes , garlic and a beef-fat brioche thrilled with refined flavours and precise technique . Recondite signature desserts combined milk chocolate ganache with seaweed oil and even bits of shiitake mushroom , or candied salted egg yolk with frozen yogurt meringue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48389;TOOLONG ... title= " Milk chocolate ganache with seawood , strawberry and shiitake mushroom , a fanciful culinary creation at Smyth , a restaurant in Chicago " alt= " Milk chocolate ganache with seawood , strawberry and shiitake mushroom , a fanciful culinary creation at Smyth , a restaurant in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " Eugene ( Huge ) Galdones " **28;48425;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1477943 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Less outr&amp;eacute ; but still deeply satisfying was a dinner at Sepia , a sophisticated West Loop spot with one Michelin star . There , succulent King salmon with white asparagus , rhubarb , sorrel , cashews and coconut , as well as slices of supremely beefy Wagyu tri-tip with Asian pear , maitake mushrooms and cabbages , were delightful main courses . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48455;TOOLONG ... title= " Wagyu beef tri-tip at Sepia in Chicago " alt= " Wagyu beef tri-tip at Sepia in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48491;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1478026 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Further north , in Lincoln Park , we enjoyed a deluxe dinner at the more casual but still one-Michelin-starred Boka . There , dry-aged duck breast with impeccably crispy skin was the stunner among many fine main courses . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48521;TOOLONG ... title= " Dry-aged duck breast at Boka in Chicago " alt= " Dry-aged duck breast at Boka in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48557;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1478027 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The hip Logan Square neighbourhood was worth a food-focused visit , thanks to the marvellous pork cutlet breakfast sandwich , beet bruschetta and epic carrot cake at Lula Caf&amp;eacute ; , a beloved farm-to-table haunt . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48587;TOOLONG ... title= " carrot cake at Lula Cafe in Chicago " alt= " carrot cake at Lula Cafe in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48623;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1478029 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Not far away , the funky , James Beard Award-winning eatery Fat Rice served some exceptional and unique Asian food including deeply savoury pork dumplings , massive chilli prawns and piri-piri chicken that seethed with incendiary goodness . What&amp;rsquo ; s more , around the corner from the Bakery at Fat Rice , where magnificent egg tarts and other baked treats can be bought to go . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48653;TOOLONG ... title= " Piri Piri chicken at Fat Rice in Chicago " alt= " Piri Piri chicken at Fat Rice in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48689;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1477948 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lunch at the Publican , a meat-forward , beer-hall-like place near the Fulton Market , started with a revelatory selection of top-notch hams , followed by massive portions of mussels and fries and the gastropub&amp;rsquo ; s famed roast chicken with fries . Don&amp;rsquo ; t miss pastry chef Erika Chan&amp;rsquo ; s swoon-worthy desserts. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48719;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken and fries at the Publican in Chicago " alt= " Chicken and fries at the Publican in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48755;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1478042 " /&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;48785;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;48827;TOOLONG ... vibrant theatre scene woos **32;48862;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our group also enjoyed fun , shareable meals at Sunda , a sleek Asian-fusion restaurant in River North that dispatched deluxe , opulent sushi rolls , at Tapas Valencia in the South Loop neighbourhood , where traditional Spanish small plates ruled , and at Two Lights Seafood &amp;amp ; Oyster in Old Town , an unpretentious place that served a lobster roll to make Maine proud . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48896;TOOLONG ... title= " succulent shrimp at Tapas Valencia in Chicago " alt= " succulent shrimp at Tapas Valencia in Chicago " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48932;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1478031 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If I absolutely had to have had a fine Chicago-style hot dog , garnished with mustard , relish , onion , Sport pepper , tomato , celery salt and pickle ) , an Italian beef sandwich and a deep-dish pizza , I would have taken one of the walking tours offered by Chicago Food Planet . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;48962;TOOLONG ... title= " cross-section of a Chicago-style hot dog , pic by Peter Hum , for 9999 chicago " alt= " cross-section of a Chicago-style hot dog , pic by Peter Hum , for 9999 chicago " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;48998;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1477950 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; And if pizza and nothing but pizza was my fixation in Chicago , then a tour organized by Pizza City USA and led by award-winning food journalist Steve Dolinsky would have done the trick . &lt;/p&gt;
