&lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 04 Oct 2019 18:06:46 **45;49174;TOOLONG ... Gusto Worldwide Media reboots Cook Like A Chef TV showhttp : **36;49221;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;49259;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef Katie Ardington. " alt= " Chef Katie Ardington. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;49295;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1191270 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Ottawa-based food network Gusto Worldwide Media is producing a new version of the series Cook Like A Chef , which originated 20 years ago and ran internationally for six seasons . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The original series bolstered the careers of Canadian chefs such as Michael Bonacini and Ned Bell , as well as Ottawa-area chefs Rene Rodriguez , Michael Cummings and Georges Laurier . More than 160 episodes were filmed , each dedicated to a single ingredient with a chef explaining cooking techniques , tips and tricks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Episodes of the original series still appear on the international food channel Gusto TV and can be streamed in the U.S. , United Kingdom , Latin America and Singapore . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The new version , now in production , will feature four chefs : Katie Ardington , executive chef of the Ottawa-based Beckta restaurant group and formerly the personal chef to Prime Minister Justin Trudeau and his family ; Montreal-based chefs Derek Dammann , owner of Maison Publique , and Natalia Machado ; and Toronto chef Craig Wong of the restaurant Patois Toronto . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shot in Gusto&amp;rsquo ; s east-end Ottawa studio , the new series will be available internationally on Gusto TV and the CTV Life Channel next year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; **33;49325;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;49360;TOOLONG ... City bassist Carl Mayotte finds inspiration in ' 70s musical weirdness&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;49395;TOOLONG ... tremors forced him to abandon the cello , founding NAC Orchestra member embraced vocals&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;49430;TOOLONG ... bets , Sept. 26 to Oct. 2 : Hintonburg 's Cinqhole opens , and a chocolate show&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 26 Sep 2019 18:20:12 **45;49465;TOOLONG ... ArtsVancouver star chef Vikram Vij regales sold-out crowd at Thalihttp : **35;49512;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;49549;TOOLONG ... title= " Vikram Vij on the floor during dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Vikram Vij on the floor during dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;49585;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469236 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We filed into Thali on Thursday night and received the first of many displays of effusive , personable hospitality from Vancouver celebrity chef Vikram Vij &amp;mdash ; a welcoming hug and the gift of a coloured scarf . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before long , almost 90 of us &amp;mdash ; a capacity crowd at the downtown restaurant of award-winning Ottawa chef Joe Thottungal &amp;mdash ; took seats at the long communal tables that are more typically&amp;nbsp ; with lunch-hour diners. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The evening&amp;rsquo ; s meal promised to be special , with Thottungal , who won silver at the 2017 Canadian Culinary Championships , and his friend Vij , a chef , cookbook author and television personality , teaming up to offer a creative six-course dinner that tapped North and South Indian cuisines for inspiration . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The evening was spirited and fun , in keeping with Vij&amp;rsquo ; s opening words : &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s going to be loud . It&amp;rsquo ; s going to make you feel like you&amp;rsquo ; re in India . &amp;rdquo ; Indeed , Vij , who mingled with guests and served them , was very much the life of the party . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;49615;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver celebrity chef Vikram Vij mingles with guests during dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19. " alt= " Vancouver celebrity chef Vikram Vij mingles with guests during dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;49651;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469272 " /&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;49681;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;49723;TOOLONG ... Thali 's distinctive , satisfying fare takes a minute to **25;49758;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;49785;TOOLONG ... Lagoon chef Joe Thottungal launches his first **29;49820;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;49851;TOOLONG ... Coconut Lagoon 's dishes now served in sleek , modern **28;49886;TOOLONG &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;49916;TOOLONG ... Joe Thottungal wins silver at the 2017 Canadian Culinary **34;49951;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;49987;TOOLONG ... title= " Vancouver celebrity chef Vikram Vij on the floor at Thali during dinner , Sept. 19/19. " alt= " Vancouver celebrity chef Vikram Vij on the floor at Thali during dinner , Sept. 19/19. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;50023;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469236 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Between courses , Thottungal and especially Vij addressed guests , speaking not only about their dishes but also about Indian culture at large and the warm reception that it has received in Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Who would have thought 29 years ago that you would be here , and 90 people would show up for an Indian meal ? &amp;rdquo ; Vij asked . &amp;ldquo ; Indian food was not considered delicious , &amp;rdquo ; he offered . At best , it was seen as &amp;ldquo ; ethnic , &amp;rdquo ; &amp;ldquo ; cheap , &amp;rdquo ; or buffet fare , Vij suggested . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dishes served at Thali rebutted those stereotypes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First up was Thottungal&amp;rsquo ; s tandoori romanesco with chutneys and plantain crisps. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50053;TOOLONG ... title= " Tandoori romanesco at Thali 's Vikram Vij dinner , Sept. 19/19. " alt= " Tandoori romanesco at Thali 's Vikram Vij dinner , Sept. 19/19. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;50089;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469340 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Cooked in a clay oven or not , the punchy vegetable starter was assertively flavoured , having been marinated for a day in tandoori spices . Tamarind and coriander chutneys offset its heat , which set taste buds pleasantly thrumming. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; " We have cooked food the way we like it , " Vij says . The dishes would not be punishingly spicy , he added . " The&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; idea is you eat it , and by the time you finish , you have a little sweat . It gradually grows on you . " &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The next dish , lobster mango moilee , a more luxurious spin on a richly sauced shrimp dish that Thottungal serves at Coconut Lagoon , his flagship Keralite restaurant in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s east end , was more rich and comforting , with eggplant softened with tomato , tamarind and caramelized onions and beets flecked with coconut adding breadth to the rich dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50119;TOOLONG ... title= " Lobster moilee at the Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " alt= " Lobster moilee at the Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;50155;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469237 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then came a memorable and delicious rice course , in which Thottungal had combined morsels of spiced , confit duck legs with short-grain rice cooked in the opulent style of Hyderabadi biryani. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50185;TOOLONG ... title= " Duck biryani at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " alt= " Duck biryani at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;50221;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469235 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Given the substitution of short-grain rice for longer-grained basmati , Vij called this dish &amp;ldquo ; Southern India meets Northern India . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; And this is Canada , &amp;rdquo ; he added . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Cuisines are like rivers . They need to flow constantly , &amp;rdquo ; Vij continued . &amp;ldquo ; Cooking is meant not to be a formula . It&amp;rsquo ; s meant to be a passion . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Vij did most of the talking , Thottungal did chime in . Noting Vij&amp;rsquo ; s entertaining ways , Thottungal said : &amp;ldquo ; This is why I bring him every year . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thottungal also pointed out that he and Vij had more in common than simply their country of origin . They both trained in Mumbai . They both started cooking at CP hotels when they came to Canada . They both had curly hair . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We are on speed dial now , &amp;rdquo ; Thottungal said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The meal&amp;rsquo ; s penultimate savoury course featured mahi mahi in a vibrant pollichathu preparation , with mushroom aviyal and dosa crisps accompanying . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50251;TOOLONG ... title= " Mahi Mahi at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " alt= " Mahi Mahi at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;50287;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469271 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If Vij&amp;rsquo ; s cooking was under-represented during the dinner until that point , all was forgiven when servers brought out plates of his signature lamb Popsicles with spinach , potatoes and garlic curry cream sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50317;TOOLONG ... title= " Lamb popsicles at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " alt= " Lamb popsicles at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;50353;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469239 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I wanted to rival butter chicken , &amp;rdquo ; Vij said of his famous dish . Furthermore , he said the dish was simply &amp;ldquo ; stolen from the French , &amp;rdquo ; given its combination of grilled meat , potatoes and cream . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After our lamb-sicles , I and my dinner companion were feeling pretty stuffed . Still , we got through dessert , which stacked refreshing kulfi ( India&amp;rsquo ; s own ice cream ) with carrot halwa , and nankhatai , a sort of biscuit . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50383;TOOLONG ... title= " Dessert at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19. " alt= " Dessert at Vikram Vij dinner at Thali , Sept. 19/19. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;50419;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1469238 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Thottungal might as well have been speaking about this dessert when he said : &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s a different thing . It refreshes our brains . &amp;rdquo ; Rather , Thottungal was talking about what these special-occasion meals meant to him and his staff . He has a meal planned to mark Mahatma Gandhi&amp;rsquo ; s birthday on Oct. 2 , but gave no further details . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vij closed with an appeal to diners to do their civic duty in the Oct. 21 federal election . &amp;ldquo ; Vote&amp;hellip ; it doesn&amp;rsquo ; t matter which party , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But there was a cause that he championed , &amp;ldquo ; Make sure no Indigenous person , no minority , is ever discriminated against , &amp;rdquo ; he said , prompting applause from the room before we and our scarves walked out into the night . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;50449;TOOLONG " **32;50476;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumFri , 20 Sep 2019 16:00:20 **45;50510;TOOLONG ... restaurant NeXT to hold fundraising dinner for Bahamas hurricane reliefhttp : **34;50557;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;50593;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef Michael Blackie at Next Restaurant , August 24 , 2018. " alt= " Chef Michael Blackie at Next Restaurant , August 24 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Jean Levac " **28;50629;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1261812 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Stittsville restaurant NeXT says it hopes to raise as much as $50,000 in support of a tiny , hurricane-ravaged Bahamian Island at an Oct. 4 charity dinner . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dinner will feature dishes by NeXT chef-owner Michael Blackie , Toronto chef Lora Kirk from Ruby Watch Co. , and two chefs from the Bahamas : Michael Howell , executive chef of the Green Turtle Club Resort and Marina and Tim Tibbitts , executive chef of Flying Fish Gastrobar in Freeport , Bahamas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Each chef will prepare one dish during the four-course dinner to be served that night . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Blackie said he has twice appeared at culinary events at the Green Turtle Club Resort and Marina on Green Turtle Cay , a tiny barrier island off of mainland Great Abaco in the Bahamas , which was devastated when Hurricane Dorian touched down earlier this month . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This little island and its residents hold a special place in my heart , &amp;rdquo ; he said . Kirk , too , has visited the Green Turtle Cay. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of the proceeds from the dinner at NeXT will be donated to the Green Turtle Cay/Hurricane Dorian Relief Fund , which through its &lt;a href= " https : **33;50659;TOOLONG ... has raised to date more than $555,000 of its $750,000 goal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dinner runs from 6 to 10 p.m. and tickets are $150 per person , including taxes and tip , available at nextfood.ca. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;50694;TOOLONG ... boss : Give the LRT system time to ' relax ' &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;50729;TOOLONG ... user 's guide : Everything you need to know about taking Ottawa 's LRT&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;50764;TOOLONG ... takes last crack at controversial Ch ? teau Laurier project as hotel seeks land variance&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 19 Sep 2019 14:33:41 **45;50800;TOOLONG ... NewsDining Out : Alice in Little Italy is a trailblazer , and should be recognized nationallyhttp : **30;50847;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;50879;TOOLONG ... title= " Briana Kim at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . " alt= " Briana Kim at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;50915;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462198 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **34;50945;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 40 Adeline St. , 613-733-0707 , &lt;a href= " http : //alicerestaurant.ca " &gt;alicerest ... &lt;br&gt; Open : Tuesday to Saturday 5:30 to 9:30 p.m. , closed Sunday and Monday &lt;br&gt; Prices : tasting menu $95 per person &lt;br&gt; Access : one step to gravelled area , steps with handrail to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The very first intrigue at Alice , the uniquely vegetable-focused fine-dining restaurant that chef-owner Briana Kim opened in June , involves assuring yourself that you have come to the right address . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span class= " s1 " &gt;Hers is the place on a modest residential side street in Little Italy where marc kitchen , and before that The Rex , had been . Kim , the self-taught , award-winning chef who represented Ottawa at the 2018 Canadian Culinary Championships , now presides inside a converted house with a new wood fa ? ade and a mini-greenhouse in front , but no sign of the restaurant 's name outside . &lt;/span&gt; To be clear , Alice is not the neighbouring property with an InfoWars sticker on its door , which has its own mysteries . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;50981;TOOLONG ... title= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The exterior of the restaurant . " alt= " Briana Kim with some of her dishes at Alice Restaurant on Adeline Street in Little Italy . The exterior of the restaurant . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;51017;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462204 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; When you enter Alice , the questions mount . Above all : What&amp;rsquo ; s in those jars ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; One wall of Alice&amp;rsquo ; s front dining area is a clear cabinet filled with almost three dozen jars with oddly hued contents . Looking more closely , you discover that they contain the slowly fermenting materials of Kim&amp;rsquo ; s cutting-edge cuisine &amp;mdash ; miso pastes made with sweet peas , chickpeas or sesame , vinegar-based creations involving crabapple flowers , Thai basil or spruce tips , concoctions made with jalape&amp;ntilde ; o , morels , reindeer moss and even pine cones . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fine-dining fans will know that fermentation is all the rage in the world&amp;rsquo ; s top kitchens , from global leader Noma in Copenhagen down . With her jars , Kim shows she has gone all-in on fermentation as she takes her culinary artistry to the next level . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Kim&amp;rsquo ; s previous and more casual restaurant , Caf&amp;eacute ; My House in Hintonburg , laid five years of groundwork for Alice . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But where Caf&amp;eacute ; My House , which Kim closed in January , was funky and dark , Alice , which seats 20 or so in its cosy , L-shaped , white-and-birch-coloured space , is ascetically spare . Only its jar-filled cabinet , a more artful wood wall that celebrates flowers in the rear dining area , the sleek bar that dispenses pricey but top-notch cocktails and the hubbub in the open kitchen distract from Kim&amp;rsquo ; s rarefied dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And where Caf&amp;eacute ; My House&amp;rsquo ; s plant-based offerings included brunches on weekends and five-course tasting menus at dinner , Alice aims higher , serving just an eight-course , $95 tasting menu that can be paired with natural wines or alcohol-free house-made beverages for $60 . ( Cocktails , Burdock beer and Revel cider are also available. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So , the investment and stakes are high for Kim&amp;rsquo ; s most uncompromising project to date . I can say , though , after my meal there Saturday night , that Alice served a singular and fascinating dinner that surpassed two tasting-menu experiences I had had at Caf&amp;eacute ; My House . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her food is supremely refined and arguably even virtuous , applying an ambitious chef&amp;rsquo ; s devotion to highly creative and technical culinary transformations in the service of veganism and locavorism . The results are frequently visually stunning , marvellously thoughtful and not only delicious but deliciously novel . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our dinner at Alice began with bread and snacks , with each counting as a course . But Kim&amp;rsquo ; s approach made clear that Alice means to transport guests to a culinary wonderland . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Slices of warm , perfect sourdough came with a candle , which turned out to be &amp;ldquo ; butter&amp;rdquo ; made from creamy fermented rice , which was softening thanks to a burning walnut &amp;ldquo ; wick . &amp;rdquo ; For snacks , servers brought something scarcely cooked ( a lightly charred summer radish , complete with its leaf , with cranberry bean miso that had been aged for three months ) and an exquisite little pastry ( a toasted sunflower seed and star anise butter tart with compressed plums and anise flowers ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Next came a bowl , and a table-side flourish . A sphere of coconut yogurt nestled with cranberry beans , and smoked , toasted and salted coconut flakes in a savoury broth . Kim then topped the dish with spoonfuls of an apparently supercooled powder flavoured with yuzu , the Japanese citrus fruit . Vapour , or probably nitrogen gas , billowed from the bowl . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As showy as the bowl was , it was even tastier . Bite after bite , it provided a refreshing play of sweetness , citrus , saltiness , textures and temperatures . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51047;TOOLONG ... title= " The dish seen here is Coconut , Cranberry Beans and Coffee . " alt= " The dish seen here is Coconut , Cranberry Beans and Coffee . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;51083;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462201 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Alice&amp;rsquo ; s elegant and visually arresting salad course will be very hard to displace from my memory . Kim had arranged assorted greens from Juniper Farms in a mini-bouquet , topped it with toasted flaxseed , other small , good things , and offset it with a ring of kohlrabi filled with sea buckthorn jelly . The salad&amp;rsquo ; s dressing was fermented house-made almond milk , added in another table-side manoeuvre . The dish was simply revelatory. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51113;TOOLONG ... title= " The dish seen here is Summer Bouquet . " alt= " The dish seen here is Summer Bouquet . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;51149;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462202 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The next two courses were increasingly savoury . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; First came a geometric arrangement of circles and squares that starred cherry tomatoes , precisely topped with a red pepper-jalapeno salsa , lacto-fermented basil and toasted freekeh , contrasted with a crisp , perforated smoked garlic chip and a ponzu-flavoured gel , as well as a fennel-spiced &amp;ldquo ; butter&amp;rdquo ; powder . If I&amp;rsquo ; m right , the dish also included just enough subtle Sichuan-pepper-based oil to leave the faintest tingle on the tongue . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51179;TOOLONG ... title= " The dish seen here is Tomato and Ponzu. " alt= " The dish seen here is Tomato and Ponzu. " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;51215;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462217 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Eggplant was the star of the final savoury course , cooked so as to coax out its meaty mouthfeel . The slabs of eggplant were garnished with a variety of pickles and came with a deeply flavoured cucumber-based sauce . Nudging the dish over the top was a crisp ribbon that packed crunch and umami , I believe from a dusting of sea-vegetable powder . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51245;TOOLONG ... title= " Eggplant with cucumber sauce at Alice " alt= " Eggplant with cucumber sauce at Alice " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51281;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462219 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; With these dishes , I had Alice&amp;rsquo ; s alcohol-free pairings and was struck by their quality and even ingenuity . Among those drinks were glasses or cups of blackberry , hibiscus and mint kombucha , lime leaf lemonade , a warm , clear , super-savoury tomato tea , and a cool cucumber-ginger-cilantro tonic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The first of two desserts combined house-made silken tofu with viscous elderberry honey and a scattering of fresh and freeze dried strawberries . It was good on its own but better still with its pairing , the strawberry sour topped with a vanilla and tonka bean &amp;ldquo ; meringue . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51311;TOOLONG ... title= " Strawberry , silken tofu and elderflower honey dessert at Alice " alt= " Strawberry , silken tofu and elderflower honey dessert at Alice " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51347;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462218 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The knockout dessert was the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s riff on an ice cream sandwich , that placed a quenelle of fermented-rice-based &amp;ldquo ; ice cream&amp;rdquo ; between shards of hyper-crisp oat cake , buttressed by berries in a charred pine cone syrup . Although I&amp;rsquo ; m no fan of licorice , the licorice anise soda paired well with the dessert . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51377;TOOLONG ... title= " " ice cream sandwich " dessert at Alice " alt= " " ice cream sandwich " dessert at Alice " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51413;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1462226 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Our meal ended with a mignardise &amp;mdash ; a house-made mint chocolate candy &amp;mdash ; and a keepsake copy of the menu , which abbreviated its descriptions of the dishes . Kim told us before we left that dishes come on and off the menu continually , and that roughly every six weeks there&amp;rsquo ; s a new menu , to the delight of Alice&amp;rsquo ; s repeat customers . Given that long-fermenting ingredients are involved , Kim is already planning winter dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In all , Alice packed a striking amount of pleasant stimulation into our 95 minutes there . In fact , it&amp;rsquo ; s possible the kitchen and staff were a little too efficient . Our meal could have felt a touch more relaxed in terms of pacing and delivery , and even more warm . But then , Kim and her team knew once I arrived that I was there in a critical capacity , and perhaps they switched to an overly professional , too-cool mode . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; And for all the details they dispensed , Kim and her servers could have told us a bit more about the thinking behind the dishes &amp;mdash ; the &amp;ldquo ; why&amp;rdquo ; as well as the &amp;ldquo ; what . &amp;rdquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve experienced that kind of curated dining at a Michelin-starred place or two , and found it gave a fuller appreciation of the food and the people who made it , enriching our experience and justifying the lofty prices . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Still , Alice serves notice that Kim is not simply a fine chef on Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s arguably under-appreciated dining scene , but an important culinary trailblazer worthy of recognition nationally and beyond . There are remarkable dishes to be discovered and demystified here now , and I expect there will be many more in the future . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;51443;TOOLONG " **32;51470;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 11 Sep 2019 20:33:52 **45;51504;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsCanada 's first kosher barbecue cook-off yields delicious winners in Barrhavenhttp : **31;51551;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;51584;TOOLONG ... title= " John Thomson shows off turkey entries at the Kosher BBC Competition at the Ottawa Torah Centre , Sept. 8 , 2019 " alt= " John Thomson shows off turkey entries at the Kosher BBC Competition at the Ottawa Torah Centre , Sept. 8 , 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51620;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1460815 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; On Sunday afternoon , Barrhaven once again lived up to its nickname &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; barbecue-haven. &amp;rdquo ; But this time , the event that drew hundreds to the Ottawa Torah Centre was a Canadian first . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The crowd , which included foodies from not just Ottawa but also Montreal , Toronto and New York , came for the first Ottawa Kosher BBQ Cook-Off and Festival , organized by the OTC&amp;rsquo ; s Rabbi Menachem M. Blum . While summer kosher barbecue cook-offs and Jewish food festivals are well-established in the United States , there was no Canadian precedent for the rabbi&amp;rsquo ; s event . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , in May , Barrhaven played host to Canada&amp;rsquo ; s biggest barbecue competition , when the fourth annual Capital BBQ Festival drew more than 40 professional barbecue teams from Ontario , Quebec and the Northeastern U.S. , vying to be that festival&amp;rsquo ; s grand champion . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Capital BBQ Festival&amp;rsquo ; s organizer , John Thomson , was on hand at Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s kosher event to run its centrepiece competition . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51650;TOOLONG ... title= " Ottawa pitmaster John Thomson shows off turkey entries to judges at the first Ottawa Kosher BBQ Cook-Off and Festival at the Ottawa Torah Centre on Sept. 8 , 2019. " alt= " Ottawa pitmaster John Thomson shows off turkey entries to judges at the first Ottawa Kosher BBQ Cook-Off and Festival at the Ottawa Torah Centre on Sept. 8 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51686;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1460815 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Sunday&amp;rsquo ; s contest featured seven barbecue teams squaring off in three categories ( chicken , turkey and brisket , all in keeping with a kosher diet ) and submitting their entries to a panel of five judges . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The event also allowed the public to buy tickets to sample the teams&amp;rsquo ; cooking and award a people&amp;rsquo ; s choice award . As well , a kosher food court served pulled brisket sandwiches , corned beef sandwiches , Moroccan burgers , hot dogs , veggie burgers , knishes , fries , salads and drinks as well as popcorn and cotton candy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was one of the event&amp;rsquo ; s judges , and can tell you that not only is kosher barbecue a thing &amp;mdash ; it&amp;rsquo ; s a delicious thing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I came to the event with the bonafides of having judged during the last five years at more than 20 competitions in Ontario , Quebec and New York state that were sanctioned by the Kansas City Barbecue Society . At those events , chicken and brisket feature , but so do pork ribs and pork shoulder , which can frequently appear as pulled pork . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Naturally , I was keen to see and taste the kosher approach to barbecue , and I along with my fellow judges would apply the same criteria used to assess KCBS entries , scrutinizing each piece of meat for appearance , taste and tenderness . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here&amp;rsquo ; s what I judged in the chicken category : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51716;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken entries at Canada 's first kosher barbecue cook-off , held in Barrhaven on Sept. 8/2019 " alt= " Chicken entries at Canada 's first kosher barbecue cook-off , held in Barrhaven on Sept. 8/2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51752;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1460873 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The chicken entries yielded an enjoyably diverse range of flavours . Over the years , KCBS competitions have seen the flavour profiles that teams present narrow practically to the point of homogeneity , and I was happy to see variety . The chicken thigh in the lower left was the most &amp;ldquo ; KCBS&amp;rdquo ; entry in terms of looks and taste , given its sweet , sticky glaze . Other entries were relatively un-sauced and different spices came into play . In 20 KCBS competitions , I&amp;rsquo ; ve never come across a curry-tinged chicken entry like the one on the bottom right , which I nonetheless thought quite highly of . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Here are entries in the turkey category : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51782;TOOLONG ... title= " Turkey entries at the Kosher BBC Competition at the Ottawa Torah Centre , Sept. 8 , 2019 " alt= " Turkey entries at the Kosher BBC Competition at the Ottawa Torah Centre , Sept. 8 , 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51818;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1460876 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I was a little surprised not to receive any dark meat in this category , not that I have anything against well-cooked turkey breast . I just think that it might have been easier for dark-meat entries to score higher on tenderness , and indeed , some of these turkey entries were a little dry . The &amp;ldquo ; pulled turkey&amp;rdquo ; was really very good , though , and I also was partial to the entry beside it that went big with roasted garlic and herbs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The competition saved the best for last : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;51848;TOOLONG ... title= " Brisket entries at Canada 's first kosher barbecue competition at the Ottawa Torah Centre on Sept. 8 , 2019 " alt= " Brisket entries at Canada 's first kosher barbecue competition at the Ottawa Torah Centre on Sept. 8 , 2019 " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;51884;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1460814 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Beef brisket enjoys a reputation as one of the hardest meats to barbecue well , and when things go wrong , that expensive cut can be rendered inedibly tough and dry . Also , even pro teams at KCBS events can turn in brisket that&amp;rsquo ; s punishingly salty or even bouillon-y . ( The competitors are , it should be said , trying to wow judges who take at most two bites of meat , with punchy , flavour-forward samples. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; However , the level of brisket at the kosher event was strikingly high . I certainly have had much worse brisket at KCBS events . The best brisket samples on Sunday had notable succulence , fine beefiness and a balance of smoke and seasoning . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We judged our entries without knowing which teams had submitted them , as per the KCBS rules . It was only on Sunday night that I learned that the competition&amp;rsquo ; s grand champion was the Irving Rivers team . In the individual categories , the top finishers were : &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Chicken : 1st : Coal Nidrei ( Ottawa Jewish Community School ) . 2nd : Big Macher BBQ ( Camp Bnai Brith Ottawa ) . 3rd : Irving Rivers . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Turkey : 1st : Big Macher BBQ ( Camp Bnai Brith Ottawa ) . 2nd : Irving Rivers . 3rd : Up in Smoke . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Brisket : 1st : Coal Nidrei ( Ottawa Jewish Community School ) . 2nd : Irving Rivers . 3rd : Up in Smoke . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All seven teams this year were from Ottawa . &amp;ldquo ; We had no financial prizes as it was our first year , just recognition with medals and trophies , &amp;rdquo ; Blum said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Congratulations to all who cooked . I look forward to sampling your kosher barbecue again when the cook-off returns next year . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;51914;TOOLONG " **32;51941;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //instagram.com/peterhum " &gt;insta ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;51975;TOOLONG ... Trucks are the new family ride . What 's happened to us ? &lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumMon , 09 Sep 2019 14:57:59 **45;52080;TOOLONG ... Out : Some pretty dishes bloomed , others wilted at Tulip Restauranthttp : **30;52127;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;52159;TOOLONG ... title= " Cornish hen with polenta at Tulip " alt= " Cornish hen with polenta at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52195;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1451835 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tulip Restaurant &amp; Bar&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 361 Queen St. ( inside the Hilton Garden Inn Ottawa Downtown ) , 613-234-6363 , &lt;a href= " http : //tulipottawa.com " &gt;tulipottawa. ... &lt;br&gt; **33;52225;TOOLONG : Daily from noon to 2:30 p.m. and 5:30 to 9:30 p.m. , also weekday breakfasts from 6:30 to 10:30 a.m. , weekend breakfasts 7 to 11 a.m. &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; appetizers $7.95 to $21.95 , mains $17.95 to $29.95 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; no steps to front door , washrooms &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the fall of 2017 , when David Vinoya was the chef of Wild Sage Kitchen &amp;amp ; Bar in Regina , he won that city&amp;rsquo ; s Gold Medal Plates competition after wowing judges with a striking dish that he had enigmatically dubbed &amp;ldquo ; The Nest . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Equal parts still life and small plate , it was , in the words of head judge James Chatto , &amp;ldquo ; sensationally beautiful . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Vinoya&amp;rsquo ; s dish , watercress became a gel . Black olives were turned into powder and beets were made into &amp;ldquo ; soil . &amp;rdquo ; Molecular gastronomy turned chicken into a crisp tuile . The dish&amp;rsquo ; s star was a sphere of ice cream made with the yolks of eggs that had been cured by soaking them for a month in mud and salt , as is done in the Philippines , where Vinoya was born and raised . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These days , Vinoya cooks in Ottawa . Wild Sage was inside a DoubleTree by Hilton hotel , and more than a year ago , Vinoya moved here to become the executive chef at the Hilton Garden Inn Ottawa Downtown , where Tulip Restaurant and Bar lies beyond its Queen Street entrance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; This summer , I had two dinners and a lunch at Tulip , hoping for some medal-winning brilliance . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tulip&amp;rsquo ; s menus definitely intrigued . Vinoya has a wide-roaming curiosity about food , so that his multicultural menu makes stops in Japan , Italy , China , the Middle East , the Philippines and the rest of Canada , sometimes mashing up influences from here and there . Among the menu&amp;rsquo ; s details are ingredients such as crab , sea urchin , bacon jam , black garlic aioli , and truffle oil &amp;mdash ; the stuff that makes connoisseurs and especially umami-lovers salivate. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In line with Chatto&amp;rsquo ; s kudos , Vinoya&amp;rsquo ; s plating is definitely a strong point . His dishes were usually pretty and exuberant , if on occasion a touch contrived . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But on the whole , our meals were uneven . Some dishes were interesting , solidly made and satisfying . Others were just OK and a few let us down . For too many dishes , some components had little to no impact , making you wonder if simpler food would not have been better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; My second dinner there was markedly my best experience at Tulip , and I wondered if who was cooking in the kitchen is as significant as what we ordered . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At my first visit , a weekday lunch , we had several appetizers that were not as tasty as they looked . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Three steamed pork belly buns ( $11.95 ) were presented like treasures on a black stone slab in a wooden tray , with a small salad and dipping sauce on the side . But visuals aside , the bao buns needed work . The too-dry pork belly sorely lacked succulence . The odd move of dredging the buns in creole seasoning added significant heat , but unpleasantly so . The sweet-salty sauce on the side would have been better inside the bun . Overall , the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s twists on a classic snack did not improve it . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52260;TOOLONG ... title= " Bao buns at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street , " alt= " Bao buns at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street , " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52296;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1451340 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Spicy tuna ( $13.95 ) arrived atop oblongs of deep-fried rice and , more ostentatiously , on a bed of rocks in its bowl . It was not bad , but would have been better had its rice been less crisp . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52326;TOOLONG ... title= " Spicy Tuna appetizer at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street " alt= " Spicy Tuna appetizer at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52362;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450611 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Fried flatbread ( $12.95 ) topped with mushrooms , truffle oil , whipped goat cheese , a cloud of parmesan and pea shoots was tasty , although the bread did feel heavy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52392;TOOLONG ... title= " Mushroom flatbread at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street " alt= " Mushroom flatbread at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52428;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1451337 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Lunch&amp;rsquo ; s heftiest item was Tulip&amp;rsquo ; s doughnut-burger hybrid ( $18.95 ) , seemingly built for Instagram or for eating on a dare . Perhaps predictably , it was too massive and dulce-de-leche-drizzled to be eaten by hand , which to me is a big point of eating a hamburger . In all , this burger struck me as overladen . ( To be fair , Tulip offers a &amp;ldquo ; standard&amp;rdquo ; burger and six sandwiches that are more streamlined. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52458;TOOLONG ... title= " Sweet and savoury doughnut burger and salad at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street " alt= " Sweet and savoury doughnut burger and salad at Tulip restaurant in the Hilton Garden Inn on Queen Street " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52494;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450567 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At a weekend dinner , three dishes were meh , while a fourth stood out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The texture of a large slab of pan-fried falafel ( $14.95 ) varied from cake-y to moist to mushy , and it was a little bland . The accompanying salad , which featured a lemon br&amp;ucirc ; l&amp;eacute ; e peanut chutney , arugula , red cabbage , avocado herb sauce , cured onions and sumac vinaigrette , could have been edited , as not every element sang . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52524;TOOLONG ... title= " Falafel and salad at Tulip " alt= " Falafel and salad at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52560;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1453857 " /&gt; &lt;div class= " item-title-row " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Of two mains , best was a&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; tasty , if oily , spaghetti dish dubbed " porkuni " ( $23.95 ) . &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; Billed as pasta with bacon , a sea urchin and crab sauce , dried seaweed , herb pistou " jus , " sesame seeds , pork crackling crumbs and nori , this dish too could have been simplified . Above all , the pasta felt heavy on the bacon and seaweed but light on other garnishes , and the presence of sea urchin ( uni ) and crab was more of a savoury aftertaste than something explicit . **25;52590;TOOLONG &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52617;TOOLONG ... title= " Spaghetti with bacon , crab and uni at Tulip Restaurant " alt= " Spaghetti with bacon , crab and uni at Tulip Restaurant " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52653;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450561 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;p&gt; A corn meal-crusted filet of sea bream ( $27.95 ) , a mild Mediterranean fish , had three more pin bones that we would have liked . I liked even more the indulgence of chicharron ( $18.95 ) , its fresh-tasting and light fried pork rinds topped as if they were nachos. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52815;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicharron at Tulip " alt= " Chicharron at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52851;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1452141 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tulip&amp;rsquo ; s pork chop ( $28.95 ) was big , juicy and worth returning for , and the veg on the plate &amp;mdash ; sweet potato pur&amp;eacute ; e , mushrooms , broccolini &amp;mdash ; hit the mark . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52881;TOOLONG ... title= " Sous-vide cooked pork chop at Tulip " alt= " Sous-vide cooked pork chop at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52917;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1454780 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A half cornish hen ( $26.95 ) impressed with its moist flesh , although its glaze was a touch too sweet and its polenta was too salty . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;52947;TOOLONG ... title= " Cornish hen with polenta at Tulip " alt= " Cornish hen with polenta at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;52983;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1451822 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Vinoya&amp;rsquo ; s dish called &amp;ldquo ; Anarchy&amp;rdquo ; ( $ &lt;span class= " price " &gt;24.95 ) , was a ( cleanly organized ) plate of seafood pasta . Squid ink pasta topped with pea shoots sat beside clams , scallops and tiny shrimp in a red pepper sauce . The dish was fine and its seafood was tender , but it was a little under-seasoned. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53013;TOOLONG ... title= " " Anarchy " seafood bpasta at Tulip " alt= " " Anarchy " seafood bpasta at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53049;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1454785 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; That night , desserts were a highlight . A block of Minoya&amp;rsquo ; s calling-card salted egg ice cream ( $8.95 ) was luscious and scarcely salty . The &amp;ldquo ; tree of sweetness&amp;rdquo ; dessert ( $10.95 ) nodded to the restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s name , planting purple cotton candy in rich , chocolate-soil-topped chocolate mousse . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53079;TOOLONG ... title= " Coconut pudding at Tulip " alt= " Coconut pudding at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53115;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1451824 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53145;TOOLONG ... title= " Salted egg ice cream at Tulip " alt= " Salted egg ice cream at Tulip " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53181;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1451826 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53211;TOOLONG ... title= " Desserts : salted egg ice cream , Tree of sweetness , coconut pudding at Tulip , " alt= " Desserts : salted egg ice cream , Tree of sweetness , coconut pudding at Tulip , " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53247;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1451825 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tulip includes both a bar and a more restrained dining area that we understood was more for breakfast guests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As last week&amp;rsquo ; s dinner finished , Vinoya emerged from the kitchen to personably check in with guests about their meals . We responded that we&amp;rsquo ; d enjoyed our food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I wish I could have told him the same about my two other visits . Billy Talent say MacLeod tweet ' misrepresents ' meeting over funding cuts&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 28 Aug 2019 21:04:54 **45;53443;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : No-frills grills at Suya Palace and African Grill turn out tasty , highly seasoned meathttp : **36;53490;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;53528;TOOLONG ... title= " Beef suya combo from Suya Palace " alt= " Beef suya combo from Suya Palace " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53564;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450368 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Suya Palace&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 460 Bronson Ave. , 613-619-4022 , &lt;a href= " http : **30;53594;TOOLONG " &gt; ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Friday 3 to 9 p.m. , Saturday 3 to 10 p.m. , Sunday 3 to 7:30 p.m. , closed Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; dishes up to $17.99 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; step to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Suya Joint / African Grill&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 1383 Clyde Ave. , 613-225-8584 , &lt;a href= " http : //africangrills.com " &gt;africangri ... , &lt;a href= " http : //suya-joint.com " &gt;suya-joint.co ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Monday 3 to 9 p.m. , Tuesday to Thursday noon to 9 p.m. , Friday and Saturday noon to midnight , Sunday 1 to 8 p.m. &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; up to $20 for dishes &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; no steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the real world , my travels to Africa have been scant . Decades ago , I was in Morocco for an afternoon . More recently , I spent a few days playing tourist in Cape Town and its surrounding winelands , which some say hardly represents the continent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All that to say , these days I&amp;rsquo ; m feeling I&amp;rsquo ; ve had more revelatory encounters with the cultures of African countries during my visits to Ottawa restaurants that serve their national dishes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most recently , I&amp;rsquo ; ve been eating and enjoying the meat-forward fare from Suya Palace in Centretown , which serves some key Nigerian dishes and especially the grilled items called , as you would guess , suya. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve also visited &amp;mdash ; and been pleased by some dishes at &amp;mdash ; Suya Joint/African Grill , which is tucked away in a Clyde Avenue strip mall . There , a chef from Lesotho serves his version of suya , plus the barbecue style known as braai , which is native to South Africa , which happens to encircle Lesotho . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These modest eateries share a dedication to authentic flavours that rely on spices imported from Africa . The dishes I liked most , and even some that I cared less for , were well-seasoned and deeply savoury . It did seem that both restaurants had been discovered by African expats. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Suya Palace , which this spring replaced African Slow Food on Bronson Avenue near Gladstone Avenue , is a tiny place more geared to take-out orders , although it does seat about 10 people . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Best here were orders of chicken and beef suya . Both were beguilingly seasoned and tasty , although those with peanut allergies should know that peanuts are usually included in suya marinades. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The chicken was all bone-in , a thigh and two drumsticks , and it was sufficiently moist , if not juicy , after its time on Suya Palace&amp;rsquo ; s charcoal grill . Beef suya was tender , if not moist . Goat suya was flavourful , but its toughness turned eating into exercise for our jaws . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53626;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken suya at Suya Palace " alt= " Chicken suya at Suya Palace " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53662;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450582 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53692;TOOLONG ... title= " Beef suya combo with plantain , jollof rice and cole slaw from Suya Palace " alt= " Beef suya combo with plantain , jollof rice and cole slaw from Suya Palace " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53728;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450368 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53758;TOOLONG ... title= " Goat suya from Suya Palace " alt= " Goat suya from Suya Palace " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53794;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450361 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Suya Palace&amp;rsquo ; s counter , there&amp;rsquo ; s a small metal bin filled with orange powder . Although the cook that took your order already asked you how spicy you wanted your suya , he will still add a few spoonfuls of that orange powder , which turns out to be a made-in-Nigeria chilli powder that adds some fierce , persistent , swelling heat . Try some . It&amp;rsquo ; s good . But don&amp;rsquo ; t say you weren&amp;rsquo ; t warned . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Less pleasing at Suya Palace was its suya-spiced grilled quarter chicken , only because its white meat was dry . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53824;TOOLONG ... title= " Quarter chicken with plantain from Suya Palace " alt= " Quarter chicken with plantain from Suya Palace " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53860;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450364 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I can recommend Suya Palace&amp;rsquo ; s jollof rice , a Nigerian side dish that was moist , tomato-y , well-seasoned and markedly smoky . If you order plantain here , the serving will be generous , but also very chunky and a little more firm than I like . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Several Nigerian soups complete Suya Palace&amp;rsquo ; s wall-placard menu . Knowing next to nothing about it , I tried the ogbonna okra soup , and found I could not get past its mucilaginous sticky-slimy texture . Had I simply consulted Wikipedia first , I would have known better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53890;TOOLONG ... title= " Ogbonna okra soup at Suya Palace " alt= " Ogbonna okra soup at Suya Palace " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53926;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450560 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurant is not licensed . Apart from basic soft drinks , it also offers vitamalt , the non-alcoholic Caribbean malt beverage . Its meat is halal. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Suya Joint/African Grill , which opened late last year , chef-owner Koele Khutlang , a native of Lesotho , serves his take on suya , which presents its beef , goat , chicken and gizzards in smaller morsels , closer to the street-food roots of the dish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The suya here is heavily seasoned due its marinating , and smoky , too . But it&amp;rsquo ; s not as fiery as Suya Palace&amp;rsquo ; s suya . Khutlang says his suya recipe is &amp;ldquo ; 90 per cent authentic . My suya is two countries mixed , Nigeria and Cameroon . &amp;rdquo ; Best was the toothsome goat suya . Assorted suya , a sort of chef&amp;rsquo ; s choice , included chewier bits of offal and firm , meaty gizzards . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;53956;TOOLONG ... title= " Goat suya with plantains and jollof rice at African Grill , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Goat suya with plantains and jollof rice at African Grill , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;53992;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450566 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;54022;TOOLONG ... title= " Assorted suya at African Grill " alt= " Assorted suya at African Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;54058;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450369 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The crowd-pleaser here was Khutlang&amp;rsquo ; s braai , which he says he&amp;rsquo ; s been working on for two decades . Chicken &lt;span class= " markp6x4sljmo " &gt;braai was rounded in flavour , markedly but&lt;/span&gt; &amp;nbsp ; not overpoweringly spiced , charbroiled and then glazed with a sweet-salty finish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;54088;TOOLONG ... title= " Chicken Braai at African Grill " alt= " Chicken Braai at African Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;54124;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450362 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Khutlang notes that peanuts figure in his suya preparation , but not in his braai , and he says he runs his kitchen to try to isolate food that comes into contact with peanuts . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The fries here were very good , but the jollof rice was dry and a little bitter . &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Khutlang sometimes has moi moi , a savoury Nigerian bean pudding , available as a special . We ordered it and were counselled that it would be very spicy . It was not . But it was tasty , with a salty-fishy undertone. &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;54154;TOOLONG ... title= " Nigerian bean pudding ( moi moi ) at African Grill " alt= " Nigerian bean pudding ( moi moi ) at African Grill " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;54190;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1450367 " /&gt; &lt;div class= " cellCell-g0fptp-0 jWrvEb " &gt; &lt;p&gt;Khutlang says he wants to expand the offerings at Suya Joint/African Grill to offer nyama choma , a Kenyan preparation for grilled meat . When that happens , I 'll be curious enough to return . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href= " **25;54220;TOOLONG " **47;54247;TOOLONG href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... href= " https : **33;54296;TOOLONG ...
