Food ReviewsWhat makes restaurant food ' authentic , ' and who gets to decide ? http : **33;54610;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;54645;TOOLONG ... title= " Coconut Lagoon 's award-winning owner/chef Joe Thottungal prepares a delicious salmon curry and talks about the importance of authenticity in cooking his Indian dishes . He imports the spices and root ingredients from his homeland and even his chefs are from Kerala in South India , where he 's from . " alt= " Coconut Lagoon 's award-winning owner/chef Joe Thottungal prepares a delicious salmon curry and talks about the importance of authenticity in cooking his Indian dishes . He imports the spices and root ingredients from his homeland and even his chefs are from Kerala in South India , where he 's from . " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;54681;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446060 " /&gt; &lt;iframe width= " 100% " height= " 100% " frameborder= " 0 " allowfullscreen= " true " src= " https : **34;54711;TOOLONG ... &gt; &lt;/iframe&gt; &lt;p&gt; Par Chiturai was beaming . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On a Wednesday night in late July , the Ottawa businesswoman&amp;rsquo ; s restaurant , Khao Thai on Murray Street , was throwing a milestone dinner . Thailand&amp;rsquo ; s ambassador to Canada was there , as were staff from the Thai Trade Centre in Toronto and some guests from Global Affairs Canada . One table was filled with young , social media-savvy foodies keen to propagate photos of green mango salad and chu chee prawns on Instagram. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The deluxe dinner was called , somewhat belatedly , to celebrate Khao Thai&amp;rsquo ; s certification of authenticity from the Royal Thai Government . Opened in 2004 , Khao Thai last year received its Thai Select designation from Thailand&amp;rsquo ; s Ministry of Commerce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Khao Thai applied for the certification &amp;ldquo ; to show that we are truly Thai , &amp;rdquo ; Chiturai said that night in an interview . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;54747;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;54789;TOOLONG ... ' authentic ' Chinese **28;54824;TOOLONG &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Thai Select program assesses cooking methods in restaurant kitchens and verifies their use of Thai ingredients and food products . &amp;ldquo ; At least 60 per cent of dishes offered on the menu must be authentic Thai cuisine and the cooking methods must be the same or very similar to those in Thailand , &amp;rdquo ; says the Thai Select website. &amp;ldquo ; Head chefs may be either Thai or other nationalities . In case of non-Thai head chefs , the chefs must produce proof of at least a two-year experience in cooking Thai foods or possess a Thai cuisine training certificate from an accredited institution . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;54854;TOOLONG ... title= " Par Chiturai , owner of Khao Thai Restaurant in the ByWard Market area . Khao Thai recently celebrated receiving its " Thai Select " certification of authenticity from the Thai Government , which runs this program for Thai restaurants abroad . " alt= " Par Chiturai , owner of Khao Thai Restaurant in the ByWard Market area . Khao Thai recently celebrated receiving its " Thai Select " certification of authenticity from the Thai Government , which runs this program for Thai restaurants abroad . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;54890;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446058 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The program also considers hospitality and atmosphere among its criteria , and Khao Thai certainly qualifies as one of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s more posh Thai eateries when compared to more humble , mom-and-pop places . Also , according to the Thai Select website , an official from the Thai Trade Office would have visited Khao Thai unannounced and evaluated the dining experience . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Having met the Thai government&amp;rsquo ; s standards , Khao Thai joins about 1,000 other Thai Select eateries around the world , of which 100 are in Canada . Three other Ottawa restaurants &amp;mdash ; Pookie&amp;rsquo ; s Thai on Carling Avenue , Talay Thai in Centretown and Thai Lanna Cuisine in South Keys &amp;mdash ; are similarly designated . So too are Nakhon Thai in Kemptville and Thum&amp;rsquo ; s Kitchen in Cornwall . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; To enjoy Thai dining , you don&amp;rsquo ; t actually need to be in Thailand . But how can you ensure that you get the real experience ? Simply look for the &amp;lsquo ; Thai Select&amp;rsquo ; logo from ( the ) Royal Thai Government , &amp;rdquo ; the Thai Select program&amp;rsquo ; s website says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Receiving this prestigious award isn&amp;rsquo ; t easy . The bar is set high and the guidelines are strict , &amp;rdquo ; the website says . &amp;ldquo ; Still , through training and inspections , high standard Thai restaurants worldwide have achieved this distinction . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; All that said , Chiturai said her restaurant makes concessions to Canadian palates : &amp;ldquo ; Here we try to keep it authentic , but sometimes Canadians , they can not take the real spice . We have to reduce the spiciness , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;54920;TOOLONG ... title= " Mango with sticky rice at Khao Thai Restaurant in the ByWard Market area . " alt= " Mango with sticky rice at Khao Thai Restaurant in the ByWard Market area . " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;54956;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446067 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; *** &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For many restaurateurs and restaurant-goers , the notion of authentic cuisine is prized , and even synonymous with quality . If you&amp;rsquo ; ve had it with ketchup-y pad Thai , you know what I mean . If you recoil at Hawaiian pizza and think thin-crust Neapolitan pizza is the one true style by which all pizza should be judged , you concur . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In my reviews of restaurants , I&amp;rsquo ; ve used the word &amp;ldquo ; authentic&amp;rdquo ; scores of times , and always with a positive intention . There are , I think , food traditions worth upholding , which in Canada have often been transported from the Old World to the New World and which speak to the preservation of not only what tastes good , but also of the culture from which the food came . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There&amp;rsquo ; s something admirable about the Thai Select program , or the Associazione Verace Pizza Napoletana , which translates to the True Neapolitan Pizza Association and counts among its members more than 800 pizzerias in Italy and beyond , none of which are in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I would also agree that some inauthentic food could only be considered flops . I&amp;rsquo ; ve had pintxos , the gourmet-level bar snacks of Spain&amp;rsquo ; s Basque region , that were heavenly in their homeland , while crude representations of that kind of cooking in the Ottawa area missed the point utterly . I&amp;rsquo ; d rather skip refrigerated sushi that not only flouts the Japanese standard calling for sushi rice to be warm , at room temperature or even body temperature . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There have also been recent cases in which culinary authenticity , or the apparent lack thereof , became a cause c&amp;eacute ; l&amp;ecirc ; bre , not simply because of ingredients or recipes , but in connection with who was doing the cooking or who owned the restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In more than a few cases , the same questions kept reverberating . What makes food authentic ? And who gets to decide ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On June , when Shook , a Toronto restaurant that touted itself as serving &amp;ldquo ; modern Israeli cuisine , &amp;rdquo ; opened , some critics on Twitter contended that its fare was Palestinian rather than Israeli , and that the restaurant was guilty of culinary appropriation. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;blockquote class= " twitter-tweet " data-width= " 550 " data-dnt= " true " &gt;&lt;p lang= " en " dir= " ltr " &gt;There 's a new " Israeli " restaurant in Toronto , **27;54986;TOOLONG chef is n't Israeli&lt;br&gt;*No Israelis made up the menu&lt;br&gt;*The name is taken from Arabic&lt;br&gt;*The food is Palestinian &lt;br&gt;*The spices are described as " borrowed " from other **30;55015;TOOLONG is a case of appropriation that must be fought . &lt;a href= " https : //t.co/xO64NtpGru " &gt;pic.twitter ... -- Davide Mastracci ( @DavideMastracci ) &lt;a href= " https : **33;55047;TOOLONG ... 15 , **33;55082;TOOLONG &lt;script async src= " https : **33;55117;TOOLONG " charset= " utf-8 " &gt;&lt;/script&gt; &lt;p&gt; Some responders defended the restaurant , including a writer for Forward.com , a news website for American Jewish readers , who wrote : &amp;ldquo ; No Israelis work in the kitchen at Shook , whose name comes from the Hebrew for &amp;lsquo ; market . &amp;rsquo ; And no Israelis were involved in creating its vegetarian menu , Chef Ben Heaton told the Forward . But a meal there , on a busy recent Wednesday , was all it took to overcome doubts about the cuisine or its authenticity . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When the fast-food restaurant Wolf Down opened in Centretown in May , it raised some eyebrows when it branded its specialty &amp;mdash ; d&amp;ouml ; ner kebab sandwiches &amp;mdash ; as German street food . Wolf Down&amp;rsquo ; s website does acknowledge that Turkish immigrants brought d&amp;ouml ; ner to Germany , but it places greater stress on the &amp;ldquo ; German-ness&amp;rdquo ; of d&amp;ouml ; ner . &amp;nbsp ; While it is true that d&amp;ouml ; ner are immensely popular in Germany , I confess that when an early PR campaign for Wolf Down said &amp;ldquo ; German street food&amp;rdquo ; was on the bill , I awaited sausages with great anticipation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; More problematically , in April , a non-Chinese New York restaurateur opened Lucky Lee&amp;rsquo ; s , with the concept of serving Chinese food that is gluten-free , dairy-free , wheat-free , corn-free , MSG-free and nut-free . Arielle Haspel initially &lt;a href= " https : **33;55152;TOOLONG ... her food as &amp;ldquo ; clean&amp;rdquo ; Chinese cuisine , and she wrote on Instagram : &amp;ldquo ; We heard you&amp;rsquo ; re obsessed with lo mein but rarely eat it . You said it makes you feel bloated and icky the next day ? &amp;rdquo ; After being criticized for cultural appropriation and casting insensitive aspersions at the food she was basing her business on , Haspel took down the Instagram post and apologized at large . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Late last year , Andrew Zimmern , the U.S. food personality , celebrity chef and restaurateur , wound up scalding himself when he &lt;a href= " https : **33;55187;TOOLONG ... his new Chinese restaurant in Minneapolis , Lucky Cricket , and its &amp;ldquo ; authentic&amp;rdquo ; Chinese cooking by saying : &amp;ldquo ; I think I&amp;rsquo ; m saving the souls of all the people from having to dine at these horses&amp;ndash ; t restaurants masquerading as Chinese food that are in the Midwest . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Zimmern subsequently apologized , but not before he endured a slamming from some chefs and pundits . In an opinion piece published in the Washington Post , Ruth Tam wrote that &amp;ldquo ; the Midwest&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;lsquo ; horses&amp;ndash ; t restaurants&amp;rsquo ; are what paved the way for Zimmern&amp;rsquo ; s venture and more broadly , Chinese cuisine in America . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But for all that , I worry that the strictures of culinary authenticity run risks of becoming too limiting . How does a chef &amp;mdash ; or for that matter , a home cook &amp;mdash ; express himself or herself creatively , or satisfy his or her personal palate , beyond simply following the dictates of tradition ? Why can&amp;rsquo ; t fusion food be good ? Why shouldn&amp;rsquo ; t a Chinese chef cook top-notch Japanese food , or a white chef cook traditional Thai fare ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Indeed , if culinary authenticity casts back in time and to another place , to how grandma made her perogies or ragu or biryani or curry in the old country , then how do new dishes come to exist ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55222;TOOLONG ... title= " Koele " Kay " Khutlang , chef and owner of African Grill , poses for a photo at his restaurant in Ottawa Friday Aug 2 , 2019. " alt= " Koele " Kay " Khutlang , chef and owner of African Grill , poses for a photo at his restaurant in Ottawa Friday Aug 2 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;55258;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446062 " /&gt; **28;55288;TOOLONG MAKES FOOD AUTHENTIC ? **26;55318;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; Whether they&amp;rsquo ; re making South African sausages , Northern Vietnamese soups or Neapolitan pizzas , there&amp;rsquo ; s no shortage of Ottawa chefs who proudly and straightforwardly affirm they&amp;rsquo ; re cooking the genuine fare of their homelands . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At African Grill on Clyde Avenue , chef-owner Koele Khutlang cooks suya , the grilled meat of West Africa , and braai , the grilled meat of South Africa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 37-year-old is more closely connected to braai , as he&amp;rsquo ; s from Lesotho , the tiny country encircled by South Africa . The son of a former diplomat at the Lesotho High Commission in Ottawa , Khutlang had planned to work as an electrical engineering technologist and studied at Algonquin College , but more recently he turned to cooking . African Grill , which Khutlang opened in December , is the first restaurant he&amp;rsquo ; s owned by himself . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Khutlang says he&amp;rsquo ; s been perfecting his braai for almost two decades . &amp;ldquo ; For most people who like cooking like me , braai is something you play with , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At African Grill , Khutlang serves chicken and goat braai , with both meats boldly but not overpoweringly spiced , charbroiled and then glazed with a sweet-salty finish . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The West African suya that Khutlang offers is another preparation entirely , with bits of heavily spiced and then grilled beef , goat and offal , served with slices of onion and extra chili powder . His recipe is &amp;ldquo ; 90 per cent authentic . &amp;nbsp ; My suya is two countries mixed , Nigeria and Cameroon . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Khutlang says the authenticity of his food stems from spices he imports from Africa . &amp;ldquo ; What I&amp;rsquo ; m targeting is Africans far away from home , to feel at home with the taste of my food , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55346;TOOLONG ... title= " Peter Tran and his mom Hue Nguyen of the Authentic Vietnamese Pho House with a bowl of their specialty crab soup . August 6 , 2019. " alt= " Peter Tran and his mom Hue Nguyen of the Authentic Vietnamese Pho House with a bowl of their specialty crab soup . August 6 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;55382;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446059 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Naturally , a restaurant named Authentic Vietnamese Pho House stands up for unadulterated pho and more . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Being 100 per cent real is the best you can get , &amp;rdquo ; says Peter Tran , who with his family owns three Authentic Vietnamese Pho House locations in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s western suburbs . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A cook in a Somerset Street West soup shop for eight years before he opened his first location in 2010 , Tran , 35 , says his soups reflect the tastes of northern Vietnam , where his family is from . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His Bun Rieu crab soup , a specialty little-found in Ottawa , relies on flavours and details that his mother passed on to him , Tran says . His restaurants serve bo luc lac , also known as shaking beef , a Vietnamese specialty that wakes up taste buds with its highly seasoned , savoury dipping sauce . &amp;ldquo ; We do it right , the way they do in Vietnam , &amp;rdquo ; Tran says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That said , Tran makes one allowance for Canadian tastes by offering vegetarian tweaks and options . &amp;ldquo ; In Vietnam , it&amp;rsquo ; s very hard to cook vegetarian food . The majority of people eat meat . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55412;TOOLONG ... title= " Joe and Anna Crupi pose with one of their wood oven pizzas at the Pizza All'Antica Food Truck in Manotick Friday Aug 2 , 2019. " alt= " Joe and Anna Crupi pose with one of their wood oven pizzas at the Pizza All'Antica Food Truck in Manotick Friday Aug 2 , 2019. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;55448;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446063 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The owners of Pizza All&amp;rsquo ; Antica , the Manotick Main Street food trailer , say one reason for them selling Neapolitan-style pizzas is that they were tired of driving to Montreal for what they saw as authentic thin-crust pizzas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We&amp;rsquo ; ve mastered the recipe of a true Neapolitan pizza , &amp;rdquo ; says Anna Crupi , who co-owns the business &amp;mdash ; its name means Pizza the Antique Way &amp;mdash ; with her husband , Joe . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The seasonal business , run by the Barrhaven couple since 2013 , values authenticity so much that Joe looked into securing a membership in the rigorously traditional Associazione Verace Pizza Napoletana. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; AVPN regulations call for pizza dough to be made just so , and they state a preference for ingredients to be from Italy&amp;rsquo ; s Campania region , which includes Naples . AVPN-compliant pizza must be stretched by hand so that final diameters do not exceed 35 centimetres . The consistency of the pizza , which must be cooked in a wood-fired oven , should be &amp;ldquo ; soft , easy to manipulate and fold . &amp;rdquo ; The centre should be particularly soft and 0.25 cm in height , plus or minus 10 per cent . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Pizza All&amp;rsquo ; Antica , the pizzas certainly seem like models of authenticity . They are made with sauce and flour sourced from Naples and stretched by the hands of Tina , Anna&amp;rsquo ; s mother , a Naples native . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Joe , who also works as a contractor , took nine days to build his oven and four days to tile the 2,800-pound behemoth , which is mounted in a former landscaping trailer . He went to Boulder , Colorado for a week of intense training in using a wood-fired oven in a mobile environment . Additional pizza-making advice came from Tina&amp;rsquo ; s brother , Franco , a pizzaiolo in Italy for 40 years . Tina would reach out to him on Skype regarding the finer points of pizza . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Joe says he spoke years ago with an AVPN official who was impressed by his business and had never heard of a food truck or trailer that made authentic Neapolitan-style pizzas . But Joe didn&amp;rsquo ; t pursue the matter of AVPN membership . &amp;ldquo ; It didn&amp;rsquo ; t end up being a priority , &amp;rdquo ; he says . One day he might apply for membership , but mostly for the marketing buzz it could confer , he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Besides , for Joe , love , passion and family are the most vital ingredients of the pizzas he sells . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That love , that passion , that bloodline &amp;mdash ; to become more authentic than what we are , it&amp;rsquo ; s almost impossible . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Joe admits to one fact that might ring alarms with the pizza police . Among the 20 pizzas on his menu is a Hawaiian Neapolitan pizza , complete with mozzarella , prosciutto cotto , fresh pineapple and tomato sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We need to serve our clients as well , &amp;rdquo ; Joe says with a smile . &amp;ldquo ; My thinking is you can put anything you want on a pizza . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You&amp;rsquo ; d be surprised how many people like Hawaiian pizza . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55478;TOOLONG ... title= " Tina Franco making pizzas at the Pizza All'Antica Food Truck in Manotick Friday Aug 2 , 2019 . Tina helps make very authentic Neapolitan-style pizza at their food truck . " alt= " Tina Franco making pizzas at the Pizza All'Antica Food Truck in Manotick Friday Aug 2 , 2019 . Tina helps make very authentic Neapolitan-style pizza at their food truck . " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;55514;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446064 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; During the past few years in Ottawa , a proliferation of Chinese restaurants opening from downtown to Kanata has driven up the authenticity quotient in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Among them are eateries such as Spicy House on Rideau Street or Delicious Sichuan Cuisine on Prince of Wales Drive , where dishes score much higher in terms of spiciness and pungency compared to the so-called &amp;ldquo ; Szechuan&amp;rdquo ; dishes that first appeared in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Chinese restaurants in the 1980s. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Other regional Chinese specialties have also arrived . At several La Noodle locations , hand-pulled noodles with their roots in the northwestern Chinese city of Lanzhou are the stars . &amp;ldquo ; Crossing The Bridge&amp;rdquo ; noodles like those made in the southwestern Chinese province of Yunnan show up at Fusion Yunnan in Vanier , Dagu Rice Noodle on Riverside Drive and Yunshang Rice Noodle in Centretown. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;At these and other places , I 'm always curious to see who is eating at the other tables . The old clich ? -- that it 's a good sign when a Chinese restaurant is packed with Chinese customers -- seems to hold at many of the new Ottawa eateries , where much of the business can consist of&lt;/span&gt; &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt; Asian university students or young families . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But what if what they&amp;rsquo ; re eating is , for all its authenticity , just too daunting ? If you didn&amp;rsquo ; t grow up eating potently spicy food , or offal , do you really want to eat what other diners having ? &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s encouraging that some of the Chinese business owners say authenticity sells not just to expats but to non-Asians who are food-curious or who have already had their tastebuds opened . The large and diverse crowds at last month&amp;rsquo ; s Ottawa Asian Fest in Chinatown , and at similar events in recent years , suggests that more palates seem to be widening for grilled squid and octopus , spicy lamb skewers and stinky tofu. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55544;TOOLONG ... title= " Owners Simon Huang ( left ) and Nara Sok at their bubble tea cafe , Chatime. " alt= " Owners Simon Huang ( left ) and Nara Sok at their bubble tea cafe , Chatime. " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;55580;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1067781 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Simon Huang , a co-owner of Chatime , the Taiwanese bubble tea franchise in Chinatown , told this newspaper in 2017 that these food-centred street parties prove that authentic Asian food has found a larger following in Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It showed there was a market for it , &amp;rdquo ; said Huang , who has helped organize and sponsor Asian Fest events . &amp;ldquo ; With the night market , and all the lineups , it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t just Asian , it was multicultural. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Before , people thought of Chinese food as just egg rolls and chicken balls , &amp;rdquo ; Huang says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h3&gt;&lt;strong&gt; " IT HAS TO BE VIABLE BUSINESS-WISE " **26;55610;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; And yet , one of Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s most successful chefs and restaurateurs says he&amp;rsquo ; s only been able to flourish by tempering the authenticity of his food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Joe Thottungal has been a culinary ambassador for Kerala , the southwestern Indian state , since he opened Coconut Lagoon on St. Laurent Boulevard 15 years ago . That restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s acclaim led to other achievements &amp;mdash ; including Thottungal&amp;rsquo ; s participation in the 2017 Canadian Culinary Championships , the opening last year of a downtown restaurant , Thali , and this year&amp;rsquo ; s publication of the Coconut Lagoon cookbook. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But Thottungal is candid about adjusting his dishes to reach a broader audience beyond fellow Indian expats. &amp;ldquo ; If you go true authentic , the same thing that we serve in my home or the neighbour&amp;rsquo ; s house in Kerala , people won&amp;rsquo ; t take it here , &amp;rdquo ; Thottungal says . &amp;ldquo ; We are looking for foreigners to enjoy our food . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; For example , Thottungal questions whether yucca root , commonly served in Kerala , would have caught on at Coconut Lagoon , especially in its early days . &amp;ldquo ; Yucca is very authentic . ( But ) if you try to serve it here , not much people will like it . They like potatoes , &amp;rdquo ; Thottungal says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Similarly , he serves salmon instead of smelts in his cuisine . &amp;ldquo ; Not everybody here will enjoy the smelts . If you feed them salmon , it will be much better off . They are familiar with the taste . But we are giving the authentic sauce , &amp;rdquo ; says Thottungal. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Typically in Kerala , beef can be cooked beyond well-done until it&amp;rsquo ; s crisp . The response from one customer , Thottungal says , was : &amp;ldquo ; What is this &amp;mdash ; beef or cardboard ? &amp;rdquo ; Another customer said he should &amp;ldquo ; never serve anymore beef like this , &amp;rdquo ; the chef recalls . He switched to serving striploin and keeping them juicy . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55638;TOOLONG ... title= " Coconut Lagoon 's award-winning owner/chef Joe Thottungal prepares a delicious salmon curry and talks about the importance of authenticity in cooking his Indian dishes . " alt= " Coconut Lagoon 's award-winning owner/chef Joe Thottungal prepares a delicious salmon curry and talks about the importance of authenticity in cooking his Indian dishes . " data-portal-copyright= " Julie Oliver " **28;55674;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446060 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; With chicken , Thottungal prefers bone-in and dark meat , but received pushback from customers who wanted boneless white meat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; You have to go with what the majority of the population tells us , &amp;rdquo ; Thottungal says . &amp;ldquo ; You need to entice people to come . When you have a business , you need to be broad-minded . It has to be viable business-wise. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He also speaks about more accessible dishes opening the door for customers to sample more authentic preparations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Now , people are trying a lot of things , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &amp;ldquo ; Now we can slowly given them one dish or two dishes more , people can enjoy it . People have the confidence . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; **28;55704;TOOLONG FUSION FOOD **31;55734;TOOLONG &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s kind of a funny trajectory , &amp;rdquo ; says Ottawa chef Tarek Hassan , of his fascination with Cantonese food that goes back more than two decades . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The 40-year-old &amp;mdash ; who was born in the Middle East , has spent most of his life in Canada , and who makes Chinese steamed buns to die for at his Gongfu Bao shop on Bank Street &amp;mdash ; offers one of the most thoughtful takes on culinary authenticity I&amp;rsquo ; ve come across . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I distinctly remember the first time I went to a Chinese restaurant , &amp;rdquo ; Hassan continues . &amp;ldquo ; I was in my teens . In Egypt , there wasn&amp;rsquo ; t really Chinese food . I was just blown away by the complexity of the flavour and just the whole experience . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hassan moved to Ottawa to attend Carleton University for computer systems engineering , but drifted over to cooking . Roughly a decade ago , he worked at Savana Cafe , the now-closed Centretown restaurant that was an early proponent of multicultural cooking &amp;mdash ; think Thai and Caribbean-influenced dishes on the menu &amp;mdash ; and fusion cooking . &amp;ldquo ; A friend of mine let me know Savana Caf&amp;eacute ; was hiring and it&amp;rsquo ; s a place that had a proper wok station . I was just like , &amp;lsquo ; Yes please . &amp;rsquo ; I dropped whatever things I was doing and jumped into that . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55767;TOOLONG ... title= " Gongfu Bao chef/owner Tarek Hassan with a variety of Asian items from his menu . November 20 , 2018. " alt= " Gongfu Bao chef/owner Tarek Hassan with a variety of Asian items from his menu . November 20 , 2018. " data-portal-copyright= " Errol McGihon " **28;55803;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446068 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Seven years ago , when the City of Ottawa put out a call for street food vendors , &amp;ldquo ; steamed buns were a no-brainer , &amp;rdquo ; Hassan says . Years before , he had eaten closed buns from Chinese supermarkets . The game-changing success of New York chef David Chang , who built his Momofuku empire in the 2000s on selling pork belly-filled steamed buns , &amp;ldquo ; inflamed a curiosity , &amp;rdquo ; Hassan adds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; His Gongfu Bao cart debuted in the spring of 2013 near Confederation Park and later won fans at the Westboro Summer Market on Byron Avenue . A year ago , he opened his bricks-and-mortar shop in Centretown. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hassan says he&amp;rsquo ; s faced sceptics who immediately doubted he could make good Chinese food . Selling buns outside Confederation Park , he met a lot of Chinese tourists &amp;mdash ; &amp;ldquo ; people who came to Ottawa from China and walked past this cart with this brown guy with a beard serving steamed buns that he&amp;rsquo ; s saying he made , &amp;rdquo ; he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Their reaction ? &amp;ldquo ; I got anything from , on one extreme , people literally pointing and laughing and continuing to walk , to on the other extreme , people stopping , being curious , asking questions , &amp;rdquo ; Hassan says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve had people specifically say , &amp;lsquo ; You could sell this in China . &amp;rsquo ; Those are the compliments that made my day . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I don&amp;rsquo ; t feel entitled to this cuisine , &amp;rdquo ; Hassan continues . &amp;ldquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; ve put a lot of work into developing the craft behind working with it , but I always invite conversations and questions or concerns regarding appropriation because I feel the most important thing is to not be causing any harm . That&amp;rsquo ; s something I take very seriously . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; To some degree , for any chef experimenting with food that he or she didn&amp;rsquo ; t grow up with , appropriation may well be baked-in , Hassan suggests . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I recall Jordan Holley , chef de cuisine of Datsun , the modern , pan-Asian restaurant on Elgin Street , addressing his interest in Asian food after I praised his restaurant in a 2015 review : &amp;ldquo ; We respect and appreciate authenticity . But it will always be our interpretation of food , &amp;rdquo ; he said . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Holley also offered an anecdote reminiscent of Hassan&amp;rsquo ; s run-ins with Chinese tourists : &amp;ldquo ; We have to admit , we always get nervous when people from strong cultural culinary backgrounds visit Datsun . I think with our culinary training and experience we can feel confident in delivering a flavour that is familiar . For example , we had a friend bring her parents ( old-school Chinese ) , and they were very skeptical to see a young , white , tattooed chef preparing their dinner . They ordered the congee , curry buns , etc. &amp;hellip ; and were extremely impressed . She even told me she never eats congee anywhere other than her mother&amp;rsquo ; s kitchen , not in Hong Kong even , but says ours is an exception . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55833;TOOLONG ... title= " Tofu bao at Gongfu Bao. " alt= " Tofu bao at Gongfu Bao. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;55869;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1308522 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; For his part , Hassan steeped himself in the tradition of what he&amp;rsquo ; s cooking , using a rolling pin and bamboo baskets to craft and cook his food because after experimenting with more modern measures , he determined that &amp;ldquo ; traditional methods and traditional tools trumped everything . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Furthermore , he continues to delve deep into Cantonese cooking . He visited Hong Kong in 2017 with culinary training in mind , and has &amp;ldquo ; set some thing in motion&amp;rdquo ; for a return trip there . He is looking for a mentor in Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s Chinatown who will help him hone his skills at barbecuing pork &amp;mdash ; a filling for his buns that he prepares even if his parents , for religious reasons , don&amp;rsquo ; t eat pork . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s a little bit of a friction , but I don&amp;rsquo ; t have a problem with it , &amp;rdquo ; Hassan says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Hassan feels he can respect authenticity and tradition but also put a personal , Canadian spin on his food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I was letting all the authenticity happen in the buns and the dough , but then I took creative freedom with the fillings , &amp;rdquo ; he says of some experiments during his food-cart days . &amp;ldquo ; For street food , mashups and fusion-y things like that were kind of the norm . People are expecting kind of zany mashup things to happen . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Once , after he ran out of barbecue pork , he made things like bao with halloumi , the brined Cypriot cheese that takes well to grilling . While customers liked the improvised cross-cultural food , Hassan had misgivings . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I realized there&amp;rsquo ; s a big responsibility toward the cuisine I&amp;rsquo ; m working with to , at the very least , represent it in a way that doesn&amp;rsquo ; t confuse people about what it is. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; If I&amp;rsquo ; m going to do playful , wacky things , those need to be secondary to me showing someone who&amp;rsquo ; s not familiar with this cuisine what it&amp;rsquo ; s all about . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He calls trendy Asian fusion cooking &amp;ldquo ; dangerous , &amp;rdquo ; explaining that &amp;ldquo ; all these narrative trajectories are going into this melting pot and terminating . And people are losing track of what&amp;rsquo ; s what . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A competent chef might make a reasonable dish from a hodge-podge of Asian ingredients , but it could still be culturally problematic . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Here&amp;rsquo ; s some pickled ginger and here&amp;rsquo ; s some gochujang ( a spicy , Korean fermented bean paste ) and here&amp;rsquo ; s some rice , some sesame oil . Balance it with salt , that&amp;rsquo ; s balanced , I made something new . But that&amp;rsquo ; s pretty dangerous in terms of the responsibility to the food culture you&amp;rsquo ; re working with . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; He says he allows himself to tinker with authentic recipes if the new creations point to Canadian and Quebecois comfort food . Hence , there&amp;rsquo ; s his tourti&amp;egrave ; re egg rolls , or barbecue pork that&amp;rsquo ; s glazed with maple syrup rather than the usual honey . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; To me , authenticity isn&amp;rsquo ; t just about accurate replication , &amp;rdquo ; Hassan says . &amp;ldquo ; It&amp;rsquo ; s about honouring that food trajectory and understanding it . Someone can take something and evolve it , whether it&amp;rsquo ; s to adapt to locally available foods or to local palate preferences , and still be mindful of where it&amp;rsquo ; s coming from and make sure people can see where it&amp;rsquo ; s coming from and where it&amp;rsquo ; s going . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; *** &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Earlier this year , when I spoke to the Ottawa-born , Toronto-raised celebrity chef Amanda Cohen , she spoke excitedly about the food scene in Canada . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While Cohen cooks principally at her renowned New York restaurant , a mecca for vegetable lovers called Dirt Candy , she returns to Canada for cooking events , and is a titular competitor on the Iron Chef : Canada TV series . What strikes her most about food in Canada is the cultural mingling it involves . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; When I was growing up in Toronto , you&amp;rsquo ; d have all of these different kinds of stores next to each other or within walking distance . That&amp;rsquo ; s what Canada does so well &amp;hellip ; these amazing ingredients that don&amp;rsquo ; t seem exotic . They seem like they should be used in the food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; That&amp;rsquo ; s Canadian cuisine . It&amp;rsquo ; s a little bit of everything . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; There is , of course , the danger that Hassan points out , in which a chef&amp;rsquo ; s whims can run roughshod over culinary traditions . Recently , I had another Ottawa chef&amp;rsquo ; s pork belly bao buns . That non-Chinese chef thought it would a good idea to dust his buns with spicy creole seasoning , and put the sweet-savoury hoisin sauce on the side , rather than directly on the pork . He was wrong . The dish was a flop , and furthermore , its pork belly was overcooked. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; But if you consult your food history , the long view of different cuisines suggests they are not as monolithic as they might appear . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Where cultures met , food crossed borders . With the food of Macau , the former Portuguese territory on the south coast of China near Hong Kong , the food of the colonized mixed with dishes of the colonizers . From Latin America to the U.S. South , the food of former slaves has influenced what the descendants of slaveowners eat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Gumbo in Louisiana dates back 300 years to almost a century before Napoleon sold the territory to the U.S. , but it&amp;rsquo ; s believed to blend the influences and ingredients of French , Spanish , African , Native American and Caribbean cuisines. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; On the other side of the world , in Japan , yoshoku refers to Japanized versions of Western dishes , from spaghetti topped with fish roe and seaweed to various breaded cutlets to the Japanese adaptation of British curries that themselves were adaptations of Indian curries . And lest we think that yoshoku is a recent development , just know that tempura dishes was influenced by the fritter-cooking techniques of the Portuguese residing in Nagasaki in the 16th century . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Of course , the equivalent of yoshoku in North America is the super-sized sushi roll , with eschews the minimalist aesthetic of Japanese sushi in favour of heavy sauces , avocado , mango , cream cheese and more . At the vanguard of this evolution was the California roll , which author Trevor Corson writes in his book The Zen of Fish was invented in the late 1960s at the pioneering Los Angeles eatery Tokyo Kaitan. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; The California roll has come to be seen as a stroke of genius , &amp;rdquo ; Corson writes . &amp;ldquo ; The chef who devised it must have read the American mind , the thinking goes , and adapted sushi to American tastes . &amp;rdquo ; In fact , Corson continues , the California roll was borne out of more practical concerns &amp;mdash ; avocado was substituted after chefs had difficulty obtaining fresh fatty tuna belly . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Regardless of how they came to be , those dishes were fusion dishes . It may have taken generations of evolution , but they have become traditional . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; These days , cultures continue to collide , only much more quickly and haphazardly . While authentic cuisines can be the pillars of the foods we eat and cook , they can&amp;rsquo ; t remain static in the face of global migration and creative cooks . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As chefs , both haute and homey , tweak the food they grew up with or immerse themselves meaningfully in another culture&amp;rsquo ; s food , dishes can progress and adapt , and new dishes can be born . The results aren&amp;rsquo ; t always for the better , but they aren&amp;rsquo ; t necessarily bad . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s the instances when these new culinary mashups are deep and delicious that make my mouth water . I can vouch that the Viet-Cajun food that has emerged in the recent years in Houston , Texas is not just a thing , it&amp;rsquo ; s a delicious thing . And if you texted me : &amp;ldquo ; Let&amp;rsquo ; s get some turducken siu mai from Gongfu Bao , &amp;rdquo ; I&amp;rsquo ; d be at that Centretown shop in a minute . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If turducken siu mai or tourti&amp;egrave ; re egg rolls one day came to be authentic Canadian dishes , I would be just fine with that . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;55899;TOOLONG " **32;55926;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; twitter.com/peterhum &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;55960;TOOLONG ... title= " Turducken siu mai at Gongfu Bao. " alt= " Turducken siu mai at Gongfu Bao. " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;55996;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1306894 " /&gt; Peter HumThu , 15 Aug 2019 15:26:52 **45;56026;TOOLONG ... ' authentic ' Chinese cuisinehttp : **33;56073;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;56108;TOOLONG ... title= " General Tso Chicken . " alt= " General Tso Chicken . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;56144;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1446070 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I have a very sentimental attachment to the Chinese-Canadian food that has seemingly been superseded by ostensibly more authentic Chinese cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; From the mid-1950s to the early 1980s , my father and uncle ran the Marco Polo Tavern Restaurant on Bank Street near Heron Road , serving the staples of Chinese-Canadian food . All through high school , I bagged egg rolls , put lids on containers of chicken balls and bagged the lot of them for take-out. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I worked at the Marco Polo , I grew to love Chinese-Canadian dishes , even though as a young boy , my first reaction to our restaurant&amp;rsquo ; s food was to be disappointed that it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t as tasty as my mother&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;mdash ; authentic &amp;mdash ; home cooking . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Indeed , my connection to Chinese-Canadian food extends to the paternal grandfather I never knew , who operated the Ontario Caf&amp;eacute ; on Rideau Street , where the Rideau Centre is now , during the 1920s and 1930s as far as I can tell . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;h2 class= " cat-head short " **40;56174;TOOLONG ... &lt;ul class= " relatedlinks " &gt; &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href= " https : **33;56216;TOOLONG ... makes food ' authentic , ' and who gets to decide ? &lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt; &lt;/ul&gt; &lt;p&gt; While many details of my family history are still a mystery to me , I&amp;rsquo ; m pleased that one online listing of Ottawa restaurants in the 1930s yields this : &amp;ldquo ; Ontario Caf&amp;eacute ; ( Prop . James W. Hum ) &amp;hellip ; 64-66 Rideau Street . Directory indicates &amp;lsquo ; Chinese . &amp;rsquo ; Brief search indicates name and owner . Restaurant principally appears to have served European/Canadian food , with Chinese dishes as feature . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You can knock sweet and sour pork and garlic spare ribs as outdated and inauthentic if you want . Canadian writer Ann Hui doesn&amp;rsquo ; t . She came to a fresh understanding after writing her recently released book Chop Suey Nation , which looks back on Chinese-Canadian eateries both in Hui&amp;rsquo ; s family and across the country . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; These are dishes made with local ingredients , made with local palates in mind , made here in Canada that tell this very Canadian story of immigration , &amp;rdquo ; Hui said in an interview with Postmedia News . &amp;ldquo ; To me , it&amp;rsquo ; s as Canadian of a cuisine as anything else &amp;mdash ; as maple syrup , as poutine , as the peameal bacon sandwich . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; This is Canadian&amp;nbsp ; food . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumThu , 15 Aug 2019 15:18:07 **45;56251;TOOLONG ... Pork and pig-based products figure in the food , but not overwhelmingly so , and a vegetarian could dine happily here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Portioned somewhere between small plates and main courses , Grunt&amp;rsquo ; s dishes also come with wallet-friendly prices of $15 each , more or less , taxes in . Basically , the prices are in keeping with Grunt&amp;rsquo ; s location , tucked away on a residential side street in the historically blue-collar neighbourhood north of Scott Street and east of Tunney&amp;rsquo ; s Pasture . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Formerly a corner-lot pizzeria , Grunt now seats about 20 at blocky wooden tables and at the counters of its open kitchen where McLelland and his sous-chef toil . Its walls are white , the art is funky and local , the seating is hard , the vintage plates don&amp;rsquo ; t match , and old-time jazz or Billy Idol can take turns on the sound system . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56473;TOOLONG ... title= " Chef-owner Jason McLelland at Grunt , his new restaurant in Mechanicsville. " alt= " Chef-owner Jason McLelland at Grunt , his new restaurant in Mechanicsville. " data-portal-copyright= " Tony Caldwell " **28;56509;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1445199 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; In all , few extravagances distract from Grunt&amp;rsquo ; s pretty , tasty dishes when they quickly land in front of you . &lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;There may not be a more relaxed , human-scale , dining-out experience in Ottawa , especially if McLelland 's wife , Marie , is serving . Once , after she brought us our lunch , she gave her young daughter , Maisie , a ride on her shoulders . &lt;/span&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; McLelland , 31 , most recently worked at the somewhat fancier Town on Elgin Street . Before coming to Ottawa , McLelland worked in fine-dining brigades in London , Paris , Australia and his homeland , Scotland . He even did stints in such Michelin-starred kitchens as Le Gavroche in London , and Le Jules Verne , on the second floor of the Eiffel Tower . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In Mechanicsville , McLelland still makes food for discriminating palates , even if he prioritizes , as he says , &amp;ldquo ; honest and wholesome food&amp;rdquo ; over haute cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The to-the-point , all-day menu &amp;mdash ; typically a one-page list of three or four savoury dishes and a dessert or two &amp;mdash ; changes every two weeks at Grunt . I spread out my visits accordingly for a longer view of McLelland&amp;rsquo ; s fare . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If there&amp;rsquo ; s a signature dish at Grunt , it&amp;rsquo ; s McLelland&amp;rsquo ; s porchetta sandwich ( $14.50 ) . The versions that I tried never followed the usual Italian preparation . McLelland preferred to dress up a slab of nicely roasted pork belly like an English Sunday dinner , with Yorkshire pudding , a mushroom demi-glace , crushed root vegetables and apple sauce . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56539;TOOLONG ... title= " Porchetta sandwich , apple sauce , crushed root veg , mushroom demi glace , Yorkshire pudding , at Grunt " alt= " Porchetta sandwich , apple sauce , crushed root veg , mushroom demi glace , Yorkshire pudding , at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;56575;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1444931 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; It needs an extra napkin , &amp;rdquo ; Marie said when she dropped off a sandwich . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; Oh , that&amp;rsquo ; s promising , &amp;rdquo ; my dining companion said . After polishing off most of it , she said the sandwich justified a return visit to Grunt . That said , the latest menu&amp;rsquo ; s version takes the sandwich in a South Asian direction , swapping in mango chutney and a spicier treatment . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We&amp;rsquo ; ve equally enjoyed other savoury and starchier dishes . Butternut squash risotto ( $16 ) was beautifully balanced and complex , with the indulgences of mascarpone and pancetta and the accents of sage and spiced almonds enlivening the dish&amp;rsquo ; s on-point base of rice and veg . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56605;TOOLONG ... title= " Butternut squash risotto with mascarpone , sage , crispy pancetta , spiced almond crumb at Grunt " alt= " Butternut squash risotto with mascarpone , sage , crispy pancetta , spiced almond crumb at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;56641;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1434387 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Grunt&amp;rsquo ; s &amp;ldquo ; gnocchi&amp;rdquo ; made with p&amp;acirc ; te &amp;agrave ; choux ( $15.50 , also known as gnocchi parisienne ) featured tender , pillowy dumplings in a pleasing carbonara sauce , garnished with egg yolk , bacon , goat cheese and green onions . Bread made with bacon fat completed the dish , which despite the richness of its elements left us feeling invigorated rather than weighed down . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56671;TOOLONG ... title= " Pate a choux " gnocchi " with carbonara sauce , sieved egg yolk , bacon , goat 's cheese , bacon fat bread , green onions , shallots , at Grunt " alt= " Pate a choux " gnocchi " with carbonara sauce , sieved egg yolk , bacon , goat 's cheese , bacon fat bread , green onions , shallots , at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;56707;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1444933 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; A kale Caesar salad topped with a fried egg ( $13.50 ) was a more simple win , zinging with the big flavours of its creamy , anchovy-powered dressing . Raising the dish up was the focused pop of its sweet semi-dried tomatoes , toasted almonds , crisp onions and shavings of pig fat that McLelland dispensed table-side. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56803;TOOLONG ... title= " Bison Kofta with mint cream , sunblushed tomatoes , toasted almonds , crispy onions , pig fat , at Grunt " alt= " Bison Kofta with mint cream , sunblushed tomatoes , toasted almonds , crispy onions , pig fat , at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;56839;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1444932 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Desserts at Grunt have been more expensive than many in town , just a little cheaper than the savoury dishes . But don&amp;rsquo ; t let that dissuade you &amp;mdash ; you&amp;rsquo ; ll likely miss out on a treat . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; in June , a strawberry shortcake ( $12.50 ) knocked me out , even if its crumb could have been a bit more tender . Nonetheless , its fruit , cream and sauce were good enough to almost set me cussing in appreciation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56869;TOOLONG ... title= " Strawberry shortcake at Grunt " alt= " Strawberry shortcake at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;56905;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1445188 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; With its table-side splash of fizzing prosecco , lemon sorbet ( $13.50 ) with torched melon , olive oil , lemon balm , zippy pink pepper and some confit lime felt like a celebration . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;56935;TOOLONG ... title= " Lemon sorbet with extra virgin olive oil , confit lime , pink pepper , lemon balm , torched melon , prosecco , at Grunt " alt= " Lemon sorbet with extra virgin olive oil , confit lime , pink pepper , lemon balm , torched melon , prosecco , at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;56971;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1434389 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the semifreddo ( $14 ) that came with roasted strawberries , raspberry coulis and cracked black pepper had set too hard , this was one more dish whose strong points and thoughtfulness made its lapse forgivable. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57001;TOOLONG ... title= " Roasted strawberry and raspberry coulis , cracked black pepper , semifreddo at Grunt " alt= " Roasted strawberry and raspberry coulis , cracked black pepper , semifreddo at Grunt " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57037;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1444930 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Grunt is licensed , with a dozen wines mostly from the Old World priced between $40 and $65 a bottle , with five-ounce glasses also available . Beers by Beau&amp;rsquo ; s Brewing Co. , its offshoot Halcyon Barrel House and Stalwart Brewing Co. are available , as is coffee from a fine espresso machine that shares counter space with a miniature garden of micro-greens and a trusty immersion circulator. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; If Grunt wasn&amp;rsquo ; t special enough already , it is also becoming a booster for Mechanicsville , which McLelland calls &amp;ldquo ; beautiful&amp;rdquo ; but also &amp;ldquo ; rough around the edges , &amp;rdquo ; not unlike the part of Scotland where he grew up . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After a fire down the street left a family homeless last month , Grunt gave over its walls to paintings by Andrea Stokes , all of which were sold to raise money to help the family . In June , Grunt hosted a 90th birthday party for Keith Brown , the life-long neighbourhood resident and volunteer who has been dubbed &amp;ldquo ; the Mayor of Mechanicsville. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; McLelland has begun giving free monthly cooking classes . He says if there are families who can&amp;rsquo ; t afford a night out at a restaurant , he&amp;rsquo ; s not opposed to feeding them for free if they make reservations . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; McLelland , who lives in Gloucester with his in-laws , says he was happy to open Grunt in Mechanicsville . Elgin Street and Wellington Street West have enough restaurants , he says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; I could use this as a platform to do some good , &amp;rdquo ; McLelland says of his business . That&amp;rsquo ; s what he&amp;rsquo ; s doing , and deliciously so . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;57067;TOOLONG " **32;57094;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;57128;TOOLONG ... Hum 's restaurant reviews&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;57163;TOOLONG ... of Come From Away arrives at the NAC&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;57198;TOOLONG ... the beloved Broadway musical , is bound for the NAC this fall&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;57233;TOOLONG ... out best bets , Aug. 8 to 14 : An Indigenous arts fest , and neo-folk at Bar Robo&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 14 Aug 2019 15:00:13 **45;57268;TOOLONG ... Food ReviewsDining Out : Nikosi Bistro Pub a distinctive , uncomplicated eatery in Wakefieldhttp : **31;57315;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;57348;TOOLONG ... title= " Wild boar burger with salad and fries at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Wild boar burger with salad and fries at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57384;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1443633 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Nikosi Bistro Pub&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 721 Riverside Dr. , Wakefield , 819-459-3773 , &lt;a href= " http : //nikosibistropub.com " &gt;nikosibi ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Wednesday 4 to 9 p.m. , Thursday 4 to 10 p.m. , Friday and Saturday 11 a.m. to 10 p.m. , Sunday 10 a.m. to 9 p.m. , closed Monday and Tuesday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; main courses $19 to $45 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; ramp to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; A week ago , we got a jump on the long weekend by spending Friday afternoon in Wakefield . Some time kayaking on the Gatineau River would do us good , my wife and I thought . But first , we had lunch at Nikosi Bistro Pub . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In early 2017 , this casual spot was opened by Wapokunie Riel-Lachapelle , who grew up in Wakefield . She went out west and worked in restaurants for a time , but eventually returned and launched Nikosi , where the fare is simple and from-scratch . Fish and game meat frequently star on Nikosi&amp;rsquo ; s plates , and Riel-Lachapelle told me when we chatted on the phone this year that she makes a connection between the food she serves and her M&amp;eacute ; t is heritage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We ate on Nikosi&amp;rsquo ; s river-facing patio , shielded from the blazing sun by big umbrellas . My wife and I enjoyed our starter of smoked salmon ( $18 ) , served on thin wedges of house-made bannock bread . The fish was luscious and maple-tinged , while fried capers added a salty pop and the side salad was fresh . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57414;TOOLONG ... title= " Smoked salmon on bannock appetizer at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Smoked salmon on bannock appetizer at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57450;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1442260 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Two generously portioned mains followed . My boar burger ( $20 ) was heftily meaty and fully loaded with caramelized onions , maple-sweetened mushrooms , bacon , aged cheddar and maple-garlic aioli . With some fine fries and a heap of salad , my order amounted to lunch and then some . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57480;TOOLONG ... title= " Wild boar burger with salad and fries at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Wild boar burger with salad and fries at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57516;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1443633 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; My wife , who can never get too much salmon , thought her barbecued salmon ( $24 ) , which came with the same fries and salad that I received , was just a touch too dry , but nicely flavoured . She too had leftovers , and we combined everything into a container that Riel-Lachapelle graciously kept in Nikosi&amp;rsquo ; s fridge until we came back after kayaking. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57546;TOOLONG ... title= " Barbecue salmon at Nikosi Bistro Pub " alt= " Barbecue salmon at Nikosi Bistro Pub " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57582;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1443639 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At another lunch earlier this summer , a friend and I spent some time perusing Nikosi&amp;rsquo ; s cocktail menu . The $10 options intrigued us , from the black currant margarita to a smokey Caesar to the &amp;ldquo ; &amp;eacute ; lectrique powwow&amp;rdquo ; made with Chic-Choc spiced rum from Quebec&amp;rsquo ; s Eastern Townships , ginger ale , lime juice , berries and mint . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At Nikosi , the negroni is Canadian-ized with a bit of spruce reduction . I don&amp;rsquo ; t if Anthony Bourdain would have approved of this tweak of his favourite , potent , Italian cocktail , but I did . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; After that drink came a mound of elk tartare ( $17 ) , which was lean , quite mild of flavour and enhanced with pickled mushrooms and parmesan . While its seasoning could have been more bold , the tartare nonetheless disappeared quickly as we scooped the stuff up with bannock crostini. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57612;TOOLONG ... title= " Elk tartare at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Elk tartare at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57648;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1442251 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The two main courses at that lunch were pleasant enough , but needed a bit more finesse to sparkle as they might have . Chunks of duck breast ( $27 ) had an admirably crisp exterior , but were otherwise overcooked . Wilted kale , pickled blueberries and crisp potatoes made up somewhat for that flaw . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57678;TOOLONG ... title= " Duck breast with kale and potatoes at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Duck breast with kale and potatoes at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57714;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1442261 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Pickerel , the day&amp;rsquo ; s fish special ( $24 ) , was fried straightforwardly , but let down by the mix of wild rice and veg on the side , which did very little for me. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57744;TOOLONG ... title= " Pickerel with beets and wild rice at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Pickerel with beets and wild rice at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57780;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1442250 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last winter , I also ate twice inside Nikosi&amp;rsquo ; s rustic , woody dining room that seats about 40 . Then , some of the cooking was uneven . There was a flank steak ( $27 ) that was fine , but the fries with them were the opposite of crisp . Confit duck wings with house-made barbecue sauce ( $17 ) , which remain on the summer menu , were under-seasoned and flaccid-skinned. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57810;TOOLONG ... title= " Flank steak and fries at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Flank steak and fries at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57846;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1361171 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57876;TOOLONG ... title= " Duck wings at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " alt= " Duck wings at Nikosi Bistro Pub in Wakefield " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57912;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1361172 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Better was Nikosi&amp;rsquo ; s grilled cheese ( $19 ) , a still-available deluxe bannock-based sandwich made with some of the kitchen&amp;rsquo ; s go-to ingredients ( caramelized onions , maple-sweetened mushrooms , aged cheddar , spinach , maple-garlic aioli ) along with chunks of duck sausage . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;57942;TOOLONG ... title= " Bannock grilled cheese with duck sausage , maple mushrooms , cheddar , spinach " alt= " Bannock grilled cheese with duck sausage , maple mushrooms , cheddar , spinach " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;57978;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1361175 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Duck sausage , spinach , caramelized onions and maple-sweetened mushrooms also turned in last winter&amp;rsquo ; s rib-sticking mac and cheese , which was topped with a rich mornay sauce ( $20 ) . I look forward to having this dish again when the weather turns cold . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58008;TOOLONG ... title= " Nikosi mac and cheese with duck sausage , Mornay sauce , caramelized onions , maple mushrooms , spinach and bannock breadcrumbs " alt= " Nikosi mac and cheese with duck sausage , Mornay sauce , caramelized onions , maple mushrooms , spinach and bannock breadcrumbs " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58044;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1361174 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve only had one dessert at Nikosi &amp;mdash ; a slice of salted caramel cheesecake that did the trick even if it wasn&amp;rsquo ; t made in house . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When I spoke to Riel-Lachapelle , she told me that while she had a hand in developing the recipes at Nikosi , she is more of a do-everything general manager who has left the kitchen in other hands . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Her father , a keen hunter and fisherman , was a good cook , she continued . &amp;ldquo ; I didn&amp;rsquo ; t realized back then how lucky I was , &amp;rdquo ; Riel-Lachapelle said . &amp;ldquo ; Growing up &amp;hellip ; that impacted my life and my appreciation of food . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Nikosi Bistro Pub , then , is an uncomplicated but distinctive eatery of which its owner and her father can be proud . &lt;/p&gt; Food ReviewsDining Out : Tamis Caf ? brings Filipino favourites to Centretownhttp : **30;58287;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;58319;TOOLONG ... title= " Longanisa burger with chips at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Longanisa burger with chips at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58355;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430908 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Tamis Caf ? &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 374 Bank St. , 613-567-7550 , &lt;a href= " http : //tamiscafe.ca " &gt;tamiscafe.ca&lt; ... or &lt;a href= " http : **27;58385;TOOLONG " &gt;fa ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Thursday 11 a.m. to 9 p.m. , Friday and Saturday 11 a.m. to 11 p.m. , Sunday 9:30 a.m. to 3 p.m. , closed Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; brunch dishes up to $15 , mains up to $17 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; one step to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Three years ago , I learned that the word for &amp;ldquo ; sweet&amp;rdquo ; in Tagalog , the national language of the Philippines , was &amp;ldquo ; tamis. &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I owed my education to several enjoyable visits to the Tamis Caf&amp;eacute ; , a tiny place in the Glebe that opened in early 2016 and featured unique Filipino baked goods in its showcase. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That eatery , operated by the Sare family , was tucked away on Fourth Avenue east of Bank Street . It moved late last year to Centretown , into the Bank Street space that had been The Buzz for more than 15 years . With the move has come an expanded menu with more savoury choices , and also a liquor licence that supports the array of Filipino San Miguel beers that serve as the backdrop for Tamis&amp;rsquo ; s bar , as well as some exotic cocktails and a more ordinary and perfunctory wine list . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; So , I&amp;rsquo ; ve tried the new Tamis Caf&amp;eacute ; &amp;mdash ; a long , narrow , brick-walled spot that seats 40 or so in black banquettes and seats &amp;mdash ; a few times this summer to sample some of its savoury moves and investigate Filipino cuisine , which in Ottawa is in short supply . ( I know only of Ka Familia in Barrhaven , the Meryenda food truck , plus a handful of grocery stores and bakery that dedicate themselves to the intriguing east-meets-west fare of the Philippines. ) &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; New to me at the relocated , larger Tamis was its brunch menu , which is available daily and melds Western staples with Filipino items . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The most visually striking plate at brunch paired hefty buttermilk pancakes ( $12 ) &amp;mdash ; coloured a Seussian bright green by a syrup flavoured by pandan , the Southeast Asian plant &amp;mdash ; with thick , stubby , skinless longanisa sausages , which appealed with their porky goodness perked by garlic and pineapple . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58414;TOOLONG ... title= " Pandan pancakes and loganisa sausage at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Pandan pancakes and loganisa sausage at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58450;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430916 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Those delectable sausages also showed up with home fries or garlicky rice , both equally good , plus eggs , cooked as we liked , and toast ( $15 ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; We were also able to order from the regular menu during that brunch visit . That meant we could sample Tamis&amp;rsquo ; s embutido ( $13 ) , a cold pork meatloaf that was mellow in flavour and slightly crumbly , served with a simple , cucumber-heavy salad , and lumpia ( $13 ) , a thin , soft , freshly made , neutral-tasting crepe stuffed with saut&amp;eacute ; ed green beans , carrots , sweet potatoes , potatoes , lettuce , and served with peanut sauce . Both items were humble and homey. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58480;TOOLONG ... title= " Embutido meatloaf and salad at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Embutido meatloaf and salad at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58516;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1432749 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; During two dinner visits , my friends and I tried four of Tamis&amp;rsquo ; s appetizers. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Most accessible was a heaping platter ( $20 ) of deep-fried spring rolls , wontons and chips , all served with a sweet and sour sauce that we thought needed more punchiness. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58546;TOOLONG ... title= " Appetizer platter at Tamis Cafe " alt= " Appetizer platter at Tamis Cafe " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58582;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1434165 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; More daunting , and more oily , was the fried whole squid ( $20 ) , which was tender enough of tentacle but not as crisp as we might have liked . Overall , the squid amounted to a heavy offering that we could not polish off . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58612;TOOLONG ... title= " Fried whole calamari at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Fried whole calamari at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " Postmedia " **28;58648;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1432562 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We did finish off our sizzling sisig ( $16 ) , a dish in which bits of grilled and saut&amp;eacute ; ed pork belly mingled with pigs ears and a smattering of chilli peppers and red onions . While I recall having some meatier sisig during a trip to the Cayman Islands this year , the chewiness and fattiness of this dish dominated this time out . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58678;TOOLONG ... title= " Sizzling sisig ( pork belly and pig ears ) at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Sizzling sisig ( pork belly and pig ears ) at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58714;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1432559 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The dish is designated as mild on Tamis&amp;rsquo ; s menu , which I take as a reference to the amount of chilli peppers at play . I would have welcomed more heat on this and other dishes , but in general , Tamis&amp;rsquo ; s kitchen skews to the milder side of things , even as it proffers the sour , sweet and salty aspects of Filipino cuisine . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Another revelation at Tamis Caf&amp;eacute ; was that cheese pimento dip is beloved by many Filipinos . A hunk of the dense , house-made stuff is available as an appetizer , and a mound of it figured as the crowning garnish on the caf&amp;eacute ; &amp;rsquo ; s plump longanisa burger . My friend ( who is not Filipino , it should perhaps be said ) liked everything about that juicy burger except the cheese dip , which he found overpowering . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58744;TOOLONG ... title= " Pimento cheese appetizer at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Pimento cheese appetizer at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58780;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430910 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;58810;TOOLONG ... title= " Longanisa burger with chips at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Longanisa burger with chips at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;58846;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430908 " /&gt; &lt;div id= " **31;58876;TOOLONG " class= " sqs-block image-block sqs-block-image sqs-text-ready " &gt; &lt;div id= " yui31721156390167868589 " class= " sqs-block-content " &gt; &lt;div id= " yui31721156390167868588 " class= " **25;58909;TOOLONG layout-caption-hidden design-layout-inline **29;58936;TOOLONG **25;58967;TOOLONG **30;58994;TOOLONG animation-loaded " &gt; &lt;div id= " yui31721156390167868587 " class= " intrinsic " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;br&gt; Among other mains , the mechado ( $17 ) , a braise of sirloin , was not as tender as I recall having had when Tamis was in the Glebe . Pancit Bihon ( $12 ) , the rice noodle stir-fry , was simple and filling . **35;59026;TOOLONG &g; ... &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59063;TOOLONG ... title= " Beef mechado at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Beef mechado at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " Postmedia " **28;59099;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430912 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id= " **26;59129;TOOLONG " class= " sqs-block image-block sqs-block-image sqs-text-ready " &gt; &lt;div id= " yui317211563901678685112 " class= " sqs-block-content " &gt; &lt;div id= " yui317211563901678685111 " class= " **25;59157;TOOLONG layout-caption-hidden design-layout-inline **26;59184;TOOLONG **25;59212;TOOLONG **30;59239;TOOLONG " &gt; &lt;div id= " yui317211563901678685110 " class= " intrinsic " &gt; &lt;div id= " yui317211563901678685109 " class= " image-block-wrapper has-aspect-ratio " &gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59271;TOOLONG ... title= " Pancit Bihon ( noodle stir fry ) at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;59307;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430911 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt;The grilled pork chop ( $17 ) and grilled chicken leg ( $17 , called chicken inasal on the specials card on our table ) were appealing tangy , but also at least a little dry . But the garlicky rice and the spicy mango salad together brought the dishes up in our estimation . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59337;TOOLONG ... title= " Grilled pork chop with garlic rice and mango salad at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Grilled pork chop with garlic rice and mango salad at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Chicken inasal with garlic rice and mango salad at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " Postmedia " **28;59439;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430907 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt;The savoury items that I would happily take home from Tamis Caf ? for later enjoyment would be the hefty empanadas , well-stuffed with chicken or beef . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt;In addition to those empanadas , the showcase at the cash showed off many cakes that were thick with buttery frosting . While some were North American faves such as red velvet and mocha cakes , curiosity led us to the ube cake ( $7.99 ) , made with purple yam and a dulce de leche filling , which was moist and distinctive . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59469;TOOLONG ... title= " Ube cake at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Ube cake at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " Postmedia " **28;59505;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1430918 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;div id= " **32;59535;TOOLONG " class= " sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html " &gt; &lt;div class= " sqs-block-content " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;&lt;span style= " font-family : -apple-system , BlinkMacSystemFont , ' Segoe UI ' , Roboto , Oxygen-Sans , Ubuntu , Cantarell , ' Helvetica Neue ' , sans-serif ; " &gt;Purple yam also figured in the ice cream that tops the deluxe halo halo shaved ice dessert that was fully loaded with everything from corn to kidney beans to blobs of jelly to toasted Rice Krispies. **36;59569;TOOLONG ... &lt;div&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;59607;TOOLONG ... title= " Halo halo dessert and turon cupcake at Tamis Caf ? " alt= " Halo halo dessert and turon cupcake at Tamis Caf ? " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;59643;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1432561 " /&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;/div&gt; &lt;p&gt; In the Philippines , turon is a deep-fried dessert that wraps bananas and jackfruit in a crisp wrapper . At Tamis Cafe , a moist , sticky cupcake big enough to satisfy the three of us transported turon&amp;rsquo ; s flavours into a baked good . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the food at Tamis Cafe struck us as a little uneven , it&amp;rsquo ; s nonetheless not surprising that it seems to draw Filipino expats craving back-home tastes . The eatery has unpretentiousness and kind service going for it , and the best dishes would lure us back for a casual and even unique meal . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;59673;TOOLONG " **32;59700;TOOLONG &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;59734;TOOLONG ...
