Food ReviewsDining Out : Farinella serves up pizza and gelato satisfactionhttp : **28;59921;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;59951;TOOLONG ... title= " Cesare Agostini co-owner and pizza specialist at Farinella on Rochester Street , a one-stop spot for pizza and gelato. " alt= " Cesare Agostini co-owner and pizza specialist at Farinella on Rochester Street , a one-stop spot for pizza and gelato. " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;59987;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1429559 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; **38;60017;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; 492 Rochester St. , 613-422-6462 , &lt;a href= " http : //farinellaeats.com " &gt;farinellae ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Thursday 11 a.m. to 9 p.m. , Friday and Saturday 11 a.m. to 10 p.m. , Sunday 11 a.m. to 9 p.m. , closed Monday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; pizza slices cut to order and sold by weight , between $2.99 and $3.60 per 100 grams &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; no steps to front door or washrooms &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Last week , a colleague of mine was in desperate need of a pizza pick-me-up. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The day before we went for lunch , she had what she said was the worst pizza of her life . Ordered from a west-end chain , its crust was tough , but also droopy in spots . The pizza was short on cheese , but also greasy from its meaty components . In short , those slices had no redeeming qualities . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I wanted to restore her faith in pizza by taking her to a pizza purveyor whose standards had not slipped so egregiously . Off we went , then , to Farinella. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Opened in mid-May in a former scooter shop on Rochester Street , this casual eatery in Little Italy has been doing brisk business thanks to impeccably made pizza and a novel-in-Ottawa business model that strives for speedy gratification . And if that weren&amp;rsquo ; t enough , Farinella also turns out some strikingly fresh and varied gelato. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;60057;TOOLONG ... title= " Siblings Cesare and Nina Agostini co-own Farinella on Rochester Street , a one stop spot for pizza and gelato . Cesare works on one of his pizzas . " alt= " Siblings Cesare and Nina Agostini co-own Farinella on Rochester Street , a one stop spot for pizza and gelato . Cesare works on one of his pizzas . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;60093;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1429789 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Before our visit , I&amp;rsquo ; d eaten twice already at Farinella , joining the inevitable peak-hour lineups that fortunately are fast-moving . I&amp;rsquo ; ve savoured enough examples of meaty , cheesy and vegetable-forward goodness to feel that Farinella is basically a sure thing for deep and resolutely traditional pizza satisfaction . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It didn&amp;rsquo ; t take long to figure out the ordering routine here , which is common in Italy yet new to Ottawa . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When the queue brings you to one of Farinella&amp;rsquo ; s counterpersons , you ask for descriptions of the day&amp;rsquo ; s four kinds of pizzas &amp;mdash ; meaty , cheesy , veggie or plain &amp;mdash ; that are ready for consumption . The pizzas on display in front of you are Roman-style creations , with bread-y bases that are oblongs almost one-metre long and toppings that Farinella&amp;rsquo ; s co-owner Cesare Agostini , an Ottawa-raised 27-year-old who spent four years making pizza in Rome to gain his expertise , says are &amp;ldquo ; strictly traditional . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Then you ask for as much as you want of however many pizzas . The staffer cuts that much pizza off the oblong , and your order is weighed and priced , with the most expensive , meaty pizzas going for $3.60 for 100 grams . You pay and then either head home with your box filled with assorted pizza , or take your tray to one of the counters indoors or a no-frills table outside Farinella , which if the place were fancier would be equipped with sun umbrellas . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; When my co-worker bit into a slab of pizza topped with shaved asparagus and mozzarella , it washed away her pizza funk. &amp;ldquo ; Mmmm , &amp;rdquo ; she said . &amp;ldquo ; Where have you been all my life , asparagus pizza ? &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; I&amp;rsquo ; ve asked myself similar questions upon sampling Farinella&amp;rsquo ; s potato pizza , flecked with rosemary and notably peppery , some mushroom pizza that tucked its fungus under dollops of gloriously molten whipped ricotta , pizza that played vibrant fresh tomatoes against their sun-dried cousins and some utterly simple zucchini pizza about which I wouldn&amp;rsquo ; t change a thing . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;60123;TOOLONG ... title= " Assorted pizza ( including rosso , potato , zucchini and salami and olive ) at Farinella " alt= " Assorted pizza ( including rosso , potato , zucchini and salami and olive ) at Farinella " data-portal-copyright= " Peter Hum " **28;60159;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1429742 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; All of those pizzas did just fine without tomato sauce . If the red stuff is your thing , it stars in Farinella&amp;rsquo ; s&amp;nbsp ; basic but satisfying &amp;ldquo ; rossi&amp;rdquo ; pizza . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The choices for carnivores have all hit the spot , including pizzas featuring artichoke and nduja , the spicy and spreadable Italian pork product , or mellow prosciutto cotto blanketed with cheese , or the lucid punch of salami and olives . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; In all , every variety of Farinella pizza that I&amp;rsquo ; ve tried has put a smile on my face with forthright combinations of shining ingredients that play well with one another . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; While the toppings might vary with every visit , the key constant for me is how good the sturdy crust has been . Agostini told me his pizzas are baked first for up to eight minutes at up to 600F before they are topped and then baked for a few more minutes . The results are crusts that can be deliciously crisp but with a tender crumb , somewhere between pillowy focaccia and a shatteringly hard cracker . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;60189;TOOLONG ... title= " Siblings Cesare and Nina Agostini co-own Farinella on Rochester Street , a one-stop spot for pizza and gelato. " alt= " Siblings Cesare and Nina Agostini co-own Farinella on Rochester Street , a one-stop spot for pizza and gelato. " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;60225;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1429785 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , Cesare&amp;rsquo ; s 24-year-old sister and business partner Nina made her own extended trip to Italy , but specialized for four years in making ice cream rather than pizza . While Cesare plied his craft at the bakery Antico Forno Roscioli in Rome , Nina , soon after graduating from high school in Ottawa , went to Italy to study gelato making , eventually becoming the head gelato maker and general manager at Menchetti in Perugia. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; You can taste Nina&amp;rsquo ; s gelato-making cred in the super-fresh ice cream found in Farinella&amp;rsquo ; s showcase . As much as I gravitate to dark chocolate gelato at most opportunities , I&amp;rsquo ; m most likely to order vibrant flavours such as Rhone Valley apricot , Sicilian lemon , Indonesian coconut or sumptuous fior de latte when I next visit . That is , if I don&amp;rsquo ; t order an affogato &amp;mdash ; a cup of vanilla gelato with a shot with excellent , potent espresso on the side . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;60255;TOOLONG ... title= " Nina Agostini serves some gelato at Farinella on Rochester Street , which she co-owns with her brother Cesare . " alt= " Nina Agostini serves some gelato at Farinella on Rochester Street , which she co-owns with her brother Cesare . " data-portal-copyright= " Ashley Fraser " **28;60291;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1429561 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; The restaurant is licensed but does not yet serve alcohol . While customers now bake outside in the summer heat , a winterized patio is a possibility , Cesare says . Cold weather may see ice pops , cakes and &amp;ldquo ; prettier , daintier things&amp;rdquo ; added as dessert options , Nina says . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; As easy as it is to romanticize living and working in Italy , the Agostinis say that returning to Ottawa , where food-business work was in their blood , always beckoned. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;ldquo ; We did not have a glamorous life in Italy , &amp;rdquo ; Nina says . &amp;ldquo ; It was terrible . I hated it , &amp;rdquo ; Cesare adds . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It&amp;rsquo ; s a good thing for Ottawa eaters that the siblings had it hard in Italy and that they returned to their hometown , where they now employ 30 and crank out as many as 150 pizzas in a day . Italy&amp;rsquo ; s loss is Ottawa&amp;rsquo ; s distinct culinary gain , and from now on , it will be hard for me to settle for lesser pizza here . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;60321;TOOLONG " **32;60348;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO IN THE NEWS : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;60382;TOOLONG ... to grow a music festival : Kemptville fest thrives on boomers&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;60417;TOOLONG ... Food Reviews2020 Canadian Culinary Championships : Eight Ottawa-area chefs to vie for spothttp : **36;60535;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;60573;TOOLONG ... title= " Chefs prepare food as ten different chefs compete in Canada 's Great Kitchen Party with their unique dishes at the Shaw Centre . " alt= " Chefs prepare food as ten different chefs compete in Canada 's Great Kitchen Party with their unique dishes at the Shaw Centre . " data-portal-copyright= " Wayne Cuddington " **28;60609;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1425823 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Eight Ottawa-area chefs will put their best plates forward in November at the Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party Ottawa event , hoping to win a berth at next winter&amp;rsquo ; s 2020 Canadian Culinary Championships at the Shaw Centre . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The chefs invited to compete are : Shane Brown of Beckta Dining and Wine Bar ; Ian Carswell of the Black Tartan Kitchen in Carleton Place ; Justin Champagne of Bar Lupulus ; Billy Khoo of fauna food + bar ; Ben Landreville of Sidedoor ; Daniela Manrique of Soca Kitchen ; Razmon Poisson of Orto Trattoria ; and Jason Sawision of Stofa Restaurant . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; At last fall&amp;rsquo ; s event , once the judges had deliberated , Manrique came in second while Sawision finished third . The winner that night , chef Yannick LaSalle of Restaurant Les Foug&amp;egrave ; res in Chelsea , went on to win this year&amp;rsquo ; s Canadian Culinary Championships in Kelowna , B.C. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The competing chefs will square off Nov. 19 at Le Cordon Bleu Ottawa . This qualifier for the national competition will be &amp;ldquo ; a more intimate , boutique-style event&amp;rdquo ; compared to the 2018 Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party at the Shaw Centre and its precursor , the Gold Medal Plates competition , a press release stated . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Tickets for the Nov. 19 event will initially be available bundled with tickets for the national championships , to be held Jan. 31 and Feb. 1 . Later , tickets for the qualifier will be sold for $150 each . Tickets are available by emailing **28;60639;TOOLONG and will later be sold online at greatkitchenparty.com. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The Canada&amp;rsquo ; s Great Kitchen Party qualifiers are held in 12 cities across the country . The events raise funds for B2ten , which supports amateur sports in Canada ; MusiCounts , which promotes music education for children ; and Community Food Centres Canada , which addresses issues in low-income communities through food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;hr&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;ALSO : &lt;/strong&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " https : **33;60669;TOOLONG ... Out : Too many raw deals marred the meals at Happy Fish Raw Barhttp : **37;60822;TOOLONG ... &lt;img src= " https : **34;60861;TOOLONG ... title= " Seafood tower at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Seafood tower at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;60897;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1422401 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Happy Fish Raw Bar&lt;/strong&gt; &lt;br&gt; 330 Elgin St. , 613-656-6689 , &lt;a href= " http : //happyfishrawbar.ca " &gt;happyfish ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Open : &lt;/strong&gt; Tuesday to Sunday 4 p.m. to 2 a.m. , closed Tuesday &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Prices : &lt;/strong&gt; small plates $9 to $18 , mains $18 to $25 , seafood towers $20 to $75 &lt;br&gt; &lt;strong&gt;Access : &lt;/strong&gt; steps to front door &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; During my three dinners at the Happy Fish Raw Bar , the servers have been as happy as , let&amp;rsquo ; s say , fish in a raw bar . They were certainly more chipper than I would have been in their shoes . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The four-month-old bar&amp;rsquo ; s front window faces one reason for displeasure : an unsightly , much-dug-up stretch of Elgin Street . You have to feel for all of the neighbouring businesses with construction on their doorsteps , and we wondered to what extent the rubble and fences had dissuaded potential customers for Happy Fish&amp;rsquo ; s seafood and cocktails . As it was , each time I was there , Happy Fish was empty except for me , my companions and servers extolling the virtues of fresh seafood that arrived at the kitchen daily . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Mind you , we might simply have been unfashionably early , based on some of the posts on the bar&amp;rsquo ; s Facebook page . Given its social media , Happy Fish , which replaced Kat &amp;amp ; Kraken , is for lovers of DJ-powered nightlife , not just fans of fish and shellfish . Judging by my meals , the bar may well please partiers more than customers seeking a memorable seafood dinner . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; The signature item at Happy Fish is its seafood tower , a two-tiered platter starring many mussels , oysters , shrimp , some tuna , and a lobster tail , supported by the common accoutrements ( cocktail sauce , melted butter , mignonette , lemon ) . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;60927;TOOLONG ... title= " Seafood tower at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Seafood tower at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;60963;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1422401 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Few restaurant offerings signal a festive night out like a shareable seafood tower . But Happy Fish&amp;rsquo ; s tower , which at $75 is admittedly at least a good $20 cheaper than seafood towers in Ottawa , still feels like it&amp;rsquo ; s a cheaper version of a deluxe splurge. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Best among the tower&amp;rsquo ; s goodies were the tender and seasoned shrimp and lobster tail . Tuna here was designated as &amp;ldquo ; saku&amp;rdquo ; tuna , a term that was new to me. &amp;ldquo ; Saku , &amp;rdquo ; I learned later , refers not to a type of tuna but to a block-shaped cut of the fish , typically flash-frozen immediately after the fish is caught . Sadly , my taste buds suggested to me that at Happy Fish , &amp;ldquo ; saku&amp;rdquo ; might as well be translated as &amp;ldquo ; without taste . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Meanwhile , the mussels , which made up a disproportionately large part of the tower , were just OK , and the oysters were scarcely better . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; It struck us that while Happy Fish has been much renovated , with chandeliers and green and red velvet seating , there is no raw bar per se where shellfish are shucked and served . As one of my dining companions said , &amp;ldquo ; I like my raw bars to be in plain view . &amp;rdquo ; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; That night , we also ordered one cooked item from Happy Fish&amp;rsquo ; s concise , one-page menu . The lobster mac &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; cheese ( $25 ) , while very buttery , was otherwise bland , under-seasoned and lacking in creaminess , while its lobster was chewy . Again , what we ordered fell short of feeling like the indulgence it should have been . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;60993;TOOLONG ... title= " Lobster Mac and Cheese at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Lobster Mac and Cheese at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61029;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1422400 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; It took the kitchen so long to send out our seafood tower and lobster mac &amp;lsquo ; n&amp;rsquo ; cheese that the server brought us a martini glass filled with bar nibbles to tide us over and later comped us an OK slab of gluten-free chocolate peanut butter cheesecake . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;61059;TOOLONG ... title= " Chocolate peanut butter cheesecake at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Chocolate peanut butter cheesecake at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61095;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1422405 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; At my later dinners , simply prepared small plates tended to disappoint . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; Seafood chowder ( $9 ) was generously portioned and thick with shrimp . But it registered as more mushy than fresh and creamy , and it needed more seasoning . Lobster taquitos ( $15 ) were underwhelming fried corn tortillas filled with enough lobster to add flavour without adding heft. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;61125;TOOLONG ... title= " Seafood chowder at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Seafood chowder at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61161;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1426446 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;61191;TOOLONG ... title= " Lobster taquitos at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Lobster taquitos at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61227;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1425158 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; We liked the good-looking poke nachos ( $18 ) &amp;mdash ; even if they are neither poke nor nachos , really &amp;mdash ; for the crispness of their fried wontons and the flavours of lime , wasabi and ponzu . But the cubes of saku tuna once again tasted like not much at all , and they seemed stingily rationed to boot . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;61257;TOOLONG ... title= " Poke nachos at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Poke nachos at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61293;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1423130 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Best of these appetizers was an order of buttery shrimp ( $17 ) , buttressed by croutons and cheese . While it seemed to have been harshly broiled and under-garlicked , the dish was still enjoyable , if not as pure and potent as classic Spanish gambas al ajillo. &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;61323;TOOLONG ... title= " Garlic shrimp at Happy Fish Raw Bar , pix by Peter Hum " alt= " Garlic shrimp at Happy Fish Raw Bar , pix by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61359;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1425160 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; Shrimp , this time battered and seasoned , starred in a po&amp;rsquo ; boy sandwich ( $19 ) that was alright but for some avocado that had no taste and some Caesar salad on the side that felt perfunctory and excessively oily . The menu&amp;rsquo ; s one nod to red-meat lovers , the &amp;ldquo ; one&amp;rdquo ; burger ( $18 ) , was moist enough , but lacked flavour and seasoning while its bacon verged on incinerated . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;61389;TOOLONG ... title= " Shrimp Po Boy and Caesar Salad at Happy Fish Raw Bar " alt= " Shrimp Po Boy and Caesar Salad at Happy Fish Raw Bar " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61425;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1426448 " /&gt; &lt;img src= " https : **34;61455;TOOLONG ... title= " Burger at Happy Fish Raw Bar , pic by Peter Hum " alt= " Burger at Happy Fish Raw Bar , pic by Peter Hum " data-portal-copyright= " " **28;61491;TOOLONG " 1 " data-license-id= " 1423131 " /&gt; &lt;p&gt; If I were a different kind of patron , I might enjoy Happy Fish Raw Bar for its patio parties , bottle service and karaoke Wednesdays . But as someone squarely focused on eating rather than partying , I&amp;rsquo ; ve only left the place unhappy thanks to too much mediocre food . &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &lt;a href= " **25;61521;TOOLONG " **32;61548;TOOLONG &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : //twitter.com/peterhum " &gt;twitter ... &lt;br&gt; &lt;a href= " http : **34;61582;TOOLONG ... Hum 's restaurant reviews&lt;/a&gt; &lt;/p&gt; &lt;p&gt; &amp;nbsp ; &lt;/p&gt; Peter HumWed , 10 Jul 2019 15:46:58 **45;61618;TOOLONG ... Food Reviews
